# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [1] [Search for nonresonant production of strongly coupled dark matter in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33140)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验利用13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据（138 fb⁻¹）搜索非共振产生的半可见喷注（semivisible jets），采用监督式图神经网络（graph neural network）和无监督自编码器（autoencoder）两种机器学习策略。未观测到暗扇区信号显著超出，监督搜索排除0.5–2.4 TeV的媒介子质量；在稳定暗物质粒子比例较高的模型中，无监督搜索灵敏度更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半可见喷注同时含可见与不可见粒子，可指示强耦合隐扇区（hidden sector）产生的暗物质；其末态特征为缺失横向动量与喷注方向一致，而非如传统对撞机暗物质搜索那样与标准模型粒子背向反冲，因此需要专门的搜索策略。

Method: 信号模型引入一个同时耦合标准模型夸克与暗扇区夸克的双基本标量（bifundamental scalar），后者形成半可见喷注。分析使用两种机器学习策略识别半可见喷注：监督式图神经网络与无监督自编码器。

Result: 未观察到与暗扇区信号假设相符的显著超出。对每种策略给出95%置信水平（confidence level）的信号产生截面上限，并解释为信号模型参数空间的排除；监督搜索排除0.5至2.4 TeV的媒介子质量（依其他模型参数而定）。对含较高稳定暗物质粒子比例的半可见喷注模型，无监督搜索的期望（观测）媒介子质量排除范围比监督搜索高5%（11%）。

Conclusion: 两种机器学习方法在该非共振半可见喷注搜索中均未发现暗扇区信号证据，并给出参数空间排除限；无监督自编码器在暗物质粒子占比较高的信号区域具有更强灵敏度，表明无监督方法可作为监督方法的有效补充。

Abstract: A search is presented for semivisible jets produced in nonresonant processes, using proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, collected with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. These jets, containing both visible and invisible particles, would indicate the presence of dark matter arising from a strongly coupled hidden sector. The signal model includes a bifundamental scalar that couples simultaneously to standard model quarks and dark-sector quarks, the latter forming semivisible jets. The final state therefore includes missing transverse momentum aligned with a jet, rather than recoiling against standard model particles as typically required by conventional collider dark-matter searches. Two separate machine-learning strategies are employed to identify semivisible jets: a supervised graph neural network and an unsupervised autoencoder. No significant excess corresponding to dark-sector signal hypotheses is observed. Upper limits at 95% confidence level on the signal production cross section are determined for each strategy and interpreted as exclusions in the signal model parameter space. The supervised search excludes mediator masses between 0.5 and 2.4 TeV, depending on the other signal model parameters. For signal models with semivisible jets containing higher fractions of stable dark-matter particles, the unsupervised search demonstrates increased sensitivity with 5 (11)% higher expected (observed) mediator mass exclusions than the supervised search.

</details>

Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器收集的约100.87亿J/ψ事例，在J/ψ→γη′过程中搜寻稀有衰变η′→3γ，未观测到显著信号，并在90%置信水平上给出分支比上限（upper limit）B(η′→3γ) < 2.25×10⁻⁵，较此前上限改善四倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: η′→3γ为稀有衰变（rare decay）过程，其分支比（branching fraction）测量有助于检验相关理论模型并深化对η′介子性质的实验约束，此前实验上限较宽松，需要更高灵敏度的搜寻。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的(10087±44)×10⁶个J/ψ事例，经由J/ψ→γη′过程进行η′→3γ搜寻，采用事例数统计并据未观测到显著信号的结果推算出分支比上限。

Result: 未观测到显著信号（no significant signal），在90%置信水平（confidence level）上确定分支比上限为B(η′→3γ) < 2.25×10⁻⁵。

Conclusion: 该上限较此前已有结果改善逾四倍，体现了大统计量J/ψ数据在稀有衰变搜寻中的灵敏度优势，并为η′→3γ提供更严格的实验约束。

Abstract: Using a data sample of $(10087 \pm 44) \times 10^{6} J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector, we search for the rare decay $η^{\prime}\to 3γ$ via the process $J/ψ\toγη^{\prime}$. No significant signal is observed and an upper limit on the branching fraction of $η^{\prime}\to 3γ$ is set at $\mathcal{B}(η^{\prime}\to 3γ) < 2.25\times 10^{-5}$ at the 90\% confidence level. This result improves the previous upper limit by more than a factor of four.

</details>


### [3] [MATHUSLA: A Proposed Long-Lived Particle Detector for High-Luminosity LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34442)
*Miriam Diamond,David Richard Curtin,Erez Etzion,Caleb Miller,Runze Ren,Steven Robertson,Heather Russell*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: MATHUSLA（MAssive Timing Hodoscope for Ultra-Stable neutraL pArticles）是拟建于 CMS 上方地表的超大体积定时量能探测器，用于在高亮度大型强子对撞机（High-Luminosity LHC）运行期间捕捉逃逸主探测器的长寿命粒子（long-lived particles, LLPs）；本文报告 2025 年概念设计报告（Conceptual Design Report, CDR）之后的细化研究结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超出标准模型（Beyond the Standard Model）的多种理论为解释层级问题（hierarchy problem）与暗物质（dark matter）本质而预言长寿命粒子，LHC 可能大量产生质量高于 GeV 的中性 LLP，但其会不被察觉地穿出主探测器，现有探测存在空白。

Method: 探测器由多层掺波长位移光纤（wavelength-shifting fibers）的固体塑料闪烁体（plastic scintillator）构成，光纤连接硅光电倍增管（silicon photomultipliers, SiPMs），并监测一个空的充气衰变体积；CDR 给出 40m×40m×16m 的基准几何与模块化建造方案，本文进一步研究其选址、模块机械工程、数据获取系统（data acquisition）、宇宙线本底（cosmic ray backgrounds）、事例选择判据与触发算法（trigger algorithms）。

Result: 基于上述细化研究，给出了关于探测器潜在位置、模块机械工程、数据获取系统、宇宙线本底、事例选择标准与触发算法的详细结果（摘要未给出具体数值或灵敏度指标）。

Conclusion: 模块化方案使首个模块安装后即可开始取数，为 HL-LHC 期间搜寻中性长寿命粒子提供主探测器之外的补充覆盖；细化研究为后续设计与运行奠定基础。

Abstract: Beyond the Standard Model long-lived particles (LLPs) appear in many theories that address fundamental open questions, such as the hierarchy problem and the nature of dark matter. The Large Hadron Collider (LHC) may be producing copious numbers of neutral LLPs with masses above a GeV, only for these sneaky particles to escape the main detectors without being spotted. To fill this gap, we have proposed MATHUSLA (MAssive Timing Hodoscope for Ultra-Stable neutraL pArticles), which would be constructed on the surface above CMS and would take data during High-Luminosity LHC operations. The detector would be composed of several layers of solid plastic scintillator threaded with wavelength-shifting fibers that connect to silicon photomultipliers (SiPMs), and would monitor an empty, air-filled decay volume. The Conceptual Design Report (CDR) published in 2025 sets out a benchmark geometry of 40m x 40m x 16m, with a modular detector construction scheme that would allow data collection to begin as soon as the first module is installed. This proceeding documents the results of more detailed studies conducted since the CDR on the detector's potential location, mechanical engineering of modules, data acquisition system, cosmic ray backgrounds, event selection criteria, and trigger algorithms.

</details>

Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约27.1亿ψ(3686)事例，首次观测到两个ψ(3686)重子-介子衰变道，并给出其分支比；同时未在相应不变质量谱中发现隐奇异五夸克态候选者N_x(1875)与N_x(2100)的显著信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用高统计量ψ(3686)数据寻找新衰变道并检验隐奇异五夸克态（hidden-strange pentaquark）候选者，以拓展对重子-介子末态与多夸克态结构的实验认识。

Method: 基于BEPCII对撞机上BESIII探测器收集的(2712.4±14.3)×10^6个ψ(3686)事例，重建末态并测量分支比（branching fraction），并在K^+Σ*(1385)^0与K*(892)^0Σ^+不变质量谱中搜寻N_x(1875)和N_x(2100)。

Result: 测得B(ψ(3686)→p̄K^+Σ*(1385)^0+c.c.)=(3.94±0.15_stat.±0.24_syst.)×10^-5与B(ψ(3686)→p̄K*(892)^0Σ^++c.c.)=(4.93±0.14_stat.±0.30_syst.)×10^-5；未见显著信号，给出90%置信水平（confidence level）上限分别为1.58×10^-6和3.09×10^-7。

Conclusion: 两个ψ(3686)衰变道被首次观测，而隐奇异五夸克态候选者的信号未获证实；结果对相关衰变机制与五夸克态假设提供了实验约束。

Abstract: Based on a sample of $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^6$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, we report the first observation of the decays $ψ(3686)\to\bar{p} K^{+} Σ^{*}(1385)^{0}+c.c.$ and $ψ(3686)\to\bar{p} K^{*}(892)^{0} Σ^{+} + c.c.$. The corresponding branching fractions are determined to be $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686)\to\bar{p}K^+Σ^{*}(1385)^{0}+c.c.)=(3.94\pm0.15_{\text{stat.}}\pm0.24_{\text{syst.}})\times10^{-5}$ and $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686)\to\bar{p}K^{*}(892)^{0}Σ^++c.c.)=(4.93\pm0.14_{\text{stat.}}\pm0.30_{\text{syst.}})\times10^{-5}$. Furthermore, we search for the hidden-strange pentaquark candidates $N_{x}(1875)$ and $N_{x}(2100)$ in the invariant mass spectra of $K^{+} Σ^{*}(1385)^{0}$ and $K^{*}(892)^{0} Σ^{+}$, respectively. No significant signals are observed and the upper limits on the branching fractions at the 90% confidence level are $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686)\to\bar{p}N_{x}(1875)\to\bar{p}K^+Σ^{*}(1385)^{0})<1.58\times10^{-6}$ and $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686)\to\bar{p}N_{x}(2100)\to\bar{p}K^{*}(892)^{0}Σ^+)<3.09\times10^{-7}$.

</details>


### [5] [LH$μ$C: A high energy, high luminosity muon-hadron collider at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35154)
*Krzysztof Piotrzkowski*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于大型强子电子对撞机（LH$e$C）基础设施复用而实现缪子-强子对撞（muon-hadron collisions）的可行性进展，即LH$\mu$C方案：1或2 TeV的$\mu^+$束与7 TeV质子束对撞，质心能量达5.3或7.5 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于论证缪子-强子对撞的科学可行性，其中特别强调其在中微子科学（neutrino science）方面的独特前景，并借助既有LH$e$C基础设施以降低成本与实现难度。

Method: 方法上提出LH$\mu$C概念方案：从超冷反缪子（ultra-cold anti-muons）源出发，经缪子增强器（muon booster）至主缪子环；并讨论机器-探测器接口（machine-detector interface）与通用探测器（general-purpose detector）的基本考量。平均亮度接近$10^{34}$cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$。

Result: 结果为给出$\mu^+p$对撞在两种束流能量配置下的质心能量（5.3 TeV与7.5 TeV）及相应亮度指标，并概述该设施的机器与探测器设计要点。

Conclusion: 论文展望LH$\mu$C与LH$e$C、FCC及缪子对撞机（Muon Collider）项目之间存在独特而强大的协同效应（synergies），凸显其作为复合对撞机体系组成部分的价值。

Abstract: The latest developments regarding the feasibility of muon-hadron collisions, achieved through the major reuse of LH$e$C infrastructure, are presented - at the LH$μ$C, a 1 or 2 TeV $μ^+$ beam collides with a 7 TeV proton beam, resulting in the $μ^+p$ centre-of-mass energy of 5.3 or 7.5 TeV, respectively, at an average luminosity close to $10^{34}$cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$. First, a concise scientific motivation is given, including exceptional prospects for neutrino science, and is followed by a presentation of the LH$μ$C concept in a nutshell - from the source of ultra-cold anti-muons, through the muon booster, to the main muon ring. Basic considerations regarding the machine-detector interface and the general-purpose detector are also presented. The paper concludes with an outlook underlying unique and powerful synergies between the LH$μ$C, LH$e$C, FCC and Muon Collider projects.

</details>

Zhukov,J. Zhuo Gao,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于LHCb的$B^0 \to D^{*-}D^0K^+$衰变振幅分析表明，仅用已知激发态$D_s^+$共振无法描述$D^0K^+$谱，引入四夸克态$T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$后拟合显著改善（超过$5\sigma$），首次观测到$T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^- \to D^{*-}D^0$衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$B^0 \to D^{*-}D^0K^+$衰变中$D^0K^+$谱的共振结构，检验已知激发$D_s^+$共振能否完整描述数据，并探究四夸克态（tetraquark state）$T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$在该末态中的贡献。

Method: 利用LHCb实验在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）$\sqrt{s}=7$、8、13 TeV下采集的质子-质子（proton-proton）碰撞数据，对应积分亮度（integrated luminosity）9 fb$^{-1}$，对$B^0 \to D^{*-}D^0K^+$衰变进行共振结构分析（amplitude analysis）。

Result: 仅包含已知激发$D_s^+$共振的模型不足以描述$D^0K^+$谱；加入$T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$后数据描述改善，统计显著性（statistical significance）超过$5\sigma$。该结果对应$T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^- \to D^{*-}D^0$衰变的首次观测（first observation），且所测$T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$性质与先前在$\psi(2S)\pi^-$末态中的测量一致。

Conclusion: $D^0K^+$谱中存在不能被常规$D_s^+$共振解释的成分，四夸克态$T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$是必要的，其性质与在$\psi(2S)\pi^-$末态中的结果相符，支持该态的内部结构不依赖于具体衰变末态。

Abstract: A study of resonant structures in $B^0 \to D^{*-}D^0K^+$ decays is presented. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data collected by the LHCb experiment at center-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s} = 7$, 8, and 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 9 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis demonstrates that the existing known excited $D_s^+$ resonances in the $D^0K^+$ spectrum are insufficient to describe the data. The inclusion of the tetraquark state $T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$ improves the description of the data with a statistical significance exceeding $5σ$. This corresponds to the first observation of the $T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^- \to D^{*-} D^0$ decay. The measured properties of the $T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$ state are consistent with those previously measured in its decay to the $ψ(2S)π^-$ final state.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [7] [Spatial symmetry invariance of three-dimensional Navier-Stokes turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33115)
*Shijun Liao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文证明三维Navier–Stokes方程存在空间对称性不变定理（spatial symmetry invariance theorem, SSIT），并据此指出传统直接数值模拟（direct numerical simulation, DNS）会迅速破坏该对称性，说明DNS预测的湍流轨迹很快被数值噪声污染，甚至统计结果也可能不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNS被广泛用作湍流基准解，但其精度与可靠性从未从数学角度被严格检验；论文旨在为计算流体力学（computational fluid dynamics, CFD）建立绝对基准检验。

Method: 在周期性边界条件下，对确定性三维Navier–Stokes（NS）方程证明SSIT：精确解始终保持与对称初始流场相同的空间对称性；并以该定理作为检验标准，考察传统DNS的湍流模拟行为。

Result: 传统NS湍流DNS很快违反SSIT，其时空轨迹迅速被数值噪声污染；DNS的统计结果也可能对初始流场条件呈敏感性依赖。即空间对称性的丢失并非物理不稳定性，而是流动混沌本性导致的数值伪影（numerical artifact）。

Conclusion: 借助SSIT可直接推出NS湍流的若干关键性质，如“超混沌”（ultra-chaos）、“噪声膨胀级联”（noise-expansion cascade）及“对称初始流场条件的永久记忆”（permanent memory of symmetric initial flow conditions）；后者意味着受对称初始流支配的确定性NS湍流应无“混合”（mixing），即非遍历性（non-ergodicity），与湍流理论中遍历性这一基本假设相冲突。

Abstract: Direct Numerical Simulation (DNS) of the deterministic Navier-Stokes (NS) equations is widely used to provide benchmark solutions of turbulent flow, but its accuracy and reliability have not been rigorously inspected from a mathematical viewpoint. Here we prove a spatial symmetry invariance theorem (SSIT) of three-dimensional NS equations whereby an exact solution of deterministic NS equations under periodic boundary conditions maintains the same spatial symmetry as its symmetric initial flow, forever. SSIT provides an absolute benchmark test for computational fluid dynamics (CFD). It is shown herein that traditional DNS of NS turbulence quickly violates SSIT, revealing that the spatiotemporal trajectory of NS turbulence predicted by DNS soon becomes badly polluted by numerical noise. Importantly, it is found that even the statistical results of DNS might be unstable, i.e., exhibit sensitivity dependence on the initial flow conditions. In other words, the loss of spatial symmetry in traditional DNS of NS turbulence is not a physical instability but instead a numerical artifact caused by the chaotic nature of the flow. Using the SSIT, it is straightforward to deduce several key properties of NS turbulence, including `ultra-chaos', the so-called `noise-expansion cascade', `permanent memory of symmetric initial flow conditions', and so on. Note that permanent memory of symmetric initial flow conditions suggests that NS turbulence governed by deterministic NS equations subject to symmetric initial flow should have no `mixing', corresponding to non-ergodicity, although ergodicity is one of the most fundamental assumptions of turbulence theory.

</details>

Abstract: A frequency-domain response analysis of a wind turbine rotor requires the complete hub-load spectrum, but the customary scalar thrust and torque spectra retain neither the four in-plane components nor the coupling between them. This paper derives the full six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) rotor-load cross-spectral-density (CSD) matrix from the IEC Kaimal turbulence model. By decoupling the spatial order of inflow coherence from the temporal Doppler shift, the formulation introduces a two-index rotational-sampling kernel. Rotor symmetry and azimuthal averaging constrain the observed Doppler carrier strictly to blade-passing harmonics $pBf_{\mathrm{rot}}$, while the azimuthal aerodynamic transfer shifts the spatial coherence order to $pB+s$. A convergence study of the retained azimuthal order $s$ shows that $\lvert s\rvert\le4$ suffices, the assembled CSD then deviating from the full-band result by less than $10^{-5}$ in relative norm. The formulation is compared with OpenFAST simulations of a rigid floating wind turbine over nine wind speeds: the six auto-spectral densities agree closely below 1~Hz, and the present theory over-predicts the spectra near the 6P and 9P rotational harmonics.

</details>

*Christian Puntini*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文用短波不稳定性方法证明Godin（2015）构造的可压缩流体有限振幅不可压波动（等压等密波）在临界陡度以上线性不稳定，稳定性归结为双参数Floquet问题，并给出等熵流体、弱稳定分层与等温大气中的阈值及共振情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Godin（2015）指出均匀重力场中的可压缩流体可支持有限振幅不可压波（每个流体质点保持压强与密度不变），并构造了精确的Gerstner型表面波与边缘波，但其线性稳定性尚未明确，需加以判定。

Method: 采用可压缩流动的短波长不稳定性方法（short-wavelength instability method），将各流体层的稳定性化归为依赖陡度𝔢=e^{ka₂}与ν²=(1−𝔢²)ν₀²的Floquet问题，其中ν₀²=cos²α·N₀²/Ω²，并结合等熵流体与等温大气两种情形作解析求解。

Result: 等熵流体中𝔢>1/3即不稳定，重现并推广Leblanc（2004）对Gerstner波的阈值至可压缩表面波与边缘波；弱稳定分层将阈值降至1/3−(32/81)ν₀²+O(ν₀⁴)。等温大气中ν₀²=κcosα/(kH)，当水平波长接近5.5H/cosα时主次谐共振使临界陡度趋于零；对Hecht等（2002）观测的快速气辉波，对应层处于该共振上，临界陡度典型为0.1而非1/3。

Conclusion: 此类可压缩不可压波存在明确的陡度失稳阈值，且分层、压缩性（尺度高度H）与共振显著改变阈值；不稳定区局限于表面波自由面以下的薄层及边缘波岸线处的楔形区域。

Abstract: Godin (\textit{J. Fluid Mech.}, vol. 767, 2015, pp. 52--64) showed that compressible fluids in a uniform gravity field support finite-amplitude incompressible wave motions, in which every parcel conserves pressure and density, and constructed exact Gerstner-type surface and edge waves, possibly carried by currents. Using the short-wavelength instability method for compressible flows, we show that these waves are linearly unstable above a critical steepness. The stability of each fluid level reduces to a Floquet problem depending on two parameters: the steepness $\mathfrak{e}=e^{ka_2}$ and $ν^2=(1-\mathfrak{e}^2)ν_0^2$, where $ν_0^2=\cos^2α\,N_0^2/Ω^2$, $N_0$ is the buoyancy frequency of the hydrostatic reference state, $α$ the inclination of the plane of motion and $Ω=(gk\cosα)^{1/2}$ the intrinsic orbital frequency. In an isentropic fluid, a level is unstable if $\mathfrak{e}>1/3$, recovering Leblanc's threshold for Gerstner waves (\textit{J. Fluid Mech.}, vol. 506, 2004, pp. 245--254) and extending it to compressible surface and edge waves; weak stable stratification lowers the threshold to $\frac13-\frac{32}{81}ν_0^2+O(ν_0^4)$. In an isothermal atmosphere, $ν_0^2=κ\cosα/(kH)$, where $H$ is the pressure scale height. For horizontal wavelengths near $5.5\,H/\cosα$, principal subharmonic resonance makes the critical steepness vanish. For the fast airglow wave observed by Hecht et al. (\textit{J. Geophys. Res.}, vol. 107, 2002, D20, 4444), which Godin interpreted with his solution, the lidar temperature places the layer in which this interpretation is consistent on this resonance, where the critical steepness is typically 0.1 instead of $1/3$. Instability is confined to a layer beneath the free surface of surface waves and to a wedge at the shoreline of edge waves.

</details>

*Lisen Bi,Fei Wang,Ruize Yin,Britta Nestler*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该摘要提出，受迫液滴的接触角不对称性源自对随外力变化的自由能的极小化，其对外力的响应定义了一种磁化率式量，零外力极限给出不对称性与外力间的普适本构关系，并在多种液体、体积与受力水平下得到定量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态液滴在受迫保持时会出现两侧表观接触角不等的接触角不对称（contact angle asymmetry），但该不对称性与外力之间的关系此前缺乏统一的本构基础（constitutive basis）。

Method: 从理论上证明不对称性来源于对依赖外力的自由能（forcing-dependent free energy）的极小化，并将不对称性对外力的响应定义为一个磁化率（susceptibility），以其零外力取值导出不对称性与外力之间的本构关系。

Result: 所导出的本构关系在不同液体、不同液滴体积及不同受力水平下均获得定量验证，表明其具有普适性（universal）。

Conclusion: 接触角不对称性与外力之间存在由自由能极小化与零外力磁化率共同确定的普适定量关系，为受迫液滴保持行为提供了统一的本构描述。

Abstract: Under applied forcing, static droplet retention is accompanied by contact angle asymmetry, with unequal apparent contact angles on opposite sides. Yet, the relation between the asymmetry and forcing lacks a common constitutive basis. Here, we show that the asymmetry emerges from minimizing the forcing-dependent free energy. The response of the asymmetry to forcing defines a susceptibility whose zero-forcing value yields a universal constitutive relation between the asymmetry and forcing. The constitutive relation is quantitatively validated across liquids, volumes, and forcing levels.

</details>


### [11] [Generative AI in Turbulence: Foundations, Applications, Validations and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35123)
*Luca Biferale,Michele Buzzicotti*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文是一篇立场性综述（position/perspective review），系统审视生成式AI（generative AI）在湍流（turbulence）问题中的应用空间，主张以物理保真度而非视觉真实感作为验证核心，并呼吁建立标准化基准生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流中存在不完整观测与未解析自由度（unresolved d.o.f.），使多重物理上合理的解并存；生成式AI为此类随机闭合（stochastic closures）、超分辨率（super-resolution）、数据同化（data assimilation）等任务提供新途径。但湍流并非常规图像生成问题，视觉真实感、逐点误差与低阶谱一致性均不足以衡量物理保真度（physical fidelity）。

Method: 属综述性工作，不作新模型构建。重点考察扩散模型（diffusion models）、基于分数的方法（score-based methods）及相关概率输运（probability-transport）形式，讨论其在无条件生成、概率重建、降阶建模（reduced-order modeling）、时间演化与无网格表示（mesh-free representations）中的应用。

Result: 提出验证框架应覆盖湍流特有性质：能量传递（energy transfer）、结构函数（structure functions）、反常标度（anomalous scaling）、pdf尾部、相干结构（coherent structures）、守恒律、时间一致性、集合多样性与泛化能力；并指出验证基准（baseline）、数据可得性与可复现性方面的局限。

Conclusion: 文章旨在充当AI与湍流两个社群之间的桥梁：为AI研究者提供湍流入门以评估生成模型的物理相关性，为湍流研究者厘清这些工具的实际功能与局限。最终倡导构建融合开放数据、标准化挑战、隐藏测试、物理感知指标（physics-aware metrics）与失败案例记录的基准生态（benchmark ecosystem）。

Abstract: Generative AI offers new approaches to turbulence problems in which incomplete observations or unresolved d.o.f. admit multiple physically plausible solutions. Potential applications include stochastic closures, super-resolution, data assimilation, uncertainty quantification, and the generation of Eulerian and Lagrangian flow ensembles. However, turbulence is not a conventional image-generation problem. Its multiscale dynamics, intermittency, extreme events, rough velocity fields and intrinsically stochastic transport make visual realism, pointwise error, and low-order spectral agreement insufficient measures of physical fidelity. We examine where generative AI provide novel capabilities with emphasis on diffusion models, score-based methods, and related probability-transport formulations. We discuss their use in unconditional generation, probabilistic reconstruction, reduced-order modeling, temporal evolution, and mesh-free representations. We argue that validation must address turbulence properties: energy transfer, structure functions, anomalous scaling, pdf tails, coherent structures, conservation laws, temporal consistency, ensemble diversity and generalization. Finally, we discuss potential limitations in validation, baseline, data availability and reproducibility, and the need for a benchmark ecosystem combining open data, standardized challenges, hidden tests, physics-aware metrics, and documented failures. This paper serves as a bridge between two communities. For AI practitioners, we provide a fair and accessible introduction to turbulence, essential for appreciating the challenges posed by the field and for assessing the physical relevance of generative models. For turbulent practitioners, we provide an introduction to the key technological aspects of Generative AI, clarifying what these tools actually do, and where their potential and current limitations lie.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the ground state spectrum of a dynamical scalar-fermion-gauge system with an SU(2) gauge group, a scalar doublet, and two degenerate generations of vectorial fermions under the influence of the Brout-Englert-Higgs effect. To this end, we employ lattice methods using the HiRep code. We study the dependence of the system on the (bare) fermion mass and find that the spectrum remains unaffected, up to trivial fermion mass effects. The results are in line with a weakly coupled theory exhibiting the Fröhlich-Morchio-Strocchi mechanism.

</details>

*Akio Tomiya*

Main category: hep-lat

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当Boltzmann权重（Boltzmann weight）非负时，格子场论的作用量构成统计流形（statistical manifold），其Fisher度量可刻画区分两个邻近作用量所需的测量次数。作者希望借助这一几何视角理解连续极限（continuum limit）中重整化与相关/无关算符的结构。

Method: 在大N极限的二维格子Gross-Neveu模型（含Wilson费米子）中，通过辅助场σ分布的Fisher度量研究连续极限（a→0、质量间隙Λ固定）。重正化条件固定一个期望坐标，量纲嬗变（dimensional transmutation）表现为该坐标与自然坐标之间的指数关系；并分别计算长距离模式（动量以物理单位固定）与完整P(σ)的Fisher度量，比较无关方向长度随aΛ的收缩速率。

Conclusion: Fisher几何能区分长距离有效理论与完整格子分布的正则化残余：前者在连续极限下退化为二维参数空间，后者保留格距尺度的方向混合；曲率的发散形式不同于热力学几何的常见假设，临界点在Fisher度量下不可达。

Abstract: When the Boltzmann weight is nonnegative, the actions of a lattice field theory define a family of probability distributions that forms a statistical manifold, and its Fisher metric gives the number of measurements needed to distinguish two nearby actions. In the large-$N$ limit of the two-dimensional lattice Gross-Neveu model with Wilson fermions, we study the continuum limit, in which the lattice spacing $a$ is taken to zero with the mass gap $Λ$ fixed, through the Fisher metric of the distribution of the auxiliary field $σ$. The renormalization condition fixes an expectation coordinate, and dimensional transmutation becomes an exponential relation between it and a natural coordinate. The main result is that the $a\to0$ limits differ between the Fisher metric of the long-distance modes, whose momenta are fixed in physical units, and the Fisher metric of the full $P(σ)$. In the former, the lengths of the infinitely many irrelevant directions that change the kinetic term shrink, relative to the mass-term direction, to order $aΛ\log(1/(aΛ))$ or smaller, and when several modes are combined, only the two directions $(Λ,m)$ of the continuum theory remain. In the latter, the kinetic-term directions shrink at the same rate as the mass-term direction, and the difference in the regularization remains in correlations at distances of the order of the lattice spacing. An exception is the deformation that only adds a contact term to the propagator: as $a\to0$ its length goes to zero in both metrics, and it becomes indistinguishable from a change of the coupling $1/g^2$ (a lattice version of Kutasov's result). The curvature of the coupling plane at fixed kinetic term diverges as $(1/2)\log^2(1/(aΛ))$, not as the correlation volume often assumed in thermodynamic geometry, and the critical point is infinitely far in Fisher distance from any point at finite $a$.

</details>


### [14] [Large-$N$ $π^+π^+$ scattering from twisted Eguchi--Kawai reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35176)
*Claudio Bonanno,Margarita García Pérez,Antonio González-Arroyo,Ken-Ichi Ishikawa,Masanori Okawa,Fernando Romero-López*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在 't Hooft 极限（$N_{\rm f}/N\to 0$）下利用扭曲 Eguchi--Kawai 格点表述（twisted Eguchi--Kawai formulation）计算了大 $N$ QCD 中的 $\pi^+\pi^+$ 散射长度（scattering length），最高取 $N=1156$，并实现受控的大 $N$、连续极限与手征极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大 $N$ QCD 的 't Hooft 极限是理解强相互作用非微扰结构的重要途径，而 $\pi\pi$ 散射长度作为低能强子相互作用的基本量此前缺乏大 $N$ 格点计算；本文旨在填补该空白并检验手征微扰理论（Chiral Perturbation Theory）与 S 矩阵自举（S-matrix bootstrap）的预言。

Method: 采用格点理论的扭曲 Eguchi--Kawai 表述（twisted Eguchi--Kawai formulation），取 $N$ 值至 1156，并选取多组夸克质量与格点间距（lattice spacing）以控制大 $N$、连续与手征极限；将散射长度的手征行为与次领头阶（next-to-leading-order）手征微扰理论预言进行比较。

Result: 描述次领头夸克质量依赖的低能耦合（low-energy coupling）结果与现有 S 矩阵自举（S-matrix bootstrap）界一致；通过将 TEK 结果与 $3\le N\le 6$ 的标准格点 QCD 数据结合，提取出大 $N$ 散射长度的次领头 $\mathcal{O}(1/N)$ 修正。

Conclusion: 该工作首次给出 't Hooft 极限下 $\pi^+\pi^+$ 散射长度的格点计算，验证了与手征微扰理论及 S 矩阵自举的一致性，并借助与低 $N$ 数据的联合分析实现了对 $1/N$ 展开次领头修正的定量提取。

Abstract: We present the first computation of the $π^+π^+$ scattering length in large-$N$ QCD in the 't Hooft limit, $N_{\scriptscriptstyle{\rm f}}/N\to 0$, using the twisted Eguchi--Kawai formulation of the lattice theory. We employ values of $N$ up to 1156 and consider several quark masses and lattice spacings to achieve controlled large-$N$, continuum, and chiral limits. Abstract: Dynamical axion quasiparticles (DAQs) in condensed-matter systmes have emerged as a fascinating platform for exploring axion physics and nonlinear optical phenomena. Specifically, we uncover that DAQs induce optical shear that breaks the $C_{2z}$ symmetry of metagratings without geometric engineering, thereby converting symmetry-protected BICs into axion quasi-BICs (qBICs). In this context, the evolution of the quality factor and topological charge in momentum space, together with the appearance of optical resonances in far field associated with axion qBICs, offers achiral optical signatures for DAQ detection beyond the widely used Kerr and Faraday effects. Abstract: Developing self-optimizing physical systems that learn directly from their response to external stimuli offers a promising route toward adaptive and intelligent machines. We demonstrate the versatility of the protocol through broadband transmission and isolation, coherent perfect absorption, programmable wavefront shaping, and spatially selective sensing. Our results provide a route to self-optimization in complex nonreciprocal wave systems through efficient physical gradient-based learning. The number of experiments required by the protocol does not increase with the number of optimization parameters, and the method remains applicable even when parts of the system are completely inaccessible.

Abstract: Enabled by their controllable optical anisotropy, liquid crystals form a cornerstone of linear optical and display technologies. Outlining such foundational mechanisms opens avenues towards nonlinear liquid crystal devices and ultrafast soft-matter spectroscopies.

Abstract: We investigate a non-minimally coupled scalar field in non-metricity gravity, adopting a non-trivial connection class in spatially flat FLRW spacetimes. After charging, the black hole charge is approximately half of the Wald charge. When the neutral parent particle decays, charge conservation forces the infalling fragment to carry charge while the other fragment carries the opposite charge, so the black hole charge accumulates monotonically. The monopole voltage of the Wald solution decreases linearly with the charge $Q$ and crosses zero to reverse sign at a certain point, so the magnetic gain is gradually neutralized by the charge. The repetitive magnetic Penrose process is therefore forced to terminate and eventually falls back to the mechanical Penrose process. The energy extraction efficiency $>100\%$ predicted by the single magnetic Penrose process will therefore be prevented.

For a stationary extension built on Kerr, the junction is solved exactly in the spin, which fixes how the angular momentum is shared between shell and bulk and where the shell behaves as a rotating fluid. A free-precession model provides an estimate of the gravitational-wave signal of the throat.

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a metric describing rotating acoustic black holes in spherical coordinates. This formulation allows us to obtain exact analytical solutions of the massless Klein--Gordon equation governing scalar perturbations in the corresponding background spacetime. By imposing the appropriate boundary conditions, we derive an exact analytical expression for the quasibound state frequencies of the system. Quasibound states play a central role in the dynamics of black hole analog systems, as they characterize long-lived resonant modes trapped in the vicinity of the effective horizon. Their investigation provides valuable insights into wave propagation, mode stability, and energy confinement in analog spacetimes. Moreover, because acoustic black holes offer experimentally accessible realizations of horizon physics, the study of quasibound states may establish a direct connection between theoretical predictions and laboratory observations, thereby contributing to a deeper understanding of phenomena that also arise in gravitational black hole environments.

Abstract: Radio pulsars emit highly polarized radiation whose polarization properties are commonly described by the Rotating Vector Model (RVM). In this work, we investigate the effects of gravitational light bending and parallel transport on the polarization profile of a pulsar in a hypothetical binary system with a black hole (BH) companion. We combine the RVM with numerical integration of null geodesics to calculate the polarization direction along the photon trajectory and model the orbital-phase-dependent variation of the observed polarization position angle (PPA). We consider both non-spinning and spinning black hole companions and include the evolution of the orbital geometry due to relativistic spin-orbit coupling. For a non-spinning black hole, the change in the observed polarization direction arises from the geometric shift associated with gravitational light bending. For a spinning black hole, frame dragging introduces an additional geometric shift, while parallel transport produces an additional polarization rotation measured relative to our chosen reference construction. We investigate the dependence of these polarization changes on the binary parameters and the orientation of the pulsar spin. We find that gravitational light bending can significantly distort the characteristic S-shaped PPA profile, particularly near superior conjunction, and can reverse its overall sense of variation for the supermassive black hole system considered here. Relativistic spin-orbit evolution also affects the orbital-phase dependence of the polarization profile, particularly in the supermassive black hole system. These results show that relativistic photon propagation can modify both the shape and the phase dependence of pulsar polarization profiles and may provide a useful probe of the geometry of pulsar-BH binaries when such systems are discovered.

The two approaches agree in the perturbative regime, but diverge at stronger tidal fields, where perturbation theory breaks down. In the late inspiral, nonlinear tides reduce the fundamental-mode frequency by ${\sim}10\%$ for equal-mass binaries and add a GW phase shift of $\mathcal{O}(1)$ radian beyond the linear tidal response. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 研究对可重复性（reproducibility）、可复制性（replicability）、稳健性（robustness）与可泛化性（generalizability）有明确需求，但数据集在采集系统、文件格式、实验范式、受试群体、事件标注与记录条件上差异显著，且预处理选择、软件实现、参数设置与计算环境的差异会显著影响分析结果。

Method: 采用综述（overview）形式，考察支持透明与可泛化 EEG 分析的标准、开源软件与计算流水线：讨论 BIDS、HED、FAIR 数据原则与社区报告规范对数据互操作与复用的作用；评述 Brainstorm、EEGLAB、FieldTrip、MNE-Python、SPM 等主流平台及自动化预处理与质量控制（quality control）流水线；并介绍数据集验证、整理、标准化事件标注与队列级可扩展分析的基础设施。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实证数据或定量结果；其产出为对现有标准、软件平台与支撑基础设施的系统性归纳，以及对改进数据互操作性、复用性与分析透明度的路径梳理。

Conclusion: 提出实践建议：标准化数据组织、完整报告分析参数与软件环境、共享代码、预注册（preregistration）、自动化质量控制，以及在多种合理流水线（plausible pipelines）上开展稳健性分析；这些实践共同为跨数据集、跨实验室与跨分析工具的更透明、可重复、可泛化的 EEG 研究结果奠定基础。

Abstract: Reproducibility, replicability, robustness, and generalizability are essential but distinct requirements for reliable electroencephalography (EEG) research. Achieving these goals remains challenging because EEG datasets vary substantially across acquisition systems, file formats, experimental paradigms, participant populations, event annotations, and recording conditions. In addition, differences in preprocessing choices, software implementations, parameter settings, and computational environments can substantially influence analytical results. This overview examines the standards, open-source software, and computational pipelines that support transparent and generalizable EEG analysis. We discuss the role of the Brain Imaging Data Structure (BIDS), Hierarchical Event Descriptors (HED), FAIR data principles, and community reporting guidelines in improving data interoperability and reuse. We review major EEG analysis platforms, including Brainstorm, EEGLAB, FieldTrip, MNE-Python, and SPM, as well as automated preprocessing and quality-control pipelines. We also describe supporting infrastructure for dataset validation, curation, standardized event annotation, and scalable cohort-level analysis. Finally, we provide practical recommendations emphasizing standardized data organization, complete reporting of analysis parameters and software environments, code sharing, preregistration, automated quality control, and robustness analyses across plausible pipelines. Together, these practices provide a foundation for EEG findings that are more transparent, reproducible, and generalizable across datasets, laboratories, and analytical tools.

</details>

### [57] [Chemistry-resolved disagreement between Materials Project-compatible PBE(+U) and r2SCAN stability verdicts in the GNoME materials database](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33101)
*Karthikeya Aduru*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究对 GNoME 发布的 139,541 个材料在 r2SCAN 下重算的标签做按化学分层的重新解析，发现总体稳定率掩盖了固定元素数下 26.7 个百分点的分布差异，并识别出 Materials Project 的 Hubbard-U 元素为不一致性的强关联因素。作者强调所测量的是两套工作流（GGA/GGA+U 与 r2SCAN）之间的一致性差异，而非准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNoME 的稳定性标签基于 Materials Project 兼容的 GGA/GGA+U 工作流，作者仅报告了 84%（二元/三元）与 86.8%（四元）等聚合层面的 r2SCAN 一致性指标。聚合数字可能掩盖化学体系间的巨大异质性，因此需要按化学组成拆解以判断稳定性声明的适用范围。

Method: 对 GNoME 随 r2SCAN 重算一同发布的 139,541 个材料（作者选定而非抽样的子集），以 8,429 个结构原型代理簇（structural-prototype proxy clusters）进行簇自助法区间（cluster bootstrap intervals）估计；比较化学分层的差异率，并以簇稳健逻辑回归（cluster-robust odds ratio）检验 Hubbard-U 元素与不一致性的关联，控制 PBE 分解能与 hull 距离（natural cubic spline）。

Result: 固定元素数下差异达 26.7 个百分点：普通四元金属间化合物为 4.0% [3.3, 4.7]，无镧系含 3d 金属的四元氧化物为 30.7% [27.7, 33.8]；按作者对“All”参考集的读法，两格分别为 14.5% 与 37.4%，差距 22.9 个百分点。含 Hubbard-U 列表元素与不一致性的簇稳健优势比为 3.45 [3.13, 3.80]，选择性施加 U 与 hull 邻近性均无法消除（加入 PBE 分解能样条后仅降至 3.43 [3.07, 3.83]）。在 20% 验证预算下，组成信息与 hull 距离联合覆盖 45.8% 的不一致样本，优于单用 hull 距离的 40.4%。

Conclusion: GNoME 的聚合稳定率不能作为均质指标使用，稳定性可靠性在化学空间上高度不均衡，涉及 Hubbard-U 元素的体系需重点核查。对验证预算分配而言，组成特征应作为 hull 邻近性的补充而非替代。该结果衡量的是两套工作流之间的分歧程度，不构成对任一工作流准确度的评判。

Abstract: GNoME's stability labels rest on a Materials Project-compatible GGA/GGA+U workflow. Its authors report that 84% of discovered binaries and ternaries retain a negative decomposition energy under r2SCAN and 86.8% of tested quaternaries remain stable on the r2SCAN hull. We resolve that aggregate by chemistry over the 139,541 GNoME materials released with r2SCAN recomputations, a subset the dataset authors selected rather than sampled, using cluster bootstrap intervals over 8,429 structural-prototype proxy clusters.
  The aggregate conceals a 26.7-point spread at fixed element count, from 4.0% [3.3, 4.7] for plain quaternary intermetallics (no lanthanide, no 3d metal) to 30.7% [27.7, 33.8] for lanthanide-free 3d-containing quaternary oxides, a pair identified post hoc against a prospectively fixed threshold. Under our reading of the release's "All" reference set the two cells sit at 14.5% and 37.4%, preserving a 22.9-point spread. Presence of a Materials Project Hubbard-U-list element is strongly associated with disagreement, conditional on the other covariates, in a model over all 139,541 materials (cluster-robust odds ratio 3.45 [3.13, 3.80]). Neither selective U application nor hull proximity explains the association away: disagreement stays elevated outside the U-treated regime, and a natural cubic spline in the continuous PBE decomposition energy moves the odds ratio in the 117,018-material model only from 3.53 to 3.43 [3.07, 3.83]. For allocating a verification budget, composition supplements hull proximity rather than replacing it: over held-out cluster splits at a 20% budget, the two together surface 45.8% of disagreements against 40.4% for hull distance alone. An exploratory comparison against previously catalogued materials changes direction between populations. We measure disagreement between two workflows, not accuracy.

</details>


### [58] [On the Doppler Effect in Static Materials: Vortices and Black, White and Worm-Hole Like Wave Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33141)
*Tristan Lawrie,Simon Horsley*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文在静态材料中实现多普勒效应（Doppler effect）：通过对传播方向敏感的波导网络模拟运动波源引起的波压缩与膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多普勒效应依赖波源或观察者的相对运动，本文旨在静态材料中复现运动波源的场分布，以拓展波调控与类比引力（analogue gravity）系统的实现手段。

Method: 将长度为 ℓ 的每条边替换为一对波导，其长度分别为 ℓ/(1∓v/c)，分别对应两个传播方向，从而使晶格上的场分布等价于以速度 v（速度 v）运动的波源所产生的场；基于该网络设计了涡旋（vortex）、类比黑洞（analogue black hole）与虫洞（wormhole）。

Result: 该波导网络在静态结构中再现了运动波源的场图样，并可构建涡旋、类比黑洞与虫洞等结构。

Conclusion: 多普勒效应可在静态材料中通过方向依赖的波导长度设计实现，为在静态平台上模拟运动源及类比时空结构提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate the Doppler effect in a static material. The effect is achieved with a network of thin waveguides whose lengths depend on the direction of travel, chosen to simulate the compression and expansion of the wave experienced by a source travelling with velocity $v$. Each edge of length $\ell$ becomes a pair of waveguides of lengths $\ell/(1\mp v/c)$, one for each direction of travel. On a lattice this gives the field pattern of a moving source. To demonstrate the effect, we design a vortex, an analogue black hole and wormhole.

</details>


### [59] [Evolution of microstructure and crystallographic texture in a novel Zr-4Nb-2Al alloy during hot rotary swaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33247)
*Adam Strnad,Jana Šmilauerová,Josef Stráský,Lukáš Horák,Jan Čapek,Alžběta Endrychová,Martin Holčák,Jiří Zýka,Miloš Janeček,Petr Harcuba*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用多步感应加热辅助热旋锻（induction-assisted hot rotary swaging）工艺加工新型双相（dual-phase）Zr-4Nb-2Al合金，在β单相区与α+β两相区中系统考察了组织、织构与力学性能的演化。总面缩率达72%，并实现了层片组织破碎、球化与晶粒细化，同时显著降低径向硬度不均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索针对新型双相锆合金（Zr-4Nb-2Al）的有效热变形路径，以通过分步相区变形同时调控显微组织（microstructure）、晶体织构（crystallographic texture）与力学性能。

Method: 先用电阻率测量（electrical resistivity measurements）确定相变温度以选择工艺条件（880°C的β相区与780°C的α+β相区）；采用带热成像测温控制的感应加热（induction heating with thermographic temperature control）进行多步旋锻，将12 mm棒材减至6.3 mm，并沿流程表征组织、织构与力学响应。

Result: β相区旋锻将毫米级原始β晶粒细化至约100 μm，随后冷却形成细层片α+β组织；α+β相区变形破坏层片结构，促进α相球化（globularisation）与再结晶并细化β相。α相形成明显的⟨11-20⟩α∥SD纤维织构，β相以⟨110⟩β∥SD为主并含⟨111⟩β∥SD组分；残余β相在整个过程中保持在约12-15 wt.%。后续α+β旋锻使压缩屈服强度（compressive yield strength）由1103 MPa降至839 MPa，塑性变形能力提高，最大断裂应变达32%，径向硬度不均匀性逐步消除。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在选定相区中进行感应加热辅助旋锻可有效调控双相锆合金的组织、织构与力学响应，是一种可行的组织与性能定制工艺路线。

Abstract: A multi-step induction-assisted hot rotary swaging route was developed for the novel dual-phase Zr-4Nb-2Al alloy to investigate the evolution of microstructure, crystallographic texture, and mechanical properties during sequential deformation. Phase-transformation temperatures determined by electrical resistivity measurements were used to select processing conditions in the single-phase $β$ field at 880°C and the $α+β$ field at 780°C. Induction heating with thermographic temperature control enabled reduction of the initial 12 mm rod to 6.3 mm, corresponding to a total area reduction of 72%. Swaging in the $β$ field refined millimetre-sized prior-$β$ grains to approximately 100 $μ$m, while subsequent cooling produced a fine lamellar $α+β$ microstructure. Further deformation in the $α+β$ field disrupted the lamellar structure, promoted globularisation and recrystallisation of the $α$ phase, and refined the $β$ phase. The microstructural evolution was accompanied by a pronounced $\langle 11\bar{2}0\rangle_α\parallel\mathrm{SD}$ fibre texture in the $α$ phase, whereas the $β$ phase exhibited predominantly $\langle 110\rangle_β\parallel\mathrm{SD}$ together with a $\langle 111\rangle_β\parallel\mathrm{SD}$ component. The phase constitution remained comparatively stable, with approximately 12-15 wt.% retained $β$ throughout processing. Subsequent $α+β$ swaging decreased the compressive yield strength from 1103 to 839 MPa while increasing plastic deformability, with a maximum fracture strain of 32%. Radial hardness heterogeneity was progressively eliminated. These results demonstrate that induction-assisted rotary swaging in selected phase fields provides an effective route for tailoring microstructure, texture, and mechanical response in dual-phase zirconium alloys.

</details>


### [60] [Winding-Controlled Topological Descendants of Antialtermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33252)
*Sahar Izadi Vishkayi,Carmine Autieri,Meysam Bagheri Tagani*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文指出奇宇称自旋劈裂只约束磁性能带交叉，并不决定其拓扑后代；孤立反演扇区的陈数（Chern number）贡献由其横向质量对（transverse mass pair）的相位绕转（phase winding）控制，当质量与切向速度同时反号时相邻狄拉克点贡献相加而非抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识以宇称与磁结构（如奇宇称自旋劈裂的多项式阶数）标记磁性能带交叉，但作者认为这种对称性标签不能决定交叉所产生的拓扑后代，需要区分“能带交叉的对称性”与“支配其拓扑的质量结构”。

Method: 通过构造周期性四方 \(h\)-波与六方 \(j\)-波模型（在指定互补质量下）分析动量平面上的同手性外尔点（Weyl points）与有隙切片陈数（gapped-slice Chern numbers）；并用微观四能带 \(p\)-波模型论证一阶横向质量的反幺正障碍（antiunitary obstruction）；最后对 LaMnAu\(_5\) 的共面自旋涡旋织构（coplanar spin-vortex texture）进行第一性原理计算。

Result: 四方 \(h\)-波与六方 \(j\)-波模型分别在一个动量平面上承载 8 个和 12 个同手性外尔点，对应有隙切片陈数为 \(|C|=4\) 与 \(6\)；四能带 \(p\)-波模型给出四分之一填充下对称性破缺的 \(C=1\) 相；LaMnAu\(_5\) 计算揭示 \(h\)-波节点图样、各向异性自旋–轨道重构（spin–orbit reconstruction）以及在相反自旋旋转下反转的镜面通道谱响应。

Conclusion: 结果将奇宇称磁母态（odd-parity magnetic parent）的对称性与支配其后代拓扑的质量结构区分开来：拓扑绕转不等同于标记磁母态的多项式阶数。

Abstract: Odd-parity spin splitting constrains magnetic band crossings but does not determine their topological descendants. We show that the Chern contribution of an isolated inverted sector is controlled by the phase winding of its transverse mass pair: when the masses and tangential velocities reverse together, contributions from successive Dirac points add rather than cancel. This distinguishes topological winding from the polynomial degree labeling the magnetic parent. For specified complementary masses, periodic tetragonal $h$- and hexagonal $j$-wave models host eight and twelve same-chirality Weyl points on a momentum plane, with gapped-slice Chern numbers $|C|=4$ and $6$, respectively. A microscopic four-band $p$-wave model identifies an antiunitary obstruction to first-order transverse masses and demonstrates a symmetry-broken $C=1$ phase at quarter filling. First-principles calculations for the proposed coplanar spin-vortex texture of LaMnAu$_5$ reveal the $h$-wave nodal pattern, its anisotropic spin--orbit reconstruction, and a mirror-channel spectral response that reverses under opposite spin rotations. Together, these results distinguish the symmetry of an odd-parity magnetic parent from the mass structure controlling the topology of its descendants.

</details>


### [61] [Exploring Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures for Self-Supervised Representation Learning of Powder X-ray Diffraction Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33310)
*Ming-Chiang Chang,Brian DeCost*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出采用自监督联合嵌入预测架构（Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture, JEPA）从一维粉末X射线衍射（powder X-ray diffraction, XRD）图谱中学习表征嵌入，在潜空间中完成掩码自预测训练，并在下游任务中以轻量探针实现接近大型监督视觉Transformer的晶体学分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量合成与自驱动实验室（self-driving laboratories）的发展使自动化XRD图谱分析成为加速材料发现的关键；但传统矩阵分解（matrix factorization）方法在晶格变化引起非线性峰移时失效，监督学习（supervised learning）方法在多相混合物情形下面临组合爆炸（combinatorial explosion）问题。

Method: 采用自监督JEPA框架，直接在一维衍射图谱的潜空间（latent space）中优化掩码自预测损失（masked self-prediction loss），从而捕捉高层晶体学语义；下游评估使用冻结表征上训练的轻量注意力探针（attentive probes）完成空间群分类（space group classification）与晶格参数估计（lattice parameter estimation）。

Result: 轻量探针在冻结JEPA表征上取得的空间群分类与晶格参数估计精度接近规模大得多的监督视觉Transformer（vision transformer）；潜空间探测显示学到的表征空间能自然组织物理变换，并为峰展宽（peak broadening）分离出平滑轨迹；在单相晶体表征间的潜路径上解码可生成逼真的多相混合图谱。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无需显式多相监督即可涌现出连续的结构表征，说明自监督潜空间预测为自动化XRD图谱分析提供了可扩展且具结构语义的替代方案。

Abstract: Powder X-ray diffraction (XRD) is the primary high-throughput method for resolving crystal structures, a cornerstone of materials science. As high-throughput synthesis and self-driving laboratories advance, automated XRD pattern analysis has become vital for accelerating materials discovery. However, traditional matrix factorization methods fail when lattice variations cause non-linear peak shifts. At the same time, supervised learning approaches face a combinatorial explosion when handling multi-phase mixtures. To overcome these challenges, we adopt a self-supervised Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) to learn representation embeddings from 1D diffraction patterns. By optimizing a masked self-prediction loss directly within the latent space, the framework captures high-level crystallographic semantics. Downstream evaluations show that lightweight attentive probes trained on frozen JEPA representations achieve space group classification and lattice parameter estimation accuracy close to much larger supervised vision transformers. Furthermore, latent probing reveals that the learned representation space naturally organizes physical transformations, isolating smooth trajectories for peak broadening. Interestingly, decoding along latent paths between single-phase crystal representations yields realistic multi-phase mixture patterns, demonstrating an emergent continuous structure learned without explicit multi-phase supervision.

</details>


### [62] [OpenFerro: a unified framework for mesoscale ferroic dynamics and enhanced sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33335)
*Pinchen Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: OpenFerro 是一个统一的晶格-场动力学框架，整合灵活哈密顿量、自动微分、并行执行与增强采样，可在中等计算资源下实现介观尺度的原子分辨率铁性材料模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁性材料（ferroic materials）建模需要将微观涨落与介观尺度上的集体动力学相连接，而该多尺度连接在方法学上存在挑战。

Method: 提出 OpenFerro 框架，结合灵活哈密顿量（Hamiltonians）、自动微分（automatic differentiation）、并行执行与增强采样（enhanced sampling），统一处理铁电畴运动、磁无序及自旋-晶格耦合热力学。

Result: 在四块图形处理器（GPUs）上，含长程偶极相互作用（long-range dipolar interaction）的 BaTiO$_3$ 体材料基准达到 $840^3$ 晶胞（约30亿原子）；模拟了横向跨度 0.8 μm 的 PbTiO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$ 超晶格（superlattice）极化织构与直径 200 nm 的 BaTiO$_3$ 纳米颗粒，均保持晶胞分辨率；并通过增强采样在无预设多项式形式下重构了极化自由能面（free-energy surface）。

Conclusion: 在中等计算资源下，OpenFerro 实现了传统上由连续介质模型（continuum models）研究的介观尺度上的原子级分辨率（atomistic-resolution）模拟。

Abstract: Modeling ferroic materials requires connecting microscopic fluctuations to collective dynamics over mesoscales. We introduce OpenFerro, a unified lattice-field dynamics framework that combines flexible Hamiltonians, automatic differentiation, parallel execution, and enhanced sampling. The same framework can simulate ferroelectric domain motion, magnetic disordering, and coupled spin-lattice thermodynamics. On four graphics processors, a bulk BaTiO$_3$ benchmark with long-range dipolar interaction reaches $840^3$ unit cells, representing 3 billion atoms through the retained lattice variables. We simulate polarization textures in a PbTiO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$ superlattice spanning $0.8~μ\mathrm{m}$ laterally and a BaTiO$_3$ nanoparticle 200~nm in diameter, retaining unit-cell resolution. We also demonstrate enhanced-sampling simulations that reconstruct the polarization free-energy surface of bulk BaTiO$_3$ without a prescribed polynomial form. With moderate computational resources, OpenFerro achieves atomistic-resolution simulations at mesoscopic scales traditionally studied with continuum models.

</details>


### [63] [First-Principles Investigation of the Structural, Electronic, Optical, Elastic, and Thermoelectric Properties of X2MoSeO6 (X = K, Rb, Cs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33457)
*Sha Ajharul Islam Forhat,Md. Rakibul Hasan Rakib,Md. Al Noman Ruhin,Md Tashfiq Bin Kashem*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）与半经典玻尔兹曼输运（semi-classical Boltzmann transport）计算，系统研究了 X2MoSeO6（X = K、Rb、Cs）的电子、力学、光学与热电性能，揭示碱金属取代对结构与物性的调控规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碱金属取代如何影响钼硒酸盐氧化物（molybdenum selenite oxides）耦合物理性质，是建立其成分-结构-性能关系（composition-structure-property relationships）的关键；X2MoSeO6 家族中较轻成员已有研究，而较重碱金属化合物在电子、力学、光学与热电综合响应方面仍缺乏探索。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory）结合半经典玻尔兹曼输运（semi-classical Boltzmann transport）计算，对 X2MoSeO6（X = K、Rb、Cs）进行综合第一性原理研究，涵盖结构优化、电子能带、弹性、光学介电与热电输运性质。

Abstract: Understanding how alkali-metal substitution influences the coupled physical properties of molybdenum selenite oxides is essential for establishing their composition-structure-property relationships. Here, a comprehensive first-principles investigation of X2MoSeO6 (X = K, Rb, and Cs) is presented using density functional theory and semi-classical Boltzmann transport calculations. K2MoSeO6 exhibits the strongest elastic anisotropy, whereas Rb2MoSeO6 possesses the highest static electronic dielectric constant (4.614) and maximum absorption coefficient (2.291*106 cm-1). ### [64] [Charge state and hydration of vacancies in silicon and silicon carbide in contact with water: A grand-canonical first-principles stability map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33469)

TL;DR: 该工作将空位的电荷态与氢/氧含量纳入统一的大势（grand potential）最小化框架，给出以费米能级与氢化学势为坐标的稳定性图谱，并通过计算氢电极（computational hydrogen electrode）映射到 pH 与电极电位。结果表明，在水接触条件下裸空位从不是最低能态，氢钝化与羟基化行为由 pH 和偏压共同控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上半导体中空位（vacancy）的状态仅用费米能级描述，但在湿法刻蚀、电化学处理或潮湿环境下的硅与碳化硅中，空位还会从水中摄取氢和氧，其数量由 pH 与电极电位决定，单一费米能级图像不足。

Method: 对空位复合体的电荷态及氢/氧含量联合最小化大势（grand potential），借助计算氢电极（computational hydrogen electrode）将氢化学势与 pH、电位关联；采用自旋极化密度泛函理论（spin-polarized density functional theory, DFT）在 64 原子超胞中参数化，并计入束缚 H 与 O 的振动自由能，覆盖硅空位及其 H、O、OH 复合体与 3C-SiC 的两种空位。

Result: 氢以协同（cooperative）方式结合于硅空位，使其由裸空位直接跃迁至完全钝化的 VH4；接触水时裸空位永非最低能态。近中性 pH 下空位以 A center 形式结合水中氧，零偏压下约 pH 5 以下形成顺磁性羟基复合体，完全钝化为 VH4 需阴极偏压。PBE 计算中费米能级对这些边界影响甚微，因受主能级位于导带边或其上；SiC 中氢首次结合于硅空位释放 1.97 eV，而在碳空位（Z1/2 中心）不束缚，故仅靠水中氢难以钝化，除非施加阴极偏压。

Conclusion: 空位的稳定性需在费米能级与氢化学势的二维空间中描述，水环境中的氢氧摄取显著改变其基态，钝化与缺陷构型由 pH 与电极电位（偏压）决定；SiC 的 Z1/2 中心在常规条件下不易被水中氢钝化。

Abstract: The state of a vacancy in a semiconductor is normally described by the Fermi level alone. In wet-etched, electrochemically processed, or humid silicon and silicon carbide, a vacancy also takes up hydrogen and oxygen from water, in amounts set by pH and electrode potential. We treat both effects together by minimizing the grand potential of a vacancy complex over its charge state and hydrogen/oxygen content. The result is a stability map in Fermi level and hydrogen chemical potential, which the computational hydrogen electrode relates to pH and potential. We parameterize it with spin-polarized density functional theory in 64-atom cells, including the vibrational free energy of bound H and O, for the silicon vacancy, its H, O, and OH complexes, and both vacancies of 3C-SiC. Hydrogen binds cooperatively to the silicon vacancy, which goes directly from bare to fully passivated $V$H$_4$. In contact with water the bare vacancy is never the lowest-energy state. Near neutral pH it binds oxygen from water as the A center, the paramagnetic hydroxyl complex forms below about pH 5 at zero bias, and full passivation to $V$H$_4$ requires cathodic bias. In PBE the Fermi level barely moves these boundaries, because the acceptor levels lie at or above the conduction-band edge. In SiC the first hydrogen binds to the silicon vacancy by 1.97 eV but is unbound at the carbon vacancy (the $Z_{1/2}$ center), so hydrogen from water alone should not passivate it except under cathodic bias.

### [65] [Phonon-Dressed Quasiparticles in Epitaxial Scandium Nitride Resolved by Soft X-ray ARPES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33533)
*Sourav Rudra,Dheemahi Rao,Meng-Jie Huang,Subhajit Manna,Renuka Karanje,Aritra Dey,Sanjoy Kr Mahatha,Samuel Poncé,Bivas Saha*

TL;DR: 该研究利用软X射线角分辨光电子能谱（soft x-ray angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）在p型氮化钪（ScN）中给出电子-声子相互作用（electron-phonon interaction）致准粒子重整化（quasiparticle renormalization）与谱展宽的直接动量分辨实验证据，并以第一性原理谱函数计算定量复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子相互作用决定半导体的准粒子能量、寿命与温度依赖能带结构，但直接动量分辨的实验证据仍稀缺，在氮化物半导体（nitride semiconductors）中尤为不足。

Method: 采用软X射线角分辨光电子能谱测量p型ScN的准粒子谱，并与基于电子-声子耦合的第一性原理谱函数（spectral function）计算对照；采用非绝热Allen-Heine-Cardona方法计算直接带隙零点重整化（zero-point renormalization）。

Result: 计算复现了价带顶以下约2.3 eV处的显著准粒子扭折（quasiparticle kink）、源于长程电子-声子耦合的声子伴峰（phonon satellite features），以及-124.3 meV的直接带隙零点重整化；并表明ScN中温度诱导直接带隙异常增大源于热膨胀与电子-声子耦合的联合效应，室温附近两者贡献相当。

Conclusion: ScN被确立为可视化电子-声子耦合的模型平台，并为半导体准粒子重整化的预测性第一性原理描述提供定量基准。

Abstract: Electron-phonon interactions govern quasiparticle energies, lifetimes, and temperature-dependent band structures in semiconductors. However, direct momentum-resolved experimental evidence of their impact on quasiparticle electronic structure remains scarce, particularly in nitride semiconductors. Here, we present conclusive experimental evidence of electron-phonon interaction-induced quasiparticle renormalization and pronounced spectral broadening in p-type scandium nitride (ScN), using soft x-ray angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. First-principles spectral-function calculations due to electron-phonon coupling reproduce the measured spectra, capturing a pronounced quasiparticle kink ~2.3 eV below the valence-band maximum, phonon satellite features arising from long-range electron-phonon coupling, and a direct bandgap zero-point renormalization of -124.3 meV within the non-adiabatic Allen-Heine-Cardona approach. We further show that the anomalous temperature-induced increase in the direct bandgap in ScN originates from the combined effects of thermal expansion and electron-phonon coupling, contributing comparably near room temperature. Our results establish ScN as a model platform for visualizing electron-phonon coupling and provide a quantitative benchmark for predictive first-principles descriptions of quasiparticle renormalization in semiconductors.

</details>


### [66] [First-order magnetostructural transition in Ni-Mn-Sn powder - A matter of particle and grain size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33556)
*Johannes Puy,David Koch,Enrico Bruder,Sagar Ghorai,Benedikt Beckmann,Heiner Gutte,Volodymyr Baran,Oliver Gutfleisch,Franziska Scheibel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用气雾化（gas-atomized）Ni-Mn-Sn 粉末，结合多种表征手段将尺寸效应与加工缺陷影响解耦，揭示一级磁结构相变（FOMST）的转变区间与热滞（thermal hysteresis）随颗粒/晶粒尺寸的变化规律，并归因于颗粒尺寸、晶界与自由表面的共同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Ni-Mn 基 Heusler 合金中，磁热效应（magnetocaloric effect）源自一级磁结构相变，但颗粒与晶粒尺寸对热滞、转变宽度及形核-长大机制（nucleation and growth）的影响尚不清楚；机械研磨会引入残余应力和加工缺陷，难以分离本征尺寸效应。

Method: 采用气雾化 Ni-Mn-Sn 粉末，避免研磨引入的加工影响；结合微观结构表征与变温 XRD、磁测量（magnetometry）、量热法（calorimetry）及单颗粒测量（single-particle measurements），考察 FOMST 的尺寸依赖性，并用 EBSD 分析晶粒尺寸。

Result: 转变区间 (Ms-Mf) 与 (Af-As) 随颗粒尺寸减小而收窄，对约 20–30 微米以下的单晶颗粒趋近于零；热滞随颗粒尺寸增大而降低，在 100 微米以上的大多晶颗粒中降至 17 K 下限（lower limit）。EBSD 显示晶粒尺寸近乎恒定，表明颗粒尺寸减小伴随多晶向单晶颗粒的转变。

Conclusion: 多晶颗粒中晶界（grain boundaries）作为相变推进的势垒，使多个晶粒在较宽温区逐步转变；单晶颗粒缺乏优先形核位点（preferred nucleation sites）而热滞更大，但因无转变传播势垒，整颗粒在窄温区内完成转变。FOMST 的尺寸依赖性由颗粒尺寸、晶界与自由颗粒表面（free particle surface）的相互作用决定。

Abstract: In Ni-Mn-based Heusler alloys, the magnetocaloric effect originates from a first-order magnetostructural transition (FOMST). However, the influence of particle and grain size on thermal hysteresis, transition width, and nucleation and growth mechanism remains poorly understood, as mechanical grinding can introduce residual stresses and processing-induced defects. In this study, we examined the size dependence of the FOMST in gas-atomized Ni-Mn-Sn powder by combining microstructural characterization with temperature-dependent XRD, magnetometry, calorimetry and single-particle measurements, enabling decoupling of size dependence from processing-related influences. In polycrystalline particles, multiple grains transform gradually over an extended temperature interval because grain boundaries act as barriers to the progression of transformation. In contrast, single-grain particles lack preferred nucleation sites, resulting in larger hysteresis, while the absence of barriers to transformation propagation causes the entire particle to transform in a narrow range. These results demonstrate that the size dependence of the FOMST is governed by the interplay between particle size, grain boundaries, and the free particle surface.

</details>

Motivation: 铁磁/重金属（ferromagnetic/heavy-metal, FM/HM）三层异质结构是研究结构与磁性相互作用的通用平台，但 Co 层厚度对 Co/Ir/Co 体系结构-磁性关联的系统影响尚需阐明。

Result: 结构上呈纳米晶（nanocrystalline），晶粒尺寸随 Co 厚度增大；界面粗糙度比在 t_Co = 4 nm 达最大。ZFC-FC 显示厚度依赖的特征温度与多重弛豫机制。饱和磁化（saturation magnetization）随厚度增加，矫顽力（coercivity）呈显著非单调变化、在中间厚度增强；低温回线在 t_Co = 2–4 nm 出现类交换偏置（exchange-bias-like）偏移。

Structural characterization using grazing-incidence X-ray diffraction (GIXRD) and high-resolution transmission electron microscopy (HRTEM) reveals a nanocrystalline structure, with the crystallite size increasing with Co-layer thickness. Temperature-dependent zero-field-cooled and field-cooled (ZFC-FC) magnetization measurements reveal thickness-dependent characteristic temperatures and multiple relaxation regimes in selected samples, indicating complex thermally activated magnetic behaviour. In contrast, field-dependent hysteresis measurements reveal that the saturation magnetization increases with Co-layer thickness, whereas the coercivity exhibits a pronounced non-monotonic variation, with enhanced values at intermediate thicknesses. Moreover, low-temperature hysteresis loops display thickness-dependent exchange-bias-like shifts, particularly for $t_{\mathrm{Co}} = 2$-$4$ nm. These findings highlight the strong influence of Co-layer thickness on the structural and magnetic properties of Co/Ir/Co trilayers.

TL;DR: 该工作识别并表征了"电结构双稳态（electrostructural bistability）"这一物理现象，并建立统一的电导双稳态（conductivity bistability）理论，将弱极化绝缘体中的电热双稳态与强极化材料中的电结构双稳态纳入同一框架，为快速、精确的缺陷工程提供可行途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缺陷控制方法往往缓慢且不精确，而缺陷对材料功能至关重要，因此需要一种快速、精确的缺陷调控路径。

Method: 建立广义的电导双稳态理论，并以 TiO₂ 等强极化材料为对象开展实验验证，通过控制外加电流调控体内均匀缺陷浓度，并采用突然切断电流的方式将系统淬火至亚稳态。

Result: 理论与实验一致表明，电结构双稳态材料的体均匀缺陷浓度可通过电流快速、确定性地调控；突然断电可使系统淬火进入具有不同性质的长寿命室温亚稳态（metastable states）。

Conclusion: 电结构双稳态为缺陷工程提供了快速、可规模化的实用途径，并与非易失存储器件（nonvolatile memory devices）直接相关。

Abstract: Structural defects are central to the functionalities of materials. However, conventional approaches to defect control are often slow and imprecise. This work identifies and characterizes a distinct physical phenomenon of electrostructural bistability that provides a rapid and precise pathway for engineering materials with defect-controlled physical properties. In agreement with the theory, experiments on TiO$_2$ demonstrate that bulk-homogeneous defect concentrations in electrostructurally bistable materials can be tuned rapidly and deterministically by controlling applied electric current. Abruptly interrupting the current quenches the system into long-lived, room-temperature metastable states with distinct properties. This establishes a practical, rapid, and scalable pathway for defect engineering with direct relevance to nonvolatile memory devices.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统认识超高温陶瓷碳化物（ultrahigh-temperature carbides, UHTCs）在电子结构、声子与电子热输运、光学响应及激光辐照热响应方面的差异，为不同族属碳化物与SiC的性能对比提供统一的理论描述。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论结合玻尔兹曼输运计算，考察TiC、ZrC、HfC、VC、NbC、TaC与SiC的电子结构、晶格与电子热导、光学性质；并建立一维与三维连续介质热传导（continuum heat-conduction）模型，模拟指定激光辐照条件下的温度分布。

Result: TMCs呈金属性电子结构，SiC为半导体；TMCs中声子与电子对热输运的贡献随温度升高而电子贡献增强。V族碳化物晶格热导率显著低于IV族，并伴随明显的Kohn异常（Kohn anomalies）与声子色散改变，提示费米面拓扑（Fermi-surface topology）、声子动力学与晶格热输运间可能存在关联；SiC因长寿命声学声子而晶格热导率高且无电子贡献。金属性碳化物在可见与红外波段反射率较高，SiC红外反射率较低。激光致热的温度分布取决于材料热学与光学性质及样品几何形状。

Conclusion: IV族与V族碳化物及SiC在电子结构、热输运机制与光学响应上存在系统性差异，其中族属相关的费米面拓扑可能调控声子动力学与晶格热导；激光致热响应由材料本征性质与几何构型共同决定。

Abstract: Using first-principles density functional theory and Boltzmann transport calculations, we systematically investigate the electronic structure, lattice and electronic thermal transport, optical response, and laser-induced heating of ultrahigh-temperature carbides (UHTCs), including Group IV (TiC, ZrC, and HfC) and Group V carbides (VC, NbC, and TaC), and SiC. The transition-metal carbides (TMCs) exhibit metallic electronic structures, whereas SiC exhibits semiconducting character. The calculated thermal transport properties reveal distinct contributions from phonons and electrons, with electronic heat transport becoming increasingly important at elevated temperatures in TMCs. The Group V carbides exhibit substantially lower lattice thermal conductivity than their Group IV counterparts, accompanied by pronounced Kohn anomalies and modified phonon dispersions associated with their distinct electronic structures, suggesting a possible connection between Fermi-surface topology, phonon dynamics, and lattice thermal transport. In contrast, SiC exhibits comparatively high lattice thermal conductivity, consistent with its long-lived acoustic phonons, but no electronic contribution due to its semiconducting nature. Optical calculations reveal relatively high reflectivity in the visible and infrared spectral ranges for the metallic carbides, whereas SiC exhibits lower reflectivity in the infrared region. Finally, one-dimensional and three-dimensional continuum heat-conduction models are employed to compare the thermal responses of these materials under specified laser irradiation conditions. The predicted temperature distributions depend on the thermal and optical properties of the materials as well as the specimen geometry.

</details>


### [71] [Annealing-atmosphere control of paramagnetic defects in ZnO microparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33870)
*D. Savchenko,M. Holiatkina,A. Prokhorov,A. Vasin,E. Kalabukhova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用变温电子顺磁共振（electron paramagnetic resonance, EPR）谱学，揭示 N2 与空气气氛高温退火对 ZnO 微米颗粒顺磁缺陷与电子性质的影响：退火气氛决定缺陷种类构成及其温度依赖行为。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温退火气氛对 ZnO 微米颗粒中顺磁缺陷（paramagnetic defects）的种类、数量及电子响应的影响尚缺乏系统的温度分辨表征，需要明确气氛与缺陷构型之间的对应关系。
