Result: N2 退火样品中出现 Zni、VO+ 与浅施主中心（shallow-donor centers）；空气退火样品中则为 Zni、VO+ 与 Hi 相关中心。浅施主与 Hi 信号在约 50 K 以下迅速淬灭，Zni 贡献呈非单调温度依赖，而 VO+ 信号持续至室温并随温度升高而增强，与涉及深氧空位态（deep oxygen-vacancy states）的施主相关缺陷间电荷重分布一致；两种样品的 Q 因子总体随温度升高而下降。

Conclusion: 退火气氛控制 ZnO 微米颗粒的顺磁缺陷布居（defect population）、施主相关中心的温度依赖行为以及相应的电子响应，为通过气氛调控缺陷构型与电子性质提供依据。

Abstract: In this work, we used temperature-dependent electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) spectroscopy to reveal the effect of high-temperature annealing in N2 and air on paramagnetic and electronic properties of ZnO microparticles. EPR spectral simulations have shown that N2 annealing produces Zni, VO+, and shallow-donor centers, whereas Zni, VO+, and Hi-related centers are observed after annealing the ZnO microparticles in an air atmosphere. The resolved paramagnetic centers exhibit distinctly different behavior over 5-297 K. The shallow-donor and Hi signals are rapidly quenched below approximately 50 K, while the Zni contribution shows a nonmonotonic temperature dependence. In contrast, the VO+ signal persists up to room temperature and increases with temperature, consistent with charge redistribution among donor-related defects involving deep oxygen-vacancy states. The cavity Q-factor generally decreases with increasing temperature for both annealed samples, indicating atmosphere-dependent changes in the microwave losses. These results show that the annealing atmosphere controls the paramagnetic defect population, the temperature dependence of donor-related centers, and the associated electronic response of ZnO microparticles.

</details>


### [72] [Deep Scientific Reasoning under Physical Constraints: Structure-Aware Spectrum Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34021)
*Yingheng Wang,Tao Yu,Shufeng Kong,Francesco Ricci,John M. Gregoire,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出 DeepSciReasoner，一种在硬性物理约束下预测结构化科学谱的通用范式，以电子态密度（electronic density of states, eDOS）为典型示例，在四类光谱任务上同时提升精度与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化科学光谱（structured scientific spectra）编码丰富物理信息并服从守恒律与特征谱几何等硬约束（hard constraints），但现有方法多将其视为无约束序列，无法保证底层物理结构，因而预测精度与物理一致性受限。

Method: 框架结合结构感知光谱解码（structure-aware spectrum decoding）与约束保持的物理推理（constraint-preserving physical reasoning），在预测中兼顾丰富谱结构表达与底层物理约束的满足。

Result: 在 eDOS、声子态密度（phonon density of states, phDOS）、X 射线吸收近边结构（X-ray absorption near-edge structure, XANES）与拉曼光谱（Raman spectra）上评估，预测精度显著提升，同时保持物理一致性（physical consistency）。

Conclusion: 结果确立了 DeepSciReasoner 作为硬性物理约束下结构化科学光谱预测的可复用蓝图（reusable blueprint）。

Abstract: Structured scientific spectra encode rich physical information while obeying hard constraints, such as conservation laws and characteristic spectral geometry. Accurate prediction of these spectra is central to materials discovery, yet existing methods often treat them as unconstrained sequences and therefore fail to enforce the underlying physical structure. Taking electronic density of states (eDOS) as the prototypical example, we introduce DeepSciReasoner, a general paradigm for predicting scientific spectra under physical constraints. The framework combines structure-aware spectrum decoding with constraint-preserving physical reasoning, allowing predictions to capture rich spectral structure while respecting the underlying physics. We evaluate DeepSciReasoner on eDOS, phonon density of states (phDOS), X-ray absorption near-edge structure (XANES), and Raman spectra, where it substantially improves prediction accuracy while maintaining physical consistency. These results establish DeepSciReasoner as a reusable blueprint for structured scientific spectrum prediction under hard physical constraints.

</details>


### [73] [TCSP3.0: Template-Based Crystal Structure Prediction with Coefficient-Preserving Element Substitution and Structural Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34029)
*Ying Feng,Jeffrey Hu,Lai Wei,Liang Tao,Madeline Miklos,Dipannoy Das Gupta,Hossein Erfanshekooh,Jianjun Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: TCSP3.0通过修正元素映射与替换中的成分错误，并引入周期序检索、氧化态证据、半径兼容性评分、结构去重与机器学习势弛豫排序，在T180与T800基准上显著超越TCSP2.0及深度生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模板的晶体结构预测（template-based crystal structure prediction, CSP）依赖模板检索、元素映射与候选排序，但原TCSP2.0实现存在成分错误：在T180上其2,347个结构中469个（20%）成分错误，原因在于元素映射忽略化学计量系数、以及顺序文本替换导致元素符号级联或损坏。

Method: TCSP3.0强制保留系数的映射（coefficient-preserving mappings），在解析后的结构上同时替换物种并校验每个候选的成分与晶胞；另加入周期序检索、软氧化态证据、半径兼容性评分、结构去重，以及ORB-v3机器学习势（machine-learning potential）弛豫与能量排序。

Result: 在T180上Top-1/Top-20匹配率为72.2%/90.6%（TCSP2.0为28.3%/45.6%），新基准T800上Top-20为74.0%；T180上优于PNcsp+与CrystalFlow、MaskGXT（Top-1 72.2%对最高63.9%）。无弛豫时仍达Top-1 67.8%、Top-20 90.6%，双CPU核心中位28秒/目标，约为CrystalFlow的十倍速。

Conclusion: T180与T800的十臂配对消融（paired ten-arm ablations）表明，多数提升来自正确性修正；结构去重显著改善Top-5匹配率，基于嵌入的检索（embedding-based retrieval）对覆盖率必要，其余评分特征影响较小或无影响。

Abstract: Template-based crystal structure prediction (CSP) transfers a known crystal structure to a new composition by element substitution, so its accuracy depends on retrieving suitable templates, mapping elements and ranking the resulting candidates correctly. Auditing the original TCSP2.0 implementation on the T180 benchmark, we found that 469 of its 2,347 selected structures (20%) have the wrong composition, caused by element mappings that ignore stoichiometric coefficients and by sequential text substitution that cascades or corrupts element symbols. We propose TCSP3.0, which enforces coefficient-preserving mappings, substitutes species simultaneously on parsed structures and validates every candidate's composition and cell. It further adds periodic-number retrieval, soft oxidation-state evidence, radius-compatibility scoring, structural deduplication, and ORB-v3 machine-learning-potential relaxation and energy ranking of its candidates, as used by deep generative CSP baselines. TCSP3.0 achieves Top-1 and Top-20 match rates of 72.2% and 90.6% on T180, compared with 28.3% and 45.6% for TCSP2.0, and a Top-20 match rate of 74.0% on a new 800-target benchmark (T800). On T180 it also outperforms PNcsp+ and deep generative models CrystalFlow and MaskGXT (Top-1 72.2% versus at most 63.9%). Even without relaxation, TCSP3.0 reaches 67.8% at Top-1 and 90.6% at Top-20 in a median of 28 s per target on two CPU cores, about ten times faster than CrystalFlow. Paired ten-arm ablations on T180 and T800 show that most of the improvement comes from the correctness fixes; structural deduplication significantly improves the Top-5 match rate, embedding-based retrieval is required for coverage, and the remaining scoring features have small or null effects.

</details>


### [74] [Spin and Charge Photocurrents from Electric-Magnetic Dipole Interference in Centrosymmetric Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34222)
*Yiwei Zhao,Jian Zhou,Haowei Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出电-磁偶极干涉（EMDI）机制，可在中心对称（centrosymmetric）材料中产生偶数阶非线性光学效应，突破了"必须破缺反演对称"的传统观点，并将响应强度标定为精细结构常数 α 量级，但仍在实验可及范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation）与体光伏效应（bulk photovoltaic effect）等偶数阶非线性光学（nonlinear optical, NLO）效应必须依赖反演对称（inversion symmetry）的破缺，但该假设仅考虑光与材料通过电场相互作用，忽略了磁场分量。

Method: 构建统一的微观理论，说明同一光场的电场与磁场分量之间的干涉（electric-magnetic dipole interference, EMDI）如何驱动中心对称晶体中的NLO响应；以电荷光电流与自旋光电流（charge and spin photocurrents）为原型，结合对称性分析与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），将EMDI诱导的光电流分解为类位移（shift-like）与类注入（injection-like）贡献，并按其对称性、时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）与光偏振依赖性分类。

Result: EMDI响应在电场与磁场中均为双线性，随光强线性变化；其在中心对称材料中的强度比非中心对称材料中的常规响应弱约一个精细结构常数（fine structure constant）α≈10⁻³–10⁻²，但仍处于实验可探测范围。

Conclusion: 该研究为中心对称材料（如立方Si与立方SrTiO₃等具有重要功能的材料）开辟了NLO功能，可用于材料表征、光子学（photonics）与光电子学（optoelectronics）。

Abstract: A common view regarding even-order nonlinear optical (NLO) effects, such as second-harmonic generation and the bulk photovoltaic effect, is that the breaking of inversion symmetry is necessary. However, this view relies on the assumption that light interacts with materials solely through its electric field. Here, we demonstrate that interference between the electric and magnetic components of the same light field can induce even-order NLO effects even in centrosymmetric materials. Using charge and spin photocurrents as prototypical examples, we present a unified microscopic theory showing how such electric-magnetic dipole interference (EMDI) drives NLO responses in inversion-symmetric crystals. Bilinear in both electric and magnetic fields, these responses scale linearly with light intensity. Combining symmetry analysis with first-principles calculations, we decompose the EMDI-induced photocurrents into shift-like and injection-like contributions and classify their dependence on crystal symmetry, time-reversal symmetry, and light polarization. Notably, we reveal that these EMDI-induced responses in centrosymmetric materials are weaker than conventional responses in non-centrosymmetric materials roughly by a factor of fine structure constant $α\approx [10^{-3}\text{--}10^{-2}] $. Nevertheless, they remain well within experimental reach. Our work thus unlocks NLO functionalities in centrosymmetric materials for e.g., materials characterization, photonics and optoelectronics. This is vital given that many functionally importantly materials, such as cubic Si and cubic $\mathrm{SrTiO_3}$, possess inversion symmetry.

</details>


### [75] [Interfacial Orbital Rashba Edelstein Effect Identified in CoO/Cu/Pt Trilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34292)
*C. Schmitt,S. Krishnia,E. E. Galíndez-Ruales,H. Meer,T. Kikkawa,T. Denneulin,A. Kovács,O. Kuschel,R. E. Dunin-Borkowski,E. Saitoh,T. Kuschel,Y. Mokrousov,M. Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用 CoO/Cu/Pt 三层膜在可控 Cu-oxide 界面产生轨道角动量流，并通过改变 Cu 层厚度将自旋与轨道对磁阻的贡献分离；结果确认界面轨道 Rashba-Edelstein 效应是轨道流来源，且无需依赖自然氧化即可稳定复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 CoO/Cu 双层体系依赖不可控的自然氧化（natural oxidation）来产生轨道流，重复性与稳定性不足，且自旋与轨道贡献难以区分，因此需要构建可控的界面平台并厘清轨道流产生的微观机制。

Method: 制备不同 Cu 中间层厚度的 CoO/Cu/Pt 三层膜（trilayer），开展角度依赖磁阻（angular-dependent magnetoresistance）测量，比较纵向与横向电阻分量，并通过系统改变 Cu 厚度解耦自旋与轨道对磁阻响应的贡献。

Result: 角度依赖磁阻随 Cu 厚度呈系统性变化，纵向与横向电阻分量均发生改变，并出现信号符号反转（sign inversion）。分析表明大轨道流源于 CoO/Cu 界面 Cu–O 键所导致的界面轨道 Rashba-Edelstein 效应（orbital Rashba-Edelstein effect）。

Conclusion: Cu-oxide 界面是轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum）的高效来源，三层结构在环境条件下可实现可重复、稳定的轨道流产生，为无需相对论自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与轨道-自旋互转换（orbital-spin interconversion）的全轨道电子学（all-orbitronic）器件提供明确材料平台。

Abstract: We investigate the generation of orbital angular momentum currents at engineered Cu-oxide interfaces using CoO/Cu/Pt thin-film trilayers. Angular-dependent magnetoresistance measurements on samples with varying Cu interlayer thickness reveal systematic changes in both longitudinal and transverse resistance components, including a sign inversion of the signal. By systematically varying the Cu interlayer thicknesses, we disentangle the spin and orbital contributions to the magnetoresistive response. Our results show that large orbital currents originate from the interfacial orbital Rashba-Edelstein effect due to the presence of the Cu-O bonds at the CoO/Cu interface. Unlike previously studied CoO/Cu bilayers that rely on uncontrolled natural oxidation, our trilayer geometry enables reproducible and stable orbital-current generation at ambient conditions. Our results identify Cu-oxide interfaces as efficient sources of orbital angular momentum and clarify the microscopic mechanism underlying orbital current generation. Consequently, this work establishes a well-defined materials platform for all-orbitronic devices that does not require relativistic spin-orbit coupling or orbital-spin interconversion processes.

</details>


### [76] [Controlling Surface Chemistry in Tin-Lead Perovskites for High-Efficiency Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34293)
*Lijun Chen,Sitong Zhao,Gaukhar Nigmetova,David Garcia Romero,Matteo Pitaro,Sofia Lerda,Edoardo Mosconi,Michael Aidi,Douwe van Netten,Julia Kraft,Jiaxiong Li,Jacopo Pinna,Asya Serafimova,Antonija Grubišić-Čabo,Loredana Protesescu,Giuseppe Portale,Petra Rudolf,Filippo De Angelis,Maria Antonietta Loi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 中性乙二胺通过螯合富锡表面的欠配位金属并去除过量锡，重构表面至化学计量比的锡铅比，从而抑制界面非辐射复合，使无甲铵钙钛矿电池效率达25.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锡铅钙钛矿光伏（tin-lead perovskite photovoltaics）的性能受界面非辐射复合（interfacial nonradiative recombination）限制，需理解分子钝化策略的表面化学机理以实现理性设计。

Method: 对比中性乙二胺（ethylenediamine）与质子化乙二胺碘盐（ethylenediammonium iodide）两种表面处理路径，并结合原子尺度模拟（atomistic simulations）考察碘空位（iodide vacancies）对脱质子（deprotonation）与碘迁移的影响。

Result: 中性乙二胺螯合欠配位金属、削弱金属-碘键并去除过量锡，重构表面；质子化乙二胺碘盐先钝化后发生缺陷辅助脱质子。重构产生更强的n型表面特征，促进电子向C60提取并抑制非辐射复合，实现25.86%的转换效率（power conversion efficiency）。

Conclusion: 两种表面反应路径的差异及钝化物种的缺陷辅助演化表明，理解钙钛矿表面化学对分子钝化策略的理性设计至关重要。

Abstract: Interfacial nonradiative recombination limits tin-lead perovskite photovoltaics. Here we show that neutral ethylenediamine chelates undercoordinated metals at tin-rich surfaces, weakens metal-iodide bonds, and removes excess tin, reconstructing the surface towards a stoichiometric tin-to-lead ratio. In contrast, protonated ethylenediammonium iodide initially passivates undercoordinated metal sites before undergoing defect-assisted deprotonation. Atomistic simulations reveal that subsurface iodide vacancies promote iodide migration and lower the energetic cost of deprotonation. Ethylenediamine-induced reconstruction produces stronger n-type surface character, facilitates electron extraction into C60, and suppresses interfacial nonradiative recombination, enabling methylammonium-free solar cells with a power conversion efficiency of 25.86%. Together, these distinct surface reaction pathways and the defect-assisted evolution of the passivating species underscore the importance of understanding perovskite surface chemistry for the rational design of molecular passivation strategies.

</details>


### [77] [Edelstein Effect in Ferromagnetic Monolayers Fe3GaTe2 and Fe3GeTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34296)
*Tongrui Li,Lidong Zhang,Weiyi Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用第一性原理（first-principles）结合Wannier基线性响应（linear-response）计算，系统比较单层Fe3GaTe2与Fe3GeTe2的Edelstein效应（Edelstein effect）；尽管两者具有相同的非中心对称D3h对称性与选择定则，其自旋积累（spin accumulation）在大小、符号和能量依赖上差异显著，并被归因于原子位点间抵消关系的重组、能带与费米面结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Edelstein效应可实现非中心对称范德华磁体（van der Waals magnets）中本征的电流诱导自旋极化（current-induced spin polarization），但同构材料之间响应差异的微观来源尚不清晰；因此需要厘清晶体对称性、电子结构、磁化取向与晶格形变各自的作用。

Method: 采用基于Wannier的第一性原理线性响应计算（first-principles Wannier-based linear-response calculations），对比单层Fe3GaTe2与Fe3GeTe2的时间反演偶（time-reversal-even）与奇（time-reversal-odd）自旋积累，并进行原子分辨（atom-resolved）与动量分辨（momentum-resolved）分析，同时考察磁化旋转与双轴应变（biaxial strain）的影响。

Result: 强响应主要出现在Fe3GaTe2的约-0.4 eV与Fe3GeTe2的约+0.4 eV处；在本征费米能级（intrinsic Fermi level），Ga→Ge替换使非等价Fe3位点在两个面内磁化方向上的偶贡献反号，并增强其负的奇贡献，从而重组位点间抵消。Fe3GaTe2的空穴侧响应关联于少数以Γ为中心的大费米面（Fermi contours），Fe3GeTe2的电子侧响应则涉及密集的Fe-3d能带及强带间贡献。偶响应源于符号相反带内费米面贡献的不完全抵消，奇响应源于近邻态之间的带间相干（interband coherence）。磁化旋转给出受对称性约束、含高阶谐波的角依赖关系（尤其在xz面），双轴应变则引起显著振幅变化与符号反转。

Conclusion: 结果表明晶体对称性（crystal symmetry）、电子结构、磁化取向与晶格形变共同调控Fe3XTe2（X=Ga, Ge）中的本征Edelstein效应，为同构范德华磁体中自旋-电荷转换的化学与结构设计提供了依据。

Abstract: The Edelstein effect enables intrinsic current-induced spin polarization in noncentrosymmetric van der Waals magnets. Here, we compare the Edelstein responses of monolayer \ce{Fe3GaTe2} and \ce{Fe3GeTe2} using first-principles Wannier-based linear-response calculations. Despite sharing the same noncentrosymmetric $D_{3h}$ symmetry and selection rules, the two materials show markedly different time-reversal-even and -odd spin accumulations in magnitude, sign, and energy dependence. Strong responses occur mainly near $-0.4$~eV in \ce{Fe3GaTe2} and $+0.4$~eV in \ce{Fe3GeTe2}. At the intrinsic Fermi level, atom-resolved analysis shows that Ga-to-Ge substitution reverses the even contribution of the inequivalent Fe3 site for both in-plane magnetization directions and enhances its negative odd contribution, reorganizing the cancellation among atomic sites. Momentum-resolved calculations link the hole-side response of \ce{Fe3GaTe2} to a few large $Γ$-centered Fermi contours, whereas the electron-side response of \ce{Fe3GeTe2} involves dense Fe-$3d$ bands with strong interband contributions. The even response arises from incomplete cancellation of opposite-sign intraband Fermi-surface contributions, while the odd response originates from interband coherence between closely spaced states. Magnetization rotation reveals symmetry-constrained angular dependences with higher-order harmonics, especially in the $xz$ plane, while biaxial strain causes substantial amplitude changes and sign reversals. These results show how crystal symmetry, electronic structure, magnetization orientation, and lattice deformation govern the intrinsic Edelstein effect in \ce{Fe3$X$Te2} ($X=\mathrm{Ga},\mathrm{Ge}$).

</details>


### [78] [Crystal-surface polarity enables nonreciprocal transport in a centrosymmetric kagome magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34312)
*Daiki Yamaguchi,Max T. Birch,Hibiki Onodera,Akiko Kikkawa,Naoto Nagaosa,Yoshinori Tokura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心对称材料通常被认为缺乏实现非互易输运所需的对称性破缺，本文旨在揭示晶体表面极性可作为在中心对称磁性导体中获得零场整流的普适机制。

Method: 在对称的TbMn₆Sn₄.₄In₁.₆微器件中比较相对侧表面的二次谐波纵向电阻（second-harmonic longitudinal resistance），并考察自发磁化（spontaneous magnetization）反转与旋转、环状矩（toroidal moment）P×M取向、以及TbMn₆Sn₆中自旋重取向转变（spin-reorientation transition）对信号的影响，辅以空间电势映射（spatial potential mapping）。

Result: 相对两侧表面呈现量级相当、符号相反的二次谐波电阻；反转M使两个信号同时反号，而旋转M使P×M沿电流方向无分量时响应被抑制；在TbMn₆Sn₆中信号在自旋重取向转变处消失；空间电势映射显示非线性边界贡献与体线性电导（bulk linear conduction）可区分，拓扑能带相关的自旋轨道耦合表面态（spin-orbit-coupled surface states）可能增强该效应。

Conclusion: 晶体表面极性为中心对称磁性导体在室温附近实现零场整流提供了广泛适用的途径，并可通过化学有序与磁结构转变对同一对称性关系进行调控。

Abstract: Every crystal surface breaks inversion symmetry even when the bulk is centrosymmetric, creating an intrinsic boundary polarity ($\mathbf{\mathit{P}}$). Here we show that this polarity enables zero-field nonreciprocal transport in the centrosymmetric kagome magnets TbMn$_6$Sn$_{6-x}$In$_x$. In symmetric TbMn$_6$Sn$_{4.4}$In$_{1.6}$ microdevices, opposing side surfaces exhibit second-harmonic longitudinal resistances of comparable magnitude and opposite sign. Reversing the spontaneous magnetization ($\mathbf{\mathit{M}}$) reverses both signals, whereas rotating $\mathbf{\mathit{M}}$ so that the toroidal moment $\mathbf{\mathit{P}} \times \mathbf{\mathit{M}}$ has no component along the current suppresses the response. In pristine TbMn$_6$Sn$_6$, the signal switches off across the spin-reorientation transition, providing a chemically ordered control of the same symmetry relation. Spatial potential mapping indicates that the nonlinear boundary contribution is distinct from the bulk linear conduction. Spin-orbit-coupled surface states associated with topological bands may enhance the effect. Crystal-surface polarity therefore offers a broadly applicable route to zero-field rectification in centrosymmetric magnetic conductors around room temperature.

</details>


### [79] [Transition Metal-Anchored Newly Synthesized Aza-Triphenylene Monolayer for Efficient Hydrogen Evolution Reaction: Insights from DFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34354)
*Soumendra Kumar Das,Jagannath Pradhan,Prasanjit Samal,Sridhar Sahu,Brahmananda Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）研究过渡金属（transition metals）功能化的二维氮杂三亚苯（aza-triphenylene）单层的析氢反应（hydrogen evolution reaction, HER）活性，发现Co、Ru、Y功能化后氢吸附自由能显著接近理想值，Co体系倾向Volmer-Heyrovsky路径且Heyrovsky为决速步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯氮杂三亚苯单层的氢吸附自由能为-0.78 eV，偏离理想值，催化活性不足，因此需通过过渡金属功能化调控其HER性能并系统评估其作为电催化剂平台的可行性。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算，对不同过渡金属功能化的二维氮杂三亚苯单层进行氢吸附自由能评估与火山图（volcano plot）分析，并结合从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics）验证热稳定性、电子结构与电荷转移分析揭示催化剂-表面相互作用、载流子迁移率（carrier mobility）计算，以及爬升微调弹性带（climbing-image nudged elastic band）方法计算Co功能化体系的反应活化势垒。

Result: Co、Ru、Y功能化体系表现突出，氢吸附自由能分别由-0.78 eV优化至0.05、0.20和0.06 eV，火山图分析印证其增强的HER活性；从头算分子动力学确认热稳定性；金属修饰提升载流子迁移率，Ru体系尤为明显；Co功能化体系的Volmer、Heyrovsky、Tafel反应活化势垒分别为1.61、2.29和5.88 eV，表明优先走Volmer-Heyrovsky路径且Heyrovsky为决速步（rate-determining step）。

Conclusion: 过渡金属功能化的氮杂三亚苯单层是有前景的高效HER电催化剂开发平台，其中Co、Ru、Y功能化体系在热力学活性、稳定性与载流子输运方面综合表现优异。

Abstract: First-principles calculations were employed to explore the hydrogen evolution reaction (HER) activity in two-dimensional aza-triphenylene monolayers functionalized with transition metals. Among the various metals studied, Co, Ru, and Y demonstrate outstanding catalytic efficacy, lowering the hydrogen adsorption free energy from -0.78 eV for pure aza-triphenylene to 0.05, 0.20, and 0.06 eV, respectively. Volcano plot analysis corroborates their enhanced HER activity. Ab initio molecular dynamics simulations confirm thermal stability, while the electronic structure and charge transfer analysis reveal the strong catalyst-surface interaction. Metal decoration also improves the carrier mobility, particularly for Ru-functionalized aza-triphenylene. The climbing-image nudged elastic band calculations for Co-functionalized aza-triphenylene exhibit activation barriers of 1.61, 2.29, and 5.88 eV for the Volmer, Heyrovsky, and Tafel reactions, respectively, highlighting a preferred Volmer-Heyrovsky route with the Heyrovsky being the rate-determining step. These findings highlight the potential of transition-metal-functionalized aza-triphenylene as a promising platform for developing efficient HER electrocatalysts.

</details>


### [80] [Phase field dislocation dynamics prediction of stacking fault widths in Cu-Al Alloys using generalised stacking fault energy from DFT and interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34420)
*Arjun Varma R.,Ankosh D. Deshmukh,Sushil Kumar,Vaishali Shah,M. P. Gururajan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作比较了经典嵌入原子势（embedded-atom potential, ZW）、机器学习高斯近似势（Gaussian Approximation Potential, GAP）与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）生成的γ-面（γ-surface），并将其作为能量项引入相场位错动力学（phase-field dislocation dynamics, PFDD）模拟以获取平衡层错宽度（stacking fault width, SFW）。结果表明DFT导出的γ-面是预测合金SFW更可靠的途径，原子间势需准确再现剪切路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征层错（intrinsic stacking fault）的平衡宽度是影响关键变形现象的重要材料参数，需评估不同γ-面来源对层错宽度预测可靠性的影响。研究旨在明确DFT与MD（分子动力学，molecular dynamics）势函数在Cu-Al合金层错宽度预测中的适用性与局限。

Method: 针对不同Al含量的Cu-Al合金，分别用经典嵌入原子势（ZW）、机器学习高斯近似势（GAP）的MD及DFT生成γ-面，并将γ-面作为能量项纳入相场位错动力学（PFDD）模拟以获得平衡层错宽度（SFW）。三种方法均与彼此及文献实验值就晶格参数和弹性模量进行了对比，并在七个特征点处比较γ-面差异。

Result: 三种方法均能合理再现Cu及Cu-Al合金的晶格参数和弹性模量，并与实验测量基本一致；但ZW与GAP的MD导出γ-面在七个特征点处显著偏离DFT，其中T与T1点差异最大。纳入PFDD后，DFT导出γ-面成功预测出随Al含量增加而增大的平衡SFW，与独立MD测量及纯Cu实验值吻合良好；而MD导出γ-面因广义层错能（generalized stacking fault energy）景观改变导致分位错在分离后不久被钉扎，无法给出准确SFW。

Conclusion: DFT导出的γ-面仍是合金层错宽度预测更可靠的途径；原子间势必须准确捕捉剪切路径，才能实现合金层错的稳健、高效MD-PFDD建模。

Abstract: The equilibrium width of intrinsic stacking faults is an important material parameter affecting key deformation phenomena. Here, we perform a comparative assessment of $γ$-surfaces generated using MD -- with a classical embedded-atom potential (ZW) and a machine-learned Gaussian Approximation Potential (GAP) -- and density functional theory (DFT), for Cu-Al alloys of varying Al content, and incorporate them as an energy term in PFDD simulations to obtain the equilibrium SFW. All three methods reproduce the lattice parameter and elastic moduli of Cu and Cu-Al alloys in reasonable agreement with each other and with experimental measurements from literature, but the MD-derived $γ$-surfaces, for both ZW and GAP, deviate substantially from DFT at the seven characteristic points, with the largest discrepancies at $T$ and $T_1$. When incorporated into PFDD, the DFT-derived $γ$-surfaces successfully predict an equilibrium SFW that increases with Al content and agrees well with standalone MD measurements and experimental values for pure Cu, whereas the MD-derived $γ$-surfaces do not yield an accurate SFW within PFDD, as the altered generalized stacking fault energy landscape causes the dissociating partials to become pinned soon after separation. These results show that DFT-derived $γ$-surfaces remain the more reliable route to alloy SFW prediction, and interatomic potentials must capture the shear path accurately to enable robust, efficient MD-PFDD modelling of stacking faults in alloys.

</details>


### [81] [Photoinduced charging and spatially selective photochemistry on ferroelectric surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34465)
*Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一个统一的半导体输运（semiconductor transport）与电荷守恒（charge conservation）框架，分析均匀极化铁电体及畴图案（domain patterns）在光照下的充电与界面反应，区分五种边界情形并解释光沉积形貌的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数十年观测显示反应产物出现在畴内部、畴边界及其他区域，单一极化诱导能带弯曲（band bending）图像难以涵盖，需更一般的机制解释。

Method: 基于半导体输运与电荷守恒建立统一模型，区分五种情形：孤立干态铁电体、连接载流子库（carrier reservoir）的干态铁电体、阻挡型铁电-电解质结、带外部载流子回流（carrier return）的反应界面、经固体与电解质耦合的两个相反畴；以解析解与数值计算关联稳态电位、微分光谱（differential optical spectra）与有限光阶跃响应。

Result: 框架给出各情形下稳态电位随光照的变化，并分析掺杂（doping）对五种情形静电位及孤立薄膜与固定极化裸表面接触薄膜变体中动态光电压（photovoltage）的影响；对反应界面可确定还原与氧化速率、空间分布及累积反应量。

Conclusion: 分析涵盖表面充电与光化学转化（photochemical conversion），保留区分有限充电与持续转化（sustained turnover）的电学与化学边界，解释观测到的光沉积形貌范围，并为光照下SPM可实现的调制光电压成像（modulated photovoltage imaging）奠定理论基础。

Abstract: Ferroelectric surfaces exhibit polarization-dependent photoreactivity and provide a route to program chemical patterns through local electrical control of polarization. Observations accumulated over several decades show reaction products in domain interiors, at domain boundaries, and in other selected regions, indicating that the mechanisms extend beyond a single polarization-induced band-bending picture. Here we analyze light-induced charging and interfacial reactions on uniformly polarized ferroelectrics and domain patterns, for dry surfaces and surfaces in contact with an electrochemically active solution. A common semiconductor transport and charge-conservation framework distinguishes five cases: an isolated dry ferroelectric, a dry ferroelectric connected to a carrier reservoir, a blocking ferroelectric-electrolyte junction, a reactive interface with an external carrier return, and two opposite domains coupled through the solid and electrolyte. Analytical solutions and numerical evaluations connect stationary potentials versus illumination to differential optical spectra and finite light-step responses. We further examine the doping effects on static potentials in all five cases and dynamic photovoltage in the isolated film and a fixed-polarization, bare-surface contacted-film variant. For reactive interfaces, the same framework determines reduction and oxidation rates, their spatial distributions, and accumulated reaction amount. The analysis covers surface charging and photochemical conversion beyond deposition, while retaining the electrical and chemical boundaries that distinguish finite charging from sustained turnover, and explains the observed range of photodeposition morphologies. Finally, this framework builds the theoretical foundation for the modulated photovoltage imaging methods that can readily be realized in SPM under illumination.

</details>


### [82] [Trajectory Functionals in the Thermodynamics of Radiation-Induced Damage in Structural Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34983)
*V. V. Ryazanov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于剂量依赖半马尔可夫更新输运与广义 Doob h-变换的自洽非局域动力学框架，用于评估 WWER-1000/1200 燃料包壳的结构退化。结果表明包壳平均寿命呈 S 形退化轨迹，且边界附近寿命骤降，为高燃耗核燃料安全评估提供预测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需评估 WWER-1000/1200 燃料包壳（fuel cladding）的结构退化，现有方法难以刻画退化动力学中的长程记忆效应与微观缺陷路径到宏观损伤演化的映射。论文旨在建立非平衡涨落与全局 Griffiths 宏观势垒间的严格热力学联系，以支撑高燃耗（high-burnup）核燃料安全评估。

Method: 构建基于剂量依赖半马尔可夫更新输运（semi-Markov renewal transport）与广义 Doob h-变换（generalized Doob's h-transform）的自洽非局域动力学框架；借助 Feynman-Kac 形式（Feynman-Kac formalism），将由 Liouville 算子（Liouville operator）生成的微观缺陷路径轨迹映射到宏观积分损伤势的演化；通过路径 Liouville 算子与 Zwanzig-Mori 投影算子（Zwanzig-Mori projection operators）的非零结构对易子（structural commutator）纳入长程记忆效应，并联系微观汇（sinks）空间限制与能量路径惩罚。

Result: 包壳平均寿命的空间剖面呈明显的 S 形（sigmoidal）退化轨迹，包含孕育（incubation）、线性增长与雪崩击穿（avalanche breakdown）三个阶段；数值结果显示包壳边界附近工作寿命出现突然的局部缩短。

Conclusion: 边界处的脆弱性被归因于燃料包壳界面处边界应力场奇异性（boundary stress field singularities）与局部脆化（localized embrittlement）的直接物理后果；所发展的路径积分架构（path integral architecture）为非平衡涨落与全局 Griffiths 宏观势垒（Griffiths macro-barrier）提供了严格热力学联系，可作为高燃耗核燃料安全评估的稳健预测工具。

Abstract: This paper delivers a novel self-consistent non-local kinetic framework based on dose-dependent semi-Markov renewal transport and the generalized Doob's h-transform to evaluate the structural degradation of WWER-1000/1200 fuel claddings. By leveraging the Feynman-Kac formalism, we map the continuous microscopic defect path trajectories generated by the Liouville operator directly onto the evolution of the macroscopically integrated damage potential. The framework accounts for long-range memory effects in the degradation kinetics via a non-vanishing structural commutator of the path Liouville operator and Zwanzig-Mori projection operators, which mathematically links the spatial restrictions of microscopic sinks to energetic path penalties. Spatial profiling of the cladding's mean lifetime, demonstrates a distinct sigmoidal, S-shaped degradation trajectory encompassing distinct incubation, linear growth, and avalanche breakdown stages. The numerical results reveal an abrupt, localized reduction in the operational lifespan near the cladding boundaries. This marginal vulnerability is shown to be a direct physical consequence of boundary stress field singularities at the fuel cladding interface and localized embrittlement. The developed path integral architecture provides a strict thermodynamic link between non-equilibrium fluctuations and the global Griffiths macro-barrier, offering a robust predictive tool for high-burnup nuclear fuel safety assessments.

</details>


### [83] [Computational screening of amorphous lithium trivalent-metal chloride solid-state electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34505)
*Xiaolin Liu,Shuang,Xia,De-en Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用通用机器学习力场（machine-learning force field）驱动的分子动力学（molecular dynamics）对16种非晶卤化物锂导体 Li-M(III)-Cl 进行计算筛选，发现其室温 Li⁺ 扩散均较快，估算电导率为 7.7–14 mS cm⁻¹，并以 VDOS 中心与协同运动体数两个描述符解释成分依赖的输运差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶固态电解质（amorphous solid-state electrolytes, SSEs）是安全高能量密度全固态电池的候选材料；继非晶 LiTaCl₆ 与 LiNbCl₆ 报道室温离子电导率超过 10 mS cm⁻¹、活化能低于 0.2 eV 之后，亟需广泛搜索新的非晶卤化物锂导体。

Method: 采用通用机器学习力场（universal machine-learning force field）驱动的分子动力学模拟，对涵盖15种稀土元素与铟的16种 Li-M(III)-Cl 组分进行非晶结构筛选；并将电导率–扩散系数关系以实验 LiTaCl₆ 为参照进行标度，同时以 Li⁺ 振动态密度（vibrational density of states, VDOS）中心与 string-like 协同运动中的关联体平均数目作为描述符分析输运机制。

Result: 全部16个非晶相均表现出快速室温 Li⁺ 扩散，扩散系数约为 2–5 × 10⁻⁷ cm² s⁻¹；标度后估算室温电导率从非晶 Li₃InCl₆ 的 7.7 mS cm⁻¹ 到非晶 Li₃ScCl₆ 的 14 mS cm⁻¹。非晶 Li₃ScCl₆ 活化能低至 0.18 eV，被预测为最具导电性的非晶卤化物电解质之一。

Conclusion: Li⁺ 的 VDOS 中心越低、且迁移更倾向于个体跳跃而非协同运动（concerted motion）的组分，表现出更高的 Li 离子扩散系数。结果表明非晶卤化物固态电解质具有丰富的 Li 离子输运特征，为成分设计提供了可解释的描述符依据。

Abstract: Amorphous solid-state electrolytes (SSEs) are attractive candidates for safe, high-energy-density all-solid-state batteries. The recent discovery that amorphous LiTaCl$_6$ and LiNbCl$_6$ reach room-temperature ionic conductivities above 10 mS cm$^{-1}$ with activation energies below 0.2 eV has spurred a broad search for new amorphous halide Li conductors. Here we report a computational screening of amorphous lithium trivalent-metal chlorides (Li-M(III)-Cl) using molecular dynamics driven by a universal machine-learning force field. For the sixteen Li-M(III)-Cl compositions spanning fifteen rare-earth elements and indium, all phases exhibit fast room-temperature Li$^+$ diffusion, with diffusivities of roughly $2$-$5 \times 10^{-7}$ cm$^2$ s$^{-1}$. After scaling the conductivity versus diffusivity relation to an experimental LiTaCl$_6$ reference, the estimated room-temperature conductivities range from 7.7 mS cm$^{-1}$ for amorphous Li$_3$InCl$_6$ to 14 mS cm$^{-1}$ for amorphous Li$_3$ScCl$_6$. With a low activation energy of 0.18 eV, amorphous Li$_3$ScCl$_6$ is predicted to be among the most conductive amorphous halide electrolytes. To rationalize the composition-dependent transport, we identify two descriptors: the Li$^+$ vibrational density of states (VDOS) center, which correlates inversely with diffusivity, and the average number of correlated bodies in string-like Li$^+$ motion. Compositions in which Li$^+$ has a lower VDOS center and migrates more by individual hopping rather than by concerted motion exhibit higher Li-ion diffusivities. These results suggest rich Li-ion transport characteristics in amorphous halide SSEs.

</details>


### [84] [Role of site-specific coordination for the structural and electronic properties of a Co-HATCN network on silver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34543)
*Martin Anstett,Ariyan Tavakoli,Simone Mearini,Yan Yan Grisan Qiu,Vitaliy Feyer,Peter Puschnig,Martin Aeschlimann,Lu Lyu,Hans Christian Schneider,Benjamin Stadtmüller*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以HATCN为连接分子，在Ag(111)表面构建Co配位二维金属有机多孔网络，揭示双配位位点诱导的位点选择性配位及其对局域电子结构的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数二维金属有机多孔网络（2DMOPNs）仅利用单一配位位点，多配位位点对网络结构与电子性质的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）、动量分辨光电子能谱（momentum-resolved photoemission）与密度泛函理论（DFT），研究HATCN（含腈基与吡嗪型核心氮两类配位位点）与Co在Ag(111)上的配位结构形成。

Result: STM显示结构由简单蜂窝状HATCN网络转变为Co配位多孔网络，含三种结构及电子上不等价的分子，比例6:3:1；两类配位位点导致选择性Co配位几何，并伴随位点特异的Co向前最低未占据分子轨道（LUMO）的电荷转移。

Conclusion: 位点选择性Co配位既控制多孔网络的整体构架，又通过位点特异的金属-分子相互作用强烈影响局域电子性质，为在分子尺度定制2DMOPNs的位点特异电子结构提供途径。

Abstract: Two-dimensional metal-organic porous networks (2DMOPNs) on metal surfaces provide a versatile platform for exploring emergent electronic and magnetic phenomena in designed surface nanostructures. While the porous architecture is governed by coordination between molecular building blocks and metal centers, most 2DMOPNs utilize only one type of coordination site, leaving the impact of multiple coordination sites largely unexplored. We address this question by investigating the structural formation of a Co-coordinated 2DMOPN on Ag(111) with the linker molecule hexaazatriphenylene-hexacarbonitrile (HATCN), which has two chemically distinct nitrogen coordination sites at the peripheral nitrile groups and pyrazine-type core nitrogen atoms. Scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) reveals the transformation from a simple honeycomb HATCN structure to a Co-coordinated porous network with three structurally and electronically inequivalent molecules in a 6:3:1 ratio. The existence of two distinct coordination sites leads to selective Co coordination geometries for all three molecules, accompanied by site-specific charge transfer from Co into the former lowest unoccupied molecular orbital (LUMO), as revealed by momentum-resolved photoemission and density functional theory. Our results demonstrate that site-selective Co coordination not only controls the overall architecture of the porous network structure but also strongly influences the local electronic properties through site-specific metal-molecule interaction, providing a route to tailor the site-specific electronic structure of 2DMOPNs at the molecular level.

</details>


### [85] [A thermodynamic correction layer for machine learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34551)
*Sarath Menon,Braulio Sanchez Ortega,Ralf Drautz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一种“加性热力学修正层（additive thermodynamic correction layer）”，在不重新训练、不修改基座势函数的前提下，将机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potentials）的相变温度校准至评估参考值（assessed values）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础势模型（foundation models）虽可覆盖整个元素周期表，却继承了训练数据所用电子结构方法的热力学描述；相对相稳定性的微小误差会在相变温度上被放大，因此亟需一种无需重训练的修正手段。

Method: 采用一阶热力学构造（first order thermodynamic construction），将所需的相自由能位移直接转换为构型能量目标，用线性原子团簇展开（linear atomic cluster expansion）拟合，并依据测得的自由能迭代精修；应用于基于 GRACE 基础模型的熔化与固–固相变。

Result: 修正后所有相变温度均落在规定容差内，包括需同时修正固–固转变与熔化的 Ca、Ti、Zr；静态能量曲线、体模量（bulk moduli）与液态结构基本保持与基座模型一致，而空位形成能、热膨胀与液态密度在修正幅度较大处随之改变。

Conclusion: 该修正层无需重训练、无需可微动力学（differentiable dynamics），也无需访问基座势参数，为可迁移原子模型与评估相热力学的模块化结合提供了途径。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials enable atomistic simulations with near \textit{ab initio} accuracy over large length and time scales. Foundation models extend this capability across the periodic table but inherit the thermodynamic description of the electronic structure methods used to generate their training data. Small errors in relative phase stability can therefore produce large errors in transformation temperatures. We introduce an additive thermodynamic correction layer that shifts phase free energies towards an assessed reference without retraining or modifying the base potential. A first order thermodynamic construction converts the required phase free energy shifts directly into configuration energy targets, which are fitted using a linear atomic cluster expansion and refined iteratively from measured free energies. We apply the method to melting and solid to solid transformations across elements with different crystal structures using a foundation GRACE model. After the correction, all transformation temperatures lie within the prescribed tolerance of the assessed values, including Ca, Ti, and Zr, where a solid to solid transition and melting are corrected together. Static energy curves, bulk moduli, and liquid structure remain close to those of the base model. Vacancy formation energies, thermal expansion, and liquid density depend on the corrected phase balance and change where the correction is large. The method requires neither retraining, differentiable dynamics, nor access to the parameters of the base potential. It provides a modular route for combining transferable atomistic models with assessed phase thermodynamics.

</details>


### [86] [From Autonomous Knowledge Generation to Generalizable Property Prediction: A Precision-First Pipeline for Electro-Optic Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34628)
*Dibakar Datta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一套以“宁缺毋滥”（no value is better than a wrong value）为准则的文献数据构建流水线，从49篇出版物中抽取电光张量（electro-optic tensor）数据，并用法代模型验证其可外推性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电光材料（通过泡克尔斯效应（Pockels effect）实现折射率随电场线性变化）决定了光调制器与微波—光链路的速度与能耗，而材料设计需联合优化组分、温度、应变与偏压；但通用材料数据库缺乏电光张量数据。

Method: 流水线含五个阶段：物理感知采集（physics-aware acquisition）、基于大语言模型（LLM）与确定性表格解析器的版式感知抽取（layout-aware extraction）、三级验证门（three-tier verification gate）、基于中位数与中位数绝对偏差（median absolute deviation）的跨文献共识、以及经留一材料（leave-one-material-out, LOMO）检验的噪声稳健代理模型（surrogate model）。物理约束直接嵌入：符号映射、剔除点群对称性禁戒的泡克尔斯分量、合并等价标签、数值附带来源标签（provenance tags）。

Result: 在49篇文献的运行中，验证门将816个候选值处理为414个接受、215个待审、187个拒绝；对照16篇论文、303个数值的人工认证金标准，精确记录匹配的精确率为0.51、召回率为0.48。数据库从10增至22种材料时，代理模型优于均值预测基线的留出材料数由4个中的1个提升至13个中的9个。

Conclusion: 该趋势（非受控对比）支持“以精确率为导向、经物理校验的数据库可支撑超越训练集的前向模型”这一前提，并为逆向材料设计（inverse material design）明确了需求。

Abstract: Electro-optic materials, whose refractive index changes linearly with an applied electric field (the Pockels effect), set the speed and energy of optical modulators and microwave-to-optical links for quantum computing. Designing better materials requires optimizing composition alongside temperature, strain, and bias field. However, general repositories lack electro-optic tensor data. We present a pipeline that builds such a database from literature following the rule: "no value is better than a wrong value." The pipeline has five stages: physics-aware acquisition, layout-aware extraction via LLM and deterministic table parsers, a three-tier verification gate, cross-paper consensus using median and median absolute deviation, and a noise-robust surrogate model audited by leave-one-material-out (LOMO) testing. Physics enters directly: notation is mapped, point-group symmetry-forbidden Pockels components are rejected, equivalent labels are merged, and values carry provenance tags. In a run over 49 publications, the gate processed 816 candidates into 414 accepted, 215 held for review, and 187 rejected. Against a hand-certified gold standard of 303 values from 16 papers, the run achieved a precision of 0.51 and recall of 0.48 for exact record matches. Across four database snapshots, held-out materials where the surrogate model beat a predict-the-mean baseline grew from 1 of 4 to 9 of 13 as the database expanded from 10 to 22 materials. While not a controlled comparison, this trend supports the premise that a precision-oriented, physics-checked database can power forward models beyond its training set and clarifies requirements for inverse material design.

</details>


### [87] [No Value Is Better Than a Wrong Value: Finding and Handling Erroneous Data in Literature-Mined Datasets for AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34642)
*Achyut Manoj,Advai Aiyer,Andrew Lee,Nayan Saini,Pragya Birla,Dibakar Datta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作针对文献自动抽取数据集中的数值错误，提出按来源与特征划分的错误分类体系（taxonomy）及分层检测框架（layered framework），并在生产环境与基准测试中验证其拦截效果与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学、化学、能源与医学的机器学习（machine learning）日益依赖文献抽取数据，但缺乏经过整理（curated）的数据库；自动抽取会引入数字误读、单位与记号混淆、数值错配及大语言模型（LLM）幻觉等问题。

Method: 基于构建电光性质数据库的实践，按错误来源与特征建立错误分类体系，并提出分层检测框架：模式与溯源校验（schema and provenance checks）、物理可容许性（physical admissibility，含边界、对称性与控制方程）、跨来源统计共识（cross-source statistical consensus）、模型审计（model auditing）与可疑度排序复核（suspicion-ranked review）。

Result: 生产运行中验证环节拦截了816个候选值中的402个并给出分类理由；但对照人工整理参考的审计显示，112个被接受值中仍有30个存在缺陷，仅对称性规则即捕获其中21个。在BaTiO3与KNbO3张量数据上，本构恒等式（constitutive identities）捕获10个单值错误中的8个并定位出错条目。受控基准中，组合框架以85%精确率（precision）捕获81%的错误，使模型在留出数据上的精度与干净数据训练模型相差约3%以内。未清洗数据中，20个最大值里89%为错误，导致AI检索优先产生假阳性（false positives）。

Conclusion: 在错误数值受控后，测量条件缺失成为主要瓶颈——缺少完整上下文时不同记录无法区分。作者提出领域发展方向，包括共享物理规则库（physics rule libraries）与自报告数据库（self-reporting databases）。

Abstract: Machine learning across materials science, chemistry, energy, and medicine increasingly relies on literature-extracted datasets due to a lack of curated databases. However, automated extraction introduces errors: text recognition misreads digits, units and notations are confused, values are misattributed, and LLMs hallucinate numbers. Drawing on our work building an electro-optic property database, we present a taxonomy of errors organized by their origin and signatures, alongside a layered framework for error detection. The framework combines schema and provenance checks, physical admissibility (bounds, symmetry, and governing equations), cross-source statistical consensus, model auditing, and suspicion-ranked review. In a production run, our verification gate halted 402 of 816 candidate values with typed reasons. Yet, an audit against curated references showed that 30 of 112 accepted values were defective; standard symmetry rules alone caught 21 of these. On measured BaTiO3 and KNbO3 tensors, constitutive identities caught 8 of 10 single-value errors and isolated the corrupted entries. In a controlled benchmark, the combined framework caught 81% of errors at 85% precision, including errors in single-report materials, bringing model accuracy on held-out data to within 3% of a model trained on clean data. Uncleaned data severely impairs discovery: 89% of the twenty largest values were errors, causing AI searches to prioritize false positives. Once bad numbers are controlled, missing measurement conditions emerge as the main limit, as distinct records become indistinguishable without full context. We outline directions for the field, including shared physics rule libraries and self-reporting databases.

</details>


### [88] [Magnetoelectric Exchange Spring for Spin-Wave Logic with Ultralow Energy Consumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34693)
*Aiji Wang,Mingming Li,Yuben Yang,Hanchen Wang,Yuelin Zhang,Liang Yang,Kai Liu,Sheng Cheng,Yang Yang,Yu Yun,Tianxiang Nan,Ka Shen,Tao Zhu,Xiufeng Han,Haiming Yu,Ce-Wen Nan,Jinxing Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过在铁电BaTiO3与低磁阻尼铁磁(La,Sr)MnO3界面构建人工磁电交换弹簧（magnetoelectric exchange spring），利用极化调制的轨道重构（orbital reconstruction）调控局域磁各向异性，实现自旋波（spin wave/magnon）传播的可逆、超低能耗调控，并据此设计出全部16种布尔逻辑（Boolean logic）器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋波（magnon）传播无焦耳热（Joule heating），是低能耗信息器件的潜在载体，但实现低功耗、可扩展的自旋波传播调控仍是关键挑战。

Method: 在铁电BaTiO3与铁磁(La,Sr)MnO3界面人工构建磁电交换弹簧，借助极化（polarization）调制的轨道重构裁剪局域磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy），从而可逆调控自旋波传播；并通过偏离Janovec-Kay-Dunn定律降低极化翻转所需的矫顽电压（Vc~0.4 V）。

Result: 工程化BaTiO3（Ps~1.2 μC cm^-2，Vc~0.4 V）的极化翻转即可切换(La,Sr)MnO3中的相干磁子输运（coherent magnon transport），归一化至10×10 nm^2器件时可对应阿托焦（atto-joule）量级的调控能耗；利用自旋波的振幅、相位与频率编码，实现了全部16种低能耗紧凑布尔逻辑。

Conclusion: 人工磁电交换弹簧可推广至多种材料组合，为发展高能效、集成化的磁子学（magnonics）器件提供通用平台。

Abstract: Spin waves (magnons) are intrinsically free of Joule heating during their propagation in magnets. Exploring, understanding, and manipulating such quasiparticles not only helps reveal the exotic magnonic phenomena in condensed matter physics but also underpins the development of information devices with low energy consumption. However, achieving low-power and scalable control of spin-wave propagation remains a critical challenge. Herein, a magnetoelectric exchange spring has been artificially designed and constructed across an interface between ferroelectric BaTiO3 and ferromagnetic (La,Sr)MnO3 with low magnetic damping, in which polarization-modulated orbital reconstruction tailors local magnetic anisotropy and enables reversible control of spin-wave propagation. By successfully deviating from the Janovec-Kay-Dunn law, reversal polarization of engineered BaTiO3 (Ps~1.2 uC cm^-2, Vc~0.4 V) is sufficient to switch the coherent magnon transport in the (La,Sr)MnO3, indicating the magnetoelectric heterointerface may serve as a promising candidate for atto-joule control of spin-wave propagation (normalized to a 10*10 nm^2 device). Furthermore, taking advantage of amplitude, phase, and frequency to encode spin waves, all 16 compact Boolean logics with ultralow energy consumption have been designed. The artificial magnetoelectric exchange spring can be broadly applicable to various material choices, offering a versatile platform for developing energy-efficient and integrated magnonic devices.

</details>


### [89] [Optimised growth of epitaxial D019 Mn3Ge(0001) thin films via single target sputtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34736)
*Servet Ozdemir,Ping-Luen Ho,Callum Brennan-Rich,Matthew Rogers,Philippa Shepley,Ahmet Yagmur,Ismet Gelen,Sehwan Song,Zabeada Aslam,Mannan Ali,Shelly Conroy,Sandrine Heutz,Gavin Burnell,B J Hickey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文旨在建立可重复生长的 Mn3Ge 薄膜制备工艺：以 Mn5Ge 靶材、在 Ru(0001) 籽晶层（seed layer）上于 Al2O3(0001) 衬底生长出具有 D019 结构的高质量薄膜，并通过弱零场磁化与奈尔温度（Néel temperature）证实其非共线反铁磁序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn3X（X = Sn、Ga、Ge）Heusler 合金兼具拓扑能带（外尔半金属，Weyl semimetal）与由 Kagome 晶格决定的手性非共线反铁磁性（chiral non-collinear antiferromagnet）；其中 Mn3Ge 因较大的贝里曲率（Berry curvature）带来大反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity），具备自旋电子学（spintronics）应用潜力，但所需性质仅在 D019 结构下获得，故亟需易重复的生长工艺。

Method: 采用 Mn5Ge 靶材，在带有 Ru(0001) 籽晶层的 Al2O3(0001) 衬底上生长 Mn3Ge 薄膜；通过磁化测量表征其磁有序行为。

Result: 成功生长出 D019 对称性的超高质量 Mn3Ge 薄膜；测到弱零场磁化约 (0.0086 ± 0.0002) μ_B/Mn，在高磁场下不饱和，奈尔温度为 (291 ± 2) K，与非共线反铁磁序的预期一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的生长方案可重复地获得 D019 相 Mn3Ge 外延薄膜，其磁性证据支持手性非共线反铁磁基态，为该材料在拓扑与自旋电子学方向的研究提供可复现的材料平台。

Abstract: Materials with chemical identity Mn3X, where X is Sn, Ga, or Ge, are Heusler alloys with intriguing properties that are fundamentally and technologically interesting. They have topological properties with a non-trivial band structure that identify them as Weyl semimetals in the bulk. Their magnetic properties, which give rise to the topological band structure through spin-orbit coupling, are determined by their Kagome lattice structure, making them chiral non-collinear antiferromagnets. Among these materials, Mn3Ge is particularly interesting because it has a larger Berry curvature, leading to a large anomalous Hall conductivity and motivating potential spintronic applications. The desirable properties are only obtained when Mn3Ge has the D019 crystal structure, so producing an easily reproducible growth recipe is the main objective of this paper. Here we show that ultra-high-quality thin films of Mn3Ge with D019 symmetry can be grown from a Mn5Ge target on an Al2O3(0001) substrate with a Ru(0001) seed layer. The expected non-collinear antiferromagnetic order was confirmed by the finding of weak zero-field magnetisation (0.0086 +/- 0.0002) mu_B/Mn that does not saturate at high magnetic fields and has a Neel temperature of (291 +/- 2) K.

</details>


### [90] [A van der Waals Floating-Gate Platform for Broadband Optoelectronic Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34738)
*Thi Phuong Anh Bach,Sangeun Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报道了一种范德华（van der Waals）ReS2/hBN/InSe 浮栅突触晶体管，在单一器件中集成非易失存储与可见—近红外宽带光电学习功能，并演示了高阶联想学习与可重构布尔逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工突触（artificial synapse）是下一代神经形态计算（neuromorphic computing）的关键，但传统浮栅存储器（floating-gate memory）光学功能有限且可扩展性差，难以同时实现非易失存储与宽带光电学习。

Method: 构建范德华异质结浮栅晶体管：以 InSe 作浮栅层实现稳定电荷存储，hBN 作隧穿介质（tunneling dielectric），ReS2 作光响应沟道，从而在电学与光学刺激下实现载流子隧穿。

Result: 器件获得大存储窗口、超过 10^6 的开/关电流比、稳定的多级电导态与 10^4 s 以上的保持特性；可见至近红外宽带光激发可实现突触权重调控，并模拟短/长期可塑性、双脉冲易化及脉冲幅度/持续时间依赖可塑性，光增强与电抑制实现双向权重调制；进一步实现类巴甫洛夫（Pavlovian）联想与辨别条件反射的高阶学习及光电输入驱动的可重构布尔逻辑。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了集非易失存储、宽带光电学习与自适应信息处理于一体的多功能范德华浮栅平台，为紧凑、高能效的神经形态硬件提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Artificial synapses that integrate nonvolatile memory with broadband optoelectronic learning are essential for next-generation neuromorphic computing. However, their realization remains challenging because conventional floating-gate memories exhibit limited optical functionality and poor scalability. Here, we report a van der Waals ReS2/hBN/InSe floating-gate synaptic transistor that combines efficient charge storage with broadband optoelectronic learning in a single device architecture. The heterostructure incorporates InSe as a floating-gate layer for stable charge storage, hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) as a tunneling dielectric, and ReS2 as a photoresponsive channel, enabling carrier tunneling under electrical and optical stimulation. Through the combined operation of these layers, the device achieves a large memory window, an on/off current ratio exceeding 10^6, stable multilevel conductance states, and reliable retention over 10^4 s. Broadband visible-to-near-infrared optical excitation enables controllable synaptic weight modulation and the emulation of essential synaptic functions, including short- and long-term plasticity, paired-pulse facilitation, and pulse-amplitude- and pulse-duration-dependent plasticity. Optical potentiation and electrical depression further enable bidirectional modulation of synaptic weights. Beyond these synaptic functions, the device demonstrates higher-order learning through associative and discrimination conditioning inspired by the Pavlovian learning paradigm, as well as reconfigurable Boolean logic driven by electro-optical inputs. These results establish a multifunctional van der Waals floating-gate platform that integrates nonvolatile memory, broadband optoelectronic learning, and adaptive information processing within a single transistor, providing a promising strategy for compact, energy-efficient neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [91] [Rigid Band Shift in Electron-Doped Kagome Metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34996)
*M. Pinterić,E. Uykur,S. Salters,G. Pokharel,S. D. Wilson,M. Dressel,M. Wenzel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用红外光谱（infrared spectroscopy）与密度泛函理论（density-functional theory）研究 Sn 位掺杂对非磁性笼目金属（kagome metal）YV₆Sn₆₋ₓSbₓ 电子结构的影响，发现 Sb 部分替代 Sn 表现为费米能级（Fermi level）的刚性移动，而能带色散（band dispersion）基本保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索在非磁性笼目金属 YV₆Sn₆ 中实现能带填充（band filling）系统调控的途径，以利于考察依赖能带填充的电子性质。

Method: 采用红外光谱实验测量结合密度泛函理论计算，对比 x = 0 与 x = 1.4 两个样品的电子结构。

Result: 结果显示 Sb 对 Sn 的部分替代引起费米能级的刚性位移，同时能带色散大体保持不变。

Conclusion: 研究确立 YV₆Sn₆ 中的 Sn 位替代为系统研究非磁性笼目金属能带填充相关电子性质的有效手段。

Abstract: We study the effects of Sn-site doping on the electronic structure of nonmagnetic kagome metal YV$_6$Sn$_{6-x}$Sb$_x$ by infrared spectroscopy and density-functional theory. The results for samples with $x$ = 0 and 1.4 demonstrate that partial substitution of Sn by Sb acts as a rigid shift of the Fermi level while leaving the band dispersion largely intact. Our findings establish Sn-site substitution in YV$_6$Sn$_6$ as a route to systematically investigate band-filling-dependent electronic properties of nonmagnetic kagome metals.

</details>


### [92] [Nonlocal Einstein Relation in Confined Phonon Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35109)
*Guanda Quan,Ronggui Yang,Xin Qian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出在空间受限条件下，声子流体力学中的爱因斯坦关系本质上是非局域的：某位置的水动力热导率取决于整个通道截面上的集体漂移迁移率，并给出非局域爱因斯坦关系 κ_H(y)=∫P(y−y')μ(y')dy'。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统爱因斯坦关系（Einstein relation）将迁移率与宏观输运系数局部地联系起来，在声子流体力学中表现为漂移热导率正比于局域漂移迁移率；但当空间受限时，这种局部联系是否成立尚不明确，需要考察非局域效应。

Method: 推导非局域爱因斯坦关系，使水动力热导率 κ_H(y) 与全截面的集体漂移迁移率 μ(y') 通过核函数 P(y−y') 关联；并在小克努森数（Knudsen number）极限下检验其退化情形，同时在有限克努森数下分析边界滑移与尺寸标度，结合石墨（graphite）的第一性原理计算与温度相关模拟进行验证。

Result: 在小克努森数极限下，非局域理论可恢复黏性热方程（viscous heat equation）与局域爱因斯坦关系；在有限克努森数下，非局域性导致本征边界滑移（boundary slip），并使 μ 与 κ_H(y) 呈现不同的尺寸标度行为。石墨计算显示非局域性拓宽了热导率呈超线性尺寸依赖的通道尺寸窗口；温度相关模拟表明非局域理论能捕捉归一化热导率中流体力学峰（hydrodynamic peak）的出现，而局域理论无法准确预测其幅度。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了集体声子迁移率与热传导之间超越局域流体力学（local hydrodynamics）的微观联系，表明受限体系中爱因斯坦关系具有内在非局域性。

Abstract: Einstein relations connect carrier mobility to macroscopic transport coefficients and constitute a fundamental link between microscopic dynamics and transport. In phonon hydrodynamics, this connection is conventionally local: the drift-induced thermal conductivity is proportional to the local drift mobility. Here we show that this relation becomes intrinsically nonlocal under spatial confinement. We derive a nonlocal Einstein relation, $κ_H(y) = \int P(y-y')μ(y'),dy'$, in which hydrodynamic thermal conductivity $κ_H(y)$ at one position depends on collective drift mobility $μ(y')$ across the entire cross-section of the channel. In the small-Knudsen-number limit, our nonlocal theory recovers the viscous heat equation and the local Einstein relation. At finite Knudsen number, nonlocality produces intrinsic boundary slip and distinct size-scaling behavior of the drift mobility $μ$ and hydrodynamic thermal conductivity $κ_H(y)$. First-principles calculations for graphite show that nonlocality broadens the channel-size window over which thermal conductivity exhibits superlinear size dependence. Temperature-dependent simulations further show that the nonlocal theory captures the emergence of a hydrodynamic peak in thermal conductivity normalized by ballistic conductance, whereas the local theory fails to accurately predict its magnitude. Our results establish a microscopic connection between collective phonon mobility and heat conduction beyond local hydrodynamics.

</details>


### [93] [Temperature-dependent magnetic properties of the antiferromagnet Mn$_2$Au](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35153)
*Jackson L. Ross,Sarah Jenkins,Ricardco Rama-Eiroa,Paul-Iulian Gavriloaea,Jack Collins,Unai Atxitia,Oksana Chubykalo-Fesenko,Karin Everschor-Sitte,Rubén M. Otxoa,Roy W. Chantrell,Richard F. L. Evans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于第一性原理构建的原子自旋动力学（atomistic spin dynamics）模拟，给出了Mn₂Au单晶磁学性质随温度变化的定量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn₂Au因高奈尔温度（Néel temperature）、适中各向异性及层状自旋结构而成为反铁磁自旋电子学（antiferromagnetic spintronics）的有力候选材料；用超短电/光手段调控其磁序需要准确掌握磁性质对温度的依赖关系。

Method: 采用原子自旋动力学模拟，并引入一个基于既有从头算（ab initio）计算构建的自旋哈密顿量（spin Hamiltonian），模拟Mn₂Au单晶的宏观磁性质随温度的变化。

Result: 模型给出的各向异性常数（anisotropy constants）随磁化强度的温度标度符合Zener-Akulov-Callen-Callen幂律；交换刚度（exchange stiffness）随磁化强度按m_e^1.83标度，且预测的畴壁宽度（domain wall width）温度依赖与直接模拟高度一致。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了对Mn₂Au温度依赖磁性质的理解，可支撑自旋电子器件工作条件的大尺度模拟。

Abstract: Mn$_2$Au is one of the most promising materials for antiferromagnetic spintronics due to its high Néel temperature, moderate anisotropy, layered spin structure allowing current induced spin-orbit-torques, and ultrafast spin dynamics. Further understanding and control of the magnetic order in Mn$_2$Au for ultrafast spintronics applications via ultrashort electric/optical means requires accurate understanding of how its magnetic properties depend on temperature. Here we present atomistic spin dynamics simulations of the temperature-dependent macroscopic magnetic properties of Mn$_2$Au single crystals. We introduce a spin Hamiltonian based on previous ab initio calculations which produces temperature-dependent scaling of the anisotropy constants with magnetisation consistent with Zener-Akulov-Callen-Callen power laws. The calculated temperature dependence of exchange stiffness follows a scaling law with magnetisation of $m^{1.83}_e$ , and predicts a temperature-dependent domain wall width that agrees very well with direct simulation. Our model provides an understanding of temperature-dependent magnetic properties of Mn$_2$Au that enables large-scale simulation of the working conditions of spintronic devices.

</details>


### [94] [Optical Thermometry in Monolayer WS2: Raman and Photoluminescence Spectroscopy from 5 K to 1273 K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35214)
*Gyeongjun Lee,Antoine Borel,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Fausto Sirotti,Fabian Cadiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文为单层WS2建立了覆盖5–1273 K的全面光测热（optical thermometry）框架，结合拉曼（Raman）A1g模式位移与光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）中性激子（neutral exciton）红移两种独立光学温度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维（2D）半导体器件的表征与工程需要可靠温度测量，但现有二维材料光测热仅能在几百开尔文的狭窄温区内实现，缺乏宽温域方案。

Method: 在同一样品上于5–1273 K范围测量拉曼与PL光谱：追踪面外A1g Raman模式的线性温度漂移，以及PL中性激子发射随温度的红移并采用半经验Pässler模型拟合；同时对比非封装与hBN封装样品。

Result: A1g模式在100 K以上呈线性温度依赖：非封装WS2斜率为-0.0138 cm^-1/K（298–1223 K拟合），hBN封装样品为-0.0121 cm^-1/K（100–1273 K拟合）；中性激子发射随温度连续红移并可由Pässler模型良好描述。

Conclusion: 两种指标分别探测晶格非谐性（lattice anharmonicity）与带隙重整化（bandgap renormalization）两种不同物理机制，共同构成5–1273 K的非接触式测温手段，并可推广至更广泛的二维半导体。

Abstract: Reliable temperature measurement is essential for characterizing and engineering two-dimensional (2D) semiconductor devices, yet optical thermometry of 2D materials has so far been demonstrated only over narrow temperature windows spanning a few hundred kelvin. Here, we establish a comprehensive optical thermometry framework for monolayer WS2 from 5~K to 1273~K. We show that: (i) the out-of-plane A1g Raman mode shifts linearly with temperature above 100~K, with a slope of -0.0138 cm^{-1}/K in non-encapsulated WS2 (fitted over 298-1223~K) and a measurably smaller slope of -0.0121 cm^{-1}/K in an hBN-encapsulated sample (fitted over 100-1273~K); (ii) photoluminescence spectra measured over the same 5--1273~K range provide an independent optical indicator of the temperature: the neutral-exciton emission redshifts continuously with temperature and is well described by the semi-empirical Pässler model. The two indicators probe distinct physical mechanisms, lattice anharmonicity and bandgap renormalization, and together provide contact-free thermometry for monolayer WS2 from 5~K to 1273~K, an approach applicable to 2D semiconductors more broadly.

</details>


### [95] [Control of the Electronic and Atomic Structure of the Altermagnetic α-MnTe Surface via Substrate-Induced Surfactants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35236)
*Martin Heinrich,Mahdi Hajlaoui,Takuya Takashiro,Tetiana Zakusylo,Petr Babor,Ondrej Caha,Dawid Wutke,Natalia Olszowska,Gunther Springholz,Matthias Muntwiler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该摘要指出 altermagnet（交变磁体）α-MnTe 薄膜表面原子结构强烈依赖衬底，衬底诱导的 InTe 偏析层及其准周期位错网络会产生不同表面态，从而显著影响表面电子能带结构，故表面结构的控制与表征对理解与调控交变磁体性质至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnets）具有动量依赖的自旋极化能带，为自旋电子学器件（spintronic devices）提供新途径；α-MnTe 因巨大自旋劈裂（spin splitting）与室温以上保持交变磁序而备受关注，但其薄膜生长困难且受衬底相互作用支配，表面结构差异尚未被系统认识。

Method: 对比不同衬底上生长的 α-MnTe 薄膜表面：在 SrF2(111) 上形成原子级洁净、完美 (2x2) 重构表面；在 InP(111)A 上则因 In 从衬底向表面发生“超级偏析（super-segregation）”而始终为 InTe 终止。结合角分辨光电子能谱（angle resolved photoemission）与隧道谱（tunneling spectroscopy）表征表面电子结构。

Result: InTe 表面活性层（surfactant layer）形成准周期位错网络（quasi-periodic dislocation network）以容纳 InTe/MnTe 晶格失配（lattice mismatch）；不同表面终止导致出现不同的表面态，并强烈影响表面处电子能带结构。

Conclusion: 表面原子结构的控制与表征对理解交变磁体性质具有根本重要性，并为实际器件中自旋极化态（spin-polarized states）的工程化调控（engineering and tailoring）提供空间。

Abstract: Altermagnets are a novel class of materials with unique momentum dependent spin-polarization of the electronic band structure, which opens up new avenues for spintronic devices. Altermagnetic α-MnTe is particularly interesting for such applications due to its giant spin splitting and the persistence of altermagnetic order up to room temperature. Growth of thin films is, however, challenging and governed by interactions with the substrate. As a result, the surface of α-MnTe thin films exhibits very different atomic structures in dependence of the substrate material. Whereas on SrF2(111) an atomically clean, perfectly (2x2) reconstructed surface forms, on InP(111)A the MnTe surface is always InTe terminated due to super-segregation of In from the substrate to the surface. Most strikingly, the InTe surfactant layer displays a quasi-periodic dislocation network to accommodate the InTe/MnTe lattice mismatch. This leads to the emergence of distinct surface states in dependence of surface termination that strongly affect the electronic band structure at the surface as shown by angle resolved photoemission and tunneling spectroscopy. Thus, control and characterization of the atomic structure of the surface is fundamentally important for understanding the properties of altermagnets and provides room for engineering and tailoring of spin-polarized states in actual devices.

</details>


### [96] [Flexural Altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35238)
*Songbo Li,Jinxiong Jia,Zeyu Li,Xudong Zhu,Jiaqi An,Feitong Chu,Songbo Zhang,Wenguang Zhu,Hualing Zeng,Zhenhua Qiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出以弯曲形变（flexural deformation）作为在二维共线反铁磁体（collinear antiferromagnets）中实现交错磁（altermagnetism）的通用途径，并以单层 MnPS3 为例验证了 d 波与 i 波交错磁态及可切换的横向自旋流（transverse spin current）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是寻找一种可调控、多功能的机制，在二维共线反铁磁体中工程化交错磁态（altermagnetic states）并控制其自旋劈裂（spin splitting）的对称性。

Method: 通过设计弯曲方式（tailoring the flexure）选择性地破缺不同对称性，并结合紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），对单层 MnPS3 进行验证。

Result: 在单层 MnPS3 中实现了具有不同波对称性的 d 波与 i 波交错磁态，可反转其自旋劈裂，并在 d 波态中获得可切换的横向自旋流。

Conclusion: 弯曲形变可作为调控二维共线反铁磁体交错磁相与自旋输运性质的通用手段，为交错磁自旋电子学（altermagnetic spintronics）提供对称性设计思路。

Abstract: We establish flexural deformation as a versatile route for engineering altermagnetism in two-dimensional collinear antiferromagnets. By tailoring the flexure, we selectively break different symmetries to realize altermagnetic states with distinct wave symmetries and reverse their spin splitting. A tight-binding model and first-principles calculations for monolayer MnPS3 demonstrate d- and i-wave states and a switchable transverse spin current in the d-wave state.

</details>


### [97] [Impact of organic spacer cations on the light and thermal stability of two-dimensional lead halide perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35247)
*Mahesh Eledath-Changarath,Jesús Ortiga-Fibla,Andrés F. Gualdrón-Reyes,Jesús Rodríguez-Romero,Iván Mora-Seró,Ana Cros,Núria Garro,Rodolfo Canet-Albiach,Juan P. Martínez-Pastor,Andrii Boichuk,Tetiana Boichuk,Juan F. Sánchez-Royo,Marie Krecmarova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以共聚焦显微拉曼（confocal micro-Raman）与X射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, XPS）对比BA2PbI4与PEA2PbI4两种二维铅卤钙钛矿的环境光/热稳定性，发现PEA间隔阳离子可改善晶体形貌、抑制晶格缺陷，从而减缓降解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期稳定性（long-term stability）是限制二维铅卤钙钛矿（two-dimensional lead halide perovskites）光伏与光电子应用的关键瓶颈，而有机间隔阳离子（organic spacer）的选择既影响环境稳定性，也决定结构与载流子动力学（carrier dynamics），故需明确不同间隔阳离子的稳定性作用。

Method: 采用共聚焦显微拉曼光谱与X射线光电子能谱，对分别含正丁基铵（n-butylammonium, BA）与2-苯乙基铵（2-phenylethylammonium, PEA）间隔阳离子的BA2PbI4和PEA2PbI4纳米片进行环境光照与热应力下的稳定性分析。

Result: 新鲜剥离的两种纳米片室温发光（luminescence）相近，且均通过有机组分流失、快速相分解为PbI2与Pb0而降解；但PEA阳离子显著改善晶体形貌并抑制晶格缺陷（lattice defects），使降解更慢、对光照与热应力的耐受性更强。

Conclusion: 实现二维卤化物钙钛矿器件的长期稳定性需结合结构与化学工程（structural and chemical engineering）、表面钝化（surface passivation）及封装（encapsulation）策略，同时稳定性与器件性能之间的平衡仍是关键考量。

Abstract: Long-term stability remains a critical challenge limiting the photovoltaic and optoelectronic applications of two-dimensional lead halide perovskites. In these materials, the choice of the organic spacer plays a key role not only in enhancing environmental stability, but also in determining their structural characteristics and carrier dynamics. Here, we present an environmental photo- and thermal-stability analysis of BA2PbI4 and PEA2PbI4 two-dimensional lead halide perovskites by means of confocal micro-Raman and X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy techniques. We have investigated two different organic spacers, n-butylammonium (BA) and 2-phenylethylammonium (PEA), which provide comparable room-temperature luminescence in freshly exfoliated perovskite nanoflakes and degrade similarly through the removal of organic components and fast crystal phase decomposition into PbI2 and Pb0. However, we observed that the PEA organic cation significantly improves crystal morphology and suppresses lattice defects, resulting in slower degradation and enhanced resistance to light and thermal stress under ambient conditions. Our findings indicate that structural and chemical engineering, along with surface passivation and encapsulation strategies, are essential for achieving long-term stability in two-dimensional halide perovskite devices. Nevertheless, optimizing the balance between stability and device performance remains a critical consideration.

</details>


### [98] [Investigation of the Microstructural and Magnetic Properties of Ni on GeSn Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35273)
*Tharundev V,Dushyant Singh,Subha Maity,Vigneshwari S R,Dhammapriy Gayakwad,Shivesh Yadav,Krista R Khiangte*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在Ni(15 nm)/Ge₁₋ₓSnₓ/Ge/Gd₂O₃/Si(111)异质结中，573 K退火保持Ge₀.₉₂Sn₀.₀₈的织构而Ge₀.₉₀Sn₀.₁₀出现微孪晶；973 K退火导致严重结构退化与Ni-Ge-Sn互混及Sn表面偏析，并显著抑制铁磁响应，从而建立GeSn晶体质量—界面互扩散—磁性的直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeSn上Ni层的热处理会显著改变Ni薄膜及Ni/GeSn界面，但GeSn晶体质量如何影响Ni-Ge-Sn界面演化以及Ni层磁响应仍认识不足，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对x = 0.08与0.10的Ni(15 nm)/Ge₁₋ₓSnₓ/Ge/Gd₂O₃/Si(111)异质结分别进行573 K与973 K、10 s的快速热退火（rapid thermal annealing），并采用X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）、极图分析（pole-figure analysis）、X射线光电子能谱深度剖析（XPS depth profiling）以及变温与变场磁化测量进行表征。

Result: 573 K退火下Ge₀.₉₂Sn₀.₀₈保持晶体织构，而Ge₀.₉₀Sn₀.₁₀出现微孪晶（microtwin）与结构完整性下降；973 K（作为严苛上限参考）使两者均严重退化且高Sn样品更甚。XPS显示随退火温度升高Ni-Ge-Sn互混加剧并伴随明显Sn表面偏析（Sn surface segregation）；磁性上原始与573 K样品共存铁磁（ferromagnetic）与超顺磁（superparamagnetic）贡献，973 K则出现高度不饱和的M(H)回线及饱和磁化强度与矫顽力降低。

Conclusion: 磁性变化与广泛的界面合金化（interfacial alloying）及金属Ni层的稀释相一致；研究确立了GeSn晶体质量、界面扩散与磁行为之间的直接关联。

Abstract: Thermal processing of nickel (Ni) layers on germanium-tin (GeSn) can strongly modify the Ni film and the Ni/GeSn interface within a restricted thermal budget; however, the influence of GeSn crystalline quality on Ni-Ge-Sn interfacial evolution and the magnetic response of the Ni layer remains incompletely understood. Here, Ni (15~nm)/Ge$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$/Ge/Gd$_2$O$_3$/Si(111) heterostructures ($x = 0.08$ and $0.10$) were investigated following rapid thermal annealing at 573 and 973~K for 10~s using X-ray diffraction, pole-figure analysis, X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy depth profiling, and temperature- and field-dependent magnetization measurements. Annealing at 573~K preserves the crystallographic texture of Ge$_{0.92}$Sn$_{0.08}$, whereas Ge$_{0.90}$Sn$_{0.10}$ exhibits microtwin formation and reduced structural integrity. Annealing at 973~K, employed as a severe upper-bound thermal reference, causes extensive structural degradation in both heterostructures, with substantially greater deterioration in the higher-Sn sample. XPS depth profiling reveals progressive Ni-Ge-Sn intermixing accompanied by pronounced Sn surface segregation with increasing annealing temperature. Magnetization measurements show coexisting ferromagnetic and superparamagnetic contributions in the as-deposited and 573~K samples, whereas annealing at 973~K strongly suppresses the ferromagnetic response, producing highly unsaturated $M(H)$ loops together with reduced saturation magnetization and coercivity. These magnetic changes are consistent with extensive interfacial alloying and dilution of the metallic Ni layer. This study establishes a direct correlation between GeSn crystalline quality, interfacial diffusion, and magnetic behavior.

</details>


### [99] [Direct observation of a spin-polarized surface Dirac gap in the antiferromagnetic topological insulator NdBi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35353)
*Yuto Fukushima,Hiroyuki S. Suzuki,Masayuki Ochi,Takuma Nishida,Toshiya Ikenobe,Hiraku Saito,Yuto Kinoshita,Kaishu Kawaguchi,Hiroaki Tanaka,Ayumi Harasawa,Takushi Iimori,Koichiro Yaji,Masashi Tokunaga,Taro Nakajima,Kiyohisa Tanaka,Takahiro Morimoto,Ryo Mori,Takeshi Kondo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用7 eV激光自旋分辨角分辨光电子能谱（spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）在反铁磁拓扑绝缘体（antiferromagnetic topological insulator, AFM-TI）NdBi中观测到17 meV的自旋极化拓扑表面狄拉克锥能隙，并证明该能隙仅在表面磁序存在时打开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFM-TI中打开狄拉克锥能隙（Dirac gap）是实现量子化反常霍尔效应（quantized anomalous Hall effect）的关键，但原型材料MnBi2Te4观测到的能隙远小于理论预测甚至近乎缺失，实验与理论长期存在矛盾，需要找到能清晰呈现本征能隙的材料平台。

Method: 采用高分辨率7 eV激光自旋分辨角分辨光电子能谱，将自旋极化表面狄拉克锥与体态来源的电子态明确区分；并结合温度依赖测量与可控表面污染实验，考察表面磁性与狄拉克能隙的关联。

Result: 在NdBi的狄拉克点观测到17 meV的能隙，与密度泛函理论（density functional theory）预测的12 meV高度吻合；温度依赖与表面污染实验显示，能隙仅在表面磁序存在时打开。

Conclusion: 结果为AFM-TI中存在本征狄拉克能隙提供了明确的光谱学证据，并将NdBi确立为可化解实验与理论长期分歧的模型平台。

Abstract: A gapped Dirac cone in antiferromagnetic topological insulators (AFM-TIs) is essential for realizing the quantized anomalous Hall effect, yet its intrinsic magnitude remains controversial. In the prototypical AFM-TI MnBi2Te4, however, the observed Dirac gap is significantly smaller than theoretical predictions or nearly absent, highlighting a long-standing discrepancy. Here we demonstrate that NdBi hosts a spin-polarized topological surface Dirac cone with a sizable gap of 17 meV at the Dirac point, in excellent agreement with density functional theory predictions (12 meV). This direct observation is enabled by high-resolution 7 eV laser-based spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, which unambiguously disentangles the spin-polarized surface Dirac cone from bulk-derived states. Furthermore, through temperature dependence and controlled surface contamination, we establish a direct linkage between surface magnetism and the Dirac gap, demonstrating that the gap opens only in the presence of surface magnetic order. These results provide definitive spectroscopic evidence for an intrinsic Dirac gap in AFM-TIs and establish NdBi as a model platform to resolve the long-standing discrepancy between experiment and theory in AFM-TIs.

</details>


### [100] [Simultaneous suppression and temperature-driven crossover of particle-like and wave-like thermal transport in trigonal CsAg$_3$S$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35360)
*Vivek Gusain,B K Mani*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用含三阶与四阶非谐性的自洽声子理论（self-consistent phonon theory）及统一热输运理论（unified theory of thermal transport），预测三方相 CsAg₃S₂ 在 300 K 具有 0.222 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹ 的超低晶格热导率，并揭示粒子型与波动型通道可被同时抑制及主导通道随温度交叉的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体材料中粒子型（particle-like, κ_p）与波动型（wave-like, κ_c）热输运通道通常相互制约，抑制其一往往增强另一，因此二者同时抑制仍是难题；作者据此探究该问题在三方 P3̄m1 CsAg₃S₂ 中的表现。

Method: 采用包含立方与四阶非谐性（cubic and quartic anharmonicity）的自洽声子理论（self-consistent phonon theory）结合统一热输运理论（unified theory of thermal transport）计算晶格热输运；电输运在动量弛豫时间近似（momentum relaxation time approximation, MRTA）下计算。

Result: 300 K 时晶格热导率为 0.222 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，呈现 ∼T⁻⁰·⁴² 的弱温度依赖，显著偏离 ∼T⁻¹；κ_p 与 κ_c 均低。局域 Cs 模式增强非谐散射、Ag–S 骨架软化降低声子群速度，共同压制 κ_p；稀疏且彼此分离的高频 S 衍生光学模式通过限制声子模式间相干耦合压制 κ_c。主导通道存在温度交叉：300 K 时 κ_p 约占总热导率 74%，700 K 时 κ_c 约占 63%；结合 MRTA 电输运，700 K、n 型掺杂下 ZT 达 1.28。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了三方 CsAg₃S₂ 超低热导率的微观机制，并提出 AM₃Ch₂ 族三元硫族化合物（ternary chalcogenides）中共价 M–Ch 骨架调控可作为在成键层级设计原则（bonding-hierarchy design principle）下进一步降低 κ_p 的额外调节参数。

Abstract: The simultaneous suppression of particle-like ($κ_p$ ) and wave-like ($κ_c$) thermal transport channels in crystalline materials remains a challenging problem, as reducing one channel can enhance the other. Here, we investigate the thermal transport in trigonal $P\Bar{3}m1$ CsAg$_3$S$_2$ using self-consistent phonon theory with cubic and quartic anharmonicity and the unified theory of thermal transport.
  Our calculations predict an ultralow lattice thermal conductivity of 0.222 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$ at 300 K, featuring simultaneously low $κ_p$ and $κ_c$ with a weak temperature dependence of $\sim$T$^{-0.42}$, which deviates significantly from the conventional $\sim$T$^{-1}$ behavior. This simultaneous suppression arises from localized Cs modes, which enhance anharmonic scattering, and the softer Ag-S framework, which reduces phonon group velocities, together suppressing $κ_p$, in conjunction with sparse, well-separated high-frequency S-derived optical modes that suppress $κ_c$ by limiting the coherent coupling between the phonon modes. Furthermore, the dominant transport channel exhibits a temperature-dependent crossover, with $κ_p$ contributing $\sim$74 \% of total lattice thermal conductivity at 300~K and $κ_c$ contributing $\sim$63 \% at 700~K. Combining the ultralow thermal conductivity with electrical transport calculated within the momentum relaxation time approximation (MRTA), we obtain a $ZT$ of 1.28 for $n$-type doping at 700 K. The present work not only clarifies the microscopic mechanism of ultralow thermal conductivity in trigonal CsAg$_3$S$_2$ but also identifies the covalent M-Ch framework modification within the AM$_3$Ch$_2$ family of ternary chalcogenides as an additional tuning parameter for further reducing $κ_p$ within the bonding-hierarchy design principle.

</details>


### [101] [Zeeman-field-induced three-spin interactions and relativistic coupling to the scalar spin chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35401)
*Samir Lounis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 从微观电子哈密顿量出发，证明传导电子的塞曼耦合（Zeeman coupling）会在原子自旋模型中额外诱导出场线性的三自旋相互作用（three-spin interactions），并系统分类、推导其形式、对称性约束与可观测效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子自旋模型（atomistic spin models）通常只通过单个磁矩的塞曼能量引入磁场，可能遗漏电子层面产生的更高阶修正；本文旨在检验传导电子塞曼耦合是否生成时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）所允许的最低阶场线性自旋相互作用。

Method: 采用巨势（grand potential）的格林函数展开（Green-function expansion），结合Hubbard三聚体（Hubbard trimer）的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）验证，并对Rashba电子气中的磁性三聚体（magnetic trimers）作解析推导。

Result: 识别出两类相互作用：无自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）时存在的各向同性项 (S_i·S_j)(S_k·B)，其总强度满足涉及费米能处三格点电子环流的求和规则；相对论项需自旋轨道耦合，包含 K_ijk·B 与标量自旋手性（scalar spin chirality）χ_ijk 的耦合。该手性耦合在无自旋轨道耦合时于自旋通道被禁止，而轨道通道可复现Mott绝缘体的轨道通量机制；两者独立且可加。

Conclusion: 这些相互作用可移动磁化台阶场（magnetization-step fields）、改变场诱导的非共面性；在连续极限下，手性耦合将斯格明子（skyrmions）与反斯格明子（antiskyrmions）分裂，相对论项产生场依赖的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用。

Abstract: Magnetic fields are commonly incorporated in atomistic spin models through the Zeeman energy of individual magnetic moments. Starting from a microscopic electronic Hamiltonian, we show that the Zeeman coupling of conduction electrons also generates field-linear three-spin interactions, the lowest-order corrections allowed by time-reversal symmetry. Using a Green-function expansion of the grand potential, we identify two classes of interactions. Isotropic terms of the form $(\mathbf S_i\cdot\mathbf S_j)(\mathbf S_k\cdot\mathbf B)$ exist without spin-orbit coupling, and their total strength obeys a sum rule involving the three-site electronic loop at the Fermi energy. Relativistic terms require spin-orbit coupling and include $\mathbf K_{ijk}\cdot\mathbf B,χ_{ijk}$, where $χ_{ijk}=\mathbf S_i\cdot(\mathbf S_j\times\mathbf S_k)$ is the scalar spin chirality. We show that this coupling is forbidden in the spin channel without spin-orbit coupling, whereas the orbital channel recovers the orbital-flux mechanism of Mott insulators. Exact diagonalization of the Hubbard trimer confirms the independence and additivity of both channels. For magnetic trimers in Rashba electron gases, we derive analytical expressions showing that $\mathbf K_{ijk}$ lies in-plane at first order in spin-orbit coupling but vanishes for $C_{3v}$-symmetric trimers, where an out-of-plane component emerges at second order. These interactions can shift magnetization-step fields and modify field-induced noncoplanarity. In the continuum limit, the chirality coupling splits skyrmions from antiskyrmions, while the relativistic terms generate a field-dependent Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction.

</details>


### [102] [Precursor Ratio-Controlled Chloride Vacancies in Lead-Free KCuCl3-x Memristors for Forming-Free Gradual Resistive Switching and Neuromorphic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35436)
*Chinmayee Mandar Mhaskar,Sangeeta Roy Chaudhuri,Ayan Roy Chaudhuri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过湿化学法在无铅全无机KCuCl3-x薄膜中实现氯空位（chloride vacancy, VCl）浓度调控，并建立缺陷浓度—电子结构—输运动力学—神经形态性能的直接关联；最优VCl浓度（约1.3×10^21 cm^-3）下器件实现高线性电导调制与>10^4次脉冲耐久，ANN仿真在MNIST与Fashion-MNIST上分别取得98.96%与91.82%的识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻材料中的缺陷工程（defect engineering）是调控离子输运与模拟阻变（analog resistive switching）、满足神经形态计算（neuromorphic computing）需求的重要手段，但缺陷浓度与器件学习性能之间的定量关联尚不明确，且需面向无铅（lead-free）体系。

Method: 采用湿化学合成（wet chemical synthesis）并通过控制前驱体比例，系统调节KCuCl3-x薄膜中VCl浓度；结合电子结构表征与输运分析，制备W/KCuCl3-x/FTO忆阻器并测试其阻变与突触特性，进而将实测电导特性作为突触权重（synaptic weights）导入人工神经网络（artificial neural network）仿真。

Result: 前驱体比例变化可控调节VCl浓度，诱导末端氯位点优先形成空位、带隙收窄（band-gap narrowing）与电子无序增强；器件表现稳定的双极性VCM型阻变（valence change mechanism），VCl增加促进无forming（forming-free）工作；最优浓度下兼具离子迁移率与缺陷可控性，获得高线性增强/抑制（potentiation/depression）特性及>10^4次脉冲耐久，MNIST与Fashion-MNIST识别准确率分别为98.96%和91.82%。

Conclusion: 前驱体比例调控的VCl工程可同步优化离子输运、模拟阻变与神经形态学习性能，为无铅卤化物钙钛矿（halide perovskite）忆阻器提供通用材料设计原则，服务于低能耗神经形态电子学（neuromorphic electronics）。

Abstract: Defect engineering of memristive materials offers a powerful strategy for regulating ionic transport and analog resistive switching required for neuromorphic computing. Here, we report a wet chemical synthesis route for chloride-vacancy (VCl) engineering in lead-free all-inorganic KCuCl3-x thin films and establish a direct correlation between defect concentration, electronic structure, transport dynamics, and neuromorphic performance. Controlled precursor-ratio variation systematically tunes the VCl concentration in KCuCl3-x thin films, driving preferential vacancy formation at the terminal chloride sites, band-gap narrowing, and enhanced electronic disorder. W/KCuCl3-x/FTO memristors exhibit stable bipolar VCM-type resistive switching, with increasing VCl concentration promoting forming-free operation. An optimal VCl concentration (~ 1.3*10^21 cm^-3) provides a balance between ionic mobility and defect controllability, resulting in highly linear potentiation/depression characteristics and superior pulse endurance (>104 cycles). When the experimentally measured conductance characteristics of the optimized memristor are implemented as synaptic weights in artificial neural network simulations, recognition accuracies of 98.96% and 91.82% are achieved on the MNIST and Fashion-MNIST datasets, respectively.
