These findings establish precursor ratio-controlled VCl engineering as an effective strategy for simultaneously optimizing ionic transport, analog switching, and neuromorphic learning in lead-free halide perovskite memristors, providing a general materials design principle for energy-efficient neuromorphic electronics.

</details>


### [103] [Pulsed Laser Deposition of Ferroelectric SrTiO3 Shells Enables Polarization-Controlled Charge Separation in TiO2 Photoanode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35454)
*Yiqi Jin,Weizeng Zheng,Beibei Wang,Lei Zhao,Binmin Wu,Leonhard Mayrhofer,Xiangjian Meng,Hao Shen,Xiaodan Wang,Weiguang Huang,Junhao Chu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition）在水热生长的 TiO2 纳米棒阵列上制备化学计量比 SrTiO3 壳层，实现原子级核壳结构，并建立了应变—极化—内建电场—电荷分离的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电极化（ferroelectric polarization）可调控光电极界面电荷分离，但化学转化法所得铁电壳层缺乏亚纳米厚度控制、共格应变保持与化学洁净界面。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积在 TiO2 纳米棒阵列上沉积 SrTiO3 壳层，结合高分辨透射电镜（high-resolution transmission electron microscopy）与 X 射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy）表征结构与化学状态，并用开尔文探针力显微镜（Kelvin probe force microscopy）直接成像极化诱导内建电场。

Result: 光电流密度随壳层厚度呈火山型依赖，最优 15 分钟样品的 1.23 V vs. RHE 下光电流为 1.9 mA cm-2，超过水热法基准；壳层为四方畸变、氧化学计量的，铁电性源于共格界面应变（coherent interfacial strain）而非缺陷偶极；内建电场强度与电荷转移效率、光电流密度呈比例关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将铁电光阳极工程从经验优化转变为原子级可控、可预测的材料策略，并有望推广至可见光吸收体与复杂铁电异质结构。

Abstract: Ferroelectric polarization offers a powerful route to govern the interfacial charge separation in photoelectrodes; however, chemically converted ferroelectric shells lack sub-nanometer thickness control, coherent strain retention, and chemically clean interfaces. Here, we use pulsed laser deposition to fabricate stoichiometric SrTiO3 shells on hydrothermally grown TiO2 nanorod arrays, achieving atomically defined core-shell architectures while preserving the one-dimensional transport geometry. The photocurrent density followed a volcano-shaped dependence on shell thickness and reached 1.9 mA cm-2 at 1.23 V versus RHE under AM 1.5G illumination for the optimized 15-mins sample, exceeding the established hydrothermal benchmark. High-resolution transmission electron microscopy and X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy revealed a tetragonally distorted, oxygen-stoichiometric shell, identifying coherent interfacial strain rather than defect dipoles as the origin of the ferroelectric order. Kelvin probe force microscopy directly imaged a polarization-induced built-in electric field whose magnitude scaled with charge-transfer efficiency and photocurrent density. Together, these complementary structural, chemical, electrostatic, and device-level measurements established a quantitative strain-polarization-built-in field-charge separation relationship. This work transforms ferroelectric photoanode engineering from empirical optimization into an atomically controllable and predictive materials strategy for high-efficiency solar water-splitting devices. This approach is readily extendable to visible-light absorbers and complex ferroelectric heterostructures.

</details>


### [104] [Multi-objective Bayesian optimization reveals HiPIMS deposition rates exceeding direct current sputtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35535)
*Nathan Rodkey,Felix Thelen,Alfred Ludwig,Sebastian Siol*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用多目标贝叶斯优化（multi-objective Bayesian optimization, MOBO）在高维脉冲溅射（pulsed sputter）工艺空间中高效采样，发现包括单脉冲在内的多种高功率脉冲磁控溅射（HiPIMS）工艺可实现高于直流（DC）参考的沉积速率，挑战了薄膜制造中"质量-速度"权衡的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HiPIMS因高电离通量分数具备工艺设计优势，但离子被负偏压靶材回吸（back-attraction）导致沉积速率偏低；双极、多脉冲、斩波等复杂脉冲方案虽可缓解该问题，却使工艺参数组合激增至1E5–1E20量级，亟需高效智能的采样方法。

Method: 以脉冲包（pulse-packets）工艺空间为对象，采用多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）在探索过程中采样超过2,000个不同工艺，优化目标为高电离通量分数与高沉积速率。

Result: 实验发现许多HiPIMS工艺（含单脉冲）沉积速率可超过DC参考；在1.0 Pa工作气压、5–20%中等电离率条件下，毫秒尺度（ms-scale）脉冲表现最佳。作者假设此类工艺最大化了气体稀薄化（gas rarefaction），在靶材与基片之间形成低碰撞通道。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了脉冲溅射工艺此前未报道的行为，并挑战了薄膜制造中长期存在的膜质量与沉积速度之间的权衡假设。

Abstract: Pulsed sputter processes are essential for a wide range of applications from hard coatings to microelectronics. High power impulse magnetron sputtering (HiPIMS) offers unique possibilities for process design due to its high ionized flux fraction. Because of the high peak-current densities involved, correspondingly high plasma densities generate many ions. This enables tuning of kinetic energies by applying accelerating bias potentials. However, some HiPIMS processes have low deposition rates due to the back-attraction these ions experience with the negatively charged sputter target. Recent work in HiPIMS has targeted this problem, exploring complex pulsing schemes such as bipolar, multi-pulse, or chopped HiPIMS. These pulsing schemes introduce another problem: process complexity. As such, the process space, even with coarse intervals quickly blows up to 1E5-1E20 possible process combinations. This calls for efficient and intelligent sampling, which can be enabled by machine-learning algorithms such as Bayesian optimization. In this work we use multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) to efficiently sample the process space of pulse-packets, targeting high ionization flux fractions and deposition rates. Over 2,000 distinct processes were sampled during this exploration. Surprisingly, we found that many HiPIMS processes, including single pulse, can achieve deposition rates higher than DC references. In fact, at a working pressure of 1.0 Pa and moderate ionization rates of 5-20% we found that ms-scale pulses were ideal. We hypothesize these processes maximize gas rarefaction, creating a low-collision channel between the target and the substrate. These results showcase previously unreported behavior of pulsed sputter processes and challenge a long-held trade-off between film quality and speed in thin-film manufacturing.

</details>


### [105] [High-efficiency solid-state electrochemical thermal switching through phonon engineering of 2D materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35547)
*Harry Mclean,Steven Paul Hepplestone*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用通用机器学习原子间势（universal machine-learned interatomic potentials, uMLIPs）与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）筛选57种过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDCs），发现Li$_a$ZrS$_2$具有优异的可逆热导开关效应，lithiation后横截面热导率由约3.7降至0.05 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$，并验证其废热回收潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有大热对比度的可逆固态热开关（reversible solid-state thermal switches）仍十分稀缺，但对先进热管理（thermal management）至关重要，因此需要寻找可实现显著热导率调控的材料与机制。

Method: 结合通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）与密度泛函理论（DFT），系统筛选57种TMDCs，涵盖1T与2H相以及Li、Mg插层（intercalation）情形，并进行器件级建模（device-level modelling）评估废热回收性能。

Result: Li$_a$ZrS$_2$兼具优异的热导率开关性能与低热容；Li插层至Li$_{0.125}$ZrS$_2$使横截面热导率（cross-plane thermal conductivity）从约3.7降至0.05 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$，优于已有固态电化学热开关；脉冲系统可回收理想开关能量的86%。机制归因于Zr--Zr骨架显著软化（平均力常数由约4.2降至1.2 eV Å$^{-2}$）及热输运声学声子（acoustic phonons）散射增强。

Conclusion: 电化学插层（electrochemical intercalation）是调控层状材料（layered materials）热输运（thermal transport）的有效途径，为高性能固态热开关与废热回收应用提供了材料基础与机制解释。

Abstract: Reversible solid-state thermal switches with large thermal contrasts remain rare, yet are essential for advanced thermal management. Using universal machine-learned interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) and density functional theory (DFT), we screen 57 transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs), spanning the 1T and 2H phases with Li and Mg intercalation. Among the candidates, Li$_a$ZrS$_2$ combines exceptional thermal conductivity switching with low heat capacity. Lithiation to Li$_{0.125}$ZrS$_2$ reduces the cross-plane thermal conductivity from approximately 3.7 to 0.05 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$. The switching behaviour exceeds that of previously reported solid-state electrochemical thermal switches. Device-level modelling further demonstrates the potential for waste-heat recovery, with a pulsed system capturing 86\% of the energy recovered by an ideal switch. We attribute the switching behaviour to substantial softening of the Zr--Zr framework, with the mean force constant decreasing from approximately 4.2 to 1.2 eV $\mathrm{\mathring{A}}^{-2}$, alongside enhanced scattering of heat-carrying acoustic phonons. These findings establish electrochemical intercalation as a promising route to engineering thermal transport in layered materials.

</details>


### [106] [Retention-constrained fine-tuning of a pre-trained aluminium MACE potential for non-equilibrium defect dynamics and plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35653)
*Yue Wu,Yuan Tian,Deng Pan,Mingwei Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作为铝 MACE 势 QP17 建立了一种“保留约束下的微调协议（retention-constrained fine-tuning protocol）”，通过条件读出不确定度、全参数迭代更新与相对能量锚定，在改善缺陷与塑性相关精度（plastic-deformation observables）的同时保持近平衡弹性与振动响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potential, MLIP）用于非平衡缺陷动力学与塑性时，需要提升缺陷精度，但不能损害近平衡的弹性（elastic）与振动（vibrational）响应，二者间的平衡是核心难点。

Method: 方法整合条件读出不确定度（conditional readout uncertainty）、迭代全参数更新（iterative full-parameter updates）与协议兼容的相对能量锚定（relative-energy anchoring）；利用 144 个线性读出权重的敏感度指导采集，配合批内协方差更新与演化的神经表示，共进行四轮主动学习（active-learning）。

Result: 四轮主动学习（32 个采集构型、400 次梯度更新）后，力不确定度采集将平均开发集力误差从 22.43 降至 21.52 meV/Å，优于随机采样（22.70）、几何多样性选择（22.46）与全 104 个候选标签均匀训练（22.71）；相对能误差由 0.375 降至 0.207 meV/atom，谐波声子（harmonic phonon）均方根漂移低于 0.05 THz；平面缺陷、孪晶界与晶界等样本外评估显示缺陷能与偏应力（deviatoric stress）具有可迁移增益，且未拟合维里应力（virial stress）。

Conclusion: 闭环 MD-DFT 试点表明自动接受门控（acceptance gating）可拦截违反历史弹性群能量或振动容差的更新；上述组件共同构成保留约束微调协议，在保持体相力学响应测试指标的同时改善塑性变形可观测量。

Abstract: Adapting a pre-trained machine-learning interatomic potential for non-equilibrium defect dynamics and plasticity requires improving defect accuracy without degrading the near-equilibrium elastic and vibrational response. Here we achieve this balance for the aluminium MACE potential QP17 by integrating conditional readout uncertainty, iterative full-parameter updates and protocol-compatible relative-energy anchoring. Sensitivities over 144 linear readout weights guide acquisition, with within-batch covariance updates and evolving neural representations. After four active-learning rounds (32 acquired configurations and 400 gradient updates), force-uncertainty acquisition reduces mean development force error from 22.43 to 21.52 meV/Angstrom, outperforming random sampling (22.70), geometry-diversity selection (22.46) and uniform training across all 104 candidate labels (22.71) under the same update budget. All three force-uncertainty runs satisfy bulk-mechanical retention criteria: historical relative-energy error decreases from 0.375 to 0.207 meV/atom, while harmonic phonon root-mean-square drift remains below 0.05 THz. Matched objective ablations reveal that relative-energy anchoring suppresses harmonic drift. Out-of-sample evaluations across planar faults, twin boundaries and grain boundaries demonstrate transferable gains in defect energies and deviatoric stresses, even though virial stresses are never fitted. A closed-loop molecular dynamics and density functional theory (MD-DFT) pilot confirms that automated acceptance gating intercepts updates that violate historical elastic-group energy or vibrational tolerances. Together, these components establish a retention-constrained fine-tuning protocol for improving plastic-deformation observables while preserving the tested bulk mechanical response.

</details>


### [107] [Phonon-assisted shift-current from first-principles: application to AlN monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35662)
*Yuncheng Mao,Pierre Lechifflart,Elena Cannuccia,Claudio Attaccalite*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种第一性原理（first-principles）方法，将特殊位移技术（special displacement technique）与基于动力学贝里相位（dynamical Berry phase）的实时模拟结合，用于研究扩展体系中的声子辅助位移电流（phonon-assisted shift current），并在单层六方氮化铝（h-AlN）上验证了温度与声子散射对非线性光电流的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性光响应描述通常忽略晶格热涨落与声子散射，难以真实刻画有限温度下的光电流产生，因此需要能自然纳入声子辅助电子跃迁的有限温度第一性原理框架。

Method: 将特殊位移技术与基于动力学贝里相位的实时模拟相融合，在含时动力学中直接获取有限温度非线性光学响应，并自然引入声子散射与声子辅助电子跃迁；以间接带隙、无反演对称性的单层六方氮化铝（h-AlN）为代表性算例。

Result: 计算表明声子介导过程具有显著相关性，温度对层状材料中位移电流的幅度与光谱特征起决定作用；不同厚度 h-AlN 层已被制备与表征，使预测具备实验检验的可能性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可处理有限温度声子辅助位移电流的第一性原理途径，强调温度与晶格涨落在非线性光电流建模中不可忽略，并为层状材料的实验验证提供了具体候选体系。

Abstract: In this work, we present a first-principles approach to investigate phonon-assisted shift current in extended systems. Our method combines the special displacement technique with real-time simulations based on the dynamical Berry phase. Merging these two frameworks enables direct access to non-linear optical responses at finite temperatures, while naturally incorporating phonon scattering and phonon-assisted electronic transitions into the time-dependent dynamics. This allows us to capture temperature-dependent features and lattice fluctuations that are essential for a realistic description of non-linear photocurrent generation. As a representative example, we apply our approach to the shift-current response of a monolayer of hexagonal AlN (h-AlN). This system provides an ideal testing ground because it features an indirect band gap and lacks inversion symmetry. Moreover, h-AlN layers of different thicknesses have been fabricated and characterized in a variety of experimental settings, opening the concrete possibility of testing our predictions. Our results demonstrate the relevance of phonon-mediated processes and the importance of temperature in determining the magnitude and spectral features of the shift current in layered materials.

</details>


### [108] [Third harmonic generation in the transition metal trichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35803)
*Timothy Y. Chung,Vergil M. Schreiber,Randy M. Sterbentz,David P. Shelton,Eduardo Flores,Jose Ramon Ares,Isabel J. Ferrer,Joshua O. Island*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报道了剥离的过渡金属三硫族化物（transition metal trichalcogenides, TMTs）晶体——ZrS3、HfS3、TiS3 与 NbS3——在 1560 nm 飞秒激光非共振激发下的偏振依赖三次谐波（third harmonic generation, THG）响应，发现各成员均表现出强各向异性，各向异性比最高达 χ11/χ22 ~ 6.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMTs 是一类具有强各向异性电学与光学性质的层状二维材料，但其偏振依赖非线性光学响应尚缺乏系统研究；本文旨在考察这一晶体家族的三次谐波各向异性及其来源。

Method: 采用 1560 nm 飞秒激光进行非共振激发，测量剥离 TMT 晶体的偏振依赖三次谐波信号；基于单斜（monoclinic）与三斜（triclinic）晶体的预期非线性依赖关系，提取相对非线性磁化率系数（nonlinear susceptibility coefficients）。

Result: 所有研究的晶体均显示大的偏振三次谐波各向异性；各向异性比在 ZrS3 中最大并沿该家族递减，作者将其与三次谐波能量接近各材料强 b 偏振吸收边相联系。唯一的三斜晶体 NbS3 在厚片中表现出显著各向异性，而在最薄的薄片中该各向异性显著减弱，归因于三斜畸变所依赖的 Peierls 型 Nb-Nb 二聚化（Peierls-type Nb-Nb dimerization）的部分弛豫。

Conclusion: 该晶体家族的大各向异性非线性响应使其区别于其他二维材料，因而特别值得进一步研究。

Abstract: The transition metal trichalcogenides (TMTs) are an interesting class of layered 2D materials that have strong anisotropic electrical and optical properties. We report on the polarization-dependent nonlinear optical response of exfoliated crystals at the third harmonic using non-resonant excitation with a 1560 nm femtosecond laser. All members of the TMT crystal family explored (ZrS3, HfS3, TiS3, and NbS3) display large anisotropy in their polarized third harmonic generation signal, with anisotropy ratios as large as chi_11/chi_22 ~ 6.5. From the expected nonlinear dependence of these monoclinic and triclinic crystals we extract the relative nonlinear susceptibility coefficients. We find that the anisotropy ratio is largest for ZrS3 and decreases across the family, a trend we associate with the proximity of the third harmonic energy to the strongly b-polarized absorption edge of each material. NbS3, the only triclinic crystal, displays a pronounced anisotropy in thick flakes that is strongly reduced in the thinnest flakes, which we attribute to a partial relaxation of the Peierls-type Nb-Nb dimerization that underlies the triclinic distortion. The large anisotropic nonlinear response in this crystal family sets it apart from other 2D materials and makes it particularly attractive for further investigation.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [The Number of Polytope Trees and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33344)
*Ali Aliev,Natalia Amburg*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文以对称性加权方式枚举由正多胞体（regular polytope）粘合而成的复形，通生成函数（generating function）、递推关系与渐近估计给出计数结果，并推广到对偶图（dual graph）含环的情形做双变量枚举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是系统刻画由正多胞体副本粘合形成的复形的计数问题，并希望对具有树状及含环对偶图结构的构型给出可计算的枚举框架与渐近信息。

Method: 方法上对多胞体树（polytope trees）及将其附着于固定初始复形所得的复形，建立割接递推（cut-and-join recurrence）与生成函数的微分方程，导出显式计数公式与渐近估计；随后将该方法推广至对偶图含环的复形，按多胞体数与自由面（free facets）数进行双变量枚举。

Result: 所得结果包括：树情形的割接递推、生成函数微分方程、显式公式与渐近估计；含环情形下双变量生成函数的递推关系与偏微分方程，连通构型的递推关系，以及（可能非连通的）构型的显式系数公式。

Conclusion: 结论是割接递推与生成函数方法可统一处理对称性加权的多胞体复形枚举，且在有、无指定初始复形两种设定下均给出连通与非连通构型的计数结果。

Abstract: We study the symmetry-weighted enumeration of complexes formed by gluing copies of a regular polytope. For polytope trees and complexes obtained by attaching such trees to a fixed initial complex, we derive cut-and-join recurrences, differential equations for generating functions, explicit counting formulas, and asymptotic estimates. We then extend the method to complexes with cycles in their dual graphs, enumerating them by the numbers of polytopes and free facets. We derive recurrence relations and partial differential equations for the corresponding bivariate generating functions. In this setting, we obtain recurrences for connected configurations and explicit coefficient formulas for possibly disconnected configurations, both with and without a distinguished initial complex.

</details>


### [110] [The counts of parity-odd vs even late-growing permutations in the group $\mathbb{S}_{2p-1}$ differ by $(-1)^p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35779)
*Kian C. Shah,Arthemy V. Kiselev*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 论文证明：当 n=2p−1 为奇数时，满足各前缀部分和不超过平均值置换对应部分和的“晚增长置换”（late-growing permutations）集合 Φ_p 按置换奇偶性分成 Φ_p⁺ 与 Φ_p⁻，二者大小之差恰为 (−1)^{p+1}，即几乎（相差 1）对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象是受前缀部分和约束的置换集合 Φ_p，其自然问题是该集合在奇偶两类（parity-even vs odd）之间的分布是否对称；摘要指出这种分布呈“几乎对称”结构，差值仅为符号项 (±1)。

Method: 证明为构造性（constructive）：把置换 σ∈Φ_p 视为整数格（integral lattice）ℤ 上的受约束游走（constrained walks），并给出两个过滤器（filters），将 Φ_p⁺ 与 Φ_p⁻ 中除一个元素外的其余元素逐一配对。

Result: 对 n=2p−1 奇数，Φ_p = Φ_p⁺ ⊔ Φ_p⁻ 满足 |Φ_p⁺| − |Φ_p⁻| = (−1)^{p+1}；未被配对的剩余游走 σ* 具有单调递增的步长幅度（monotonically growing step amplitudes），它决定了占优的奇偶子集。

Conclusion: 晚增长置换集合的奇偶分布几乎对称，其不对称量被单个特殊置换 σ*（步长幅度单调增长）所承担；该结论由格路上的显式配对构造给出。

Abstract: A permutation $σ\in\mathbb{S}_n$ of objects $\{1,\ldots,n\}$ is called \emph{late-growing} if its partial sums never exceed the respective partial sums of the `average' permutation (with the arithmetic mean $n^{-1}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^n i = (n+1)/2$ of $1,\ldots,n$ on average at each entry $σ(i)$): $\sum_{i=1}^k σ(i) \leqslant k\cdot (n+1)/2$ for all positive $k\leqslant n$. We prove that for $n=2p-1$ odd, the set $Φ_p\subseteq \mathbb{S}_{2p-1}$ of late-growing permutations is partitioned into parity-even vs odd elements, $Φ_p = Φ_p^{+} \sqcup Φ_p^{-}$, in an almost symmetric way: $| Φ_p^{+} | - | Φ_p^{-} | = (-1)^{p+1}$. The proof is constructive: by viewing the permutations $σ\inΦ_p$ as constrained walks on the integral lattice $\mathbb{Z}$, we describe two filters that pair all but one element in $Φ_p^{+}$ versus $Φ_p^{-}$; the remaining walk $σ^*$ with monotonically growing step amplitudes determines the dominant parity subset.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [On the Energy Minimizing Problem in Plasma Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33040)
*Maurice de Gosson*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要指出，等离子体聚变理论中寻找最低能量态（minimum energy states）的问题可借助辛刚性（symplectic rigidity）概念重新表述；为回避 Qin 的 "Gromov 基态"（Gromov ground states）方案所导致的拓扑困难，作者提出基于 Wigner 变换（Wigner transform）与高斯态（Gaussian states）辛几何的量子力学形式化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体物理及其聚变应用中的一个基本问题是确定等离子体的最低能量态。既有方案中，Gardner 方法仅使用保体积微分同胚（volume-preserving diffeomorphisms），而 Qin 提出的 "Gromov 基态" 进一步纳入辛刚性现象，但其构造停留在经典（哈密顿）框架内，引发拓扑困难，这构成了本文的出发点。

Method: 作者引入该问题的量子力学表述（quantum-mechanical formulation），其基础为 Wigner 变换以及高斯态的辛几何（symplectic geometry of Gaussian states），以此绕开经典框架下的拓扑障碍。

Result: 摘要仅表明该量子力学表述可规避 Qin 方案中的拓扑困难，未给出具体计算、定理或数值结果。

Conclusion: 将最低能量态问题从经典哈密顿域迁移至基于 Wigner 变换与高斯态辛几何的量子力学框架，是处理辛刚性约束下拓扑困难的一条可行途径。

Abstract: A fundamental question in plasma physics and its applications to fusion theory is to find minimum energy stares of plasma.. Hong Qin has suggested the use of what he calls "Gromov ground states" which take into account the phenomenon of symplectic rigidity, as opposed to Gardner's classical approach which only use volume-preserving diffeomorphisms. Howev4er Qin's approach leads to topological difficulties because his constructions remain in the classical (Hamiltonian) domain. We circumvent these difficulties by introducing a quantum-mechanical formulation of the problem, based on the Wigner transform and the symplectic geometry of Gaussian state

</details>


### [112] [Dynamics of two-dimensional Bose gases in the focusing case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33212)
*Lukas Reichmann*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在二维空间中对具有奇异相互作用（可含小幅吸引部分）的玻色气体，证明局域初值下玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose–Einstein condensation）的传播及范数逼近，相互作用规模可涵盖任意多项式标度乃至指数标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于玻色气体动力学中凝聚传播与范数逼近的结果多受限于相互作用强度或标度范围，本文旨在将结论推广到更广泛的相互作用标度（含指数标度）与更一般的时变外势情形，包括无外势的情况。

Method: 采用解析证明方法，处理二维奇异相互作用（允许小幅吸引部分），并允许相互作用按 \(N^\beta\)（\(0<\beta<\infty\)）的多项式标度及 \(e^{N^\kappa}\)（\(0<\kappa<1/4\)）的指数标度进行缩放；外势取为时间依赖、至多二次且下有界。

Result: 证明了在所述条件下玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的传播，并给出相应的范数逼近（norm approximation）结果；结论覆盖任意多项式标度与特定范围内的指数标度，且适用于时变至多二次外势及无外势情形。

Conclusion: 结果将玻色气体动力学中凝聚传播与范数逼近的适用范围拓展至更宽泛的相互作用标度与更一般的外势设置，增强了此类结论的普适性。

Abstract: We prove the propagation of Bose--Einstein condensation and a norm approximation for the dynamics of a Bose gas with localized initial data in two dimensions with singular interactions that may have a small attractive part. We allow the scaling of the interaction to be any polynomial scaling $N^β$ for $0<β<\infty$ and even exponential scaling $e^{N^κ}$ for $0<κ<1/4$. Our approach covers external potentials that are time-dependent, at most quadratic, and bounded below, including the situation without an external potential.

</details>


### [113] [Ground-state spaces of generalized stabilizer codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33221)
*Cathy Cheng,Nigel Higson,Danilo Polo Ojito,Emil Prodan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将广义稳定子量子模型（generalized stabilizer quantum models）的基态空间完全确定问题纳入 $C^*$-代数（$C^*$-algebra）框架，通过扭群 $C^*$-代数与拉格朗日子群的对应，给出任意维度非紧流形上模型基态空间的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稳定子模型（stabilizer models）研究多局限于特定维度或紧致拓扑背景，缺乏对任意维度、任意拓扑类型非紧流形（non-compact manifolds）上广义模型的统一处理，尤其缺少对基态空间（ground-state spaces）结构的完整刻画。

Method: 将量子自旋代数 $\mathcal{A}$ 实现为带辛结构（symplectic structure）的无限离散泡利模（Pauli module）$P$ 的扭群 $C^*$-代数；建立稳定子模型与 $P$ 的各向同性子群（isotropic subgroups）之间的对应，并利用子群的拉格朗日扩张（Lagrangian extension）确定 $\mathcal{A}$ 的 $C^*$-对角，其外尔群胚（Weyl groupoid）通过连续 1-上闭链（continuous 1-cocycle）编码动力学。

Result: 对每个可容许的拉格朗日模型显式构造出一族极值基态（extremal ground-states）及其扇区分解（decomposition into sectors）；当扇区空间为 $T_0$ 时，得到基态空间的完全刻画。此外证明每个拉格朗日稳定子模型存在唯一的、无阻挫（frustration-free）且具谱隙（spectral gap）的基态。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于任意维度，涵盖高维阿贝尔量子双（higher abelian quantum doubles）、同调码（homological codes）及一大类稳定子模型，为稳定子模型的基态结构分析提供了统一而一般的代数工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we determine the complete ground-state spaces for a broad class of generalized stabilizer quantum models defined over CW-decompositions of non-compact manifolds of arbitrary dimensions and topological type. We introduce a $C^*$-algebraic framework in which the quantum spin algebra $\A$ is realized as the twisted group $C^*$-algebra of an infinite discrete Pauli module $P$ endowed with a symplectic structure. We establish a correspondence between stabilizer models and isotropic subgroups of $P$: Every Lagrangian extension of such a subgroup determines a $C^*$-diagonal of $\A$, whose Weyl groupoid encodes the dynamics through a continuous 1-cocycle. This realization yields an explicit family of extremal ground-states for every admissible Lagrangian model, together with their decomposition into sectors. When the sector space is $T_0$, this gives a complete characterization of the ground-state spaces. We also prove that every Lagrangian stabilizer model admits a unique frustration-free ground state with a spectral gap. Our framework applies in arbitrary dimension, including higher abelian quantum doubles, homological codes, and a broad class of stabilizer models.

</details>


### [114] [Partial Airy ideals and coloured blobbed topological recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33232)
*Gaëtan Borot,Vincent Bouchard,Swagata S. Datta*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文定义并研究“部分Airy理想（partial Airy ideals）”——一类方程数可少于变量数的PDE线性系统，证明其存在唯一的指数型解（配分函数），并借助自对偶能级下𝒲(gl_r)代数的扭曲自由场表示从谱曲线构造大量实例，导出“着色blobbed拓扑递归”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Bouchard–Borot–Chidambaram–Creutzig–Noshchenko）将Airy理想与𝒲代数及拓扑递归相联系，但可允许的谱曲线（spectral curves）范围受限；本文旨在通过允许方程数少于变量数的系统来显著扩大可处理的谱曲线类。

Method: 定义部分Airy理想，证明其指数型解在给定初始条件选取下唯一；利用自对偶能级（self-dual level）下𝒲(gl_r)代数的扭曲自由场表示（twisted free-field representations），结合附加的“颜色（colour）”数据构造谱曲线对应的部分Airy理想实例；并将配分函数的微分约束转化为关联函数（correlators）上的“着色圈方程（coloured loop equations）”，给出递归留数表述。

Result: 获得了部分Airy理想的唯一性结果及一大类由谱曲线构造的实例，显著扩大容许谱曲线集合（需附带颜色数据）；配分函数的微分约束等价于着色圈方程，其解可由着色blobbed拓扑递归（coloured blobbed topological recursion）递归留数形式给出，且包含Borot–Shadrin的blobbed拓扑递归作为特例。

Conclusion: 部分Airy理想为拓扑递归提供了统一框架：已知的blobbed拓扑递归被纳入其中并获得自然解释，同时着色版本推广了现有的拓扑递归形式。

Abstract: We define and study the notion of partial Airy ideals, which are certain linear systems of PDEs that may contain less equations than variables. We show that they admit a unique solution of exponential type (the partition function) modulo a choice of initial condition. We construct a large class of examples of partial Airy ideals attached to spectral curves using twisted free-field representations of $\mathcal{W}(\mathfrak{gl}_r)$-algebras at self-dual level. This generalizes the results of Bouchard- Borot-Chidambaram-Creutzig-Noshchenko by significantly enlarging the set of admissible spectral curves, provided they come with additional data that we call colour. We show that the differential constraints on the partition function are equivalent to a set of equations on the associated correlators, which we call coloured loop equations. We find a recursive residue formulation for the solutions of those equations, the coloured blobbed topological recursion}, which is a generalization of the currently known forms of topological recursion. It includes the blobbed topological recursion of Borot-Shadrin as a special case, giving it a natural interpretation within the formalism of partial Airy ideals.

</details>


### [115] [A non-equilibrium thermodynamics formulation of shear set-points in adaptively remodeling vascular networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33250)
*Enrique Hernández-Lemus,Daniel Pérez-Calixto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将血管半径适应性重构表述为非平衡热力学框架：血流为线性Onsager系统，半径慢重构为Rayleigh耗散泛函生成的梯度流，并给出涨落定理、互易性检验与网络拓扑配分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果指出，在幂律维护成本下于固定预算内最小化耗散会产生局部剪切设定点（shear set-point），并由纯局部适应规则趋近最优；本文旨在为这一结果提供非平衡热力学（non-equilibrium thermodynamics）表述。

Method: 将血流视为精确线性的Onsager系统，把血管半径的慢重构写成由Rayleigh耗散泛函（Rayleigh dissipation functional）生成的梯度流，由此定义结构迁移率（structural mobility）；引入含时代谢乘子（metabolic multiplier）精确实施预算约束，并构建以回路数为序参量的网络拓扑配分函数（partition function）。

Result: 结构迁移率与每根血管的维护成本成反比，可由半径涨落测得；预算乘子有闭式解，若固定乘子则瞬态期预算偏差可达三分之一。重构排开的流体通过对称非对角迁移率耦合流动与结构，使Onsager互易性（Onsager reciprocity）成为可检验命题——相邻血管半径涨落应反相关。含噪声时结构部分服从第二定律与积分涨落定理（integral fluctuation theorem）；上游传导响应等非互易信号破坏时间反演对称性并产生可从时间序列检测的驻留熵（housekeeping entropy）。数值测试显示回路数对有效温度不塌缩为单一曲线，且强烈依赖检测阈值。

Conclusion: 血管网络重构可在非平衡热力学与随机热力学语言下统一刻画，互易性、涨落定理与熵产生均给出可实验检验的预言；而拓扑层面的回路统计未能以有效温度单参数标度，并受检测阈值强烈影响。

Abstract: Blood vessels remodel in response to wall shear stress while the network operates
  under a metabolic maintenance budget. For a power-law maintenance cost, minimizing
  dissipation at fixed budget yields a local shear set-point, and a purely local adaptation rule
  drives the network towards this optimum. Here we give this result a non-equilibrium thermodynamic
  formulation. Blood flow is an exactly linear Onsager system, and the slow remodeling of vessel radii
  is a gradient flow generated by a Rayleigh dissipation functional. This identifies the structural
  mobility, which scales inversely with the maintenance cost of each vessel and can be measured from
  radius fluctuations. Enforcing the budget exactly requires a time-dependent metabolic multiplier,
  which we obtain in closed form; with a fixed multiplier the budget is violated by up to one third
  during transients. The fluid displaced by remodeling vessels couples flow and structure through a
  symmetric, non-diagonal mobility, so that Onsager reciprocity becomes a testable statement:
  neighbouring vessels should show anticorrelated radius fluctuations. With noise, the structural
  sector obeys a second law and an integral fluctuation theorem. Non-reciprocal signaling between
  vessels, such as upstream conducted responses, then breaks the time-reversal
  symmetry of radius fluctuations and produces housekeeping entropy, detectable from time series of
  realistic length. Finally, we construct a partition function over network
  topologies with the number of loops as order parameter. Numerical tests show that loop counts do
  not collapse onto a single curve against an effective temperature, and that they
  depend strongly on the detection threshold.

</details>


### [116] [A Twisted Affine Interpolation of the $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded Integrable Super-Liouville and super-Sinh-Gordon Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33485)
*Naruhiko Aizawa,Ren Ito,Luiza Miranda,Francesco Toppan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是该系列第三篇工作，将二维 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分级可积 Toda 场论（integrable Toda field theories）的构造从有限情形推广到（扭）仿射色李超代数（affine color Lie superalgebras）的无限情形，并以 $\widehat{\mathfrak{osp}(2|2)^{(2)}}$ 的 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分级扩展统一插值超Liouville与超Sinh-Gordon模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前两篇工作分别基于 Rittenberg-Wyler 色李代数构造了仲玻色子（parabosonic）模型、并利用有限 Rittenberg-Wyler 色李超代数处理了仲费米子情形（$\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分级超Liouville理论）；本文旨在扩展至无限维代数情形。

Method: 借助 $\widehat{\mathfrak{osp}(2|2)^{(2)}}$ 的 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分级扩展，在三种框架下讨论：Polyakov 焊接（soldering）、Leznov-Savel'ev 的 Lax 对（零曲率）表述（Lax pair / zero-curvature formulation）以及 Bäcklund 变换（Bäcklund transformations）。

Result: 该分级扩展可在特殊极限下分别还原超Liouville与超Sinh-Gordon模型；此外，从 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分级 $\widehat{\mathfrak{osp(2|2)}^{(2)}}$ WZNW 模型的哈密顿约化（Hamiltonian reduction）诱导出一个色李超代数，其包含三个不等价超Virasoro子代数：$\mathcal{N}=2$ 超Virasoro、$(1,\frac{3}{2},\frac{3}{2},2)$ 型与 $(\frac{3}{2},\frac{3}{2},2,2)$ 型 $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分级超Virasoro。

Conclusion: $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-分级方法可自洽地推广至无限维（扭）仿射色李超代数，并兼容焊接、零曲率与 Bäcklund 三类经典可积框架，同时给出含三种超Virasoro子代数的代数结构。

Abstract: This paper is a third one in a series devoted to the construction of $2D$, $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded, integrable Toda field theories. The first paper introduced parabosonic models obtained from Rittenberg-Wyler color Lie algebras; the second paper explained how to deal with parafermions (the $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded super-Liouville theory) from finite Rittenberg-Wyler color Lie superalgebras. This new paper extends the construction to the infinite case of (twisted) affine color Lie superalgebras. A $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded extension of ${\widehat{\mathfrak{osp}(2|2)^{(2)}}}$ allows to interpolate the super-Liouville and the super-Sinh-Gordon models, recovered as special limits. The $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded extensions of three frameworks are here discussed: the Polyakov's soldering, the Leznov-Savel'ev Lax pair (zero-curvature) formulation and the Bäcklund transformations. As a byproduct of the construction, the Hamiltonian reduction from the $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded ${\widehat{\mathfrak{osp(2|2)}^{(2)}}}$ WZNW models induces a $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded color Lie superalgebra which admits three inequivalent superVirasoro subalgebras; they are the ${\cal N}=2$ superVirasoro, the $(1,\frac{3}{2},\frac{3}{2},2)$ $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded superVirasoro and the $(\frac{3}{2},\frac{3}{2},2,2)$ $\mathbb{Z}_2^2$-graded superVirasoro.

</details>


### [117] [Admissible Subspaces for the Collective Haar Twirl: Schur-Weyl Classification and Heredity under Tensor-Factor Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33553)
*Sayantan Ganguly*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用 Schur-Weyl 对偶（Schur-Weyl duality）完整分类了张量幂空间 \(\mathcal H_d^{\otimes t}\) 中使集体 Haar 平均（collective Haar twirl）成为替换信道（replacement channel）的所有子空间，从而刻画了支持量子私有广播（quantum private broadcasting, QPB）的明文空间，并进一步研究了删除接收方下的归约与遗传性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明完整张量幂空间一般不适合 QPB，并指出对称子空间 \(\mathrm{Sym}^t(\mathcal H_d)\) 是自然的明文空间、引入了对称酉 \(t\)-设计（symmetric unitary \(t\)-designs），但遗留了"哪些其他子空间支持私有广播"这一开放问题。

Method: 借助 Schur-Weyl 对偶对所有子空间 \(\mathcal S\subseteq\mathcal H_d^{\otimes t}\) 进行分类：要求每个 Schur-Weyl 块权重与编码态无关，且在每个活跃块 \(V_\lambda\otimes W_\lambda\) 中多重度空间 \(W_\lambda\) 上的约化态固定；将该分类表述为 \(\mathcal T^{(t)}\) 的互补信道的 Knill-Laflamme 条件的 Schur-Weyl 形式。

Result: 给出了显式的双块（two-block）与一般多块（multiblock）构造；引入删除接收方下的规范归约（canonical reduction），并刻画精确受限设计（exact restricted designs）的隐私遗传性、设计遗传性与 QPB 遗传性：对称塔完全 QPB-遗传，反对称塔完全隐私-遗传且仅在 \(s\leq(d+1)/2\) 时由第 \(s\) 层到 \(s-1\) 层设计-遗传。

Conclusion: 对任意 Schur-Weyl 块中的规范载体（canonical carriers），其遗传归约由 Young 图上的无重分支（multiplicity-free branching）及相关 Peter-Weyl 系数空间的包含关系决定，从而统一刻画了支持 QPB 的子空间结构及其遗传行为。

Abstract: Quantum private broadcasting (\textsf{QPB}) asks whether a quantum message can be encrypted once, broadcast to several recipients, and decrypted locally while the joint ciphertext reveals no plaintext information. Broadbent, González-Guillén, and Schuknecht showed that the full tensor-power space \(\mathcal H_d^{\otimes t}\) is generally unsuitable for \textsf{QPB}, identified \(\mathrm{Sym}^t(\mathcal H_d)\) as a natural plaintext space, introduced symmetric unitary \(t\)-designs, and asked which other subspaces support private broadcast. Using Schur-Weyl duality, we classify all subspaces \(\mathcal S\subseteq\mathcal H_d^{\otimes t}\) on which the collective Haar twirl \(\mathcal T^{(t)}\) is a replacement channel. Precisely, every Schur-Weyl block weight must be independent of the encoded state, and in each active block \(V_λ\otimes W_λ\), the reduced state on the multiplicity space \(W_λ\) must be fixed. This yields explicit two-block and general multiblock constructions and realizes the classification as the Schur-Weyl form of the Knill-Laflamme conditions for a complementary channel of \(\mathcal T^{(t)}\). We also introduce a canonical reduction under deletion of a recipient and characterize privacy heredity, design heredity, and \textsf{QPB}-heredity for exact restricted designs. The symmetric tower is fully \textsf{QPB}-hereditary, whereas the antisymmetric tower is fully privacy-hereditary and is design-hereditary from level \(s\) to \(s-1\) exactly when \(s\leq(d+1)/2\). For canonical carriers in arbitrary Schur-Weyl blocks, hereditary reduction is governed by multiplicity-free branching along the Young graph and inclusion of the associated Peter-Weyl coefficient spaces.

</details>


### [118] [Fractional reversible chemical kinetics: Time-Driven Monte Carlo method and application to enzyme conformational dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33652)
*Nelson H. T. Lemes,Higor V. M. Ferreira*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于带Mittag-Leffler等待时间分布的连续时间随机游走（CTRW）建立可逆两态化学动力学的分数阶框架，给出Caputo导数动力学的微观基础与精确解析解，并首次提出时间驱动蒙特卡罗算法，成功拟合TbQSOX酶构象动力学（分数阶次α=0.22）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶动力学方程通常以唯象方式引入，缺乏微观随机过程基础；同时，用于模拟Caputo分数阶微分方程所支配的非马尔可夫随机动力学的数值方法尚不充分，尤其是可逆两态反应体系。

Method: 从带Mittag-Leffler等待时间分布（Mittag-Leffler waiting-time distributions）的连续时间随机游走（Continuous-Time Random Walk, CTRW）形式导出可逆两态动力学，从而为Caputo导数（Caputo derivative）支配的分数阶方程提供微观依据，并获得封闭形式精确解析解；同时开发时间驱动蒙特卡罗（time-driven Monte Carlo）算法模拟其潜在的非马尔可夫随机动力学。

Result: 获得态布居时间演化的封闭形式精确表达式；所建算法可模拟不可约的非马尔可夫随机动力学。应用于TbQSOX酶构象动力学，分数阶模型准确重现呈幂律行为的实验动力学，估计分数阶次α=0.22，与观测到的反常动力学一致。

Conclusion: CTRW框架为分数阶可逆两态动力学提供了微观基础与解析工具，配套的时间驱动蒙特卡罗算法为Caputo型非马尔可夫系统的模拟提供新手段；模型在酶构象弛豫数据上的成功应用表明分数阶描述适用于反常动力学体系。

Abstract: In this work, we present a fractional framework for reversible two-state chemical kinetics derived from the Continuous-Time Random Walk (CTRW) formalism with Mittag-Leffler waiting-time distributions. This derivation provides a microscopic basis for the fractional kinetic equations governed by the Caputo derivative. An exact analytical solution is obtained, yielding closed-form expressions for the time evolution of the state populations. To the best of our knowledge, a time-driven Monte Carlo algorithm is developed for the first time to simulate the irreversible underlying non-Markovian stochastic dynamics governed by a system of Caputo fractional differential equations. The proposed framework is applied to the conformational dynamics of the TbQSOX enzyme, whose relaxation exhibits power-law behavior. The fractional model accurately reproduces the experimental kinetics, providing an estimate of the fractional order $α= 0.22$, which is consistent with the observed anomalous dynamics.

</details>


### [119] [Linear statistics in the Coulomb gas: cumulants beyond rotational invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33659)
*Paul Bourgade,Pierre Le Doussal,Gregory Schehr*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文推导了囚禁于一般外势的高维经典库仑气体在自由液滴几何下，线性统计量 \( \mathcal{L}_N \) 的领头三阶与四阶累积量（cumulant）的显式边界积分公式，突破此前仅限径向对称情形的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性统计量 \( \mathcal{L}_N \) 的领头方差（variance）已广为人知，但更高阶累积量（higher cumulants）研究甚少，且现有累积量计算仅覆盖径向（radial）势与径向测试函数的特殊情形；对一般光滑液滴（droplet）缺乏显式结果。

Method: 引入倾斜势（tilted potential） \( U_s=U+s f/\beta \) 及其对应的液滴形变，利用狄利克雷能量（Dirichlet energy）的形状导数（shape derivative）进行渐近展开；该形变被解释为障碍问题（obstacle problem）表述下自由边界的无穷小运动。

Result: 得到以平衡液滴上内蕴边界积分（intrinsic boundary integral）表述的三、四阶累积量显式公式：三阶累积量依赖 \( f \) 调和延拓（harmonic extension）法向导数跃变的立方；四阶累积量还涉及外迪利克雷-诺伊曼算子（exterior Dirichlet-to-Neumann operator）、边界平均曲率（mean curvature）及背景密度法向导数。

Conclusion: 结果将 Wiegmann–Zabrodin 在二维 \( \beta=2 \) 情形的既有研究推广至一般维数与一般非径向设定，并对其极限累积量给出严格证明；公式在圆盘、半平面与球面上特化，并与可精确求解的非径向形变（二维平移椭圆液滴、三维类球体形变）核对一致。

Abstract: We consider a classical Coulomb gas of $N$ particles in dimension $d\geq2$, confined by a general external potential $U$ in the large-$N$ limit. In that limit the density of particles has a compact support called the droplet. We study smooth linear statistics ${\mathcal L}_N=\sum_{i=1}^N f({\bf x}_i)$ at large $N$. While the leading variance of ${\mathcal L}_N$ is by now well understood in many settings, much less is known about higher cumulants. When $f({\bf x})=f(|{\bf x}|)$ and the potential is rotationally invariant, these cumulants were computed recently at leading order. We derive explicit formulas for the leading third and fourth cumulants for general smooth droplets, without assuming that either the potential or the test function is radial. The answers are expressed as intrinsic boundary integrals over the equilibrium droplet. The third cumulant depends on the cube of the jump of the normal derivative of the harmonic extension of $f$, while the fourth cumulant also involves the exterior Dirichlet-to-Neumann operator, the mean curvature of the boundary, and normal derivatives of the background density. Our derivation uses the tilted potential $U_s=U+s f/β$, the associated deformation of the droplet, and shape derivatives of the Dirichlet energy. This deformation has a natural interpretation as the infinitesimal motion of the free boundary in the obstacle-problem formulation of the equilibrium measure. Our formulas extend previous studies by Wiegmann and Zabrodin in two dimensions for $β=2$. In their setting, we provide a rigorous proof of the limiting third and fourth cumulants, supporting the proposed formulas for general Coulomb gases. We specialize the general formulas to the disk, the half-plane and the sphere, and check them against exactly solvable non-radial deformations, including translated elliptic droplets in $d=2$ and spheroidal deformations in $d=3$.

</details>


### [120] [V-Representability Beyond Singular Times in Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory: The Hubbard Dimer Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33837)
*Éric Cancès,Théo Duez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对双粒子 Hubbard 二聚体模型（Hubbard dimer model）自旋单态（spin-singlet） sector，研究含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）中的全局 V-可表示性（global V-representability）问题：在通常反演公式失效的奇异时刻之后，相互作用体系的含时密度能否由无相互作用 Kohn–Sham 体系复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 TDDFT 方案依赖含时反演公式，在奇异时刻（singular times）处失效，全局 V-可表示性是否成立尚不明确，且缺乏对有效势解集的系统刻画。

Method: 以可控外势 u 为控制变量，在双粒子 Hubbard 二聚体单态 sector 内，同时考察基于含时变分原理（time-dependent variational principle）的传统 TDDFT 表述与近期提出的几何表述（geometric formulation）两种框架。

Result: 对两种表述均证明了全局时间 V-可表示性（global-in-time V-representability），并证明奇异时刻会导致含时有效势（time-dependent effective potential）的非唯一性。

Conclusion: 奇异时刻不破坏全局 V-可表示性，但使含时有效势非唯一；论文对相应解集给出了分类。

Abstract: We study the global $V$-representability problem in time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT) for the two-particle Hubbard dimer model in the spin-singlet sector. In particular, we investigate whether the time-dependent density of the interacting system obtained for a given one-body external potential $u$ (the tunable control variable) can be reproduced by a non-interacting Kohn--Sham system beyond the singular times at which the usual inversion formulas break down. We deal with both the conventional formulation of TDDFT based on some time-dependent variational principle and the recently introduced geometric formulation of TDDFT (Phys. Rev. Lett. 136 (2026) 256401, Phys. Rev. A 113 (2026) 062222). For both principles, we prove global-in-time $V$-representability, show that singular times induce non-uniqueness of the time-dependent effective potential, and classify the set of solutions.

</details>


### [121] [Matrix representations of ${\rm BC}_n$ associative Yang--Baxter equations and related long-range spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33913)
*M. Matushko,A. Mostovskii,A. Zotov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了 ${\rm BC}_n$ 型结合型 Yang–Baxter 代数（associative Yang–Baxter equations）的矩阵表示，并据此构建源自经典可积系统的长程量子自旋链（long-range quantum spin chains）实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是建立 ${\rm BC}_n$ 型结合型 Yang–Baxter 代数的矩阵表示，并将其应用于推导长程量子自旋链及外场中 Calogero–Moser 模型相关的 $R$-矩阵值 Lax 对（Lax pairs）。

Method: 在椭圆情形下，用 ${\rm GL}_N$ 基本表示中的 Baxter–Belavin 量子 $R$-矩阵（quantum $R$-matrix）表示两组生成元，其余生成元用 Komori–Hikami 的椭圆 $K$-矩阵表示；借助 Felder–Pasquier 程序重新审视其推导，并修正奇 $N$ 情形下的幺正关系（unitarity relation）。随后用这些矩阵表示，从经典 Calogero–Inozemtsev 系统的 $R$-矩阵值 Lax 对构造长程量子自旋链，并为外场中的 Calogero–Moser 模型构造相应 Lax 对。

Result: 得到 ${\rm BC}_n$ 型代数的矩阵表示及修正后的奇 $N$ 幺正关系；由此给出从 $R$-矩阵值 Lax 对构造的长程量子自旋链实例，以及带修正自旋位置（modified spin positions）的 Haldane–Shastry 链与 Polychronakos–Frahm 链。

Conclusion: ${\rm BC}_n$ 型结合型 Yang–Baxter 代数的矩阵表示可用于系统构造与经典 Calogero–Inozemtsev、Calogero–Moser 系统相关的长程量子自旋链，并推广至外场情形与修正自旋位置的自旋链模型。

Abstract: We construct a matrix representation of the algebra defined by the associative Yang--Baxter equations of ${\rm BC}_n$ type. In the elliptic case, two sets of generators are represented in terms of the Baxter--Belavin quantum $R$-matrix in the fundamental representation of ${\rm GL}_N$, while the remaining generators are represented by the elliptic $K$-matrix found by Komori and Hikami. We revisit its derivation using the Felder--Pasquier procedure and correct the unitarity relation for odd $N$. We then use these matrix representations to construct examples of long-range quantum spin chains from $R$-matrix-valued Lax pairs for the classical Calogero--Inozemtsev system associated with the ${\rm BC}_n$ root system. We also construct $R$-matrix-valued Lax pairs for Calogero--Moser models in an external field, leading to Haldane--Shastry and Polychronakos--Frahm chains with modified spin positions.

</details>


### [122] [The Genus-One B-Model Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33984)
*Xinxing Tang,Junrong Yan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在亏格一（genus-one）B模型（B-model）层面建立了Calabi–Yau/Landau–Ginzburg对应：由$\mathbb{C}^n/\mathbb{Z}_n$上的$f$-扭曲拉普拉斯算子（$f$-twisted Laplacian）内蕴构造Landau–Ginzburg亏格一不变量，并证明其与$X_f$的BCOV不变量相差一个仅依赖维数的加性常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Calabi–Yau/Landau–Ginzburg对应（Calabi–Yau/Landau–Ginzburg correspondence）预言Landau–Ginzburg orbifold $(\mathbb{C}^n/\mathbb{Z}_n,f)$的不变量与关联Calabi–Yau超曲面（hypersurface）$X_f$的不变量之间存在密切联系，本文旨在针对亏格一B模型验证这一预言。

Method: 设$f$为次数为$n$、在原点有孤立临界点（isolated critical point）的齐次多项式（homogeneous polynomial）。作者从$\mathbb{C}^n/\mathbb{Z}_n$上的$f$-扭曲拉普拉斯算子（$f$-twisted Laplacian）出发，内蕴地（intrinsically）构造Landau–Ginzburg亏格一B模型不变量。

Result: 所构造的Landau–Ginzburg亏格一不变量与$X_f=\{[z_1:\cdots:z_n]\in\mathbb{P}^{n-1}: f=0\}$对应的BCOV不变量（BCOV invariant）在相差一个仅依赖于维数的加性常数意义下相等。

Conclusion: 该结果在亏格一B模型层面确立了Calabi–Yau/Landau–Ginzburg对应，并表明BCOV不变量可由$f$-扭曲拉普拉斯算子内蕴刻画。

Abstract: Let $f:\mathbb C^n\to\mathbb C$ be a homogeneous polynomial of degree $n$ with an isolated critical point at the origin. The Calabi--Yau/Landau--Ginzburg correspondence predicts a close relation between invariants of the Landau--Ginzburg orbifold $(\mathbb C^n/\mathbb Z_n,f)$ and those of the associated Calabi--Yau hypersurface $ X_f=\{[z_1:\cdots:z_n]\in\mathbb P^{n-1}:f(z_1,\ldots,z_n)=0\}. $ In this paper, we establish this correspondence for the genus-one B-model by constructing the Landau--Ginzburg genus-one B-model invariant intrinsically from the $f$-twisted Laplacian on $\mathbb{C}^n/\mathbb{Z}_n$ and proving that it agrees with the corresponding BCOV invariant of $X_f$ up to an additive constant depending only on the dimension.

</details>


### [123] [Stochastic quantization and Osterwalder-Schrader axioms for quantum field theory models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34063)
*Azam Jahandideh*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该博士论文在 $\mathscr{S}'(\mathbb{R}^2)$ 上构造 $P(\Phi)_2$ 欧氏量子场论（Euclidean quantum field theory），并验证 Osterwalder-Schrader 公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于随机量子化（stochastic quantization）的构造尚未建立 Osterwalder-Schrader 公理，而该公理组合对于获得 Minkowski 时空上非平凡的局部相对论量子场论至关重要。

Method: 先借助 Nelson 超压缩性估计（hypercontractivity estimate）在二维球面 $\mathbb{S}_R$ 的对偶空间 $\mathscr{D}'(\mathbb{S}_R)$ 上构造有限体积测度；再采用抛物型随机量子化（parabolic stochastic quantization）与能量方法（energy method）构造 $\mathscr{S}'(\mathbb{R}^2)$ 上的无穷体积测度，其中需建立固定截断下相应随机偏微分方程（stochastic partial differential equations）解的全局时间存在性。

Result: 在 $\mathscr{S}'(\mathbb{R}^2)$ 上完成了 $P(\Phi)_2$ 模型的构造，并验证了 Osterwalder-Schrader 公理中的正则性（regularity）、反射正性（reflection positivity）以及平移、旋转和反射不变性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在随机量子化框架下建立了此前构造未能确立的 Osterwalder-Schrader 公理组合，从而为相应的非平凡局部相对论量子场论提供了基础。

Abstract: This PhD thesis constructs the $P(Φ)_2$ Euclidean quantum field theory on $\mathscr{S}'(\mathbb{R}^2)$. We first construct the finite-volume measure of the $P(Φ)_2$ model on $\mathscr{D}'(\mathbb{S}_R)$, where $\mathbb{S}_R$ is a two-dimensional sphere of radius $R\in\mathbb{N}_+$. To this end, we rely on Nelson's hypercontractivity estimate. We then use parabolic stochastic quantization and the energy method to construct the infinite-volume measure on $\mathscr{S}'(\mathbb{R}^2)$. This requires establishing the global-in-time existence of solutions to the corresponding stochastic partial differential equations with fixed cutoffs. The remainder of the thesis is devoted to verifying the Osterwalder-Schrader axioms: regularity, reflection positivity, and invariance under translations, rotations, and reflections. This combination of axioms, which is crucial for obtaining a non-trivial local relativistic quantum field theory on Minkowski spacetime, had not been established in earlier constructions based on stochastic quantization.

</details>


### [124] [The sharp leading asymptotics of the self-energy of the spinless Pauli-Fierz model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34122)
*Shinichiro Futakuchi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文确定 Pauli-Fierz 模型基态能量 E_0(α, Λ) 在大紫外截断（ultraviolet cutoff）极限下的精确渐近式：E_0 = (1+o(1)) c_α α^{2/7} Λ^{12/7}，c_α = 0.99338…，并证明电子-场关联（electron-field correlations）不影响领头阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lieb 与 Loss 曾给出 C_1 α^{1/2} Λ^{3/2} ≤ E_0 ≤ C_2 α^{2/7} Λ^{12/7} 的上下界，并猜测由乘积态（product states）给出的上界具有正确阶数；本文旨在严格证明这一猜想并给出显式常数。

Method: 上界由固定电子密度下场能量的相干态界（coherent state bound）导出；下界综合了场的时间周期化（periodization）、逐块应用相位乘子与场空间 Hardy 不等式（Hardy inequality）的 Born-Oppenheimer 型界，以及立方体上带限场（band-limited fields）的磁 Neumann Laplacian（magnetic Neumann Laplacian）下界，其要点是满足加权梯度条件的带限场无法集中其涡度（vorticity）。

Result: 对每个固定 α > 0，当 Λ → ∞ 时 E_0(α, Λ) = (1+o(1)) c_α α^{2/7} Λ^{12/7}，且该结论在联合极限 Λ/α → ∞、α^{5/2} Λ → ∞ 下一致成立（要求 log(2+α) = o((Λ/α)^{1/21})）；由于上界对乘积态下确界已成立，E_0 与该下确界之比趋于 1。

Conclusion: 电子-场关联对领头项无贡献；条件 α^{5/2} Λ → ∞ 不可去除，因为当 α^{5/2} Λ → 0 时真空试探态（vacuum trial state）给出更低能量。结果确认了 Lieb-Loss 的猜想并给出显式常数 c_α。

Abstract: We consider the ground state energy E_0(alpha, Lambda) of a single spinless non-relativistic electron coupled to the quantized radiation field with a sharp ultraviolet cutoff Lambda (the Pauli-Fierz model), with fine structure constant alpha = e^2/(4 pi). Lieb and Loss proved that C_1 alpha^(1/2) Lambda^(3/2) <= E_0 <= C_2 alpha^(2/7) Lambda^(12/7) for large Lambda and suggested that the upper bound, which comes from product states, gives the correct order. We prove that, for every fixed alpha > 0, E_0(alpha, Lambda) = (1+o(1)) c_alpha alpha^(2/7) Lambda^(12/7) as Lambda -> infinity, with an explicit constant c_alpha = 0.99338...; more generally, this holds uniformly in the joint limit Lambda/alpha -> infinity, alpha^(5/2) Lambda -> infinity, provided log(2+alpha) = o((Lambda/alpha)^(1/21)). Since our upper bound holds already for the infimum over product states, it follows that the ratio of E_0 to this infimum tends to one: electron-field correlations do not contribute to the leading term. The condition alpha^(5/2) Lambda -> infinity cannot be dropped, since for alpha^(5/2) Lambda -> 0 the vacuum trial state gives a smaller energy. The upper bound follows from a coherent state bound for the field energy in a fixed electron density. The lower bound combines a periodization of the field, a Born-Oppenheimer bound in which phase multiplication and a Hardy inequality in field space are applied block by block, and a lower bound on the magnetic Neumann Laplacian on a cube for band-limited fields; the key point of the last is that a band-limited field obeying a weighted gradient condition cannot concentrate its vorticity.

</details>


### [125] [The effective mass of the Pauli-Fierz electron: a cutoff-independent lower bound and the breakdown of mass renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34125)
*Shinichiro Futakuchi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究无外势的自旋为零非相对论电子与量子化横电磁场耦合的Pauli-Fierz模型，证明有效质量比 h = m_eff/m 的两个下界：其一是与截断无关的 alpha 显式下界，其二是对固定 alpha 关于 lambda 的超线性增长（约 lambda^{8/7}），从而给出裸质量与物理质量匹配时紫外截断必须有限的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lieb与Loss（2002）曾预期：给定物理质量 m_phys，能重现它的裸质量只在紫外截断 Lambda 有界时存在，但此前未被证明。本文旨在为这一质量重整化（mass renormalization）的定性图像提供严格的下界估计。

Method: 第一下界以基态经场的相干平移（coherent translation）得到的试探态构造，平移的一阶项由基态极小性消去；第二下界从姊妹论文的尖锐自能（self-energy）渐近出发，采用依赖场位形的相位改造基态作为试探态。新技术包括：用光子云浅层集局部中心估计电子位置、借无散度外部子空间实现场局部部分与电子的解耦、以及证明局域化窗口边缘附近的浅凹陷稀少。

Result: 证得 h >= 1 + 0.3441 alpha/(1 + 0.2985 sqrt(alpha) + 0.1252 alpha)，对所有 alpha > 0、lambda >= 1 成立且与截断无关，其 alpha 一阶系数与Hiroshima–Spohn的微扰结果一致；又对任意固定 alpha 与任意 epsilon > 0，大 lambda 时 h >= lambda^{8/7 - epsilon}。由此 iota_PF(alpha) := inf_{lambda>0} h/lambda > 0，并推出重现 m_phys 的裸质量仅当 Lambda <= m_phys/iota_PF(alpha) 时存在。

Conclusion: 结果严格验证了Lieb与Loss关于紫外截断必须受物理质量限制的预期，并给出有效质量比随 alpha 与 lambda 的定量下界；8/7 为该方法所能达到的指数极限。

Abstract: We consider the Pauli-Fierz model of one spinless non-relativistic electron coupled to the quantized transverse electromagnetic field, without external potential, with a sharp ultraviolet cutoff Lambda and bare mass m. Let h = m_eff/m be the ratio of the effective mass, defined by the curvature of the fiber ground state energy at total momentum zero, to the bare mass; by scaling, h depends only on the fine structure constant alpha = e^2/(4 pi) and on lambda = Lambda/m. We prove two lower bounds. First, h >= 1 + 0.3441 alpha/(1 + 0.2985 sqrt(alpha) + 0.1252 alpha) for all alpha > 0 and all lambda >= 1; the bound does not depend on the cutoff, and with the band extended to the cutoff its first order coefficient in alpha is the perturbative one of Hiroshima and Spohn. The proof uses a coherent translation of the field applied to the ground state as a trial state; the term linear in the translation vanishes by minimality of the ground state. Second, for every fixed alpha, h grows faster than linearly: for every epsilon > 0, h >= lambda^(8/7 - epsilon) for all large lambda (8/7 is the limit of the method). Consequently iota_PF(alpha) := inf_{lambda > 0} h/lambda > 0, and for a fixed physical mass m_phys a bare mass reproducing it exists only if Lambda <= m_phys/iota_PF(alpha). This was expected, but not proved, by Lieb and Loss (2002). The proof of the second bound starts from the sharp self-energy asymptotics of a companion paper and uses a trial state obtained from the ground state by a phase depending on the field configuration. The new ingredients are an estimator for the position of the electron given by the local centre of a shallow level set of the photon cloud, the decoupling of a localized part of the field from the electron through a divergence-free external subspace, and a proof that shallow depressions near the edge of the localization window are rare.

</details>


### [126] [Spectral localizer for unbounded topological Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34138)
*Hermann Schulz-Baldes*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在既有与Loring合作结果的基础上，用新视角重新阐释谱局部化（spectral localizer）方法，并将该框架推广到无界带隙哈密顿量（unbounded gapped Hamiltonians），特别是矩阵值薛定谔与正则化狄拉克算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明K-理论类与Fredholm模的指标配对（index pairing）可由有限维矩阵——谱局部化——的半符号差（half-signature）计算，但该方法此前主要限于有界或特定情形，尚需推广至无界算子。

Method: 先给出对已有结果的新视角，再构造性推广框架至二阶线性微分算子形式的无界带隙哈密顿量；关键技术包括强制性的相空间局部化（coercive phase space localization）与基于小波（wavelets）的离散化。

Result: 建立了在有界与无界带隙哈密顿量情形下通过有限维谱局部化的半符号差计算指标配对的统一框架，并识别出相空间局部化的强制性以及小波离散化为关键技术洞见。

Conclusion: 谱局部化方法可自然延拓至无界二阶微分算子类哈密顿量，提示其作为指标配对计算工具的适用性较先前认识更为广泛。

Abstract: In a series of joint papers with Loring, it was shown how index parings of $K$-theory classes with Fredholm modules can be computed as the half-signature of a suitably constructed finite-dimensional matrix, called the spectral localizer. After offering a novel perspective on earlier results, it is shown how the framework can be extended to unbounded gapped Hamiltonians given as second-order linear differential operators (in particular, matrix-valued Schrödinger and regularized Dirac operators). Important insights are the coercive phase space localization and discretizations by means of wavelets.

</details>


### [127] [Random-walk scaling without Bernoulli statistics: transport, persistence and resonances in the triangle map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34179)
*Mirko Degli Esposti*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究环面上面积保持、分段抛物且李雅普诺夫指数为零的三角形映射（triangle map），通过其符号过程证明Koopman算子无非平凡H^1本征函数，并刻画该符号过程的扩散行为、首次通过指数与共振结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角形映射（triangle map）是零李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponent）的面积保持（area-preserving）系统的典型模型，其输运与遍历性质长期缺乏精确刻画；作者希望借助无累积舍入误差的精确符号计算，厘清符号过程的统计与算子层面行为。

Method: 以环面两半定义的符号过程（symbolic process）X_n=±1为对象，其部分和（partial sums）S_n刻画提升动量的输运；使用免除累积舍入误差的积分器（integrator）与多边形柱集（polygonal cylinder sets）上的精确计算，并结合Koopman算子、Green-Kubo和（Green-Kubo sum）与上同边（coboundary）分析。

Result: 证明了Koopman算子没有非常数H^1本征函数、符号不是H^1上同边、相同符号游程一致有界；一般参数下随机游走为正常扩散且高斯，但扩散系数（diffusion coefficient）并非抛硬币值，在参数方内部为伯努利值的0.2至14倍，基准对给出D=3.09±0.01，超额来自有界事件间隔的次序而非其分布；首次通过指数为1/2但振幅非伯努利，最坏情形Birkhoff平均按唯一遍历系统方式衰减。β=0时Green-Kubo和相互抵消、D=0，但Var(S_n)仍缓慢增长，符号变化稀少、Fourier模相关按n^{-0.2}衰减；零和词（zero-sum words）在β=0时成为纯扭转（pure twist），作为共振以可计算的1/ℓ律捕获轨道，小β下交叉时间为β^{-1/2}，非对称变形下方差出现系数可精确计算的对数项。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了零李雅普诺夫指数系统中扩散系数与伯努利行为的偏离源于事件间隔次序与共振结构，而非分布本身；作者据此提出关于唯一遍历性（unique ergodicity）、混合性（mixing）、中心极限定理（central limit theorem）与符号上同调（cohomology）的若干猜想。

Abstract: The triangle map is an area-preserving, piecewise-parabolic map of the torus with vanishing Lyapunov exponent. We study the symbolic process $X_n=\pm1$ given by the two halves of the torus, whose partial sums $S_n$ are the transport of the lifted momentum, with an integrator free of accumulated rounding errors and exact computations on polygonal cylinder sets. We prove that the Koopman operator has no non-constant $H^1$ eigenfunctions, that the symbol is not an $H^1$ coboundary, and that runs of equal symbols are uniformly bounded. For generic parameters the walk is normally diffusive and Gaussian, but its diffusion coefficient is not that of a coin: away from the edges of the parameter square it varies between $0.2$ and $14$ times the Bernoulli value, and for a benchmark pair, with $D=3.09\pm0.01$, the excess is produced by the order of the bounded inter-event intervals, not by their distribution. The first-passage exponent is $1/2$ with a non-Bernoulli amplitude, and worst-case Birkhoff averages decay as for a uniquely ergodic system. For $β=0$ the Green-Kubo sum cancels, $D=0$, yet $\mathrm{Var}(S_n)$ keeps growing slowly, sign changes are rare and the correlations of Fourier modes decay as $n^{-0.2}$. We identify the zero-sum words, which become pure twists at $β=0$, as resonances that trap orbits with a computable $1/\ell$ law, set the crossover time $β^{-1/2}$ at small $β$, and, in an asymmetric deformation of the symbol, produce a logarithmic term in the variance whose coefficient we compute exactly. We formulate conjectures on unique ergodicity, mixing, the central limit theorem and the cohomology of the symbol.

</details>


### [128] [Semantic burstiness across principal directions of textual variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34188)
*Mirko Degli Esposti,Marcelo A. Montemurro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将词汇层面的爆发式重现（bursty recurrence）推广到分布式语义观测量，通过词嵌入轨迹与PCA投影构建“爆发谱（burstiness spectrum）”，并证明原始文本在各语义分量上的爆发性显著超出两类保留全局长程相关性的对照序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 书写文本的长程相关（long-range correlations）远超局部句法尺度，但其与语义组织的关系尚不清楚；既有工作仅将相关性与话题相关词的爆发式重现联系起来，尚未推广到分布式语义层面。

Method: 逐词将文本映射为预训练词嵌入（word embeddings）轨迹，用主成分分析（PCA）提取文本特异的语义变化方向，对投影序列取阈值定义事件间隔时间，以变异系数构成分量分辨的爆发谱 {B_k}；在28篇文学与科学文本的前20个分量上，与几何基线及两类对照比较：保留嵌入协方差、PCA特征系统与边际分布但打乱时序的词打乱零模型（word-shuffled null），以及保留Zipf频次结构并匹配DFA指数的分数高斯噪声（fractional-Gaussian-noise）替代序列。

Result: 以各投影上尾5%定义事件时，原始文本在每个“文本-分量”组合上均超过两类对照，而两类对照彼此在统计上不可区分；语义投影的自相关（autocorrelation）与去趋势波动分析（DFA）同样将原始文本与两类对照区分开。

Conclusion: 全局秩级（rank-level）长程相关不足以生成沿语义方向观察到的爆发式重现；结果支持以语义相关内容的有序重现（ordered recurrence）来解释该现象的解读。

Abstract: Long-range correlations in written texts extend over scales far beyond local syntax, but their relation to semantic organisation remains incompletely understood. Previous work has linked these correlations to the bursty recurrence of topically relevant words. Here we generalise that construction from individual lexical items to distributed semantic observables. We map a text token by token onto a trajectory of pre-trained word embeddings, use principal component analysis (PCA) to identify text-specific directions of semantic variation, and threshold the projected series to define inter-event times; their coefficients of variation form a component-resolved burstiness spectrum $\{B_k\}$. Across 28 literary and scientific texts and the first 20 components, the originals remain systematically above the geometric inter-event-time baseline and above two complementary controls: a word-shuffled null, which preserves the embedding covariance, the PCA eigensystem and the marginal projected distributions while destroying temporal order, and a fractional-Gaussian-noise surrogate, which preserves the Zipfian frequency structure and matches the detrended fluctuation analysis (DFA) exponent of the original rank sequence. With events defined by the upper 5% tail of each projection, the original exceeds both controls in every text-component pair, while the two controls are statistically indistinguishable from each other. Autocorrelation and detrended fluctuation analysis of the semantic projections separate the originals from both controls in the same way. Global rank-level long-range correlations are therefore insufficient to generate the bursty recurrence observed along semantic directions. The results instead support an interpretation in terms of the ordered recurrence of semantically related material.

</details>


### [129] [Boundary-condition independence of infinite-chain ground states in the $S=1$ $J$-$D$ antiferromagnetic chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34330)
*Hal Tasaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究小各向异性参数 $|D|$ 下无穷链 $S=1$ $J$-$D$ 反铁磁模型（antiferromagnetic model）的基态（ground state），证明周期偶链与开边界奇链两类有限体积构造均沿全序列收敛且极限态一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借助 OpenAI 关于 Haldane gap 证明中的解析工具与数值输入，为伴行论文（companion paper）中拓扑相变（topological phase transition）的研究提供严格的基态极限基础。

Method: 考虑两类有限体积设置：偶数格点的周期链，以及奇数格点、两端施加相同正 z 向磁场的开链；利用归纳估计（inductive estimates）证明基态存在无穷链极限（infinite-chain limit），无需取子列。

Result: 两类设置的基态均沿完整序列收敛，且两个极限态重合；收敛在零附近的固定邻域内对 $D$ 一致。共同极限是对称的、局部唯一（locally unique）的带隙基态，其局部期望值对 $D$ 连续依赖。

Conclusion: 结论仅针对所指定的两种构造，而非所有无穷链基态之间的唯一性；该结果为伴行论文修订版中拓扑相变的研究提供输入。

Abstract: Extending the analytic machinery and using the numerical inputs from OpenAI's proofs of the Haldane gap, we study infinite-chain ground states of the $S=1$ $J$-$D$ antiferromagnetic model $H=\sum_j\{\boldsymbol S_j\cdot\boldsymbol S_{j+1}+D(S_j^{\mathrm z})^2\}$ for sufficiently small $|D|$. We consider two finite-volume settings: periodic chains with an even number of sites, and open chains with an odd number of sites and the same positive magnetic field in the $\mathrm z$ direction at both endpoints. We prove that the ground states in each setting have an infinite-chain limit along the full sequence, without passing to a subsequence, and that the two limiting states coincide. Convergence is uniform in $D$ on a fixed neighborhood of zero. The common limit is a symmetric locally-unique gapped ground state, and its local expectation values depend continuously on $D$. These conclusions concern the two specified constructions, not uniqueness among all infinite-chain ground states. They provide the input for the study of a topological phase transition in a forthcoming revision of our companion paper.
  The author formulated the problem and proposed the basic strategy. ChatGPT Astra made substantial contributions to the inductive estimates and to completing the proof.

</details>


### [130] [Rigorous Lower Bounds for Atomic and Ionic Ground-State Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34480)
*Michael Hofacker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的迭代方法（iterative method），用于计算所有原子与离子的基态能量（ground-state energy）的严格下界（rigorous lower bounds）。所得下界 E(N,Z) ≥ -0.8710 N^{1/3} Z^2 显著改进了此前最佳常数 (3/2)^{1/3} ≈ 1.1447。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有估计 E(N,Z) ≥ -A N^{1/3} Z^2 中的最佳已知常数 A=(3/2)^{1/3} ≈ 1.1447 距 Thomas–Fermi 渐近（Thomas-Fermi asymptotics）给出的最优常数约 0.7687 仍有明显差距，本文旨在收紧该严格下界。

Method: 建立一种迭代方法以计算所有原子与离子基态能量的严格下界；其精度取决于初始能量估计的质量，以及所采用的束缚电子最大数目 N_c(Z) 上界（作为核电荷 Z 的函数）的质量。

Result: 给出显式下界 E(N,Z) ≥ -0.8710 N^{1/3} Z^2（N ∈ ℕ，0 < Z ≤ N），将常数从 1.1447 改进至 0.8710，接近 Thomas–Fermi 渐近的 0.7687；并对 2 ≤ N ≤ 86 的所有原子计算了 E(N,N) 的显式下界，与 Hertel–Lieb–Thirring 及 Seco 的结果进行比较。

Conclusion: 该方法在改进常数与给出数值下界方面有效，论文同时讨论了其潜力与局限，并给出基态空间上动能、势能与相互作用能期望值（expectation values）的唯一性结果（uniqueness result）。

Abstract: We establish a new iterative method for computing rigorous lower bounds for the ground-state energies of all atoms and ions. The accuracy of the resulting bounds depends on two ingredients: the quality of the initial energy estimate and the quality of the used upper bound for the maximum number of bound electrons, $N_c(Z)$, as a function of the nuclear charge $Z>0$. In particular, this new method allows us to establish the explicit lower bound $E(N,Z) \geq -0.8710\,N^{1/3}Z^2$ for the ground-state energy $E(N,Z)$ of the atom/ion with $N \in \mathbb{N}$ electrons and nuclear charge $Z \in (0,N]$, thereby considerably improving the best known constant $A=(3/2)^{1/3} \approx 1.1447$ in the estimate $E(N,Z) \geq -A\,N^{1/3}Z^2$ for $0 < Z \leq N$ and coming much closer to the best possible constant given by the Thomas-Fermi asymptotics $\lim_{N \to \infty} |E(N,N)|N^{-7/3}= -E_{\text{TF}}(1)\approx 0.7687$ derived in Adv. Math., 23(1) by Lieb and Simon. To demonstrate the potential and the limits of our method, we also compute explicit lower bounds for the ground-state energies $E(N,N)$ of all atoms with $2 \leq N \leq 86$ and we compare them with the bounds obtained in J. Chem. Phys., 62(8) by Hertel, Lieb, Thirring and the bounds obtained by Seco in his dissertation. Finally, we also provide a uniqueness result for the expectation values of the kinetic, potential and interaction energies on the ground-state space.

</details>


### [131] [Gaussian $β$-Markov Path Spaces without a Spectral Gap and the Araki--Woods Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34783)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将 Dereziński 与 Gérard 构造的高斯 β-Markov 路径空间（Gaussian β-Markov path space）及其与 Araki–Woods 表示（Araki–Woods representation）的等价性，从具有谱隙（spectral gap）的单粒子哈密顿量（one-particle Hamiltonian）推广到无谱隙情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原构造仅适用于具有谱隙的单粒子哈密顿量，限制了其在正温度构造性量子场论（constructive quantum field theory）与平衡态统计力学（equilibrium statistical mechanics）等场景中的应用，故需拓展至无谱隙情形。

Method: 在自由玻色场（free Bose field）上推广高斯 β-Markov 路径空间的构造，并同步证明其与 Araki–Woods 表示的等价性，去除谱隙假设。

Result: 所构造的路径空间及其与 Araki–Woods 表示的等价性在单粒子哈密顿量无谱隙时依然成立。

Conclusion: 该推广面向正温度构造性量子场论与平衡态统计力学，例如用于处理粒子数不守恒的准粒子（quasiparticles）不存在玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose–Einstein condensation）的问题。

Abstract: Dereziński and Gérard constructed the Gaussian $β$-Markov path space of a free Bose field in a form equivalent to the Araki--Woods representation, which suits nonequilibrium statistical mechanics with a quantum-field environment, but only for one-particle Hamiltonians with a spectral gap. We extend this construction, together with the equivalence to the Araki--Woods representation, to one-particle Hamiltonians without a spectral gap. The extension is intended for constructive quantum field theory at positive temperature and equilibrium statistical mechanics, for instance in the problem of the absence of Bose--Einstein condensation of quasiparticles whose number is not conserved.

</details>


### [132] [On the types of quasiperiodic potentials and level surfaces of quasiperiodic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34989)
*A. Ya. Maltsev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较两类准周期函数（quasiperiodic functions）：一类来自将 $\mathbb{R}^N$ 中光滑周期函数限制到低维子空间 $\mathbb{R}^n\subset\mathbb{R}^N$，另一类来自准晶结构（quasicrystalline structures）中的物理场或势场；二者既有共同特征也存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两类准周期函数来源不同（几何限制构造 vs. 准晶物理场），但此前对二者异同缺乏系统比较；明确其共性与差异对理解准周期函数的数学结构与物理意义具有必要性。

Method: 以 Novikov 问题——即描述准周期函数水平集（level sets）的全局几何（global geometry）——作为研究两类函数的关键工具，进行对比分析。

Result: 两类函数共享若干共同特征，同时表现出显著差异；Novikov 问题被证明可作为同时研究这两类函数的有效工具。

Conclusion: 研究确认两类准周期函数既相互关联又本质有别，水平集全局几何问题是统一刻画二者的适用框架。

Abstract: We examine the similarities and differences between two types of quasiperiodic functions: those obtained by restricting smooth periodic functions in $\mathbb{R}^N$ to lower-dimensional subspaces $\mathbb{R}^n \subset \mathbb{R}^N$, and those that arise as physical fields or potentials in quasicrystalline structures. Our analysis reveals that functions in these two classes share a number of common features while also exhibiting significant differences. As a key tool for investigating both classes of functions, we employ the Novikov problem - specifically, the problem of describing the global geometry of the level sets of quasiperiodic functions.

</details>


### [133] [General Relativity for Mathematicians - Part I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35194)
*Filippo Fila-Robattino,Manuel Tecchiolli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 这是苏黎世大学2025年冬季学期同名课程第一部分的讲义，涵盖经典力学变分原理、狭义相对论、光滑流形与张量分析、联络与曲率、测地运动，以及由爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量导出爱因斯坦方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该讲义面向数学背景的读者，旨在将物理动机与精确定义、详细证明和显式计算相结合，为非物理专业读者搭建通往广义相对论（general relativity）数学基础的桥梁。

Method: 采用数学化处理方式：以变分原理（variational principles）为起点，经狭义相对论进入微分几何工具（光滑流形（smooth manifolds）、张量演算（tensor calculus）、联络与曲率（connections and curvature）），并通过测地运动（geodesic motion）与爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量（Einstein-Hilbert action）的变分导出爱因斯坦方程（Einstein's equations）。

Result: 讲义系统呈现了从经典力学到广义相对论核心方程的理论链条，强调精确定义、详细证明与显式计算，未在摘要中报告具体数值或实验结论。

Conclusion: 该材料是一份面向数学读者的广义相对论数学基础课程讲义，内容覆盖必要的几何与力学预备知识，并以爱因斯坦方程的变分推导作为收束。

Abstract: These notes cover the first part of a course of the same title taught by Filippo Fila-Robattino and Manuel Tecchiolli at the University of Zurich in the Winter Semester of 2025. Topics include variational principles in classical mechanics, special relativity, smooth manifolds and tensor calculus, connections and curvature, geodesic motion, and the derivation of Einstein's equations from the Einstein-Hilbert action. The treatment is aimed at a mathematical audience, combining physical motivation with precise definitions, detailed proofs, and explicit calculations.

</details>


### [134] [Measurement-dependent reduction of open dynamics in the quantum phase estimation algorithm: the visible subspace and the damped Fejér kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35332)
*Marina O. Gordeichuk,Oleg M. Kiselev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究开放动力学下量子相位估计（quantum phase estimation, QPE）计数寄存器输出的最小可观测空间：在全傅里叶测量（Fourier measurement）下显式给出该空间结构，并证明其维数介于 M 与 M²−M+1 之间，同时给出近似约化的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QPE 在开放动力学（open dynamics）下输出分布由计数寄存器的状态矩阵决定，需确定预测全部输出概率所需的最小可观测子空间，以降低描述复杂度并刻画噪声信道的可区分性。

Method: 基于约化（reduction）的一般 Krylov 理论，对全傅里叶测量显式计算可观测空间结构；输出概率由状态矩阵的循环和（cyclic sums）决定。当伴随生成元（adjoint generator）在矩阵单元上对角时，按索引差模寄存器维数 M 的剩余类（residue classes）内的谱类（spectral classes）构成构造性基，并给出精确维数公式与可达界；近似约化采用有限时间 Gramian 与后验残差估计（a posteriori residual estimate）。

Result: 可见空间（visible space）维数介于 M 与 M²−M+1 之间；该类包含独立量子比特退相位（qubit dephasing）；一个解析可解的 GKLS 型参考模型给出阻尼 Fejér 核（damped Fejér kernel）；两个具有相同 Ramsey 对比度的随机酉信道（random-unitary channels）表明 QPE 分布可区分高次谐波（higher harmonics）。近似约化在约化无扰子空间与相容初态下具有二次误差阶（quadratic error order）。

Conclusion: 数值计算验证了各表示的一致性及非 Toeplitz 扰动（non-Toeplitz perturbations）下误差的行为；结果为开放动力学下 QPE 输出的最小可观测描述提供了显式维数刻画与可计算的近似误差界。

Abstract: For the quantum phase estimation (QPE) algorithm we study the minimal space of observables that is sufficient to predict the entire output distribution under open dynamics of the counting register. Building on the general Krylov theory of reduction, we compute the structure of this space explicitly for the full Fourier measurement. The outcome probabilities are determined by cyclic sums of the state matrix. If the adjoint generator is diagonal on the matrix units, the spectral classes inside the residue classes of the index difference modulo the register dimension $M$ give a constructive basis, an exact dimension formula, and attainable bounds: the dimension of the visible space lies between $M$ and $M^2-M+1$. The class includes independent qubit dephasing, and an analytically solvable reference model of Gorini--Kossakowski--Lindblad--Sudarshan (GKLS) type yields a damped Fejér kernel; two random-unitary channels with the same Ramsey contrast show that higher harmonics are distinguishable in the QPE distribution. For approximate reduction we use a finite-time Gramian and an a posteriori residual estimate, with a quadratic error order for a reducing unperturbed subspace and a compatible initial state. Numerical calculations check the consistency of the representations and the behaviour of the error under non-Toeplitz perturbations.

</details>


### [135] [Existence of a weak solution to the Navier-Stokes equations enjoying the energy equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35517)
*Paolo Maremonti,Alfonsina Tartaglione*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明 Navier-Stokes 方程存在满足能量等式（energy equality）的弱解（weak solution），其构造沿用 Leray 的磨光（mollified）逼近方法；但由于弱解的唯一性（uniqueness）未知，无法断言所有 Leray-Hopf 弱解均满足能量等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果中，Leray 弱解仅满足能量不等式（energy inequality），而能量等式的成立与否是刻画解正则性与耗散机制的关键问题，故需研究满足能量等式的弱解是否存在。

Method: 采用 Leray 方法（Leray's approach），考虑磨光的 Navier-Stokes 系统（mollified Navier-Stokes system），以其解的极限构造原方程的弱解，并验证该极限满足能量等式。

Result: 证明存在一个弱解满足能量等式，即能量等式在弱解类中至少对某个解成立，而非仅满足能量不等式。

Conclusion: 由于弱解的唯一性（uniqueness）尚不已知，不能推出任意 Leray-Hopf 弱解均满足能量等式；所得结论限于构造出的特定弱解。

Abstract: To date, a Leray's weak solution to the Navier-Stokes equations enjoys an energy inequality. We prove the existence of a weak solution that enjoys the energy equality. The existence of the weak solution is achieved as limit of solutions to the mollified Navier-Stokes system (Leray's approach). Since no uniqueness result is known for weak solutions we are not able to claim that any weak solution in the sense of Leray-Hopf enjoys the energy equality.

</details>


### [136] [Finite-step projective dynamics of Schrödinger cocycles: non-degeneracy, regularity, and rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35583)
*Mira Shamis*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究 Schrödinger 余循环（Schrödinger cocycles）的无确定性像条件（no deterministic images, NDI），在混合系统与无限本质值域遍历势下证明各能量点的两步 NDI，并对三角势（含 almost Mathieu 算子）建立所有步数、所有频率的 NDI 与 Hölder 界，进而刻画 Oseledets 方向分布的非原子性与零 Lyapunov 指数的新判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NDI 条件排除固定概率测度在几乎全部射影转移变换下存在公共像，是研究解方向分布与 Lyapunov 指数的关键工具，但已有结果多局限于特定势类与有限步。本文旨在把 NDI 及其定量（Hölder）版本推广到更一般的势类与一般状态空间，并借此给出 Lyapunov 指数为零的可检验判据。

Method: 对非共常可测势、混合系统及具无限本质值域的遍历势证明各能量点的两步 NDI，并对有限状态局部常值势给出持久性分类。对一般状态空间上的有界 Markov 与隐 Markov 势，在一阶 L² 范数间隙条件下证明指数截断之上解方向分布的 Hölder 界与 Hölder 条件平稳测度。对实三角势（含 almost Mathieu 算子）证明所有 k≥2 步与所有频率的 NDI 及 Hölder 界，并构造反例检验假设的尖锐性。

Result: 两步 NDI 对上述一般势类成立；有界 Markov/隐 Markov 势在 L² 间隙条件下获得 Hölder 界与 Hölder 条件平稳测度，反例表明两个假设均本质尖锐。对三角势，Hölder 指数依步数而定，常数在紧区间上关于步数、频率与能量一致；对每个无理频率与正 Lyapunov 指数的能量，Oseledets 方向分布非原子，且任一与相位无关的初始概率均收敛到不稳定分布。结果可推广到解析类，具依步数的正则性界。

Conclusion: NDI 与 Hölder 型定量估计可在相当一般的势类中建立，并在三角势情形给出统一步数的结论。非原子性与收敛性提供了零 Lyapunov 指数的新判据：对某一固定初始方向，若某子列极限含原子即可判定。整体结果构成刻画解方向统计与 Lyapunov 指数的统一框架。

Abstract: We study the no deterministic images condition (NDI) for Schrödinger cocycles, which excludes a common image of a fixed probability measure under almost all projective transfer transformations. We prove two-step NDI at every energy for nonconstant measurable potentials over mixing systems and ergodic potentials with infinite essential range, and classify persistence for finite-state locally constant potentials. For bounded Markov and hidden Markov potentials on general state spaces under a one-step $L^2$ norm gap condition on the Markov operator, we prove Hölder bounds for solution-direction distributions above an exponential cutoff and Hölder conditional stationary measures. Counterexamples show the essential sharpness of both assumptions.
  For nonconstant real trigonometric potentials, including the almost Mathieu operator, we prove NDI and Hölder bounds for distributions of solution directions at all steps $k\ge2$ and all frequencies (rational or irrational). The exponent depends on the step, while the constant is uniform in steps, frequency and energy on compact intervals. For every irrational frequency and every energy with positive Lyapunov exponent, we prove non-atomicity of both Oseledets direction distributions and convergence to the unstable distribution from every phase-independent initial probability. This gives a new criterion for a zero Lyapunov exponent: a subsequential limit with an atom for one fixed initial direction suffices. All these results extend to an analytic class, with step-dependent regularity bounds.

</details>


### [137] [Curvature-induced fracture mode coupling in doubly curved shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35597)
*Riccardo Brambilla,Siddhant Kumar,Bianca Giovanardi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于Reissner浅壳理论建立半解析框架，研究任意倾斜贯穿裂纹在正、零、负高斯曲率各向同性壳体中的断裂行为，揭示曲率导致张开、滑移与撕裂模式的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典壳体断裂理论多局限于主曲率方向裂纹及拉伸或压力载荷，对双曲壳中任意取向裂纹在一般载荷下的断裂行为认识不足，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用基于Reissner浅壳理论（Reissner shallow-shell theory）的半解析框架（semi-analytical framework），计算球、椭球、圆柱、双曲面及极小曲面中任意倾斜贯穿厚度裂纹的应力强度因子（stress intensity factors）、能量释放率（energy release rates）与裂纹唇位移（crack-lip displacements）。

Result: 壳体曲率使张开、滑移与撕裂模式相互耦合，单一载荷分量即可激活多种断裂模式，且耦合强度依赖裂纹取向及曲率的法向、平行与扭转分量；椭球耦合最弱，双曲面通常最强，圆柱呈现最复杂的取向依赖响应；面内剪切可在双曲壳中诱发裂纹闭合。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了局部曲率在断裂模式耦合中的作用，为具有工程化曲率分布的壳格（shellular）超结构分析提供力学基础。

Abstract: Classical shell fracture theories predominantly consider cracks aligned with principal curvature directions in cylindrical and spherical shells under tensile mode or pressure loading, leaving the fracture behavior of arbitrarily oriented cracks in doubly curved shells under general loading poorly understood. Here, we introduce a semi-analytical framework based on Reissner shallow-shell theory to investigate isotropic shells with positive, zero, and negative Gaussian curvature. The formulation computes stress intensity factors, energy release rates, and crack-lip displacements for arbitrarily inclined through-thickness cracks in spheres, ellipsoids, cylinders, hyperboloids, and minimal surfaces under general mechanical loading. We show that shell curvature couples opening, sliding, and tearing, allowing a single loading component to activate multiple fracture modes, unlike in flat plates. This coupling depends strongly on crack orientation and the normal, parallel, and torsional components of the shell curvature. Ellipsoids exhibit weak coupling, hyperboloids generally exhibit the strongest coupling, and cylinders display the most complex orientation-dependent responses. Moreover, in-plane shear can induce crack closure in hyperboloidal shells, suggesting a potential geometric mechanism for enhanced damage resistance. These findings elucidate the role of local curvature in fracture-mode coupling and provide a mechanistic foundation for the analysis of shellular (meta-)structures with engineered curvature distributions.

</details>


### [138] [Simplifying 2-Bundle Descriptions of Invariants and Local Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35669)
*Ferreira Bastien*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 二维规范势（two-form gauge potential）的局部描述无需拼成单一全局形式，主2-丛（principal 2-bundle）可刻画这种黏合；但表面振幅、量子配分函数、局部经典场动力学乃至全局相位与扭曲层模空间，均可在不借助2-丛形式的情况下通过余圈（cocycle）、丛、算子或模获得等价描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦与两形式规范势耦合时，其局部描述可能不具备统一的全局形式，主2-丛虽能描述这种黏合，但该形式化框架是否必要尚待澄清；论文旨在检验能否绕开2-丛表述得到相同物理与几何结论。

Method: 对三维有限规范理论（finite gauge theories）的余同调作用量（cohomological actions）作傅里叶变换（Fourier transformation），将配分函数表示为普通有限群规范理论配分函数之和；并通过可逆局部替换（invertible local substitution），把由有限维交叉模（crossed module）表示的2-丛联络的经典不变量作用量约化为普通规范与张量动力学。

Result: 表面振幅与量子配分函数、局部经典场动力学均可复现；全局相位、拓扑与指数不变量（index invariants）及扭曲层模空间（twisted-sheaf moduli）可由余圈、丛、算子或模的既有等价性给出。有限与局部约化显式消去场变量；四维二次型（quadratic）例子即使普通规范理论约化失效，仍可直接做余链（cochain）求值。

Conclusion: 上述结果说明物理与几何结论不依赖于2-丛的额外表述，主2-丛形式并非必需；在特定有限、局部情形下可显式消去场变量，而在约化失效的四维情形下余链方法仍提供直接计算途径。

Abstract: A string couples to a two-form gauge potential whose local descriptions need not fit together as a single global form. Principal 2-bundles provide a geometric description of this gluing. However, we recover surface amplitudes and quantum partition functions, as well as local classical field dynamics, without the 2-bundle formalism.
