For global phases, topological and index invariants, and twisted-sheaf moduli, established equivalences give alternative descriptions through cocycles, bundles, operators, or modules, with the required global compatibility data. For three-dimensional finite gauge theories with cohomological actions, Fourier transformation expresses the partition function as a sum of partition functions of ordinary finite-group gauge theories. An invertible local substitution reduces invariant classical actions for a class of 2-bundle connections represented by finite-dimensional crossed modules to ordinary gauge and tensor dynamics. These finite and local reductions explicitly eliminate field variables. A four-dimensional quadratic example instead admits a direct cochain evaluation even when an ordinary gauge-theory reduction fails. These results therefore do not require the additional 2-bundle presentation.

</details>


### [139] [Entropic hypocoercivity via the Polchinski flow: an application to the lattice sine-Gordon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35759)
*Bowen Li,Jianfeng Lu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为格点场论的典型随机量子化（canonical stochastic quantization）中欠阻尼朗之万动力学（underdamped Langevin dynamics）建立相对熵（relative entropy）指数衰减的多尺度判据（multiscale criterion）。判据等价于沿 Polchinski 流的平滑相互作用 Hessian 在尺度变量上的 L² 范数的一致算子范数界很小，并应用于二维大质量 sine–Gordon 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机是为格点场论中欠阻尼朗之万动力学的相对熵指数衰减提供一个可验证的判据，从而刻画动力学趋近平衡的收敛性与速率，并反映其对格距（lattice spacing）与体积的均匀性。

Method: 针对高斯场（Gaussian fields）的光滑有界扰动，构造多尺度判据：要求沿 Bauerschmidt–Bodineau 的 Polchinski 流平滑后的相互作用 Hessian 的算子范数在尺度变量上具有小的 L² 范数。证明中在分解熵的同一尺度上度量动量耗散向位置的转移，并用相空间（phase-space）版 Polchinski 流给出与自由动能演化可交换的高斯粗粒化；位置—动量交叉项积分得到由熵控制的修正项，力缺陷（force defect）刻画平均相互作用力偏离平衡的变化并控制动能估计误差。

Result: 对二维大质量格点 sine–Gordon 模型，当 0<β<4π 且无量纲局域耦合较小时，判据成立，从而给出对所有有限熵初始分布（finite-entropy initial laws）的熵衰减，且关于格距与体积一致。衰减常数仅依赖该 L² 范数与摩擦系数；当摩擦正比于质量 m 时，速率达到最优阶 m。

Conclusion: 该多尺度判据将相对熵指数衰减归结为沿 Polchinski 流对相互作用 Hessian 的一致 L² 尺度范数控制，为格点场论欠阻尼朗之万动力学提供统一分析框架，并在二维大质量 sine–Gordon 模型中给出关于格距与体积一致、且随质量达到最优阶的指数收敛速率。

Abstract: We develop a multiscale criterion for exponential decay of relative entropy in the canonical stochastic quantization of lattice field theories, the underdamped Langevin dynamics of the field and its momentum. For smooth bounded perturbations of Gaussian fields, the criterion requires the uniform operator-norm bound on the smoothed interaction Hessian along the Polchinski flow of Bauerschmidt and Bodineau to have a small $L^2$ norm in the scale variable. The decay constants depend only on this $L^2$ norm and the friction. The proof measures the transfer of momentum dissipation to position on the same scales that decompose entropy. A phase-space extension of the Polchinski flow gives Gaussian coarse-graining that commutes with free kinetic evolution. Integrating position--momentum cross terms over these scales yields a corrector bounded by entropy. A force defect accounts for changes in the averaged interaction force away from equilibrium; its evolution in scale controls the errors in the kinetic estimate. For the massive two-dimensional lattice sine--Gordon model with $0<β<4π$ and small dimensionless local coupling, the criterion gives entropy decay for all finite-entropy initial laws, uniformly in the lattice spacing and the volume. For friction proportional to the mass $m$, the rate has optimal order $m$.

</details>


### [140] [Hidden Singularity and Fractal Dimensions for Extended Harper Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35822)
*Simon Becker,Svetlana Jitomirskaya,Igor Krasovsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 对任意无理频率，扩展 Harper 族在零测度区域内谱的 1/2-维 Hausdorff 测度有限且下盒维数不超过 1/2；在各项同性临界边界上改进为 1/3，并给出盒计数维数不存在的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究扩展 Harper 族（extended Harper family）在零测度区域（zero-measure regime）中谱的精细分形结构，特别是其 Hausdorff 测度与盒维数的定量上界，并将临界几乎 Mathieu（critical almost Mathieu）情形已有的手征变换方法推广到更一般的族。

Method: 构造一族酉变换（unitary transformations），其在临界几乎 Mathieu 情形退化为 [BJK] 的手征变换（chiral transform）；借助该变换使该区域中每个算子谱等价于奇异 Jacobi 算子（singular Jacobi operator），再对奇异代表元套用 [BJK] 方法，用 O(q_n) 个区间、总长 O(q_n^{-1}) 的覆盖（q_n 为 α 的规范逼近分母）。

Result: 证明对每个无理频率，零测度区域内谱的 1/2-维 Hausdorff 测度有限、下盒维数（lower box dimension）至多 1/2；在各项同性临界边界（isotropic critical boundary）上两个数值均改进为 1/3，覆盖总长也由 O(q_n^{-1}) 改进为 O(q_n^{-2})。

Conclusion: 所构造的覆盖给出盒维数上界；结合填充估计（packing estimates）进一步得到盒计数维数（box-counting dimension）不存在（即上、下盒维数不相等）的具体例子，说明该族谱的分形维数行为比单一维数刻画更复杂。

Abstract: We prove that, for every irrational frequency, the spectra of the extended Harper family throughout the zero-measure regime have finite $1/2$-dimensional Hausdorff measure and lower box dimension at most $1/2$. Both numbers improve to $1/3$ on the isotropic critical boundary. The proof is based on a family of unitary transformations, that specializes to the chiral transform of [BJK] for the case of the critical almost Mathieu. This family allows to make every operator in this regime spectrally equivalent to a singular Jacobi operator. Using these singular representatives, we apply the method of [BJK] to construct covers by $O(q_n)$ intervals of total length $O(q_n^{-1})$, improving to $O(q_n^{-2})$ on the isotropic boundary, where $q_n$ is the denominator of a canonical approximant to $α$. The covers give upper box bounds and, with packing estimates, examples where the box-counting dimension fails to exist.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum dynamics on the sparse Erdős-Rényi graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35867)
*Johannes Alt,Raphael Ducatez,Antti Knowles*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Erdős-Rényi图（Erdős-Rényi graph）上自由量子粒子薛定谔时间演化（Schrödinger time evolution）的大时间渐近行为，精确刻画了从单点局域态出发后近似径向波函数的渐近形式，并给出局域模式（localized mode）与色散波（dispersive wave）共存的动力学对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作从谱角度确立了Erdős-Rényi图上局域态与离域态（localized and delocalized states）的共存，但缺乏从动力学视角的理解。本文旨在提供这一共存的动力学对应物，特别关注度接近2d的顶点上束缚态（bound states）的涌现。

Method: 以图$\mathbb{G}(N,d/N)$的邻接矩阵（adjacency matrix）作为量子哈密顿量，从定域于某顶点的初态出发，利用波函数近似径向的性质，精确刻画其大时间渐近形式；分析在$\sqrt{\log N} \ll d \ll N^{o(1)}$的参数区域及$1 \ll t \ll T_N$的时间尺度上成立。

Result: 给出了波函数渐近形式的精确刻画，覆盖度接近$2d$的顶点，显示局域部分形成局域模式、其余部分形成色散波，从而在动力学层面重现局域与离域态的共存。结果覆盖$\sqrt{\log N} \ll d \ll N^{o(1)}$这一此类动力学现象可能存在的完整区域，并适用于某个显式$T_N \gg 1$之前的长时间尺度。

Conclusion: 研究为Erdős-Rényi图上局域/离域态共存提供了动力学对应解释，并表明所得结果在相应现象可能出现的完整参数区域内有效。

Abstract: We analyse the large-time asymptotics of the Schrödinger time evolution of a free quantum particle on the Erdős-Rényi graph $\mathbb{G}(N,d/N)$, where the quantum Hamiltonian is given by the adjacency matrix of $\mathbb{G}$. Starting from a state localized at a vertex, the wave function is approximately radial, and we characterize precisely its asymptotic form. Our results in particular cover vertices whose degree is close to $2d$, hence probing the emergence of bound states from a dynamical perspective. This provides a dynamical counterpart of the coexistence of localized and delocalized states established in [arXiv:1905.03243,arXiv:2005.14180,arXiv:2106.12519,arXiv:2109.03227,arXiv:2305.16294], with the former giving rise to a localized mode and the latter to a dispersive wave. Our results hold in the regime $\sqrt{\log N} \ll d \ll N^{o(1)}$, which is the full region in which such dynamical phenomena may exist, and for times $1 \ll t \ll T_N$ for some explicit $T_N \gg 1$.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Path space large deviation on a lattice for interacting particle systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33524)
*Cristian Giardinà,Tomohiro Sasamoto*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究有限离散环面上相互作用粒子系统（interacting particle systems）的路径空间大偏差原理（large deviation principle, LDP），以最大占据数为 s 的部分排斥过程（partial exclusion process）为核心示例，给出速度为 s 的变分形式速率函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对密度依赖型马尔可夫跳过程（density-dependent Markov jump processes），需刻画路径空间层面的稀有事件概率，并将典型的大数定律（law of large numbers）确定性流推广至涨落分析。

Method: 先验证原始过程与倾斜过程（tilted processes）的 Kurtz 型大数定律以确定确定性流；再借助鞅技术（martingale techniques）、指数紧性（exponential tightness）与基于指数倾斜（exponential tilting）的测度变换（change-of-measure）证明路径空间 LDP，并将速率函数刻画为非线性哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的勒让德变换（Legendre transform）。

Result: 获得速度为 s 的路径空间 LDP 及变分形式的速率函数；该策略进一步适用于包含过程（inclusion process，保守非紧有限跳情形）与调和过程（harmonic process，限制性哈密顿量呈对数形式且定义域非平凡），后者超出常规有限反应框架。

Conclusion: 同一哈密顿量大偏差（Hamiltonian large-deviation）形式亦延伸至连续态能量交换模型（energy-exchange models），如布朗能量过程（Brownian energy process）与一类可积热传导模型。

Abstract: We investigate the path space large deviation principle (LDP) for a class of interacting particle systems on a fixed finite discrete torus. As an illustrative example, we use the partial exclusion process with maximal occupation of $s$ particles. We first verify the standard Kurtz-type law of large numbers for the original and tilted processes, thereby identifying the deterministic flows around which the large deviations are formulated. We then establish the path space LDP, with speed $s$, and identify the associated rate function in variational form as the Legendre transform of a nonlinear Hamiltonian. The proof relies on martingale techniques, exponential tightness, and a change-of-measure argument based on exponential tilting, which renders atypical trajectories typical under a modified dynamics.
  We also explain how the same strategy extends to further interacting particle systems. The inclusion process illustrates the conservative non-compact finite-jump case. The harmonic process is qualitatively different: a single transition may move a variable number of particles, and the limiting Hamiltonian is logarithmic with a non-trivial domain. This places the harmonic process outside the usual finite-reaction framework for density-dependent Markov jump processes. Finally, we discuss continuous-state energy-exchange models, such as the Brownian energy process and an integrable heat-conduction model, to indicate how the same Hamiltonian large-deviation formalism appears beyond discrete-state particle systems.

</details>


### [143] [Stationary Measures and Boundary Correlations for Multispecies Half-Line ASEP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34530)
*Alexey Bufetov,Yuan Tian*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文借助顶点模型（vertex models）构造正半轴上多种类非对称简单排斥过程（multispecies ASEP）的平稳测度（stationary measures），其中左边界注入最高优先级粒子。结果将全直线情形的既有构造推广至半直线，并对两类情形计算前两个格点的联合占据分布，同时部分讨论与半空间 ASEP 速度过程（speed process）的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Martin；Aggarwal–Nicoletti–Petrov）仅在全直线（full-line）上构造了多物种 ASEP 的平稳测度，半直线（half-line）情形及边界注入最高优先级粒子的设定仍属空白，故有必要将其推广。

Method: 采用顶点模型（vertex models）作为主要工具，在正半轴上设定左边界注入最高优先级粒子（highest-priority particles），据此构造多种类 ASEP 的平稳测度。

Result: 成功构造出半直线多种类 ASEP 的平稳测度，推广了全直线情形；对两物种（two-species）过程给出前两个格点占据颜色（occupation colors）的联合分布。

Conclusion: 半直线推广成立且可显式计算局部联合分布；与半空间 ASEP 速度过程（half-space ASEP speed process）的联系得到部分解决，相关问题仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We construct stationary measures of the multispecies ASEP on the positive half-line, with injection of highest-priority particles at the left boundary, using vertex models. Our results extend existing constructions of Martin and Aggarwal-Nicoletti-Petrov for the full-line ASEP to the half-line. For the two-species process, we compute the joint distribution of occupation colors at the first two sites. We also partially address the connection with the half-space ASEP speed process.

</details>


### [144] [Uniform Large Deviations, Barrier Identity and the Néel--Arrhenius Law for the One-Dimensional Stochastic Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34823)
*Zdzisław Brzeźniak,Fan Gu,Liang Li*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional stochastic Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation with a regularised, multiplicative, degenerate trace-class noise of strength sqrt(eps), the regularisation being Q_delta^{1/2} = (I + delta A^{beta/2})^{-1}, A = -d^2/dx^2, beta > 3/2, and determine the exponential rate of thermally induced magnetisation reversal.
  (i) Uniform large deviations at fixed delta: for every delta > 0 the solution satisfies a large deviation principle on C([0,T];H), with a good rate function, uniformly in the initial datum over a V-bounded set with compact H-closure; no non-degeneracy of the noise is assumed.
  (ii) Removal of the regularisation: as delta tends to 0 the noise tends to space-time white noise. With U_delta the state-space quasi-potential and U_0 that of the limiting control problem (Q_0^{1/2} = I), U_delta converges to U_0 in the Gamma-sense, and the barrier is delta-independent: the infimum of U_delta over the boundary of D equals that of U_0 and equals c_* times the infimum of the micromagnetic energy E over that boundary, c_* = 2 lambda_2/(lambda_1^2 + lambda_2^2), under two structural assumptions only and with no geometric or transversality input. Pointwise convergence fails, so the Gamma-limit is the sharp form of the statement.
  (iii) Neel-Arrhenius law: combining (i) and (ii) with the classical exit-time theorem, the escape time tau from D = {E < r} satisfies both levels of the law - for every fixed delta > 0, eps log E[tau] converges, uniformly on compact subsets of the initial set, to the barrier, and lim_{delta->0} lim_{eps->0} eps log E[tau] = c_* times the infimum of E over the boundary of D. The Arrhenius exponent is therefore stable under the regularisation of the noise and is determined by the micromagnetic energy alone.

</details>


### [145] [Log-Sobolev inequality for 2D $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ lattice gauge theory at all couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35286)
*Jack Piazza,Hao Shen*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对二维 $SU(N)$ Wilson 格点杨–米尔斯测度（Wilson lattice Yang–Mills measure），在任意固定逆耦合（inverse coupling）$β$ 下对数 Sobolev 不等式（log-Sobolev inequality）成立，且其常数对格点体积一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅给出所处理的对象与结论方向，即建立二维格点规范测度的对数 Sobolev 不等式及体积一致常数。

Method: 证明采用双尺度分解论证（two-scale disintegration argument），并对测度取二维 $SU(N)$ Wilson 格点杨–米尔斯测度、逆耦合 $β$ 任意固定。

Result: 对数 Sobolev 不等式成立；其对数 Sobolev 常数依赖于 $β$ 与 $N$，但在底层格点体积上保持一致（uniform in the volume）。

Conclusion: 二维 $SU(N)$ Wilson 格点杨–米尔斯测度满足具有体积一致常数的对数 Sobolev 不等式，双尺度分解是其证明的关键工具。

Abstract: We prove that the log-Sobolev inequality holds for the two-dimensional $SU(N)$ Wilson lattice Yang--Mills measure with arbitrary fixed inverse coupling $β$. The log-Sobolev constant depends on $β$ and $N$, but is uniform in the volume of the underlying lattice. The proof relies on a two-scale disintegration argument.

</details>


### [146] [Self-intersection local times of Volterra Gaussian bridges with large end-to-end distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35769)
*Olga Izyumtseva,Wasiur R. KhudaBukhsh*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究Volterra高斯桥（Volterra Gaussian bridges）在终点趋于无穷时的多重自交局部时（multiple self-intersection local times），刻画其存在性与各阶矩的精确衰减阶数，并在平面上对小时间窗截断的双重自交局部时给出相变（phase transition）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自交局部时（self-intersection local times）是连续聚合物模型（continuous polymer models）Gibbs测度中的Hamiltonian，而端点距离（end-to-end distance）是关键量，因此需要刻画桥过程在终点发散时自交局部时的行为。

Method: 针对d维(2,ζ)-局部非确定性（locally nondeterministic）过程，ζ∈(0,2/d)，先证明局部时在所有L^p中存在；在核的两项附加条件下计算m重自交局部时（m-multiple self-intersection local time）的p阶矩；平面上对k(0)≠0的核在强局部非确定性（strong local nondeterminism）下将双重自交局部时限制于{t_2−t_1⩾‖a‖^(−α)}。

Result: 当ζ∈(0,2/d)时，局部时在任意L^p中存在；在附加条件下，桥的m重自交局部时的p阶矩精确阶数为‖a‖^(−γ)，其中γ=p(m−1)(2−ζd)/(2−ζ)，p为任意正整数。平面上双重自交局部时的期望在α<2、α=2、α>2时分别呈exp(−c‖a‖^(2−α))衰减、收敛到显式正极限、对数增长。

Conclusion: Volterra高斯桥的多重自交局部时具有可精确刻画的矩衰减阶数，且平面情形下截断双重自交局部时的期望随参数α出现由指数衰减经有限极限到对数增长的相变。

Abstract: Self-intersection local times serve as the Hamiltonian in the Gibbs measure of continuous polymer models, for which the end-to-end distance is a key quantity. We study multiple self-intersection local times of Volterra Gaussian bridges, \emph{i.e.}, Volterra Gaussian processes conditioned on their final location $a$, as $\|a\|\to\infty$. For $d$-dimensional $(2,ζ)$-locally nondeterministic processes with $ζ\in(0,2/d)$, we prove that these local times exist in every $L^p$. Under two further conditions on the kernel, the $p$-th moment of the $m$-multiple self-intersection local time of the bridge is of exact order $\|a\|^{-γ}$ for every integer $p\geqslant1$, with $γ=p(m-1)(2-ζd)/(2-ζ)$. In the plane, for kernels $k(t-s)$ with $k(0)\neq0$ and under strong local nondeterminism, we restrict the double self-intersection local time to $\{t_2-t_1\geqslant\|a\|^{-α}\}$, $α>0$, and establish a phase transition: its expectation decays like $\exp(-c\|a\|^{2-α})$, converges to an explicit positive limit, or grows logarithmically, for $α<2$, $α=2$ and $α>2$ respectively.

</details>


### [147] [Operator limits of random matrices: Edge universality and sharp approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35808)
*Hyung-Gil Kwak*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: We establish operator universality at the spectral edge of general Wigner matrices by showing that their suitably rescaled Lanczos tridiagonalizations, constructed from deterministic initial vectors, converge to the stochastic Airy operator with Dirichlet boundary condition.
  The result requires control of the entire tridiagonal structure on the scale relevant to the limiting differential operator. The Lanczos coefficients for non-Gaussian Wigner matrices are strongly dependent. We analyze their fluctuations by combining multi-resolvent local laws with quantitative control of Krylov subspaces and a comparison of the resulting Jacobi coefficients with those of the Gaussian ensembles. This approach identifies the limiting Brownian potential directly from the original matrix entries, without invoking Dyson Brownian motion in the step of Green function comparison. The resolvent limit yields joint convergence of edge eigenvalues, embedded eigenfunctions, and spectral measures.
  For Gaussian, Laguerre and circular $β$-ensembles we construct sharp couplings to the stochastic Airy, Bessel and Sine operators, preserving the finite-matrix laws. For fixed $β> 0$, their squared Hilbert-Schmidt inverse errors have almost-sure orders $n^{-1/3}, n^{-1}$ and $n^{-1}$, respectively, with $a > 0$ in the hard-edge upper bound. We also identify the positive leading order coefficient for the circular ensemble, whose second derivative jumps at $β= 2$.

</details>

### [148] [How characterisations of traces and central elements fail: sharp violation constants via michelson contrast and noncentrality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34388)
*Sami Abdullah Abed,Irina Nikolaeva,Andrej Novikov*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 对迹（trace）与中心元（central elements）的各类不等式刻画，本文量化其失效强度：在矩阵代数上以 Michelson 对比度（contrast）δ 为尺度，在有限冯诺依曼代数（finite von Neumann algebra）上以由中心条件数（central condition number）κ_Z 定义的非中心性（noncentrality）为尺度，给出精确常数、证明界及数值下界，并建立把矩阵常数提升为一般代数的转移原理（transfer principle）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gardner 不等式 |φ(X)|≤φ(|X|) 等条件给出迹与中心元的刻画，但刻画在近似情形下失效的量化强度此前未被系统研究；同时矩阵代数上的自然参数（对比度）在一般有限冯诺依曼代数上失效，需要寻找正确的控制参数。

Method: 以 φ=Tr(ρ·) 的密度对比度 δ 及 |X| 的对比度 δ' 刻画矩阵代数上的尖锐常数；引入由中心包络（central envelopes）的中心条件数 κ_Z(ρ) 定义的非中心性，并证明转移原理，将矩阵尖锐常数映射为有限冯诺依曼代数中关于 κ_Z 的精确常数；辅以经认证为各上确界下界的数值搜索。

Result: Gardner 不等式的尖锐常数为 (1−δ²δ'²)^(−1/2)，仅通过乘积 δδ' 依赖两个对比度；在有限冯诺依曼代数上该常数为 cosh(½ ln κ_Z)，同样适用于模的次可加性（subadditivity）。38 个迹刻画中 23 个常数精确、13 个有已证界、2 个仅有数值结果；矩阵代数上有限精确违背按 δ、δ² 或 δ⁴ 阶消失。另给出 Novikov–Tikhonov 中心元刻画的尖锐常数、三个非归一化迹泛函的尖锐界，以及平方根次可加性与幂平均不等式（power-mean inequality）失效的对比度阈值。

Conclusion: 对比度仅在矩阵代数（及非因子、非交换情形之外）是恰当参数，一般有限冯诺依曼代数中非中心性 κ_Z 才是支配参数；转移原理使矩阵常数可系统迁移，多数迹刻画的不等式常数由此得到确定或界定，剩余情形由数值下界支持猜想。

Abstract: A positive functional is a trace if and only if it satisfies any one of many inequalities, the first being Gardner's $|\varphi(X)|\le\varphi(|X|)$; positive central elements are characterised similarly. We ask how strongly these characterisations fail. For $\varphi=\mathrm{Tr}(ρ\,\cdot)$ on a matrix algebra the natural scale is the Michelson contrast $δ$ of the density. For Gardner's inequality the sharp constant depends on $δ$ and on the contrast $δ'$ of $|X|$ only through their product: it is $(1-δ^2δ'^2)^{-1/2}$, the secant of the angle whose sine is $δδ'$. On a finite von Neumann algebra the contrast is the wrong parameter, since every central density gives a trace; the governing parameter is the noncentrality, defined through the central condition number $κ_Z(ρ)$ of the central envelopes of $ρ$ (the largest and smallest elements of Halpern's central spectrum). A transfer principle lifts sharp matrix constants to exact constants in terms of $κ_Z$ in every finite von Neumann algebra. For 38 trace characterisations the constant is exact in 23 cases, has proved bounds in 13 and is known only numerically in two; for instance it is $\cosh(\frac12\lnκ_Z)$ for Gardner's inequality and for the subadditivity of the modulus. On matrix algebras the finite exact violations vanish at the trace to the orders $δ$, $δ^2$ or $δ^4$, and outside factors and abelian algebras no function of the contrast describes the constants. We also find the sharp constants of the Novikov-Tikhonov characterisations of central elements, sharp bounds for three unnormalised trace functionals, and sharp contrast thresholds below which subadditivity of the square root and the power-mean inequality cannot fail. A numerical search, certified as a lower bound for each supremum, supports conjectured values for the remaining inequalities.

</details>

### [149] [Near-exact Upper Confidence Limits for Axion Haloscopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34325)
*Andrea Gallo Rosso,Jan Conrad*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 针对轴子晕探测器（axion haloscope）顺序自适应频率扫描（sequential adaptive frequency scans）场景，提出一种基于似然（likelihood-based）的轴子-光子耦合（axion-photon coupling）上置信限计算方法，并与既有专用方法进行定性与定量比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子晕探测器的数据获取通常采用顺序自适应频率扫描，而现有专用上限设定方法（Geometric 方法及贝叶斯功率测量 BPM 框架）在该情形下的统计基础与约束效力尚不明确，需要更严谨且更紧致的替代方案。

Method: 构建基于似然的上置信限计算框架，并将其与 Brubaker 等提出的 Geometric 方法及 Palken 等提出的贝叶斯功率测量（Bayesian Power-Measured, BPM）框架在概念与数量层面进行对比，同时厘清这两种方法的统计依据。

Result: 似然方法给出的平均上限显著更具约束力（more constraining）；在 ALPHA Plasma-haloscope 的投影中，预期约束相较当前最先进水平提升约 1.4 倍。另指出 BPM 与统计学中作为传统 p 值（p-values）替代方案提出的 e 值（e-values）相对应。

Conclusion: 似然方法在顺序自适应扫描下优于 Geometric 与 BPM 等专用方法，并可为 BPM 提供 e 值意义下的统计解释，从而统一并改进轴子-光子耦合上限的统计处理。

Abstract: The data acquisition of axion haloscopes commonly implements sequential adaptive frequency scans. We present a method to calculate likelihood-based upper confidence limits on axion-photon coupling under this circumstance. We compare the likelihood-based method conceptually and quantitatively with tailor-made methods, i.e. the one proposed by Brubaker et al. (Geometric) and its upgrade the Bayesian Power-Measured (BPM) framework proposed by Palken et al. We find that the likelihood method yields average upper limits that are significantly more constraining than those obtained from these methods. For example, expected constraints improve by a factor of 1.4 as compared with the current state of the art when applied to projections for the ALPHA Plasma-haloscope. We further clarify the statistical basis for the Geometric and BPM methods. In particular, we point out the correspondence of BPM to e-values, proposed as an alternative to conventional p-values in statistics.

</details>

### [150] [From one particle to N particles: uniform transport-information and log-Sobolev inequalities for Coulomb gases on the torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35350)
*A Guillin,W Liu,B Nectoux,L Wu*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文研究在奇异相互作用下，如何将单粒子条件分布（one-particle conditional distributions）满足的泛函不等式（functional inequality）以与 N 无关的常数传播到整个 N 粒子系统，并以环面 T³ 上的平均场库仑气体（Coulomb gas）为模型，提出两种机制分别得到一致 Poincaré、运输-信息（transport-information）W₂I 不等式与在小电荷下的对数 Sobolev（log-Sobolev）不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：当相互作用奇异时，能否把单粒子条件分布层面的不等式以不依赖 N 的常数推广到全 N 粒子系统。作者以平均场库仑气体为具体场景，并需解释不同机制（如依赖型张量化）为何在 log-Sobolev 情形失效。

Method: 区分两种机制：一是由 Dobrushin-Wu 相互依赖系数（interdependence coefficients）支配的依赖张量化（dependent tensorization）；二是带缺陷的张量化（tensorization with a defect），借助 Duerinckx 与 Jabin 给出的配分函数（partition function）一致界。并刻画各机制适用的相互作用假设。

Result: 第一种机制给出常数完全显式的一致 Poincaré 与 transport-information W₂I 不等式；但 Zegarlinski 型系数因需要对相互作用作逐点（pointwise）界而为无穷，故该机制不能推广到 log-Sobolev 不等式。第二种机制在小电荷下给出 T³ 上库仑气体的一致 log-Sobolev 不等式。结果还推广到二维库仑气体与周期 Riesz 气体 1/|x|^s（0 < s ≤ 1），并讨论了 d ≥ 4 的拓展。

Conclusion: 在奇异相互作用下，通过与 N 无关的常数实现从单粒子到 N 粒子系统的泛函不等式传播是可行的，但需按机制区分适用范围：依赖张量化适用于 Poincaré 与 W₂I 型不等式，log-Sobolev 则需带缺陷的张量化配合配分函数一致界。

Abstract: We study how a functional inequality satisfied by the one-particle conditional distributions of a Gibbs measure, with a constant independent of the positions of the other particles, can be propagated to the whole N -particle system, with a constant independent of N , when the interaction is singular. Our model is the Coulomb gas on the torus T 3 in the mean-field regime. We distinguish two mechanisms. The first one is a dependent tensorization, governed by Dobrushin-Wu interdependence coefficients. It yields uniform Poincar{é} and transport-information W 2 I inequalities with fully explicit constants. We explain why this mechanism does not extend to the log-Sobolev inequality: the Zegarlinski-type coefficients, which require pointwise bounds on the interaction, are infinite. The second mechanism is a tensorization with a defect. Using a uniform bound on the partition function due to Duerinckx and Jabin, it yields a uniform log-Sobolev inequality for the Coulomb gas on T 3 at small charge. We identify the assumptions on the interaction under which each mechanism applies. All the results are then extended to the two-dimensional Coulomb gas and to periodic Riesz gases 1/|x|^s , 0 < s $\le$ 1. The extension to d $\ge$ 4 is also discussed.

</details>


### [151] [Converse symplectic interlacing via low-rank singular value perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35376)
*Priyanka Grover,Hemant K. Mishra,Sarishti Singh,Temjensangba*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了辛交错定理（symplectic interlacing theorem）的逆命题：满足 $2k$-步交错不等式的一对全局与局部辛谱，必可由某个实对称正定矩阵及其 $s$-主子矩阵实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辛交错定理原本只给出辛谱与 $s$-主子矩阵辛谱之间的必要交错关系，其逆命题（这些不等式是否也是充分的）尚未解决；该问题与高斯量子边际问题（Gaussian quantum marginal problem）中的谱兼容性判定直接相关。

Method: 核心技术手段是将 $s$-主子压缩（$s$-principal compression）约化为秩-$k$ 奇异值扰动（rank-$k$ singular value perturbation），并据此构造具有指定全局辛谱的 $2n \times 2n$ 实对称正定矩阵及其具有指定局部辛谱的 $2(n-k) \times 2(n-k)$ $s$-主子矩阵。

Result: 对任意给定的 $n$ 阶正全局辛谱与 $n-k$ 阶正局部辛谱，只要满足 $2k$-步交错不等式，即可显式构造出对应的实对称正定矩阵及其 $s$-主子矩阵，从而完成逆定理的证明。

Conclusion: 在高斯量子边际问题背景下，该结果表明辛交错不等式不仅是高斯边际（Gaussian marginals）的必要条件，同时也是全局-局部模边际问题（global-versus-local-mode marginal problem）的完整谱兼容条件（complete spectral compatibility conditions）。

Abstract: The symplectic interlacing theorem states that the symplectic spectrum of a $2n \times 2n$ real symmetric positive definite matrix satisfies a $2k$-step interlacing relation with the symplectic spectrum of every $2(n-k) \times 2(n-k)$ $s$-principal submatrix. We prove the converse in this paper. More precisely, given a positive global symplectic spectrum of size $n$ and a positive local symplectic spectrum of size $n-k$ satisfying the $2k$-step interlacing inequalities, we construct a $2n \times 2n$ real symmetric positive definite matrix having the prescribed global symplectic spectrum and a $2(n-k) \times 2(n-k)$ $s$-principal submatrix of it having the prescribed local symplectic spectrum. Our main tool is a reduction of $s$-principal compression to a rank-$k$ singular value perturbation. In the context of the Gaussian quantum marginal problem, our result implies that the symplectic interlacing inequalities are not merely necessary conditions on Gaussian marginals, but are also the complete spectral compatibility conditions for the global-versus-local-mode marginal problem.

</details>

### [152] [Interplay Between Network Heterogeneity and Activation Thresholds in Global Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33927)
*Aidan Meador-Woodruff,Gourab Ghoshal*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在Watts阈值模型中同时引入beta分布激活阈值与beta-binomial度分布，系统考察阈值异质性与网络拓扑异质性的交互作用，识别出决定级联能否广泛传播的两条临界边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂网络中的全局级联（global cascades）同时受激活阈值分布与网络拓扑影响，但这两类异质性（heterogeneity）如何交互尚缺乏系统分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用Watts阈值模型（Watts threshold model），以beta分布刻画激活阈值、以beta-binomial度分布刻画节点度，从而连续调节两类异质性的性质；并从极小种子（infinitesimal seeds）出发分析级联的发生条件。

Result: 识别出支配易感分支（vulnerable branching）大连通性行为的两条边界：第一条区分连通性增加会促进还是抑制传播的情形，取决于低阈值个体的丰度；第二条区分两种抑制机制，即低阈值个体稀缺与低度节点随连通性增加而减少。两机制交界处，其竞争贡献产生对数修正（logarithmic correction）的渐近标度。

Conclusion: 网络结构与个体激活阈值的相互作用共同决定广泛传播（extensive propagation）的发生条件，研究揭示了阈值异质性与拓扑异质性耦合下的级联起始机制。

Abstract: The onset of global cascades in complex networks depends on both the distribution of activation thresholds and the underlying network topology. Here we investigate how these two sources of heterogeneity interact within the Watts threshold model, using beta-distributed activation thresholds and beta-binomial degree distributions to systematically vary their respective properties. Analyzing the onset of cascades from infinitesimal seeds, we identify two distinct boundaries governing the large-connectivity behavior of vulnerable branching. The first separates regimes in which increasing connectivity enhances or suppresses propagation, depending on the abundance of individuals with low activation thresholds. The second distinguishes two mechanisms of suppression: one controlled by the scarcity of low-threshold individuals and the other by the diminishing abundance of low-degree nodes as connectivity increases. At the boundary between these mechanisms, their competing contributions produce a logarithmic correction to the asymptotic scaling. Our results demonstrate how the interplay between network structure and individual activation thresholds determines the conditions for extensive propagation.

</details>


### [153] [Designing sustainability under Earth-system rate constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34026)
*José Halloy,Petros Chatzimpiros,François Graner,Thomas Gregor*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出可持续性讨论被总量指标（如总能耗、物质存量、回收量）主导，而地球物质封闭、能量开放条件下的长期可行性实际由“速率”约束决定；作者构建物理框架，将技术代谢、物质稀释与回收极限通过功率密度、面功率密度及弥散太阳能通量下的材料再纯化速率联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可持续性辩论被总量化的量化指标（total energy use、material stocks、recycling volumes）所垄断，这些指标虽有信息量，却掩盖了更根本的约束；需要揭示工业活动与地球系统动力学之间的速率层面不匹配。

Method: 构建一个物理框架（physical framework），通过速率约束——功率密度（power density）、面功率密度（areal power density）以及弥散太阳能通量（diffuse solar fluxes）下分散材料可被再纯化（repurified）的速率——将技术代谢（technological metabolism）、物质稀释（material dilution）与回收极限（recycling limits）联系起来。

Result: 该视角解释了为何许多当代技术仅靠存量消耗（stock drawdown）与高功率缓冲（high-power buffering）才能维持可行性，以及为何绿色增长（green growth）策略往往只是转移而非解决工业活动与地球系统动力学之间的根本失配。

Conclusion: 长期可持续性要求两方面并举：一是将工业总吞吐量（aggregate industrial throughput）定量削减至速率受限（rate-bounded）区间；二是发展在性质上与生物圈运行条件（biospheric operating conditions）相兼容的技术。

Abstract: Sustainability debates are monopolized by quantities: total energy use, material stocks, or recycling volumes. These metrics are informative, but they obscure a more fundamental constraint. On a materially closed but energetically open Earth, long-term viability is governed by rates: power density, areal power density, and the rate at which dispersed materials can be repurified under dif- fuse solar fluxes. We develop a physical framework that links technological metabolism, material dilution, and recycling limits through these rate con- straints. This perspective clarifies why many contemporary technologies remain viable only through stock drawdown and high-power buffering, and why green growth strategies often shift rather than resolve the underlying mismatch between industrial activity and Earth-system dynamics. We argue that long-term sustain- ability requires both quantitative reduction in aggregate industrial throughput toward rate-bounded regimes and the development of technologies qualitatively compatible with biospheric operating conditions.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Elasto-Plastocapillarity: Surface-Tension-Driven Flows of Yield-Stress Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33345)
*Maziyar Jalaal*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出“弹性-塑性毛细（elasto-plastocapillarity）”这一视角，聚焦屈服应力流体（yield-stress fluids）中表面张力驱动的自由表面流动，其中毛细应力与屈服及弹性松弛相互竞争。文章梳理参数空间、代表性问题和开放挑战，以指导未来实验、理论与计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屈服应力流体的自由表面流动中，毛细应力（capillary stresses）与屈服（yielding）和弹性松弛（elastic relaxation）的耦合尚缺乏统一框架。该视角文章旨在界定这一交叉领域的参数空间与研究议程，服务于增材制造、涂层及生物流动等应用。

Method: 采用视角/评论（perspective）式论述：构建相关参数空间（parameter space），并归纳代表性几何问题——液滴（drops）、气泡（bubbles）、射流/细丝（jets/filaments）、薄膜（thin films）和多孔介质（porous media）。

Result: 文章给出参数空间框架与代表性问题的分类，并列出若干紧迫的开放挑战：表面张力测量、微观结构附近尺度分离（scale separation）的失效、体相-界面耦合流动的实验诊断、界面流变（interfacial rheology）与表面应力、历史依赖性（history dependence）、本构模型（constitutive models）与边界条件、简化理论模型，以及人工智能与微重力（microgravity）带来的新机遇。

Conclusion: 弹性-塑性毛细构成一个兼具基础意义与应用价值的交叉研究方向；所列开放问题旨在引导未来的实验、理论与计算工作。

Abstract: In this perspective article, we introduce elasto-plastocapillarity: surface-tension-driven free-surface flows of yield-stress fluids in which capillary stresses compete with yielding and elastic relaxation. We frame the relevant parameter space, and highlight representative problems (drops, bubbles, jets/filaments, thin films, and porous media) that are central to applications ranging from additive manufacturing and coatings to biological flows. We then outline a set of timely open challenges, including surface-tension measurements, breakdown of scale separation near the microstructures, coupled bulk-interface flow experimental diagnosis, interfacial rheology and surface stresses, history dependence, constitutive models and boundary conditions, reduced theoretical models, and the emerging opportunity provided by artificial intelligence and microgravity; aimed at guiding future experiments, theory, and computation.

</details>


### [155] [Mapping bimodal colloidal depletion systems onto the monodisperse gelation phase diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33394)
*Robert A. Campbell,Ziye Zhuang,Ali Mohraz,Safa Jamali*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文通过系统模拟研究吸引型双峰耗尽凝胶（attractive bimodal depletion gel）相图，发现可用接触加权第二维里系数（contact-weighted second virial coefficient）将吸引条件与组成折叠为单一参数，从而把双峰相图投影到单分散相图之上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界依赖胶体凝胶化（colloidal gelation）与相行为模型来预测加工设备对新产品配方的处理能力；近单分散体系仅需三个状态变量即可预测凝胶形成，但向双峰及多峰体系的扩展尚不清楚。

Method: 对吸引型双峰耗尽凝胶相图开展系统性模拟研究（simulation study），考察总体积分数、吸引条件与双峰组成对凝胶化的影响。

Result: 凝胶化高度依赖总体积分数（total volume fraction）、吸引条件与双峰组成；用接触加权第二维里系数可将吸引与组成有效折叠为单一参数，成功将双峰相图投影到既有的单分散（monodisperse）相图上，并较准确地描述多数体相组装性质（bulk assembly properties）。

Conclusion: 双峰体系的宏观相行为可借单参数映射归约至单分散框架，但其凝胶底层网络结构（network structure）仍高度依赖双峰组成与吸引的具体细节，表明相图层面的普适性并不完全传递到微观结构。

Abstract: Many industries rely on models of colloidal gelation and phase behavior to predict how processing equipment will handle new product formulations. In colloidal systems with nearly uniform particle size it is possible to predict the formation of a gel from only three state variables; however, the extension of this model to bimodal and multimodal systems is not well described. Here we present a systematic simulation study of the attractive bimodal depletion gel phase diagram. For these systems, we find that gelation is highly dependent on total volume fraction, attraction conditions, and bimodal composition. Attraction and composition can be effectively collapsed into a single parameter using the contact-weighted second virial coefficient. This successfully projects the bimodal phase diagram onto the established monodisperse phase diagram, including accurately describing most bulk assembly properties; however, the underlying network structure of these gels remains highly dependent on the details of bimodal composition and attraction.

</details>


### [156] [Diffusion of chiral active Brownian particles in oscillatory Poiseuille flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33466)
*Anshika Sharma,Vikas Sharma,Narender Khatri,Suneet Kumar Awasthi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 在二维振荡Poiseuille流的微通道中，手性自驱动粒子（chiral self-propelled particles）的有效扩散系数随振荡频率、流强度、内禀角速度和旋转扩散率显著变化，并可因共振扩散（resonant diffusion）出现极大与极小；手性可放大该共振增强达数个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微纳游动体（micro- and nanoswimmers）在受限微通道流场中常以过阻尼活性布朗粒子（overdamped active Brownian particle）动力学描述，但手性自驱动粒子在含振荡Poiseuille流（oscillatory Poiseuille flow）的受限环境下扩散行为尚不明确，该研究旨在填补此空白。

Method: 采用数值模拟（numerical simulations），考察二维微通道（two-dimensional microchannel）中手性自驱动粒子在振荡Poiseuille流下的扩散行为，系统分析振荡频率、流强度、内禀角速度（intrinsic angular velocity）与旋转扩散率（rotational diffusion rate）对有效扩散系数（effective diffusion coefficient）的影响。

Result: 有效扩散系数可随振荡频率与流强度呈振荡变化，出现明显的极大值和极小值；增强扩散峰源于流振荡时间尺度与内禀特征扩散时间尺度匹配的共振扩散，并识别出额外的涡度匹配（vorticity matching）机制，导致粒子在连续流动半周期内交替于通道壁面累积；手性粒子显著放大共振扩散，相比非手性粒子产生数个量级的扩散增强。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为利用时间依赖流（time-dependent flow）调控微流控通道（microfluidic channels）中自驱动粒子的扩散提供依据，并揭示手性、共振与涡度匹配在受限活性输运中的作用。

Abstract: Micro- and nanoswimmers diffusing in a fluid flow confined to microchannels are often described by overdamped active Brownian particle dynamics, in which viscous effects dominate and inertia is negligible. Here, we investigate the diffusive behavior of chiral self-propelled particles in a two-dimensional microchannel with oscillatory Poiseuille flow. Using numerical simulations, we show that the effective diffusion coefficient strongly depends on the oscillation frequency, flow strength, intrinsic angular velocity, and rotational diffusion rate. Interestingly, the effective diffusion coefficient can oscillate with oscillation frequency and flow strength, exhibiting distinct maxima and minima. The enhanced diffusion peaks arise from resonant diffusion associated with the matching between the flow oscillation time scale and the intrinsic characteristic diffusion time scale. Moreover, we identify an additional vorticity matching mechanism, leading to alternating channel wall accumulation of particles during successive flow half-cycles. Additionally, chiral particles strongly amplify the resonant diffusion, producing diffusion enhancements of several orders of magnitude compared to nonchiral particles. The results of this study could have implications for controlling the diffusion of self-propelled particles in microfluidic channels using time-dependent flow.

</details>


### [157] [Effect of Size Asymmetry and Zwitterionic Character of Solvent Ions on Impedance Spectra of Ultradilute Colloidal Suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33541)
*Meenakshi,Shalini Gupta,Gaurav Goel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究揭示缓冲液化学（buffer chemistry）对羧基化聚苯乙烯（PS）微球胶体阻抗（colloidal impedance）的调控作用，发现在超稀浓度下存在振荡区（Region 1）与较高浓度下的单调区（Region 2），并将其归因于两性离子（zwitterion）驱动的界面有序化（interfacial ordering），而非经典模型所能解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标记生物传感（label-free biosensing）与电动诊断（electrokinetic diagnostics）中胶体阻抗至关重要，但经典模型忽略缓冲液特异性相互作用，缺乏对缓冲液化学如何影响阻抗的机制理解。

Method: 在电导匹配的 HEPES（两性离子缓冲液）与磷酸盐缓冲液（PBS）中研究羧基化 PS 微球，结合阻抗测量、动态光散射（DLS）、zeta 电位（zeta potential）与分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，并改变 pH（6.9 与 8.1）。

Result: Region 1 中 pH 6.9 的 HEPES（约 80% 两性离子）使 delta Z 偏离 O'Brien 预测最大，电导增量符号与经典预期相反；DLS 在 0.008 w/v% 检出团簇，pH 改变显著改变阻抗幅度而 DLS 谱不变；模拟显示界面电荷与数密度分布呈可比振荡周期性；zeta 电位显示 HEPES 界面稳定，PBS 中幅值随离子强度增大，与经典屏蔽理论相悖。

Conclusion: 界面有序化可部分解释电导增强，但未证实离散层或配准结构；这些非线性特征与介观尺度组织（mesoscale organization）凸显缓冲液选择在高灵敏阻抗生物传感中的关键作用。

Abstract: Buffer chemistry modulates colloidal impedance critically for label-free biosensing and electrokinetic diagnostics, yet classical models neglect buffer-specific interactions. We studied carboxylated polystyrene (PS) microspheres in conductivity-matched zwitterionic 4-(2-hydroxyethyl)-1-piperazineethanesulfonic acid (HEPES) and phosphate-buffered saline (PBS). Two concentration-dependent regimes emerged: oscillatory at ultradilute concentrations (<0.02 w/v%; Region 1) and monotonic at higher concentrations (Region 2). In Region 1, HEPES at pH 6.9 (approximately 80% zwitterionic) produced the largest deviation of delta Z from the O'Brien prediction. The conductivity increment was opposite in sign to classical expectation. We attribute this to zwitterion-driven interfacial ordering that enhances local conductivity and promotes self-limiting particle clustering. Dynamic light scattering detected cluster formation at 0.008 w/v%, well below aggregation thresholds. Changing pH from 6.9 to 8.1 strongly altered impedance amplitude; DLS profiles remained invariant. This indicates zwitterions modulate electrochemical polarization, not physical assembly. Molecular dynamics simulations showed pH-dependent HEPES organization at the PS surface. At pH 6.9, interfacial charge- and number-density profiles showed comparable oscillatory periodicity. At pH 8.1, anionic HEPES produced a more diffuse profile with increased Na+ compensation. These results support interfacial ordering as a contributor to enhanced conductivity but do not establish discrete layers or registry. Zeta potentials distinguished stable HEPES interfaces from PBS, where magnitude increased with ionic strength, contrary to classical screening theory. These nonlinear signatures and mesoscale organization underscore the role of buffer selection in high-sensitivity impedance biosensing.

</details>


### [158] [Aging dynamics and generalized superposition principles in carbon black-carboxymethylcellulose hydrogels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33682)
*Gauthier Legrand,Guilhem P. Baeza,Jarno L. Waeterloos,Christian Clasen,Sébastien Manneville,Thibaut Divoux*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合流变学与电学阻抗谱，揭示由疏水炭黑（CB）与羧甲基纤维素（CMC）构成的水凝胶存在两种老化机制：导电凝胶呈对数弹性恢复，绝缘凝胶呈对数与幂律复合硬化并服从时间-老化叠加原理，剪切下还服从时间-剪切速率叠加原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CB-CMC 水凝胶的微观结构随 CB/聚合物比例改变，低 CMC 含量下形成渗流导电网络，高聚合物含量下 CB 颗粒则成为绝缘聚合物基体中的孤立物理交联点；但两种区域的老化动力学差异及其微观结构基础尚不清楚，需要阐明其非线性流变行为的结构来源。

Method: 将流变学（rheology）与电学阻抗谱（electrical impedance spectroscopy）联用，对两类水凝胶进行时间分辨测量；在稳态剪切下测量垂直于流动方向的黏弹响应，并在剪切速率循环过程中开展时间分辨流变-超小角 X 射线散射（rheo-USAXS）测量。

Result: 导电水凝胶表现出与渗流 CB 网络相关的对数弹性恢复（logarithmic elastic recovery）；绝缘水凝胶则呈现对数与幂律（power-law）复合的硬化，并伴随与受限离子运动有关的介电极化。绝缘水凝胶黏弹谱可坍缩为单一主曲线，体现时间-老化叠加原理（time-aging superposition principle）；剪切下亦服从时间-剪切速率叠加原理，且移动因子（shift factors）间的标度关系类似临界凝胶（critical-gel）行为，与静置老化时不同。rheo-USAXS 显示 CB 颗粒与团簇的结构重排与流变滞后（rheological hysteresis）相关。

Conclusion: 结果为理解绝缘 CB-CMC 水凝胶的非线性流变学提供了微观结构基础，并将广义叠加原理（generalized superposition principles）确立为其老化与流动行为的统一框架。

Abstract: Aqueous dispersions of hydrophobic carbon black (CB) particles can be stabilized by carboxymethylcellulose (CMC), forming CB-CMC hydrogels whose microstructure is controlled by the relative CB-to-polymer content. At low CMC content, CB particles form a percolated, electrically conductive network, whereas at higher polymer content, CB particles act as isolated physical crosslinkers within an electrically insulating polymer matrix [see Legrand \textit{et al.}, Macromolecules 56, 2298-2308 (2023)]. Combining rheology and electrical impedance spectroscopy, we show that these two regimes exhibit markedly different aging dynamics. Conductive hydrogels exhibit logarithmic elastic recovery associated with a percolated CB network, whereas insulating hydrogels exhibit a combination of logarithmic and power-law stiffening, accompanied by dielectric polarization associated with confined ionic motion. The insulating hydrogels further exhibit a time-aging superposition principle, as their viscoelastic spectra measured in a time-resolved fashion can collapse onto a single master curve. Under steady shear, the viscoelastic response measured perpendicular to the flow also obeys a time--shear-rate superposition principle. Remarkably, the scaling between the shift factors under shear is reminiscent of critical-gel behavior, unlike that observed during aging at rest. Finally, time-resolved rheo-USAXS measurements performed during shear-rate cycles reveal structural rearrangements of CB particles and clusters that correlate with rheological hysteresis. These results provide a microstructural basis for understanding the nonlinear rheology of insulating CB-CMC hydrogels and establish generalized superposition principles as a unifying framework for their aging and flow behavior.

</details>


### [159] [Inverse origami inspired by insect morphogenesis: geometric design of wrinkle structures for curved surface formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34903)
*Kentaro Morikawa,Yuuki Shimizu,Nen Saito,Yasuhiro Inoue*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 受昆虫变态期体表褶皱展开过程启发，研究提出逆向折纸（inverse-origami）策略：将预成型的褶皱结构在内部压力下展开以获得曲面，并通过把褶皱视为单向拉伸场（unidirectional stretching field）来反推褶皱方向与间距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统折纸（origami）由平面薄片折叠得到三维物体，而昆虫外骨骼在幼虫到蛹的变态（metamorphosis）中通过体内预制褶皱结构在内部压力下的展开，突变性地获得复杂三维形态；该生物过程为可展开曲面的制造提供了逆向设计思路。

Method: 核心设计问题是由给定目标曲面反求褶皱结构。方法将褶皱结构建模为单向拉伸场，描述结构展开时各点产生的拉伸量；先由目标曲面几何导出该场，再转化为空间变化的褶皱方向与间距，使褶皱展开后逼近目标曲面，所得数值设计近似目标曲面的内蕴度量（intrinsic metric）。

Result: 通过数值构建、机械展开仿真（mechanical unfolding simulation）与3D打印样件（3D-printed prototype），在多个目标曲面上验证了该方法的有效性，褶皱结构展开后的形状可近似目标曲面。

Conclusion: 工作提供了将预设曲面形状编码于褶皱结构中的仿生几何设计原理，并形成可展开曲面（deployable curved surface）设计与制造的框架。

Abstract: Insect exoskeletons abruptly acquire complex three-dimensional forms during metamorphosis from larva to pupa. This transformation occurs through the unfolding of preformed wrinkle structures inside the larval body under internal pressure. Inspired by this process, we investigate an inverse-origami strategy for fabricating curved surfaces. Whereas conventional origami produces three-dimensional objects by folding a flat sheet, this strategy produces curved surfaces by unfolding preformed wrinkle structures. The central design problem is to derive a wrinkle structure from a prescribed target surface. Our key idea is to regard a wrinkle structure as a unidirectional stretching field, which specifies at each point the stretch produced when the structure unfolds. We first derive such a field from the target surface geometry and then translate it into spatially varying wrinkle directions and spacing, so that the wrinkle structure unfolds into the target. The resulting numerical designs approximate the intrinsic metric of the target surface. We demonstrate the method on multiple target surfaces through numerical construction, mechanical unfolding simulations, and 3D-printed prototypes. This work provides a biologically inspired geometric design principle for encoding prescribed surface shapes in wrinkle structures, as well as a framework for designing and fabricating deployable curved surfaces.

</details>


### [160] [Interfacial bacteria-driven rotation of free-floating active droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35242)
*Dixi Yang,Anheng Wang,Chunming Wang,Hajime Tanaka,Jiaxing Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作在三维水两相体系（aqueous two-phase system）中实现了由铜绿假单胞菌（Pseudomonas aeruginosa）界面聚集驱动的液滴自主旋转，并揭示角速度随半径呈 R^{-1} 标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往细菌驱动的液滴旋转多依赖邻近固体边界（solid boundaries）、外场（external fields）或密集活性向列相（active nematics），本文旨在寻找无需这些条件的自主旋转实现途径。

Method: 结合共聚焦显微（confocal microscopy）与流体粒子动力学模拟（fluid particle dynamics simulations），并构建理论模型解释标度律。

Result: 两亲性细菌在葡聚糖–聚乙二醇界面（dextran–polyethylene glycol interface）富集并沿局部切向运动与取向；界面局域化将无外力游泳偶极（force-free swimming dipole）分配到两相，使单一相承受有限活性扭矩（active torque）而整体保持扭矩平衡；模拟分布比实验更空间异质；宏观角速度呈 R^{-1} 依赖。

Conclusion: 研究提出一种以流体界面（fluid-interface）为中介将游泳体活动转化为宏观旋转流的路径，并确立利用界面活性物质（interfacial active matter）调控液滴动力学的策略。

Abstract: Active droplets provide a platform for converting microscopic propulsion into macroscopic flow. Previous realizations of bacteria-driven droplet rotation typically rely on nearby solid boundaries, external fields, or dense active nematics. Here, we report autonomous rotation of droplets in a three-dimensional aqueous two-phase system driven by interfacial population of \textit{Pseudomonas aeruginosa}. Combining confocal microscopy with fluid particle dynamics simulations, we show that amphiphilic bacteria accumulate at the dextran--polyethylene glycol interface and preferentially move and orient along the local tangent. Interfacial localization partitions the force-free swimming dipole between the two liquid phases, allowing either phase to experience a finite active torque while the combined system remains torque balanced. The simulated interfacial population is more spatially heterogeneous than in the experiments, emphasizing that both the bacterial distribution and its signed orientational organization can influence the instantaneous torque. Over the accessible size range, the macroscopic angular velocity exhibits an inverse-radius ($R^{-1}$) dependence. We also formulate a theoretical model to rationalize this scaling law, revealing how the macroscopic rotation is governed by collective interfacial swimming and surface coverage. These results identify a fluid-interface-mediated route for converting swimmer activity into macroscopic rotational flow and establish a strategy for harnessing interfacial active matter to regulate droplet dynamics.

</details>


### [161] [Synchronization-controlled transport near an absorbing transition: Effective Enskog kinetic theory of a driven granular monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35270)
*David González Méndez,Rubén Gómez González,Raphaël Maire,Vicente Garzó,Ignacio Pagonabarraga*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文在Δ模型框架下构建了振动边界驱动颗粒单层的Enskog动理学理论，通过状态依赖的有效碰撞规则纳入同步效应，并结合Chapman–Enskog展开给出输运系数的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振动边界下的同步（synchronization）可定性改变受限颗粒流动力学，并控制碰撞诱导的能量向水平运动转移，但缺乏相应的动理学描述。

Method: 在Δ模型框架内建立Enskog动理学理论，以碰撞频率与特征同步时间的竞争构造状态依赖的有效碰撞规则，并引入黏性阻力（viscous drag）描述与限制板的耗散；以均匀活跃态为参考态进行Chapman–Enskog展开，求解至Navier–Stokes阶。

Result: 得到剪切黏度（shear viscosity）、体积黏度（bulk viscosity）、热导率（thermal conductivity）、扩散热系数（diffusive heat coefficient）及冷却率（cooling rate）一阶修正的显式表达式，一致计入非弹性、阻尼、有限密度与同步效应；事件驱动分子动力学（event-driven molecular dynamics）模拟表明理论在活跃态较广区域内准确描述稳态温度与剪切/体积黏度。

Conclusion: 理论提供了同步控制的输运的动理学描述；在同步控制的非连续吸收转变（absorbing transition）附近，因微观同步动力学的平均场粗粒化，碰撞依赖注入幅度的涨落及同步与碰撞前速度的相关性变得重要，导致偏差。

Abstract: Synchronization with vibrating boundaries can qualitatively alter the dynamics of confined granular fluids and control the collision-induced transfer of energy to horizontal motion. We develop an Enskog kinetic theory for the active phase of a driven granular monolayer within the framework of the $Δ$-model, which accounts for the transfer of energy from the vertical to the horizontal degrees of freedom of grains. Synchronization effects are incorporated through a state-dependent effective collision rule governed by the interplay between the local collision frequency and a characteristic synchronization time. A viscous drag force is also included to account for energy dissipation arising from interactions with the confining plates. Within this framework, we characterize the homogeneous active state and use it as the reference state for a Chapman--Enskog expansion. The solution of the Enskog equation to Navier--Stokes order then yields explicit expressions for the shear and bulk viscosities, the thermal conductivity, the diffusive heat coefficient, and the first-order contribution to the cooling rate, consistently accounting for inelasticity, damping, finite-density effects, and synchronization. Event-driven molecular-dynamics simulations show that the theory accurately describes the steady temperature and shear and bulk viscosities over a broad region of the active phase. Close to the discontinuous synchronization-controlled absorbing transition, deviations are mainly attributed to the mean-field coarse-graining of the microscopic synchronization dynamics: fluctuations of the collision-dependent injection amplitude and correlations between synchronization and pre-collisional velocities become increasingly important. These results provide a kinetic description of synchronization-controlled transport and delineate its range of validity near the absorbing transition.

</details>


### [162] [Rigid Particle-Induced Anomalous Softening in Elastomer Composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35380)
*Yonghyun Cho,Hyeongho Min,Hongdeok Kim,Dongeun Lee,Minjune Kim,Siyeon Jang,Ali Amini,Zitang Wei,Hosub Lim,Hwan-ho Cho,Bo-yong Park,Sang Won Lee,Joonmyung Choi,Metin Sitti,Sangyul Baik*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作提出以粒子主动引导凝胶化（gelation）而非被动填充的策略，在粒子含量增加时实现反常软化（anomalous softening），并据此获得兼具功能性与柔顺性的软复合材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规粒子填充软复合材料（soft composites）随粒子负载增加而硬化，导致功能性与柔顺性（compliance）之间存在固有权衡，需要新的设计思路突破该权衡。

Method: 在富溶胶弹性体基体（sol-rich elastomer matrix）中利用溶胶相动力学（sol-phase kinetics）将网络形成导向粒子介导组装（particle-mediated assembly），并建立基于结构的模型（structure-based model）描述该行为的组成依赖性起始条件。

Result: 随粒子含量增加反而出现反常软化，该响应由凝胶化过程中承载网络（load-bearing network）的拓扑重构（topological reconfiguration）主导而非仅由填料负载决定；模型可捕捉其起始条件，且该响应在不同粒子体系中可重现。

Conclusion: 所得材料兼具功能性、柔软性、能量耗散（energy dissipation）与大变形下的可恢复性（recoverability），确立拓扑引导凝胶化（topology-guided gelation）作为设计柔顺功能复合材料的通用策略，适用于软体机器人驱动、共形界面等场景。

Abstract: Particle-filled soft composites generally stiffen as particle loading increases, imposing a trade-off between functionality and compliance. Here, we show that this trade-off can be overcome by designing particles to actively direct gelation rather than act as passive fillers. In a sol-rich elastomer matrix, sol-phase kinetics redirect network formation towards particle-mediated assembly, yielding anomalous softening despite increasing particle content. This response is governed by topological reconfiguration of the load-bearing network during gelation rather than by filler loading alone. A structure-based model captures the composition-dependent onset of this behaviour, and the same response is reproduced across distinct particle systems. The resulting materials combine functionality with softness, energy dissipation, and recoverability under large deformation. These findings establish topology-guided gelation as a general strategy for engineering compliant functional composites for soft robotic actuation, conformal interfaces, and other systems that require both deformability and embedded material functionality.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [The mathematical journey of spatial reciprocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35161)
*Chaoqian Wang,Xiang Li,Attila Szolnoki*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该综述梳理了结构化种群（structured populations）中进化博弈论（evolutionary game theory）的数学理论进展，重点介绍配对近似（pair approximation）与溯祖理论（coalescent theory）在网络上进化博弈的解析求解作用，并讨论其局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去三十年，结构化种群中的进化博弈论研究进展迅速，模拟中发现的违反直觉的合作现象催生了对严格数学理论的需求，以刻画其底层动力学。

Method: 采用综述方式，系统整理配对近似与溯祖理论等数学方法，这些方法为网络上的进化博弈提供解析解（analytical solutions）。

Result: 归纳了上述理论取得的主要结果，同时指出其局限，包括计算复杂度高（high computational complexity）以及在应用于复杂模型时灵活性受限（constrained flexibility）。

Conclusion: 数学方法与模拟（simulations）相辅相成，是理解结构化种群中合作行为的互补工具（complementary tool）。

Abstract: Over the past three decades, evolutionary game theory in structured populations has witnessed explosive progress. Counterintuitive cooperative phenomena discovered in simulations have stimulated the development of rigorous mathematical theories to describe the underlying dynamics. In this paper, we review these theoretical advancements, including pair approximation and coalescent theory, which offer analytical solutions to evolutionary games on networks. We summarize the key results obtained by these theories, while also discussing their limitations, such as high computational complexity and constrained flexibility when applied to complex models. We therefore suggest that mathematical approaches provide a complementary tool for understanding cooperation in structured populations alongside simulations.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Autonomous atomistic-continuum coupling through uncertainty-aware active learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33272)
*Daniel Abdulah,Hariswaran Sitaraman,Ross E. Larsen,Kevin Patrick Griffin,Grace Wei,Janelle Domantay,Ethan Young,Marc T. Henry de Frahan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知（uncertainty-aware）代理模型，用于耦合原子尺度KMC表面化学求解器与连续介质多组分流体动力学求解器，实现高效多尺度耦合并自动触发补充训练，在线学习框架将原子模拟次数减少两到三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子尺度动力学蒙特卡洛（kinetic Monte Carlo, KMC）与连续介质（continuum）流体求解器跨越大跨度物理长度与时间尺度，直接耦合计算代价高昂，需要一种既能保持精度又能降低原子模拟成本的耦合策略。

Method: 构建预测六种组分边界质量通量（boundary mass fluxes）及其不确定性的代理模型（surrogate model），连续介质求解器监测不确定性并在需要新训练数据时自动触发额外KMC模拟；同时构建基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）标定的能量学模型，并以反应性卤化物钙钛矿（halide-perovskite）气相沉积为案例验证。

Result: 在保守不确定性阈值下，代理耦合方法的累积质量通量与直接耦合方法误差通常在10-20%以内，并能捕捉净沉积对组分分压的非单调依赖；结果表明无需显式表征详细晶体结构，组分分辨质量通量即可作为原子尺度响应的低维表示；该自主在线学习（online-learning）方法使所需原子模拟次数相对直接多尺度耦合减少两到三个数量级。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知代理耦合可在用户指定精度水平下高效实现原子-连续介质多尺度耦合，组分分辨质量通量足以表征原子尺度响应，在线学习显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: We develop an uncertainty-aware surrogate modeling approach for coupling an atomistic kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) surface-chemistry solver with a continuum multi-species fluid dynamics solver across disparate physical length and time scales. The surrogate model predicts boundary mass fluxes for six species and their uncertainties, which the continuum solver monitors to automatically trigger additional KMC simulations when new training data are needed. This enables efficient multiscale coupling and maintains a user-specified level of accuracy. We demonstrate the approach for reactive halide-perovskite vapor deposition, which involves a multi-species vapor jet impinging on a reactive substrate. The KMC solver uses a DFT-calibrated energetics model that we construct as part of this work and provides net deposition and desorption rates for six species. With a conservative uncertainty threshold, the surrogate-coupled approach reproduces cumulative mass fluxes of the directly coupled approach with errors typically within 10-20%, while capturing the non-monotonic dependence of net deposition on species partial pressure. Importantly, the results show that detailed crystal structure need not be explicitly represented to predict quantities exchanged between atomistic and continuum solvers; species-resolved mass fluxes provide a sufficiently low-dimensional representation of the atomistic response. For this accuracy target, the autonomous online-learning approach reduces the number of required atomistic simulations by two to three orders of magnitude relative to direct multiscale coupling.

</details>


### [165] [A neural accelerated surface-roughness-based Discrete Element Method (SR-DEM) for modeling granular systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33363)
*Xiwei Pan,Liuchi Li*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于表面粗糙度的离散元方法（surface-roughness-based DEM, SR-DEM），以显式解析颗粒表面形貌、由无摩擦微凸体集体相互作用中"涌现"摩擦的方式，替代传统唯象摩擦定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散元方法（Discrete Element Method, DEM）框架均以唯象摩擦定律（如 Amontons-Coulomb 型）描述颗粒间摩擦，缺乏物理基础；受摩擦学（tribology）研究启发，作者希望以更具物理依据的方式处理接触摩擦。

Method: 在该框架中不再使用摩擦定律，而是显式解析每个颗粒的表面形貌（surface topography），使摩擦从表面微凸体（asperities）的集体无摩擦相互作用中涌现；以增加计算量为代价换取物理性，并引入类 FE$^2$ 算法与基于特征级线性调制（feature-wise linear modulation, FiLM）的神经网络代理模型，二者均在 GPU 上并行化以加速接触计算。

Result: 在二维情形下：(1) 使用简单的表面形貌表征即可重现基于两体（two-body）的 Amontons-Coulomb 型摩擦行为；(2) 识别出基于多体（many-body）的摩擦行为——具体为休止角（angle of repose）数值——而现有 DEM 框架在同等模型复杂度下并不总能捕捉这些行为。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了 SR-DEM 的未来发展方向，以及其可能为广泛新兴应用带来的新机遇，包括（设计）悬浮流变学（suspension rheology）与超材料力学（metamaterial mechanics）。

Abstract: The Discrete Element Method (DEM) is an indispensable tool for modeling and understanding the basic mechanical behaviors of (dry) granular media, a class of disordered material systems of significant interest to multiple scientific domains from (geo-)physics to (chemical) engineering. At the core of all present DEM frameworks is a phenomenological friction law--commonly chosen to be of the Amontons-Coulomb type--that models the frictional interaction among constituent grains. Here, inspired by research in the tribology community, we present an alternative, fundamentally different framework, termed surface-roughness-based DEM (SR-DEM), in which contact friction is handled in a more physics-based manner. In this framework, instead of using a friction law, we explicitly resolve the surface topography of each grain and let friction emerge from collective frictionless interactions of surface asperities. In essence, we trade phenomenology for physics at the cost of an increased computational demand. To mitigate the cost, we introduce an FE$^2$-like algorithm and a feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM)-based neural network surrogate, both of which are parallelized on GPU to accelerate contact computation. Crucially, using this framework in 2D, we (1) demonstrate the possibility of recovering a two-body-based Amontons-Coulomb-type friction behavior using a simple representation of surface topography, and (2) identify many-body-based frictional behaviors--more specifically, the angle of repose values--that cannot always be captured by current DEM frameworks under the same level of model complexity. We conclude by discussing future directions for SR-DEM, as well as exciting new opportunities it could unlock for a wide range of emerging applications, including (designing) suspension rheology and metamaterial mechanics.

</details>


### [166] [kstress: a LAMMPS package for calculating the 2D wavevector-dependent stress tensor and its anisotropy in amorphous solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34206)
*Olivier Benzerara,Jörg Baschnagel,Alexander N. Semenov,Liudmyla Klochko*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出了 LAMMPS 中计算波矢（wavevector）与时间分辨应力张量（stress tensor）模块的优化实现，并给出代码验证流程；基于二维多分散 Lennard-Jones（2DpLJ）玻璃形成液体，揭示了仿射模量（affine modulus）随波矢模长 q 的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波矢与时间分辨的应力张量可用于获取非晶固体（amorphous solids）随探测长度尺度变化的弹性模量，以及区分非晶固体与液体的长程应力关联（stress correlations）。但其计算需计入所有相互作用对，因而必须对接分子动力学（molecular dynamics）代码的力循环（force loop），带来实现与验证上的需求。

Method: 工作包含两部分：i) 对 LAMMPS 中计算 σ_{αβ}(q,t) 模块的优化实现——三角函数仅对基础波矢计算，且相关波矢以显式列表而非完整网格提供，从而只计算与存储感兴趣的波矢；ii) 一个代码验证协议（validation protocol），并在二维多分散 Lennard-Jones（2DpLJ）玻璃形成液体上加以示例说明。

Result: 结果表明，2DpLJ 模型的纵向仿射模量（longitudinal affine modulus）在对应结构因子（structure factor）主峰的 q_max 附近出现极小值；数据还显示，横向与纵向模式的振动频率（vibrational frequencies）在远低于 q_max 的 q 处存在峰值。

Conclusion: 所提出的优化实现与验证协议为非晶固体应力张量的波矢分辨计算提供了技术支持；仿射模量的 q 依赖行为以及低频振动频率峰值的发现，其与“玻色峰”（boson peak）的可能关联亦被讨论。

Abstract: The stress tensor resolved in wavevector $\qvec$ and time $t$, $σ_{αβ}(\qvec,t)$, gives access to the elastic moduli of amorphous solids as a function of the probed length scale, and to the long-range stress correlations that distinguish an amorphous solid from a liquid. Its evaluation requires the contribution of every interacting pair and therefore an interface to the force loop of the molecular dynamics code. In the current work, we present: i) an optimized implementation of the LAMMPS~\cite{lammps} module for calculating $σ_{αβ}(\qvec,t)$, in which trigonometric functions are calculated only for the basic wavevectors and, in addition, all the relevant wavevectors are supplied as an explicit list rather than generated as a full grid, so that only the wavevectors of interest are computed and stored, and ii) a validation protocol for the code illustrated for a two-dimensional polydisperse Lennard-Jones (2DpLJ) glass-forming liquid. Based on these technical achievements we elucidated how the affine moduli for the 2DpLJ model depend on the norm $q$ of the wavevector. In particular, we demonstrate that the longitudinal affine modulus shows a minimum near $q_\text{max}$ corresponding to the main peak of the structure factor. Our data also point to the peaks in the $q$-dependencies of vibrational frequencies well below $q_\text{max}$ for both transverse and longitudinal modes. The potential relevance of this feature to the ``boson peak'' is discussed as well.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Controlling transport with structured long-range hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33393)
*Boris Gurevich,Michael H. Kolodrubetz*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该摘要研究一维无序链中长程跃迁（long-range hopping）幅度取均匀或随机两类情形下对输运（transport）的影响，揭示均匀长程跃迁在弱无序区强烈压制退局域化（delocalization），而引入长程跃迁随性可消除该压制区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序（disorder）与长程跃迁的联合效应在低维量子系统的局域化（localization）与输运中作用重要，但二者相互作用的微观机制尚需厘清，尤其是此前报道的“无序增强输运”现象（disorder-enhanced transport）的来源不明。

Method: 构建一维无序链模型：在最近邻跃迁（nearest-neighbor hopping）基础上叠加长程跃迁项，长程耦合幅度可为均匀或随机的位点依赖形式；并在安德森本征态基（Anderson eigenstate basis）下给出解析解。

Result: 在均匀长程跃迁幅度下，最近邻跃迁在弱无序区间强烈压制退局域化；此前的“无序增强输运”区可理解为体系从该被压制态恢复退局域化的过程。解析解揭示了该行为的微观起源，且向长程跃迁幅度引入随机性可消除该低无序强压制区，构成增强输运并定性改变体系对无序响应的额外机制。

Conclusion: 长程跃迁幅度的均匀与随机性质是调控一维无序体系输运的关键：均匀情形产生弱无序下的输运压制，随机情形则消除该压制，从而提供一种不依赖降低无序强度、而通过改造长程耦合的无序化来增强输运的途径。

Abstract: The combined effects of disorder and long-range hopping play an important role in determining localization and transport in low-dimensional quantum systems. We study transport in a one-dimensional disordered chain with nearest-neighbor hopping supplemented by a long-range hopping term. The long-range coupling is characterized by site-dependent amplitudes that may be either uniform or random. We investigate the interplay between diagonal disorder and long-range hopping, characterizing the resulting transport efficiency. We find that nearest-neighbor hopping in the presence of uniform long-range hopping amplitudes strongly suppresses delocalization over a range of weak disorder. The disorder-enhanced transport regime, established in previous studies, can be understood here as the recovery of delocalization from this strongly suppressed regime. An analytical solution in the Anderson eigenstate basis reveals the microscopic origin of this behavior. In particular, we show that the low-disorder regime of strongly suppressed delocalization can be eliminated by introducing randomness into the long-range hopping amplitudes, providing an additional mechanism for enhancing transport and qualitatively changing the response of the system to disorder.

</details>


### [168] [Dynamic Pseudogap Model: Analytic Results vs Monte Carlo Numerics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33436)
*Michael V. Sadovskii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作将动态赝能隙模型（dynamic pseudogap model）的解析谱密度结果与蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo, MC）精确数值解进行系统对比，旨在检验解析近似的有效性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态赝能隙模型的解析结果依赖于若干近似（如对易近似下忽略Magnus型修正），其适用范围与准确性尚未被精确数值解系统检验，因此需要与MC精确解对照。

Method: 对受随机含时高斯场（具有限嵌套矢量Q、特征振荡频率ω₀与阻尼γ）散射的电子谱密度，分别在公度（实）与非公度（复）涨落、宽参数范围内，将双泊松级数（含历史依赖Bogoliubov能隙）与精确静态连分数（continued fraction）解两种解析近似同MC精确解比较。

Result: 相干区（coherent regime）双级数表示可描述谱密度中的动态边带（sideband）形成；随相干性丧失，理论过渡到由精确连分数解描述的准静态涨落能隙区。MC解弥补了解析方法忽略Magnus型修正等缺陷，界定了其适用极限，并确认连分数解在静态极限下是精确的。

Conclusion: 总体而言，解析表达式在其适用范围内与MC数值结果吻合较好，支持动态赝能隙形成的整体定性物理图像。

Abstract: Recently proposed dynamic pseudogap model analytical results for spectral density of an electron scattered by random time-dependent Gaussian field representing fluctuations with finite nesting vector ${\bf Q}$, characteristic oscillation frequency $ω_0$ and damping $γ$, leading to pseudogap formation, are compared with exact numerical solution by Monte Carlo (MC) approach. We consider both commensurate (real) and incommensurate (complex) fluctuations, for a wide range of parameters of the model. Electron Green's function is approximated either by a double Poisson series with history-dependent Bogoliubov gaps or by exact static continued fraction solution. In the coherent regime double-series representation provides description of dynamic sideband formation in the spectral density. As coherence is progressively lost, the theory crosses over to the quasistatic fluctuating-gap regime described by the exact continued-fraction solution. The exact MC solution overcomes the limitations of analytic approach, such as neglect of Magnus-type corrections in commuting approximation, and establish the limits of analytic approach, as well as confirming the exactness of continued fraction solution in static limit. In general, analytic expressions demonstrate rather satisfactory agreement with exact MC numerics within their limits, confirming the overall qualitative physical picture of dynamic pseudogap formation.

</details>


### [169] [Matter that learns what matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34428)
*Natalia G. Berloff*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文在模型光学网络（model optical network）中展示材料可通过动作结果在每条链路中训练持久记录，从而"学会"对信息来源的注意力分配；并推导出使相关信号引导答案、无关信号被拒斥、存储答案可修订的界限，用于设计学习规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注一个核心问题：材料能否自行判断哪些信息值得关注。传统方法需预先识别有用来源，本文希望网络通过自身经验塑造未来学习可用的信息，从而在不预先识别来源的情况下完成注意力分配。

Method: 构建模型光学网络，链路中的持久记录（persistent record）由动作结果训练，并偏置"是否查询某来源"及"该来源改变存储答案的强度"。通过共振读出（resonant readout）分析学习脆弱性，推导相关信号受引导、无关信号被拒斥及答案可修订的参数界限，据此设计在记录形成时限制强化（reinforcement）的学习规则；并引入局部状态（local state），在动作失败后加强负向写入（negative writing）。

Result: 共振读出使学习脆弱：强化可使记录越过共振点，记录虽持续存在但影响力减弱。所推导界限所指导的学习规则在仿真（simulation）中能获得有用记录并保持其功能；当相关性改变时，过时记录会阻碍自身被替换，而失败后加强负向写入的局部状态可释放过时记录，并更常完成学习序列。

Conclusion: 记忆的读取方式应决定其写入方式（“How a memory is read should decide how it is written”）：读出特性（如共振）约束了学习规则的设计，将写入门控在记录可行范围内是维持记录功能性、兼顾稳定性与可修订性的关键。

Abstract: Can a material learn what deserves its attention? We show that it can in a model optical network, in which action outcomes train a persistent record in each link. The record biases whether a source is consulted and sets how strongly it changes a stored answer, so useful sources gain attention and influence without being identified in advance. The network's experience thus shapes the information available for its future learning. Resonant readouts make this learning fragile: reinforcement can carry a record past resonance, where it persists but its influence weakens. We derive bounds within which relevant signals guide the answer, irrelevant signals are rejected and stored answers remain open to revision. These bounds guide a learning rule that limits reinforcement as records form; in simulations, it acquires useful records and keeps them functional. When relevance changes, an obsolete record can block its own replacement. A local state that strengthens negative writing after unsuccessful actions releases obsolete records and completes the learning sequence more often. How a memory is read should decide how it is written.

</details>


### [170] [Symmetry-driven in-situ physical adjoint computing beyond reciprocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34637)
*Cunshen Li,Rong Li,Jiongning Che,Cheng-Zhen Wang,Liang Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作提出对称性驱动的原位物理伴随计算（symmetry-driven in-situ physical adjoint computing, Sym-iPAC），通过三类转置对称性在原硬件上获取伴随响应，从而将原位伴随优化拓展至非互易（nonreciprocal）系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易性（nonreciprocity）可实现定向输运，丰富波控制与信息处理，但破坏了依赖互易性（reciprocity）获取伴随响应的传统原位物理伴随优化方法，因而需要新的机制在非互易体系中获得伴随梯度。

Method: 提出并验证三类转置对称性（transpose symmetry）：局部几何置换（local geometric permutations）、复规范重加权（complex gauge reweighting），以及由Schur约化（Schur reduction）揭示的有效端口动力学中的潜对称性（latent symmetry）。在可编程非互易微波网络（programmable nonreciprocal microwave networks）实验中验证这些转置关系并提取伴随梯度，实现闭环优化。

Result: 实验验证了上述转置关系，并获得伴随梯度（adjoint gradients），成功支撑闭环优化（closed-loop optimization）。

Conclusion: 实验上可实现的转置对称性可作为超越互易性、实现原位物理伴随计算的途径，为非互易系统中的波控制与信息处理优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Nonreciprocity enriches wave control and information processing through directional transport, yet challenges conventional in-situ physical adjoint optimization, which relies on reciprocity to acquire adjoint responses. Here we demonstrate symmetry-driven in-situ physical adjoint computing (Sym-iPAC) in nonreciprocal systems, which enables access to adjoint responses on the original hardware through three classes of transpose symmetry: local geometric permutations, complex gauge reweighting, and latent symmetry in effective port dynamics revealed by Schur reduction. Experiments with programmable nonreciprocal microwave networks validate these transpose relations and yield adjoint gradients, enabling closed-loop optimization. These results establish experimentally accessible transpose symmetry as a route to in-situ physical adjoint computing beyond reciprocity.

</details>

### [171] [Lunar tidal response-induced clock frequency variations on the Moon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33117)
*Hongbin Zhang,Baocheng Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 该研究采用开普勒展开方法（Keplerian expansions method）计算月球潮汐响应（lunar tidal response, LTR）引起的分数频率漂移（fractional frequency shift, FFS），量级达 $10^{-18}$，并给出月球附近时钟同步的偏差估计，为月球时间体系建设提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着时钟性能提升，其分数频率漂移的不稳定度与不确定度已与月球潮汐响应所致漂移相当，因此需研究潮汐响应对月球时间（lunar time）的影响。

Method: 采用开普勒展开方法，考虑月球沿椭圆轨道（elliptical orbit）运动这一更接近实际的情形，计算位于月球不同位置的两台时钟由潮汐响应引起的 $10^{-18}$ 量级分数频率漂移。

Result: 两台时钟之间的经度差（longitude difference）会改变潮汐响应所致 FFS 的振幅与相位，而纬度差（latitude difference）仅改变振幅；当一台时钟位于月球表面、另一台位于地球表面时，潮汐响应引起的钟差约为 $5.68\times 10^{-7}$ μs/day。

Conclusion: 月球潮汐响应对分数频率漂移及钟差的影响对月球时间构建与月球附近时钟同步（clock synchronization）具有显著意义。

Abstract: As clock performance improves, the instability and uncertainty of the clock's fractional frequency shift (FFS) become comparable to the FFS caused by the lunar tidal response (LTR), so the influence of the LTR on lunar time needs to be investigated. In this paper, we used the Keplerian expansions method to study the FFS to the level of $10^{-18}$ caused by the LTR between two clocks placed in different positions on the Moon. We consider the case of the Moon orbiting in an elliptical orbit, which is closer to the actual situation compared to the case of the Moon moving in a circular orbit. An interesting phenomenon is found that the longitude difference between two clocks can cause a change in the amplitude and phase of the FFS due to the LTR, but the latitude difference only changes the amplitude of the FFS. We further estimated the clock difference between two clocks near the Moon caused by the LTR. When one clock is on the lunar surface, and the other is on the Earth's surface, the LTR will cause a clock difference of $% -5.68\times 10^{-7}$ $μ$s/day between the two clocks. These are significant for the construction of a lunar time and clock synchronization near the Moon.

</details>
