### [172] [Electrically Reversible Layer-Tensor Responses in Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34067)
*Zehou Li,Shenda He,Pan Zhou,Baoru Pan,Rui Tan,Lizhong Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文提出层张量效应（layer tensor effect, LTE）的对称性选择定则，用Jahn符号统一刻画双层体系中由面外电场$E_z$选择性开关的隐藏层响应，涵盖霍尔、位移电流、非线性Drude、压电、自旋霍尔与自旋光电流等通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层霍尔效应（layer Hall effect）此前被视为范德华双层中隐藏层响应的典型例子；本文旨在论证该电场开关机制并非霍尔输运专属，而源于响应张量的一般对称性原理，从而为搜寻更多可电控的层分辨响应提供统一判据。

Method: 基于Jahn符号（Jahn symbol）建立层张量效应选择定则：对Jahn符号为$J$、阶为$r$的张量，交换双层的堆叠操作作为陪集代表元（coset representative）联系$+E_z$与$-E_z$态；在该操作下为奇的张量分量在$E_z=0$时被迫层间抵消，并满足$T_\alpha(+E_z)=-T_\alpha(-E_z)$。借此枚举对称性允许的电可逆张量分量，并结合模型计算与第一性原理模拟进行验证。

Result: 该判据可系统列出不同Jahn符号对应的对称性允许电可逆张量分量，并在反常霍尔、位移电流（shift-current）、非线性Drude、压电、自旋霍尔及自旋光电流等常见响应张量中识别其物理实现；模型计算与第一性原理模拟进一步证实了所预言的电场奇响应。

Conclusion: 研究确立了电可开关的层张量效应框架，将层霍尔效应纳入更普遍的对称性图景，为双层材料中的多功能层电子学（layertronics）响应提供统一路径。

Abstract: The layer Hall effect provides a prototypical example of a hidden layer response in van der Waals bilayers: opposite layers carry compensating Hall currents at zero out-of-plane electric field, while an applied out-of-plane electric field $E_z$ selects one layer over the other and switches the net Hall response. Here we show that this field-switching mechanism is not restricted to Hall transport, but follows from a general symmetry principle for response tensors. We formulate a selection rule for the layer tensor effect (LTE) using Jahn symbols. For a tensor with Jahn symbol $J$ and rank $r$, the stacking operation that exchanges the two layers acts as a coset representative relating the $+E_z$ and $-E_z$ states. Tensor components that are odd under this operation are forced to cancel between layers at $E_z=0$, and acquire a macroscopic response satisfying $T_α(+E_z)=-T_α(-E_z)$. This criterion allows us to enumerate the symmetry-allowed electrically reversible tensor components associated with different Jahn symbols and to identify their physical realizations in common response tensors. We apply the theory to representative anomalous Hall, shift-current, nonlinear Drude, piezoelectric, spin Hall, and spin-photocurrent channels. Model calculations and first-principles simulations further confirm the predicted field-odd responses. Our results establish an electrically switchable LTE framework, providing a unified route to multifunctional layertronic responses in bilayer materials.

</details>


### [173] [The Evolution and Current State of Swift-Heavy-Ion Track Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34353)
*N. Medvedev,A. E. Volkov*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文综述快重离子（swift heavy ion, SHI）径迹形成（track formation）的建模方法，逐步推导双温模型（two-temperature model, TTM）并检视其假设失效条件，进而介绍蒙特卡罗-分子动力学（Monte Carlo-molecular dynamics）联合模型及其验证，最后讨论多尺度方法（multiscale approach）的瓶颈与理论发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快重离子径迹形成涉及电子激发与晶格响应的强耦合、多尺度过程，现有理论模型（尤其是解析近似）在典型径迹条件下适用性存疑，需明确其假设边界并寻求超越途径，故有必要系统梳理建模方法并识别其薄弱环节。

Method: 采用综述与推导相结合的方式：逐步给出双温模型的推导，明确其基本假设与近似；重点识别在典型 SHI 径迹条件下失效的假设，并归纳现有的超越方法；作为解析近似的替代，展示蒙特卡罗-分子动力学联合模型，说明其基本假设、合理性依据与验证情况。

Result: 指出双温模型中部分假设在典型 SHI 径迹条件下不再成立，并总结了相应的改进途径；表明蒙特卡罗-分子动力学联合模型可作为解析近似之外的替代方案，且已被给出基本假设依据与验证。

Conclusion: 当前多尺度方法仍存在瓶颈与不足，需要理论与模型的进一步发展；本文通过厘清假设失效点与替代建模路径，为后续模型改进提供方向。

Abstract: This paper briefly reviews the modeling approaches to the swift heavy ion (SHI) track formation. It presents a step-by-step derivation of the two-temperature model, highlighting its underlying assumptions and approximations. Special attention is given to identifying assumptions that fail under the typical SHI track conditions, and what methods currently exist to go beyond them. As an alternative to analytical approximations, a combined Monte Carlo- molecular dynamics model is demonstrated, outlining its basic assumptions, their justifications, and validations. Finally, the paper addresses bottlenecks of the current multiscale approach, its remaining weaknesses, and necessary further developments of the theory and models.

</details>


### [174] [Equivalent Pseudo-angular Momentum Governs Selective Magnon-Phonon Hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34857)
*Hong Sun,Hao Chen,Lifa Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文提出晶体赝角动量（pseudoangular momentum）可作为非共线多子格磁性体系中磁振子-声子选择性杂化的通用判据，并在堆叠kagome模型中验证了该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子-声子杂化（magnon-phonon hybridization）是耦合自旋与晶格激发的有效途径，但决定模式选择性的对称性原理尚不明确，需要建立统一判据。

Method: 引入晶体赝角动量作为选择定则，其旋转本征值编码极化变换、子格置换与Bloch相位，要求磁弹耦合（magnetoelastic coupling）只发生在同一旋转扇区内的模式之间；并在具有非共线自旋序的堆叠kagome模型中加以说明。

Result: 面外磁场将一个磁振子扇区调至与简并声子二重态共振时，仅赝角动量匹配的声子出现显著反交叉（avoided crossing），另一支几乎不混合；磁场反转切换所选择的旋转扇区并反转杂化声子分量的旋转极化，而杂化模式能量不变。

Conclusion: 赝角动量匹配构成理解和调控模式选择性自旋-晶格杂化的通用框架。

Abstract: Magnon-phonon hybridization provides a powerful route to couple spin and lattice excitations, yet the symmetry principle governing mode selectivity remains unclear. Here, we show that crystalline pseudoangular momentum provides a general criterion for selective magnon-phonon hybridization in noncollinear multisublattice magnetic systems. The rotation eigenvalue associated with pseudoangular momentum encodes polarization transformations, sublattice permutations, and Bloch phases, requiring magnetoelastic coupling between modes belonging to the same rotation sector. We illustrate this principle in a stacked kagome model with noncollinear spin order. An out-of-plane magnetic field tunes one magnon sector into resonance with a degenerate phonon doublet, yet only the pseudoangular-momentum-matched phonon develops a pronounced avoided crossing while the other remains nearly unmixed. Field reversal switches the selected rotation sector and reverses the rotational polarization of the hybridized phonon component without changing the hybrid-mode energies. Our results reveal pseudoangular-momentum matching as a general framework for understanding and controlling mode-selective spin-lattice hybridization.

</details>


### [175] [Giant third-order nonlinearity amplifies second-harmonic transport in Ca3Ru2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35614)
*Subin Mali,Yufei Zhao,Yu Wang,Binghai Yan,Zhiqiang Mao*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作利用关联极性金属 Ca3Ru2O7 的巨三阶非线性，通过直流偏置叠加交流激励实现对二次谐波输运的放大与电学调控，并给出非线性电导的量子几何起源解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性电输运可用于电磁信号的转换与探测，服务于无线能量收集、传感与频率转换，但同时实现大响应与电学可调性仍是核心材料挑战。

Method: 在 Ca3Ru2O7 中，于交流激励上叠加直流偏置（dc bias），测量二次谐波（second-harmonic）响应随偏置、交流幅值与角度的依赖关系，并结合理论计算分析非线性电导来源。

Result: 直流偏置使二次谐波信号增强逾一个数量级，并可调控响应极性、激活零偏压下对称性禁止的通道；横向与纵向分量均随交流激励二次方增长且对直流偏置呈奇函数，角依赖符合三阶电导；三阶电场响应度达 4.5×10⁻⁶ m²V⁻²（3 K）。响应在约 48 K 自旋重取向转变（spin-reorientation transition）以下出现，理论计算识别出量子几何（quantum-geometric）对非线性电导的贡献并再现了观测的响应对称性。

Conclusion: 强高阶非线性可放大等效的二阶响应，为关联材料（correlated materials）中电学可调非线性输运提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Nonlinear electrical transport enables the conversion and detection of electromagnetic signals, supporting wireless energy harvesting, sensing and frequency conversion. Achieving both a large response and electrical tunability remains a central materials challenge. Here we harness giant third-order nonlinearity to amplify and electrically control second-harmonic transport in the correlated polar metal Ca3Ru2O7. Superposing a dc bias on an ac excitation enhances the second-harmonic signal by more than an order of magnitude. The bias controls the polarity of the induced response and activates channels forbidden by symmetry at zero bias. Both transverse and longitudinal components scale quadratically with the ac excitation and are odd in the dc bias, with angular dependences consistent with third-order conductivity. The underlying third-harmonic electric-field responsivity reaches 4.5 \times 10-6 m2V-2 at 3 K, providing a quantitative measure of the strong nonlinearity. The induced response emerges below the spin-reorientation transition near 48 K, linking its enhancement to the reconstructed low-temperature electronic state. Theoretical calculations identify quantum-geometric contributions to the nonlinear conductivity and reproduce the observed response symmetry. These results demonstrate how strong higher-order nonlinearity can amplify an effective second-order response, providing a route to electrically tunable nonlinear transport in correlated materials.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Spectral zeta functions in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35306)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文在 $\mathbb F_q((\pi))$ 代数闭包的完备化 $\mathbb C_\infty$ 上，针对特征 $p$ 造成的两个障碍（复幂指数仅在 $\mathbb Z_p$ 中连续变化、标量迹只能记录模 $p$ 的重数）分别建立基于预解式（resolvent）的理论，从而重构谱 zeta 函数与 zeta 正则化行列式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典谱 zeta 函数与 zeta 正则化行列式依赖指数解析变化与特征值重数的精确记录，而在特征 $p$ 的 $\mathbb C_\infty$ 上这两点均失效，因此需要一套分别处理两种障碍的分析框架。

Method: 对第一个障碍，采用逆 Stieltjes 对偶（inverse Stieltjes duality）把标量预解式转化为 $\mathbb F_q[[\pi]]$ 上的局部解析谱分布，并用 Carlitz 矩的增长条件刻画可实现该变换的幂级数；对第二个障碍，用 Frobenius 缺陷（Frobenius defect）$F/\sigma(F)$ 替代迹，证明其在系数较小的幂级数上是保持 Gauss 距离的群自同构，并可由收敛无穷乘积求逆。

Result: 单个 Mellin 变换指数即可确定谱分布及其整个 Mellin 族；对平稳数字测度，该变换是显式压缩映射的唯一不动点，且前 $q-1$ 个非平凡矩可恢复测度。多项式缺陷给出整函数，并精确刻画了按重数计为前向 Frobenius 轨道有限并的对角谱；对平移 Carlitz 算子，所得 Fredholm 行列式为 Anderson–Thakur 函数的标准化倒数，其 Newton 多边形被精确确定。

Conclusion: 特征 $p$ 下的谱 zeta 理论可通过分离处理“指数不可解析变化”与“迹模 $p$ 丢失重数”两个障碍来重建，并由此获得测度恢复、对角谱刻画及 Fredholm 行列式的显式结果。

Abstract: Spectral zeta functions and zeta-regularized determinants encode the spectrum of an operator in analytic functions. Over the completion $\mathbb C_\infty$ of an algebraic closure of $\mathbb F_q((π))$, two features of characteristic $p$ break this classical picture. Complex powers have exponents that vary only continuously in $\mathbb Z_p$, not analytically. Scalar traces record eigenvalue multiplicities only modulo $p$. We develop a resolvent-based theory that treats these two obstructions separately.
  For the first, inverse Stieltjes duality turns suitable scalar resolvents into locally analytic spectral distributions on $\mathbb F_q[[π]]$. A single exponent of the Mellin transform already determines the distribution and the entire Mellin family. We characterize exactly which power series arise in this way, by a growth condition on Carlitz moments. The criterion depends on the support: bounded Taylor coefficients are not sufficient. For stationary digit measures, the transform is the unique fixed point of an explicit contraction. The first $q-1$ nonconstant moments recover the measure.
  For the second, we replace traces by the Frobenius defect $F/σ(F)$, where $σ$ raises coefficients to their $q$th powers. On power series with small coefficients, this map is a group automorphism that preserves Gauss distances, and a convergent infinite product inverts it. Polynomial defects produce entire functions. They characterize exactly the diagonal spectra that are finite unions of forward Frobenius orbits, counted with multiplicity. For the shifted Carlitz operator, the resulting Fredholm determinant is a normalized reciprocal of the Anderson--Thakur function, and its Newton polygon is determined exactly.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Magnetic billiards:duality and magnetic Radon property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33782)
*Michael,Bialy*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究弱恒定磁场下凸平面区域内的Birkhoff磁台球（Birkhoff magnetic billiards），证明存在一族中心对称的凸台球桌满足“磁Radon性质”（magnetic Radon property），并证明具有离散旋转对称的磁台球桌中k-周期轨道不变曲线存在当且仅当台球桌为圆形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对磁台球中类似经典Radon性质的周期轨道结构问题，探究哪些凸台球桌存在由4-周期轨道构成、旋转数为1/4或3/4的旋转不变曲线，并厘清该现象与对称性之间的界限。

Method: 不采用扰动方法，而是对刻画磁Radon性质的常微分方程组（system of ODEs）使用拓扑论证来构造台球桌；同时对具有角度为2π/k离散旋转对称的磁台球桌进行对比分析；并引入“磁对偶”（magnetic duality）工具及外磁台球（outer magnetic billiard）的标准与非标准生成函数（generating functions）。

Result: 证明了存在函数族式的中心对称凸台球桌满足磁Radon性质，且可在圆附近构造；相反地，对具有2π/k离散旋转对称的磁台球桌，此类由k-周期轨道构成的旋转不变曲线存在当且仅当台球桌为圆形。

Conclusion: 磁Radon性质可在圆附近的中心对称凸区域中非微扰地实现，而离散旋转对称下的周期轨道不变曲线则严格约束为圆形情形；所发展的磁对偶与外磁台球生成函数工具对磁台球的其他研究方面具有潜在价值。

Abstract: We study Birkhoff magnetic billiards inside convex planar domains for a constant weak magnetic field.
  We prove that there exists a functional family of centrally-symmetric convex billiard tables satisfying the so-called magnetic Radon property.
  These are tables which have a rotational invariant curves consisting of $4$-periodic orbits with the rotation numbers either $1/4$ or $3/4$. These billiard tables can be constructed in a neighborhood of circles. However in our construction we do not use perturbation methods but rather some topological argument for the system of ODEs governing the magnetic Radon property.
  In contrast with this result we consider the magnetic billiard tables having discrete rotational symmetry for the angle $2π/k, k\in\mathbb{Z}$. We prove that in this case a rotational invariant curve consisting of $k$-periodic orbits exists, if and only if the billiard table is circular.
  We develop several tools, which, we believe, are important for other aspects of magnetic billiards. The first is the "magnetic duality" which enables to work with the dual object---the outer magnetic billiard.
  Secondly, we find the generating functions of outer magnetic billiard, both the ''standard" and the "non-standard" ones.

</details>


### [178] [Local Koopman modes of saddle-point diffusions: exact singular functions and a data-driven estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35627)
*Xuehao Ding*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文为鞍点（saddle）附近的相干集（coherent sets）建立了局部解析理论：以非逃逸条件化（Doob's $h$-transform）修复朴素构造的病态，给出闭式慢模与数据驱动估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干集通常只能作全局数值计算，固定点附近缺乏局部解析理论；而固定点及其间跃迁恰恰组织流场，鞍点作为盆地间概率流动的过渡态尤需理论刻画。

Method: 对非逃逸事件作 Doob $h$-变换条件化，证明所需 $h$ 为代数 Riccati 方程（algebraic Riccati equation）的唯一镇定解；在典范坐标下以 Hermite 多项式（Hermite polynomials）与 $h$ 函数之积给出慢模闭式，并利用其酉结构导出条件轨迹上的典型相关分析（canonical correlation analysis）估计量。

Result: 条件化后不变测度可归一化、奇异值不超过一、奇异函数回到 $L^2$；所得慢模沿不稳定流形以逃逸率衰减；主导不稳定方向占优时，首模即反应坐标（reaction coordinate），局部复原稳定流形与 committor。

Conclusion: 该构造将相干集与过渡路径理论（transition-path theory）、线性控制的镇定 Riccati 解以及生成式扩散模型（generative diffusion models）的时间反演动力学联系起来。

Abstract: Coherent sets-regions of phase space that resist mixing-organize transport in stochastic dynamical systems and are encoded in the leading singular functions of the stochastic Koopman operator. These are typically computed numerically and globally; a local, analytic theory near a fixed point has been missing, although fixed points and the transitions between them organize the flow. We supply the local theory for a saddle, the transition state through which probability flows between basins. Naively the construction fails-probability escapes along unstable directions, the invariant measure is non-normalizable, the singular functions leave $L^2$, and singular values exceed one. We show that conditioning on non-escape (a Doob's $h$-transform) repairs this; that the required $h$ is the unique stabilizing solution of an algebraic Riccati equation; and that the resulting slow modes decay at the escape rate along the unstable manifold. We obtain these modes in closed form-products of Hermite polynomials in canonical coordinates times the $h$-function-and use their unitary structure to derive a data-driven estimator: canonical correlation analysis on conditioned trajectories. When a single unstable direction dominates, the leading mode is the reaction coordinate, locally recovering the stable manifold and committor of transition-path theory. The construction connects coherent sets to transition-path theory, to the stabilizing Riccati solution of linear control, and to the time-reversed dynamics of generative diffusion models.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.GA'></div>

# astro-ph.GA [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [The Hosts and Mass Distribution Function of the Most Massive Black Holes: Implications for the nHz Gravitational Wave Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33162)
*Marc Postman,Tod R. Lauer,Daniel J. D'Orazio,Bellamy Bailey*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用最亮团星系（BCG）核心半径 r_γ 与星系中心核的体积受限样本，构建 z=0 时最大质量超大质量黑洞（SMBH）的质量分布函数，并用于解释纳赫兹引力波背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测上缺乏对最大质量超大质量黑洞（SMBH）在 z=0 处质量分布函数（MDF）的直接约束，需要借助可测的星系核心结构量来间接推断。

Method: 采用星系中心核半径 r_γ 与体积受限的最亮团星系（BCG）样本，标定 M_BH–r_γ 关系；基于 HST 与 DLS 图像导出核心分布函数（CDF），再与 M_BH–r_γ 关系结合得到黑洞质量分布函数。

Result: M_BH ≈ 1.3×10^10 (r_γ/kpc) M_⊙，σ=0.25±0.03 dex；r_γ>0.50 kpc 时 CDF 陡降为 N(r_γ)~r_γ^-3，仅约5%的 BCG 有 r_γ>1 kpc。对 M_BH ≥ 7×10^9 M_⊙ 得到 log[dN/(dM_BH/10^10 M_⊙)] = (-3.05±0.42) log(M_BH/10^10 M_⊙) - 5.11 (+0.41, -0.36)。

Conclusion: r_γ≥0.50 kpc 的核几乎仅存在于 BCG 中，提示 M_BH ≥ 7×10^9 M_⊙ 的 SMBH 可能也为 BCG 所独有；r_γ 与刻画并合增长包层的参数 α 相关，说明最大质量 SMBH 与其宿主共同增长。该 MDF 含有大量 M_BH>7×10^9 M_⊙ 的源，与 Liepold & Ma (2024) 的 MDF 结合后可自洽解释纳赫兹引力波背景。

Abstract: We use the core-radius, $r_γ,$ of a galaxy's central core along with a volume-limited sample of brightest cluster galaxies (BCG) to determine the $z=0$ mass distribution function of the most massive super-massive black holes (SMBH). We recover a $M_{BH}-r_γ$ relationship between the SMBH mass, $M_{BH},$ and $r_γ$ of form $M_{BH} \approx1.3\times10^{10}~(r_γ/{\mathrm{kpc}})~M_\odot,$ with $σ= 0.25\pm0.03$ dex scatter in $M_{BH}.$ We derive a core distribution function (CDF), based on HST and DLS images of the BCGs. The CDF falls steeply as $N(r_γ)\sim r_γ^{-3}$ for $r_γ>0.50$ kpc. Only $\sim5\%$ of BCGs have $r_γ>1$ kpc. Combining the CDF and $M_{BH} - r_γ$ relationship yields the BH mass distribution function (MDF). For $M_{BH} \geq 7\times10^{9}~\mathrm{M_\odot}$ (the mean $M_{BH}$ at $r_γ=0.50$ kpc) we derive: $\log\left(\frac{dN(M_{BH})}{d{M_{BH}/10^{10} M_\odot}}~[\rm Mpc^{-3}]\right)=(-3.05\pm0.42)~\log\left(\frac{M_{BH}}{10^{10}M_\odot}\right)~-~5.11\ (+0.41, -0.36)$. Cores with $r_γ\geq 0.50$ kpc are found almost exclusively in BCGs, implying that SMBHs with $M_{BH} \geq7\times10^{9}~\mathrm{M_\odot}$ may also be exclusive to BCGs. Core-radius correlates with $α,$ a parameter that characterizes the growth of BCG envelopes by mergers, suggesting that the most massive SMBHs grew in tandem with their BCG hosts. The BH MDF embodies a significant population of SMBH with $M_{BH} >7\times10^{9} M_\odot.$ When combined with the Liepold & Ma (2024) BH MDF to account for SMBH with $M_{BH} < 7\times10^{9} M_\odot,$ it can comfortably explain the nHz gravitational wave background.

</details>

### [180] [$Δ(1232)$ isobars and neutrino opacities in hot dense matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35156)
*Subha Mitra,Bharat Kumar*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文计算了含 Δ(1232) 同质异能态的热、中微子囚禁物质中电子中微子与反中微子的扩散平均自由程，发现三体反中微子吸收过程在特定化学势条件下主导不透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原中子星（proto-neutron star）与中子星并合遗迹中物质处于热且中微子囚禁状态，Δ(1232) 同质异能态的存在可能显著改变中微子输运，而已有研究对相关反应（尤其三体吸收与弱π产生顶点）考虑不足，需定量评估其影响。

Method: 采用相对论平均场（relativistic mean-field）运动学，在物质中纳入带电流吸收（含弱π产生的 NΔ 弱顶点）、所有重子上的中性流散射，以及电子—重子对上的三体反中微子吸收，据此计算电子中微子与反中微子的扩散平均自由程。

Result: 反应 ν̄e+e−+n→Δ− 在中子与电子化学势超过介质内 Δ− 静止能量时主导反中微子不透明度，即使 Δ 粒子丰度可忽略；在二至四倍饱和密度下，该反应使反中微子平均自由程由核子尺度缩短至厘米量级，仅在低轻子分数时改变轻子数扩散（该密度下最多约数十个百分点，1.5 倍饱和密度处达一因子）。对电子中微子，Δ− 吸收及低轻子分数下核子吸收开启使平均自由程缩短，轻子分数 0.3 时最多缩短至一定倍数，0.1 时约数倍。此外，给定轴向耦合时，Δ 的自旋使其每粒子 Gamow–Teller 强度由 3 降为 5/3。

Conclusion: Δ(1232) 相关的带电流与三体吸收过程可显著缩短中微子平均自由程并影响低轻子分数下的轻子数扩散，应在原中子星与并合遗迹的中微子输运建模中予以纳入；同时 Δ 自旋导致每粒子 Gamow–Teller 强度的重新标定。

Abstract: We calculated the diffusion mean free paths of electron neutrinos and antineutrinos in hot, neutrino-trapped matter containing $Δ(1232)$ isobars, as found in proto-neutron stars and neutron star merger remnants. We included charged-current absorption with the $NΔ$ weak vertex of weak pion production, neutral-current scattering on all baryons and three-body antineutrino absorption on electron--baryon pairs, with relativistic mean-field kinematics. The reaction $\barν_e+e^-+n\toΔ^-$ dominates the antineutrino opacity whenever the neutron and electron chemical potentials exceed the in-medium $Δ^-$ rest energy, even where the $Δ$ population is negligible. At two to four times saturation density, where the reaction is open, it shortens the antineutrino mean free path from \nubarLamNucMin--\nubarLamNucMax~m to \nubarLamDMin--\nubarLamDMax~cm. Because antineutrinos are scarce at high lepton fraction, this changes lepton-number diffusion only at low lepton fraction, by up to \DtwoRedHi\% at these densities and by a factor of \DtwoRedLow\ at 1.5 times saturation density. For electron neutrinos, absorption on the $Δ^-$ and, at low lepton fraction, the opening of nucleonic absorption shorten the mean free path by factors of up to \nuRatioYLthreeMax\ at lepton fraction 0.3 and up to about \nuRatioYLoneMax\ at lepton fraction 0.1. We also showed that, for a given axial coupling, the spin of the $Δ$ reduces its Gamow--Teller strength per particle from 3 to $5/3$.

</details>


### [181] [Ubiquity of ocular resonances in scattering phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33319)
*Cullen Gantenberg,David B. Kaplan*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 量子散射（quantum scattering）在具有特征势面（characteristic surface）的势场中，会于复动量平面（complex momentum plane）生成一族聚集在反斯托克斯线（anti-Stokes lines）构成的近眼形曲线（eye-shaped curve）附近的共振。短程奇异性强于 $1/r^2$ 的势中，该势面对应捕获与偏转经典轨迹的分界线（separatrix），其共振与低能核子散射及经典衍射中的爬行波（creeping waves）、Regge 极点（Regge poles）相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出此类共振近期被证明在低能核子散射（low energy nucleon scattering）中起重要作用，因而需要系统刻画其特征，并厘清其与经典衍射散射中已有概念的联系。

Method: 以势场中的量子力学散射（quantum mechanical scattering）为出发点，分析复动量平面中共振的分布结构，用反斯托克斯线刻画其聚集曲线；对短程奇异性超过 $1/r^2$ 的势，将散射面识别为经典轨迹捕获与偏转的分界线，并通过 Regge 极点方法进行分析。

Result: 共振族在复动量平面中聚集于以原点为中心、大致呈眼形并由反斯托克斯线形成的曲线附近；短程强奇异势的散射面对应捕获-偏转经典分界线，相应共振可关联到经典衍射的爬行波与 Regge 极点描述。

Conclusion: 具有特征势面的势普遍产生一类结构化的共振族，其几何由反斯托克斯线决定；该图像统一了低能核子散射中的相关现象与经典衍射的爬行波、Regge 极点框架。

Abstract: We demonstrate how quantum mechanical scattering off a potential with a characteristic surface generically leads to a family of resonances in the complex momentum plane clustered near a roughly eye-shaped curve centered on the origin and formed by anti-Stokes lines. A particularly interesting class are potentials that are more singular than $1/r^2$ at short distance, where the scattering surface is the separatrix between captured and deflected classical trajectories. These resonances have been recently shown to play an important role in low energy nucleon scattering, and can be related to ``creeping waves'' familiar from classical diffractive scattering and analyzed by means of Regge poles.

</details>

### [182] [Classifying Leibniz racks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35534)
*Mohamed Elhamdadi,Bryce Virgin*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 论文研究一类非线性泛函微分方程组，其解为在给定光滑流形上分类光滑李群族光滑rack结构提供途径，并给出实用拟设构造新例子，同时证明Bloch球面在某种意义下是球面上唯一的李quandle结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 李quandle（Lie quandle）由T. Fritz定义为李代数（Lie algebra）的非线性推广，用作广义哈密顿力学（Hamiltonian mechanics）中的非线性可观测量代数；作者此前的工作又将其推广为光滑李群族光滑rack。本文旨在推进此类结构的分类问题。

Method: 研究一类特定的非线性泛函微分方程组（nonlinear functional differential equations），其解一般地给出在任意给定光滑流形（smooth manifold）上分类光滑李群族光滑rack结构的路径；并讨论一种特别实用的拟设（ansatz）来求解这些方程。

Result: 利用该拟设构造出新的结构族例子，并证明关于Bloch球面（Bloch sphere）是球面上唯一李quandle结构这一结论在特定意义下成立。

Conclusion: 所提出的泛函微分方程框架为光滑李群族光滑rack结构的分类提供了可行途径，实用拟设可有效生成新例子，并支撑球面上李quandle结构唯一性（以Bloch球面为代表）的结论。

Abstract: Lie quandles were defined by T. Fritz as a nonlinear generalization of Lie algebras, to serve as a nonlinear algebras of observables in generalized form of Hamiltonian mechanics. These objects were subsequently generalized in a prequel article to objects referred to as smooth Lie group families of smooth racks by the authors. In this article, we investigate a particular system of nonlinear functional differential equations whose solution in general provides a pathway to the classification of smooth Lie group families of smooth rack structures on any given smooth manifold. We then discuss a particularly practical ansatz for solving these equations, and use it to construct new families of examples, as well as prove a result on the sense in which the Bloch sphere is the unique Lie quandle structure on the sphere.

</details>

### [183] [Quantum Reversibility Meets Classical Reverse Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18512)
*Ryota Nasu,Gota Tanaka,Asato Tsuchiya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：对Petz映射对应的Lindblad方程作半经典近似，所得方程与经由贝叶斯法则从Fokker-Planck方程得到的反向扩散方程一致，从而在量子可逆性与经典反向扩散之间建立直接对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典概率论中贝叶斯法则（Bayes' rule）连接正向与反向过程，其量子类比以Petz（转置）映射表述；而现代扩散生成模型依赖由贝叶斯法则导出的反向扩散方程。二者之间是否存在统一联系尚待明确。

Method: 从Lindblad方程出发，取其对应Petz映射的Lindblad形式；利用半经典近似（semiclassical approximation）将Lindblad方程化为Wigner函数（相空间上的准概率分布）的Fokker-Planck方程，并对Petz映射对应的Lindblad方程施加同一近似。

Result: 对Petz映射相关Lindblad方程作半经典近似所得方程，与通过贝叶斯法则从Fokker-Planck方程得到的方程完全一致。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了Petz映射与贝叶斯法则之间的直接对应，将量子可逆性（quantum reversibility）与经典反向扩散（classical reverse diffusion）统一起来。

Abstract: Bayes' rule connects forward and reverse processes in classical probability theory, and its quantum analogue has been discussed in terms of the Petz (transpose) map. For quantum dynamics governed by the Lindblad equation, the corresponding Petz map can also be written in Lindblad form. In classical stochastic systems, the analogue of the Lindblad equation is the Fokker-Planck equation, and applying Bayes' rule to it yields the reverse diffusion equation underlying modern diffusion-based generative models. It is known that a semiclassical approximation of the Lindblad equation yields the Fokker-Planck equation for the Wigner function, which is a quasiprobability distribution defined on phase space as the Wigner transform of the density operator. Here we demonstrate that applying the same approximation to the Lindblad equation associated with the Petz map produces an equation that coincides with that obtained from the Fokker-Planck equation via Bayes' rule. This finding establishes a direct correspondence between the Petz map and Bayes' rule, unifying quantum reversibility with classical reverse diffusion.

</details>


### [184] [Direct tests of conformal symmetry with Rydberg atoms: assessment and proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33156)
*Junchen Rong,Slava Rychkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出利用里德堡原子阵列（Rydberg-atom arrays）直接检验共形对称性（conformal symmetry），并通过优化绝热制备脉冲实现高效率目标临界态制备，论证当前技术条件下实验可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形场论（conformal field theory）预言的特征关联函数形式缺乏直接的实验检验手段，本文旨在设计可在现有里德堡原子平台上实施的直接检验方案。

Method: 将原子排布于圆环上并测量等时两点关联函数（equal-time two-point correlation functions），直接比对共形场论预言的函数形式；同时估算达到统计精度所需的实验快照（snapshots）数目，并优化拉比频率（Rabi frequency）Ω与失谐（detuning）Δ的时间依赖爬升曲线以实现绝热制备目标临界态。

Result: 优化后的脉冲曲线在远短于此前实验所用演化时间的条件下达到更高的保真度（fidelity），并给出了分辨共形行为所需的实验快照数量估计。

Conclusion: 综合结果表明，基于当前里德堡原子技术，对共形对称性进行直接实验检验是可行的。

Abstract: We propose a direct test of conformal symmetry using Rydberg-atom arrays. By arranging the atoms on a circle and measuring equal-time two-point correlation functions, the characteristic functional form predicted by conformal field theory can be tested directly. We estimate the number of experimental snapshots required to resolve this behavior with sufficient statistical precision. We further optimize the time-dependent ramp profile of the Rabi frequency~$Ω$ and detuning~$Δ$ used to prepare the target critical state adiabatically. The optimized profile achieves higher fidelity over much shorter evolution times than those employed in previous experiments. Taken together, these results suggest that a direct experimental test of conformal symmetry is feasible with current Rydberg-atom technology.

</details>


### [185] [The diffusive spectral gap of the qubit eSWAP chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05152)
*Yanying Liang,Haoran Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明开放量子比特 eSWAP 链（open qubit eSWAP chain）的二阶矩哈密顿量（second-moment Hamiltonian）具有扩散谱隙（diffusive spectral gap），并给出若干相关谱性质与电路推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在解决 Li、Zheng、Liu、Jiang 与 Liu 提出的关于开放 eSWAP 链二阶矩哈密顿量谱隙行为的猜想，并扩展对相关相互作用图上基空间与谱隙的理解。

Method: 方法为解析证明：刻画任意至少五个顶点的连通相互作用图（connected interaction graph）上的基空间（ground space），并证明未归一化完全图谱隙（unnormalised complete-graph gap）的一致下界；随后将结果应用于顺序电路（sequential circuits）与砖墙电路（brickwork circuits）。

Result: 主要结果为开放 eSWAP 链二阶矩哈密顿量具有扩散谱隙，从而证实上述猜想；同时得到连通图基空间刻画、完全图谱隙的一致下界，并证明周期链（periodic chain）亦具有扩散谱隙。

Conclusion: 研究表明扩散谱隙性质在开放链、周期链及相关电路模型中成立，为 eSWAP 类模型的谱隙猜想提供了肯定性解答。

Abstract: We prove that the second-moment Hamiltonian of the open qubit eSWAP chain has a diffusive spectral gap, resolving a conjecture of Li, Zheng, Liu, Jiang, and Liu. We also characterise the ground space on every connected interaction graph with at least five vertices and prove a uniform lower bound for the unnormalised complete-graph gap. We apply these results to sequential and brickwork circuits and prove that the periodic chain also has a diffusive spectral gap.

</details>


### [186] [A new analytical finding for the Born-Infeld integral potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33326)
*Leonardo Paulo Guimarães De Assis,Priscila Valdênia dos Santos,Sergio de Oliveira Vellozo,José Abdala Helayël Neto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文指出Born-Infeld势（Born-Infeld potential）的积分形式可近似精确地表示为库仑型势（Coulomb-type potential）与汤川型势（Yukawa-type potential）之差，并据此提出覆盖全空间的新势函数U(r)，用于类氢原子（hydrogen-like atoms）一阶能移计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短距离下Born-Infeld势的物理含义不清晰，需要一种能给出直观物理解释并适用于全空间范围的表述形式。

Method: 利用Born-Infeld势积分形式与库仑型势和汤川型势之差的近似等价关系，将其解释为经典真空极化（classical vacuum polarization）与有效质量（effective mass）效应；随后提出覆盖整个空间的新U(r)，并以闭合形式计算类氢原子的一阶能移（first-order energy shifts）。

Result: 将计算出的一阶能移与高精度光谱数据（high-precision spectroscopic data）比较，得到最大场强参数b的模型依赖下界（model-dependent lower bound）。

Conclusion: Born-Infeld势可用库仑–汤川差形式作直观物理解释，所得U(r)可用于原子能移计算，并借助光谱数据约束最大场强参数b。

Abstract: A new finding regarding the Born-Infeld potential for short range distances from the charge revealed that its integral form can be almost exact written as the difference between a Coulomb-type potential and a Yukawa-type potential. This allowed for an immediate physical interpretation in terms of classical vacuum polarization and an effective mass. Subsequently, a new U(r) is proposed to cover the entire space. This formulation is then applied to calculate, in a closed manner, the first-order energy shifts in hydrogen-like atoms; a comparison of these shifts with high-precision spectroscopic data provides a model-dependent lower bound for the maximum field intensity parameter b.

</details>


### [187] [Krylov geometry and optimal recovery under uncertain coherent noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33607)
*Abhishek Chowdhury*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对"未观测角度的相干旋转（coherent rotation）"的恢复问题，在有限维生成元与单次最终恢复设定下，给出编码方式与校准信息对噪声认知的联合极限：两个多项式投影分别刻画可纠正误差幅度与状态依赖失真，并在弱噪声下导出精确的最优纠缠不保真度（entanglement infidelity）及可达恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从未知概率分布的相干旋转中恢复，既取决于编码，也取决于校准（calibration）揭示了噪声的哪些信息；需要确定二者结合后可达到的恢复精度极限，并刻画校准数据不足（如仅知低阶矩、仅有少数校准时长）时的代价。

Method: 对已知有限维生成元（generator）与一次最终恢复，构造两个多项式投影：物理 Krylov 链（Krylov chain）分离可纠正误差幅度与其首个状态依赖失真，向低角度幂的投影识别相干解码器可消除的部分。在显式秩与矩条件（rank and moment conditions）下求得精确领头阶最优不保真度；角度分布未知时对与校准相容的所有分布优化单一解码器，并借助矩正定性（moment positivity）与普适矩问题（universal moment problem）处理频率公倍情形。

Result: 弱噪声下得到任意有限逻辑维度可达到的精确领头阶最优纠缠不保真度，最优最坏输入误差在领头阶介于该不保真度的 1 倍与 d 倍之间（d 为逻辑维度）。若唯一校准数据为 β_r=E[sin^{2r}(θ/2)]，最优最坏先验不保真度为 O(β_r) 当且仅当生成元幂至 r−1 阶可纠正；首个不可纠正幂为 m<r 时损失按 β_r^{m/r} 缩放，系数由普适矩问题确定。无损耗四光子存储器中，两个校准时长的布居测量可保留完全信息下的四阶误差系数，单一时长仅给出噪声宽度二次的损耗。

Conclusion: 编码结构与校准信息共同决定相干旋转恢复的最优精度；多项式投影框架给出可纠正性的阶数判据与最优误差的精确标度律，并表明校准时长/矩数据的数量直接决定可达误差阶数，为量子存储器等场景的噪声标定设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: Recovery from a coherent rotation with an unobserved angle depends on both the encoding and what calibration reveals about the noise. We determine their combined limit for a known finite-dimensional generator and one final recovery. Two polynomial projections organize the answer. A physical Krylov chain separates correctable error amplitudes from their first state-dependent distortion; projection onto lower-angle powers identifies the part that a coherent decoder can cancel. Under explicit rank and moment conditions, their combination gives the exact leading optimal entanglement infidelity at weak noise and a recovery attaining it for any finite logical dimension. The optimal worst-input error lies between one and $d$ times that infidelity at leading order, for logical dimension $d$. When the angle distribution is unknown, we optimize one decoder for every distribution compatible with the calibration. When spectral and calibration frequencies are integer multiples of a common frequency, moment positivity turns this into an exact finite optimization. Small measured means can still allow rare finite rotations. If the sole calibration datum is $β_r=\mathbb E[\sin^{2r}(θ/2)]$, with $θ$ the wrapped rotation phase at that common frequency, the optimal worst-prior infidelity is $O(β_r)$ exactly when generator powers through degree $r-1$ are correctable. When the first uncorrectable power has degree $m<r$, the optimal loss scales as $β_r^{m/r}$, with a sharp coefficient fixed by a universal moment problem. In a loss-free four-photon memory, population measurements at two calibration durations retain the fully informed quartic error coefficient for a fixed symmetric narrow-noise family with positive variance and finite fourth moment; a single duration permits loss quadratic in the noise width.

</details>


### [188] [A New Non-Affine Family in the Karlsson-Szollosi Intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33207)
*André Ranchin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明六阶 Karlsson 族与 Szöllősi 族的交集在标准 Hadamard 等价意义下等于仿射 Diţă 曲线与一个新的非仿射单参数族的并，从而表明该交集严格大于已知的 Diţă 曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认识中，两个参数化复 Hadamard 矩阵族的交集仅被认为包含仿射 Diţă 曲线；作者希望厘清该交集的实际结构，并为六维互无偏基（mutually unbiased bases）的搜索提供线索。

Method: 采用构造性方法：在 Karlsson 坐标下给出 Szöllősi 族的新参数化，并对 Karlsson–Szöllősi 交集进行构造性分类，结合块可约形式（block-reducible forms）与内摆线约束（hypocycloid constraints）进行分析。

Result: 在标准 Hadamard 等价（standard Hadamard equivalence）下得到 $K_6^{(2)} \cap X_6^{(2)} = D_6^{(1)} \cup C_6^{(1)}$，其中 $C_6^{(1)}$ 为一类新的非仿射单参数复 Hadamard 矩阵族。

Conclusion: 两参数 Hadamard 族的交集严格大于已知的仿射 Diţă 曲线 $D_6^{(1)}$，揭示了块可约形式与内摆线约束的交汇结构，并可能有助于六维互无偏基的寻找。

Abstract: We show that $K_6^{(2)} \cap X_6^{(2)} = D_6^{(1)} \cup C_6^{(1)}$ up to standard Hadamard equivalence, where $C_6^{(1)}$ is a new non-affine one-parameter family of complex Hadamard matrices. This demonstrates that the intersection of the two-parameter Hadamard families of Karlsson and Szöllősi is strictly larger than the known affine Diţă curve $D_6^{(1)}$. Our new parametrisation of the Szöllősi family $X_6^{(2)}$ in Karlsson coordinates and our constructive classification of the Karlsson-Szöllősi intersection shed light on the intersection of block-reducible forms and hypocycloid constraints. This may support the search for mutually unbiased bases in six dimensions.

</details>


### [189] [Induced Bell-CHSH observables in the Fewster-Verch measurement scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33773)
*M. S. Guimaraes,I. Roditi,S. P. Sorella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 Fewster-Verch 测量框架内，为自由 Majorana 场（free Majorana field）给出了 Summers-Werner 定理的算子级显式实现，通过对 Majorana 探针的局域耦合构造二分 Bell 可观测量，并在显式可实现性假设下使诱导可观测量在真空制备态中达到 Tsirelson 界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Summers-Werner 定理仅证明互补楔形（complementary wedges）上真空最大违反 Bell-CHSH 不等式，属于存在性陈述，既未给出可观测量，也未给出测量装置；本文旨在弥补这一构造性缺口。

Method: 在 Fewster-Verch 测量框架下，将 1+1 维自由 Majorana 场与紧支撑耦合剖面（compactly supported coupling profile）k 的 Majorana 探针局域耦合，诱导出系统涂抹（smearing）严格分解为 f_h = k·b_{λ,h}；通过校准与归一化诱导涂抹实现二分性（dichotomy），并借助仅对探针的正规序（normal ordering）得到 Alice 与 Bob 诱导双线性（bilinears）之积。

Result: 在显式可实现性（attainability）假设下，诱导可观测量在由真空制备的态中饱和 Tsirelson 界（Tsirelson bound），且该构造可推广至耦合标量场（coupled scalar fields）。

Conclusion: 该工作把 Summers-Werner 的存在性结论转化为可操作的算子级测量方案，说明 Bell 违反可由探针耦合诱导的可观测量实现，并具有向标量场情形推广的潜力。

Abstract: The Summers-Werner theorem shows that the vacuum of a relativistic quantum field theory violates the Bell-CHSH inequality maximally on complementary wedges, but it is an existence statement: it exhibits neither the observables nor an apparatus that measures them. We give an explicit operator-level realization within the Fewster-Verch measurement framework, obtaining dichotomic Bell observables of a free Majorana field in $1+1$ dimensions as observables induced by local couplings to Majorana probes. The induced system smearing factorizes exactly as $f_h=k\,b_{λ,h}$, so its support is controlled by the compactly supported coupling profile $k$; dichotomy is imposed by calibrating and normalizing the induced smearings rather than required of the probe observable; and a probe-only normal ordering yields the product of Alice's and Bob's induced bilinears, which the non-multiplicative induced map alone does not provide. Under explicit attainability hypotheses the induced observables saturate the Tsirelson bound in a state prepared from the vacuum. The construction extends to coupled scalar fields.

</details>


### [190] [Quantum-Relativistic Anomalous Proper Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33220)
*Pablo L. Saldanha,Nelson Yokomizo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究引力与固有时（proper time）服从量子叠加原理（quantum superposition principle）这一假设的推论，指出在特定前后选择条件下，他处空间区域的有效固有时可超出经典广义相对论（general relativity）允许的范围，甚至变为负值，并提出了一个当前技术条件下原理上可行的干涉实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于探索量子力学（quantum mechanics）与广义相对论（general relativity）之间超越平直时空中牛顿势（Newtonian potential）效应的相互作用，并寻找可实验检验的理论推论。

Method: 方法为理论假设推演：假定引力与固有时满足量子叠加，考察大质量物体处于两个位置叠加态并经适当末态后选择（post-selection）时的有效固有时；同时设计一个在地球引力场中利用干涉仪（interferometer）观测量子粒子内部自由度（internal degrees of freedom）异常有效固有时的实验方案。

Result: 结果表明，在适当后选择下，另一空间区域流逝的有效固有时可呈现异常值，位于经典广义相对论所允许范围之外，且该有效固有时甚至可为负，对应有效的时间倒流演化。

Conclusion: 所提实验在原理上借助现有技术即可实现，其实现有望首次提供量子力学与广义相对论相互作用超越平直时空牛顿势效应的实验证据。

Abstract: We investigate surprising consequences of the hypothesis that gravity and proper time obey the quantum superposition principle. We show that, for a massive body prepared in a superposition of two positions and post-selected in a suitable final state, the elapsed proper time in another region of space can assume an anomalous effective value lying outside the range allowed by classical general relativity. Remarkably, the effective elapsed proper time can even become negative, corresponding to an effective evolution backward in time. We also propose an experiment to observe an anomalous effective proper time associated with the internal degrees of freedom of a quantum particle in an interferometer under Earth's gravitational field. The proposed experiment is, in principle, feasible with current technology, and its realization could provide the first experimental evidence of an interplay between quantum mechanics and general relativity beyond the effects of the Newtonian potential in flat spacetime.

</details>


### [191] [Semiclassical dynamics of higher-order squeezing: self-adjoint extensions and coherent returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33378)
*A. E. Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过边界振幅与相干态的半经典传播，建立高阶压缩哈密顿量量子自伴扩张与经典有限时间爆炸之间的对应，并给出 Husimi 测度在反复穿越无穷后的极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶压缩哈密顿量（higher-order squeezing Hamiltonians）存在多种自伴实现（self-adjoint realizations），而其经典轨迹在有限时间内到达无穷，二者之间的关联尚待刻画。

Method: 借助离散傅里叶变换（discrete Fourier transformation）得到各量子实现对应的“汇—源”射线矩阵（ray matrix）；证明结合满足精确扩张边界条件的有限阶近似尾部解、全范数高斯匹配（full-norm Gaussian matching）与平方可和残差估计。

Result: 首次穿越无穷后，Husimi 概率测度收敛为出射经典分支上的点质量，权重等于射线矩阵元素模的平方；对正能量，任意固定穿越次数后的振幅按酉射线矩阵的幂演化，而非其模方矩阵的迭代。

Conclusion: 这些极限可由能量保持 Liouville 扩张下的集中复振幅实现，从而区分确定性经典粘合（classical gluings）与相干边界混合；量子扩张选择与经典分支权重由同一射线矩阵统一描述。

Abstract: Higher-order squeezing Hamiltonians have several self-adjoint realizations, while their classical trajectories reach infinity in finite time. We relate these extension problems through boundary amplitudes and the semiclassical propagation of coherent states. A discrete Fourier transformation gives the sink-to-source ray matrix of each quantum realization. After the first passage through infinity, the full Husimi probability measure converges to point masses on outgoing classical branches, with weights equal to the squared moduli of the matrix entries. The proof combines finite-order approximate tail solutions satisfying the exact extension boundary condition with full-norm Gaussian matching and a square-summable residual estimate. For positive energy, we determine the limit after any fixed number of passages: amplitudes evolve by powers of the unitary ray matrix, not by iterations of its squared-modulus matrix. We also realize these limits by concentrated complex amplitudes for an energy-preserving Liouville extension, distinguishing deterministic classical gluings from coherent boundary mixing.

</details>


### [192] [Estimation of covariance matrix for Stokes-like measurement under phase-space deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34176)
*Pinaki Patra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究各向异性谐振子中相空间非对易性（phase-space noncommutativity）的可观测效应，提出通过光学探针的类Stokes测量（Stokes-like measurements）来实验表征非对易参数θ与η的光力协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相空间非对易性（noncommutativity）缺乏可及的实验检验途径，需要将其动力学效应映射到可测量的光学观测量上。

Method: 从含位置-位置（position-position）与动量-动量（momentum-momentum）非对易参数θ、η的变形相空间代数出发，用Darboux变换构造正则变量下的等效哈密顿量；推导协方差矩阵（covariance matrix）与高斯态（Gaussian-state）关联，并将谐振子耦合至相干光学探针，解析给出时间依赖的光学相干衰减与非线性Kerr相位（Kerr phase），进而设计基于平衡分束器与光电探测器的干涉式类Stokes探测方案。

Result: 获得光学相干衰减与Kerr相位的解析表达式，光学响应可读取被修正的简正模频率（normal-mode frequencies），据此可估计θ与η的实验值。

Conclusion: 论文在非对易空间动力学、高斯态关联与可实验获取的光学观测量之间建立了理论联系，提出一条检验相空间非对易性特征的光力（optomechanical）途径。

Abstract: We investigate the signatures of phase-space noncommutativity in an anisotropic oscillator and propose an optomechanical protocol to experimentally characterize it through Stokes-like measurements of an optical field. Starting from a deformed phase-space algebra characterized by the position-position and momentum-momentum noncommutativity parameters $θ$ and $η$, we employ the Darboux transformation to construct an equivalent Hamiltonian in canonical variables. The resulting effective dynamics exhibits correlations between the oscillator degrees of freedom. We derive the corresponding covariance matrix and examine how the noncommutative parameters enter the mechanical dynamics and the optical response.
  By coupling the oscillator to a coherent optical probe, we obtain analytical expressions for the time-dependent optical coherence attenuation and the nonlinear Kerr phase. We then formulate an interferometric detection scheme through Stokes-like measurements, in which the outgoing optical field is combined with a phase-controlled reference beam at a balanced beam splitter, and the resulting photocurrents are measured using photodetectors. The optical response provides access to the modified normal-mode frequencies, which enables us to estimate experimental values of $θ$ and $η$. Our results establish a theoretical connection between NC-space dynamics, Gaussian-state correlations, and experimentally accessible optical observables, suggesting an optomechanical route toward testing signatures of phase-space noncommutativity.

</details>


### [193] [Complete characterization of internal-external entanglement trade-offs in two-mode Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35183)
*Yasusada Nambu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在固定局部辛本征值下，刻画非对称双模高斯态内禀纠缠与外禀纠缠之间的精确权衡关系，并给出可及区域的解析边界与饱和的单配性型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是量化高斯态中内部纠缠（两模之间）与外部纠缠（经高斯纯化后跨分割）之间的相互制约关系，将此前的对称情形推广至非对称双模高斯态。

Method: 以负度（negativity）N_{A:B} 度量内部纠缠、N_{AB:CD} 度量跨高斯纯化（Gaussian purification）的外部纠缠；先数值采样推测 (N_{AB:CD}, N_{A:B}) 平面的可及区域边界，再解析确定之，并通过参数化全局辛谱（symplectic spectrum）给出固定 N_{AB:CD} 下剩余谱参数的闭合容许区间，构造性证明区间内每点对应物理协方差矩阵。

Result: 得到 (N_{AB:CD}, N_{A:B}) 平面可及区域的解析边界，并证明两尖锐界之间所有中间 N_{A:B} 值均可实现；上界由等幅值反号关联达到，下界的正分支出现在某一全局辛本征值等于 1 时；同时识别出两个外禀纠缠阈值，并导出饱和的单配性型（monogamy-type）不等式。

Conclusion: 内部与外禀纠缠之间存在精确、可构造实现的权衡结构；结果给出饱和的单配性型不等式，并在对称态极限下回到已知结论。

Abstract: We characterize the exact trade-off between internal and external entanglement in asymmetric two-mode Gaussian states at fixed local symplectic eigenvalues. Internal entanglement is quantified by the negativity $\mathcal{N}_{A:B}$ between the two modes, while external entanglement is quantified by the negativity $\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD}$ across a Gaussian purification. Numerical sampling suggests the boundary structure of the attainable region in the $(\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD},\mathcal{N}_{A:B})$ plane, which we then determine analytically. Parametrizing the global symplectic spectrum yields a closed admissible interval for the remaining spectral parameter at fixed $\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD}$. We prove constructively that every point in this interval corresponds to a physical covariance matrix, implying that all intermediate values of $\mathcal{N}_{A:B}$ between the resulting sharp bounds are achievable. The upper bound is attained for opposite-sign correlations of equal magnitude, while the positive branch of the lower bound occurs when one global symplectic eigenvalue equals unity. We also identify two external-entanglement thresholds, derive a saturated monogamy-type inequality, and recover the symmetric-state limit.

</details>


### [194] [Helstrom geometry with time-dependent spectral calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33848)
*Kaushlendra Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文分析有限标量–量子比特–标量谱三元组（spectral triple）中连接强度随时间变化的情形，将谱速率（spectral rate）分解为可区分性与校准两部分，并给出谱距离增长与迹距离收缩并存的反直觉示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作已表明该谱三元组的Connes距离在因子2/Λ意义下重现Bloch球迹度量（trace metric），但尚未考察连接强度随时间变化时谱距离与迹距离的动力学关系，尤其是二者是否保持一致演化。

Method: 在独立于量子比特动力学的前提下引入连接强度的时间依赖，对谱速率作可区分性（distinguishability）与校准（calibration）的分解，并用去极化（depolarizing）示例及一般性分析刻画与给定正谱记录相容的迹距离历史。

Result: 去极化示例显示，迹距离单调收缩时谱距离仍可增长；一般情形下存在收缩型与复苏型（reviving）两类可实现。在固定Λ时，正弦型连接失配保持Dirac谱与量子比特度量不变，而标量–标量距离以失配周期的一半振荡。

Conclusion: 谱距离与迹距离在时变连接下并不等价演化，谱记录本身不足以唯一确定迹距离历史；固定Λ下的失配可作为调控标量–标量距离而不扰动Dirac谱与量子比特度量的手段。

Abstract: In a recent work we introduced a finite scalar-qubit-scalar spectral triple whose Connes distance reproduces the Bloch-ball trace metric up to a factor $2/Λ$, where $Λ^2=μ_L^2+μ_R^2$ for isotropic link strengths $μ_L,μ_R$ appearing in the proposed Dirac operator. Here we analyse time-dependent variation of these links, independent of the qubit dynamics. In doing so, we separate the distinguishability and calibration contributions to the spectral rate. A depolarizing example demonstrates spectral growth despite a monotonic contraction of the trace-distance. More generally, we characterize the admissible trace-distance histories compatible with a given positive spectral record and exhibit contracting and reviving realizations. At fixed $Λ$, we also study a sinusoidal link imbalance that preserves the Dirac spectrum and qubit metric, while the scalar-scalar distance oscillates with half the period of the imbalance.

</details>


### [195] [Multipartite dynamics beyond bipartite entanglement equilibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35377)
*Yan Liu,Ya-Wen Sun,Ren-Jie Ying,Jun-Yuan Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出纠缠平衡并不意味量子信息动力学的终结：固定三分割的完整Schmidt谱可先达到稳态，而由局域酉不变量刻画的多体关联结构仍在演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子热化（quantum thermalization）研究以纠缠平衡作为动力学趋于稳态的标志，但这一判据可能掩盖多体关联结构的持续演化，需厘清二者是否属于同一松弛过程。

Method: 在自对偶踢动Ising动力学（self-dual kicked-Ising dynamics）的有限时间窗口内，以及Haar随机线路（Haar-random circuits）的渐近行为中，分析固定三分割（tripartition）的完整Schmidt谱与局域酉不变量（local-unitary invariants）所区分的多体关联结构；并借助量子纠错码（quantum-error-correcting codes）构造可独立调节的时间尺度。

Result: Schmidt谱的稳态与多体关联结构的持续演化可以分离；当耦合到局域扰乱浴（locally scrambling bath）时，普通纠缠经快速局域扰乱趋于平衡，而较弱的逻辑—浴耦合驱动多体关联以更慢速率重组。

Conclusion: 双分纠缠（bipartite entanglement）与多体关联结构探测的是量子信息动力学中不同的物理通道，而非同一松弛过程的不同阶段，这一区分比平衡时间的先后排序更为深刻。

Abstract: Entanglement equilibration need not mark the end of quantum-information dynamics in quantum thermalization. We show that the complete Schmidt spectra of a fixed tripartition can become stationary while its multipartite correlation structure, distinguished by local-unitary invariants, continues to evolve. This separation occurs exactly within a finite window in self-dual kicked-Ising dynamics and asymptotically in Haar-random circuits. We further demonstrate an independently tunable timescale for multipartite dynamics using quantum-error-correcting codes. When coupled to a locally scrambling bath, ordinary entanglement approaches equilibrium through rapid local scrambling, while weak logical--bath coupling drives a slower reorganization of multipartite correlations. These results reveal a deeper distinction than the ordering of equilibration times: bipartite entanglement and multipartite correlation structure can probe distinct physical channels of quantum-information dynamics, rather than merely different stages of the same relaxation process.

</details>


### [196] [Path integral control in the complex plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34483)
*Aarón Villanueva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将路径积分控制（path integral control）推广至复平面，借助圆复维纳过程（circular complex Wiener processes）构建驱动噪声空间上的精确测度变换，并应用于随机薛定谔方程（stochastic Schrödinger equations），给出成对非厄米跃迁通道的紧凑处理与交叉熵最优（cross-entropy-optimal）的反馈及开环控制公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径积分控制结合随机量子轨迹已被证明是量子最优控制（quantum optimal control）的一种有前景的替代方案，但原有形式主要限于实值噪声框架；本文旨在将其扩展至复平面以适配量子场景中的复数噪声与耗散结构。

Method: 引入圆复维纳过程将路径积分控制推广到复平面；在精确的控制-噪声匹配条件（control-noise matching condition）下，于驱动噪声空间定义测度变换（change of measure），该条件亦可容纳秩亏的状态扩散（rank-deficient state diffusions）；随后将形式体系应用于随机薛定谔方程。

Result: 所得形式体系对具有不相等正耗散率（unequal positive dissipation rates）的成对非厄米跃迁通道（paired non-Hermitian jump channels）给出紧凑处理，并导出交叉熵最优的反馈控制（feedback）与开环控制（open-loop）公式。

Conclusion: 复数域路径积分控制为量子最优控制提供了一个统一且紧凑的框架，能够处理秩亏扩散与不等耗散的非厄米通道，并自然产生交叉熵最优的控制律。

Abstract: Path integral control is a stochastic optimal control framework that computes optimal policies as weighted averages over state trajectories. It was recently shown that, combined with stochastic quantum trajectories, the path integral control approach constitutes a promising alternative for quantum optimal control. In this work, we propose an extension of path integral control to the complex plane using circular complex Wiener processes. The change of measure is defined on the driving noise space under an exact control-noise matching condition, which also accommodates rank-deficient state diffusions. Applied to stochastic Schrödinger equations, the resulting formalism gives a compact treatment of paired non-Hermitian jump channels with unequal positive dissipation rates and yields cross-entropy-optimal feedback and open-loop control formulas.

</details>


### [197] [Membership in the cone of quantum states over time is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34972)
*Matt Hoogsteder-Riera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究“跨时状态（state over time, stote）”所生成凸锥 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 的几何与复杂性：证明该锥含有一个与两体可分锥（bipartite separable cone）线性同构的暴露面，从而由可分性判定问题的 NP-困难性推出 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 及其对偶的成员判定与优化问题均为 NP-困难，且该锥一般不具有有限半定表示（semidefinite representation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画 stote 算子所生成凸锥 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 的结构，是前作（HoogstederCalsamigliaWinter2025）提出的公开问题；理解该锥的几何面与计算复杂度，对量子信息中跨时关联的判定与优化具有基础意义。

Method: 构造输出系统分解 $B=A'R$（$A'$ 为输入副本），给出显式见证元 $W_0\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^*$ 与显式单射线性映射 $L$，使 $σ\in\mathrm{SEP}_{+}(A{:}R)\iff L(σ)\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$，即将可分锥嵌入为暴露面；并结合可分性的 NP-困难性与定量稳定性界（stability bound）完成弱成员判定归约。

Result: 精确成员判定（exact membership）、弱成员判定（weak membership）与弱优化（weak optimization）对 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 及其对偶均为 NP-困难，稳定性界保证弱成员归约在逆多项式精度下成立；故除非 P=NP，不存在多项式时间成员判定算法。此外，当 $d=\dim A\ge2$、$r=\dim R\ge2$ 且 $d+r>5$ 时，由 Fawzi 定理可知 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$ 不存在有限半定表示。

Conclusion: $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 在计算上难以处理且缺乏有限半定表示，说明基于半定规划（semidefinite programming）的通用方法无法刻画该锥；这从复杂度与表示论两方面界定跨时状态锥的可处理边界。

Abstract: A state over time (stote) pairs a state $ρ$ with a channel $\mathcal{E}$ as the Hermitian, generally non-positive operator $(ρ\otimes\mathbf{1})\star J_\mathcal{E}$. Characterizing the convex cone $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ these operators generate was posed as an open problem in~\cite{HoogstederCalsamigliaWinter2025}. We exhibit a linear copy of the bipartite separable cone as an exposed face of $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$: for output systems of the form $B = A'R$ with $A'$ a copy of the input, there is an explicit witness $W_0\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^*$ and an explicit injective linear map $L$ with $σ\in \mathrm{SEP}_{+}(A{:}R)\iff L(σ)\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$. Composing with the NP-hardness of separability shows that exact membership, weak membership, and weak optimization are NP-hard for $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ and its dual. A quantitative stability bound makes the weak-membership reduction valid at inverse-polynomial accuracy. Thus polynomial-time membership tests do not exist unless $\mathrm{P}=\mathrm{NP}$. Independently of this complexity assumption, Fawzi's theorem implies that $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$ admits no finite semidefinite representation when $d=\dim A\ge2$, $r=\dim R\ge2$, and $d+r>5$.

</details>


### [198] [Geometric aspects of fault tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35513)
*Jonathan Conrad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文从几何视角研究容错（fault tolerance），提出算子代数化的纤维丛（fiber bundle）框架，将Knill-Laflamme条件的推广与平坦联络（flat connection）相联系，并将其应用于GKP码，刻画其模空间嵌入格拉斯曼流形（Grassmannian）的结构及相关的magic态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在以几何与算子代数语言统一刻画容错结构，并把该框架推广到连续变量情形下的Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill（GKP）码，从而理解其模空间与magic态来源。

Method: 构造容错纤维丛框架的算子代数表述，证明Knill-Laflamme条件的推广蕴含平坦联络；随后针对GKP码发展该框架，研究各极化类型（polarization type）的模空间（moduli space）向正则化分布空间（regularized distribution space）的有限维子空间格拉斯曼流形的光滑嵌入。

Result: 对每一极化类型，GKP码的模空间可光滑嵌入正则化分布空间有限维子空间的格拉斯曼流形；与Jacobian相关的GKP码产生可由高斯态（Gaussian states）码空间投影得到的magic态，并识别出一种CCZ型magic态。

Conclusion: 容错具有可由纤维丛与平坦联络刻画的几何结构，该结构可延伸至GKP码，并借助Jacobian型构造将高斯态投影与magic态（含CCZ型）联系起来。

Abstract: We highlight three geometric aspects of fault tolerance. First, we construct an operator-algebraic formulation of the fiber bundle framework for fault tolerance, and show that a generalization of the Knill-Laflamme conditions implies a flat connection for this framework. Next, we develop this framework for Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes. We show that, for each polarization type, their moduli space embeds smoothly into a Grassmannian of finite-dimensional subspaces of a regularized distribution space. Finally, we explain how GKP codes associated with Jacobians give rise to magic states that can be obtained by code space projections of Gaussian states and identify a $\rm CCZ$-type magic state arising this way.

</details>


### [199] [Competing coherence geometries in positive maps: positivity hierarchies, atomicity, and optimal entanglement witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35758)
*Massimiliano F. Sacchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入一类作用于 qudit 的正映射族（positive maps），其 Choi 算子由两类相互竞争的相干模式组合而成；对该族的每个混合成员，精确给出正性、完全正性及所有中间 k-正性（k-positivity）的条件，并刻画可分解性与原子性的完整相结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构造一个可精确求解的框架，使 k-正性、可分解性（decomposability）、原子性（atomicity）、正偏转置边缘态（PPT edge states）几何与最优纠缠见证（entanglement witness）等概念可在任意局部维度下被系统比较。

Method: 针对由独立交换耦合与集体对角耦合两类相干模式叠加而成的 Choi 算子族，推导正性、完全正性与各 k-正性阈值的精确条件；通过显式的正加余正分解（positive-plus-copositive decomposition）配合相匹配的正偏转置态族，证明可分解性边界，并借助嵌入 qutrit 判据分析不可分解性与原子性的关系。

Result: 高 k-正性阈值依赖信道重数（channel multiplicities），而可分解性边界在无标度坐标（scale-free coordinates）下具有普适性；相应 PPT 态构成检测区域为椭圆（集体重标度后为圆盘）的 PPT 边缘态。嵌入 qutrit 判据表明在整个混合族内不可分解性与原子性一致；纯集体端点在其整个正性区间内均可分解；在最大相干处，显式正减除构造出具有张成性质（spanning property）且 PPT 检测集严格更大的原子最优纠缠见证。

Conclusion: 该族提供了一个精确可解的场景，可在任意局部维度下统一比较 k-正性、可分解性、原子性、PPT 边缘态几何与见证最优性。

Abstract: We introduce a family of positive maps on qudits whose Choi operators combine two competing coherence patterns: independent exchange couplings and a collective set of diagonal couplings. For every mixed member of the family, we derive exact conditions for positivity, complete positivity, and all intermediate levels of k-positivity, and we determine the complete phase structure of decomposability and atomicity. The higher-positivity thresholds depend on the channel multiplicities, whereas the exact decomposability boundary is universal in scale-free coordinates. Its proof combines an explicit positive-plus-copositive decomposition with a matching family of positive-partial-transpose (PPT) states. The latter form PPT edge states whose detection region is an ellipse, or a disk after collective rescaling. An embedded-qutrit certificate shows that indecomposability and atomicity coincide throughout the mixed family. In contrast, the purely collective endpoint is decomposable over its entire positive interval. Finally, at maximal coherence, an explicit positive subtraction produces an atomic optimal entanglement witness with the spanning property and a strictly larger PPT- detection set. The study thus provides an exactly solvable setting in which k-positivity, decomposability, atomicity, PPT-edge-state geometry, and witness optimality can be compared in arbitrary local dimension.

</details>


### [200] [The $\varepsilon$-smoothed Entanglement of Formation and its relation to the Moreau-Yosida approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35797)
*M. E. Shirokov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文借助 Mora-Piani-Briegel 的 ε-测度（ε-measure）构造，将形成纠缠（Entanglement of Formation, EoF）以迹范数距离为基本度量正则化为凸连续函数族 \(E_{F,\varepsilon}\)，并系统刻画其性质、收敛速率与可推广性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形成纠缠（EoF）在一般情况下缺乏凸性以外的一批良好解析性质（如连续性、可计算上界），而 ε-测度构造与 Moreau-Yosida 近似（Moreau-Yosida approximation）提供了一条将其平滑化的途径，故需系统研究正则化后函数族的性质与收敛行为。

Method: 对 EoF 应用 Mora-Piani-Briegel 的 ε-测度构造，以迹范数距离（trace norm distance）作为基本度量，得到双分量子系统（有限维或无限维子系统）状态集上的凸连续函数族 \(E_{F,\varepsilon}\)；并利用其与 Moreau-Yosida 近似族 \(\{E^\lambda_F\}\) 的关系进行分析。

Result: 得到 \(E_{F,\varepsilon}\) 的变分表达式（ε=0 时退化为 EoF 的标准变分表达式）、弱次可加性（weak subadditivity）与连续性性质；借助 EoF 的半连续性界（semicontinuity bounds），在单侧边际态有界秩/有界能量的状态集上给出 ε→0⁺ 时一致收敛速率的易计算上界。辅助结果：无限维双分系统中任一状态均存在关于迹范数距离的最近可分态（nearest separable state），且该最近态不唯一。

Conclusion: \(E_{F,\varepsilon}\) 随 ε 单调不增且逐点收敛到 EoF，构成 EoF 的合理解析正则化；所得结论可推广至其他纠缠度量，特别是相对熵纠缠（relative entropy of entanglement）。

Abstract: We describe a family of convex continuous functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$, $\varepsilon\in(0,1]$, on the set of states of a bipartite quantum system (consisting of finite-dimensional or infinite-dimensional subsystems) obtained by applying the Mora-Piani-Briegel construction of $\varepsilon$-measure (proposed in [Phys. Rev. A 109, 052413]) to the Entanglement of Formation (EoF) with the use of the trace norm distance in the role of basic metric. These functions are non-increasing in $\varepsilon$ and converge pointwise to the EoF as $\varepsilon\to0^+$. We conducted a detailed analysis of properties of the functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$, using (among others) the relationship between the family $\{E_{F,\varepsilon}\}_{\varepsilon\in[0,1]}$ and the family $\{E^λ_F(ρ)\}_{λ>0}$ of Moreau-Yosida approximations of the EoF explored in [arXiv:2609.30246].
  We obtain a variational expression for the functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$ (which for $\varepsilon=0$ turns into the standard variational expression for the EoF). We also describe the weak subadditivity property of the functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$ and analyse their continuity properties.
  The semicontinuity bounds for the EoF (presented in [Lob.J.Math., 46, 2632-2658],[arXiv:2404.07978]) allow us to obtain easily computable upper bounds on the rate of uniform convergence of the function $E_{F,\varepsilon}$ to the EoF as $\varepsilon\to0^+$ on the sets of states with bounded rank/energy of one of the marginal states.
  Applicability of the obtained results to other entanglement measures, in particular, to the relative entropy of entanglement, is discussed.
  As an auxiliary result, it is shown that for any state of an infinite-dimensional bipartite quantum system, there exists a nearest separable state w.r.t. the trace norm distance. The non-uniqueness of such a state is also shown.

</details>


### [201] [Study of localization properties under subsystem periodic driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33189)
*Arpita Goswami,Ranjan Modak,Shaon Sahoo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 仅驱动两腿梯子（two-leg ladder）的一个子系统，无法使静态局域化的 Aubry-André 链完全离域化，而是形成一个局域态与离域态共存的混合相（mixed phase），其离域态比例随驱动频率非单调变化。局域有效哈密顿量（local effective Hamiltonian）虽由高频区解析导出，却能预测中频区的局域化转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动（periodic driving）可用于调控量子系统中的局域化（localization）并构造有效哈密顿量；已有结果表明，当整条一维 Aubry-André 链被低于临界频率驱动时可进入完全离域相。本文据此提出：若只驱动系统的部分区域，是否同样能实现离域化。

Method: 构造两腿梯子（two-leg ladder）：一条 Aubry-André 链与一条受周期驱动的干净紧束缚链（clean tight-binding chain）耦合，并采用精确数值计算（exact numerical calculations）研究其局域化性质；同时解析推导高频区的局域有效哈密顿量，并通过量化 Floquet 哈密顿量（Floquet Hamiltonian）的非局域性（nonlocality）界定其适用频率范围。

Result: 从局域化的静态梯子出发，子系统驱动在低于临界频率时不能实现完全离域化，而是出现混合相（mixed phase），离域态比例随驱动频率非单调变化。该行为可由局域有效哈密顿量解释；有效哈密顿量的有效描述上限对应一个交叉频率（crossover frequency），该频率低于局域化转变的临界频率。

Conclusion: 结果证明存在一条 Floquet 工程（Floquet engineering）的替代途径：通过仅驱动某一子系统即可改变量子晶格系统的输运性质（transport properties），且局域有效哈密顿量的适用范围可延伸至中频区。

Abstract: Periodic driving provides a powerful route for controlling localization and engineering effective Hamiltonians in quantum systems. A localized one-dimensional Aubry-André chain can be driven into a fully delocalized phase when the entire system is driven below a critical frequency. Here, we ask whether such delocalization can be achieved by driving only a part of the system. We address this question using a two-leg ladder comprising an Aubry-André chain coupled to a periodically driven clean tight-binding chain. Exact numerical calculations show that, starting from a localized static ladder, subsystem driving below a critical frequency cannot achieve complete delocalization. Instead, a mixed phase emerges, with localized and delocalized states coexisting and the fraction of delocalized states varying nonmonotonically with the driving frequency. This behavior can be understood using local effective Hamiltonians. Although these effective Hamiltonians are derived analytically in the high-frequency regime, they can nevertheless predict the localization transition occurring in the moderate-frequency regime. In fact, by quantifying the emergent nonlocality of the Floquet Hamiltonian, we show that these effective Hamiltonians provide a valid description up to a crossover frequency that is lower than the critical frequency associated with the localization transition. Our results demonstrate an alternative route to Floquet engineering, in which the transport properties of a quantum lattice system can be modified by driving only a subsystem.

</details>


### [202] [Hidden Fisher Information in Many-Body Quantum Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33893)
*Atta ur Rahman,Muhammad Noman,Samira Elghaayda,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出“矩阶费舍尔层级”$F_M^{(k)}$与读出复杂度尺度$k_{90}$，用以刻画量子费舍尔信息（QFI）中有多少可被低阶集体自旋矩读出，并在非线性集体自旋传感中揭示多体动力学使最优读出转移至高阶张量扇区、形成有限尺寸读出复杂度瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QFI仅给出优化所有允许测量后的精度极限，却无法量化最优读出（optimal readout）实现的难易程度，缺少对读出复杂度的度量。

Method: 引入矩阶费舍尔层级（moment Fisher hierarchy）$F_M^{(k)}$，度量至多$k$阶集体自旋矩可获取的QFI比例；并定义读出复杂度尺度（readout-complexity scale）$k_{90}$，即恢复90% QFI所需的最小阶数。将该框架应用于非线性集体自旋传感器，结合弱阻尼下对称对数导数（symmetric logarithmic derivative, SLD）的秩分辨分析。

Result: 在$N$至100的系统中，一阶矩仅获取约$10^{-2}$的可用QFI，恢复90%所需阶数增至$k_{90}\simeq 28$；多体动力学在保持大QFI的同时将最优读出转移至低阶集体可观测量无法触及的高秩$SU(2)$张量扇区，弱阻尼下非线性协议将最优读出推向最高可用张量秩并显著削弱其低秩权重。

Conclusion: 非线性切空间剪切（nonlinear tangent-space shear）是多体量子计量（many-body quantum metrology）中有限尺寸读出复杂度瓶颈的机制来源。

Abstract: Quantum Fisher information (QFI) determines the ultimate precision of a quantum sensor after optimization over all allowed measurements, but it does not quantify how difficult the optimal readout is to implement. We introduce a moment Fisher hierarchy $F_M^{(k)}$, which measures the fraction of QFI accessible to collective spin moments up to degree $k$, together with a readout-complexity scale $k_{90}$ defined by the minimum degree required to recover $90\%$ of the QFI. Applying this framework to a nonlinear collective-spin sensor, we show that many-body dynamics can preserve large QFI while transferring the optimal readout into high-rank $SU(2)$ tensor sectors inaccessible to low-order collective observables. For system sizes up to $N=100$, first moments access only $\sim 10^{-2}$ of the available QFI, while the degree required to recover $90\%$ of it grows to $k_{90}\simeq 28$. A rank-resolved analysis of the symmetric logarithmic derivative (SLD) under weak damping shows that the nonlinear protocol pushes the optimal readout toward the highest available tensor ranks while strongly depleting its low-rank weight. These results identify nonlinear tangent-space shear as the mechanism behind a finite-size readout-complexity bottleneck in many-body quantum metrology.

</details>


### [203] [Real-Time Correlation of Relaxation, Dephasing, and Two-level System Dynamics in Transmon Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34485)
*Ambrose Yang,Alexander M. Young,Paal H. Prestegaard,Atharv Joshi,Matthew S. Ai,Q. Rumman Rahman,Faranak Bahrami,Matthew P. Bland,Jui-Lin Niu,Ourania-Maria Glezakou-Elbert,Ray D. Chang,Jocelyn Liu,Zhengyi Lu,Robert J. Cava,Nathalie P. de Leon,Andrew A. Houck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 超导量子比特（superconducting qubit）性能存在持续数分钟至数小时的时间涨落，被称为T1与T2偏移（excursions），其实质由单个或少数强耦合双能级系统缺陷（two-level-system defect, TLS）谱扩散至与比特频率共振所引起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料改进已显著提升transmon量子比特的寿命（T1）与相干时间（T2），但当前最优超导量子比特仍存在性能的时间涨落，限制了门保真度（gate fidelity）与量子处理器性能，其噪声来源尚需厘清。

Method: 采用交错进行的寿命与相干时间测量、噪声谱学（noise spectroscopy）以及双能级系统谱学（TLS spectroscopy）三种手段联合分析。

Result: 观测到弛豫速率与退相位速率（dephasing rate）呈相关性的同步上升，持续时间从数分钟到数小时；测量表明这些偏移源于单个或少数强耦合TLS缺陷谱扩散进入与量子比特频率共振的状态。

Conclusion: 该测量协议为理解导致超导量子比特性能时间涨落的噪声源提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Materials improvements in transmon qubits have yielded remarkable advances in qubit lifetimes (T1)and coherence times (T2). However, state-of-the-art superconducting qubits experience temporal fluctuations in their performance, which limit gate fidelities and quantum processor performance. We observe correlated increases in qubit relaxation and dephasing rates that last for minutes to hours, which we call T1 and T2 excursions. Using interleaved lifetime and coherence time measurements, noise spectroscopy, and two-level-system (TLS) spectroscopy, we find that these excursions are caused by single or few strongly-coupled TLS defects that spectrally diffuse into resonance with the qubit frequency. Our measurement protocol provides a powerful tool for understanding the noise sources that cause temporal fluctuations in superconducting qubit performance.

</details>


### [204] [Wave Is All You Need: A Quantum Language Model Based on Fully Connected Quantum Boltzmann Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34492)
*Jun Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于全连接量子玻尔兹曼机（fully connected quantum Boltzmann machine, FC-QBM）的自注意力机制（self-attention mechanism），并以其为核心构建量子语言模型 FC-QBM-LM，在唐诗语料上以 8 量子比特实现位置编码与自注意力计算的整体流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自注意力需将连续语义流分解为离散矩阵并显式执行矩阵乘法，该工作试图通过量子态幺正演化隐式完成位置编码与注意力计算，并借助测量结果天然满足的归一化条件规避上述分解与显式乘法过程。

Method: 对语义量子态执行幺正演化（unitary evolution）以隐式实现位置编码与自注意力计算；严格推导该机制的计算原理并证明其与经典自注意力机制的数学等价性；推导训练与生成原理并开发相应两个算法；构建 8 量子比特 FC-QBM-LM，在唐诗语料上训练。

Result: 8 量子比特设置下量子层仅用 44 个参数即完成整个位置编码与自注意力计算流程；无噪声情形下模型收敛良好；以 Y 轴旋转门结尾的量子线路（quantum circuit）收敛优于以 X 轴旋转门结尾的对照方案；在小样本与八种噪声强度下保留超过 99% 的无噪声收敛性能；五言绝句生成实验初步展现联想发散、意象内容生成与符合人类语序三项核心能力。

Conclusion: 实验表明基于 FC-QBM 的自注意力机制可作为量子语言模型的核心引擎，在参数效率与噪声鲁棒性方面具有优势，并在小型生成任务中初步呈现类人的联想与语序组织能力。

Abstract: This work proposes a self-attention mechanism based on the fully connected quantum Boltzmann machine (FC-QBM), and further constructs a fully connected quantum Boltzmann machine language model (FC-QBM-LM) using the mechanism as the core engine. By performing unitary evolution on semantic quantum states, the mechanism implicitly implements positional encoding and self-attention computation, eliminating the need to decompose continuous semantic streams into discrete matrices for explicit matrix multiplication, with the measurement results naturally satisfying the normalization condition. Specifically, this work rigorously derives the computational principle of the self-attention mechanism based on FC-QBM, and demonstrates that this mechanism is mathematically equivalent to the classical self-attention mechanism. The mathematical principles governing the training and generation of FC-QBM-LM are thoroughly derived, and the two corresponding algorithms are subsequently developed. An 8-qubit FC-QBM-LM is finally constructed and trained on the corpus of Tang poetry. Under the 8-qubit setting, the quantum layer implements the entire positional encoding and self-attention computation pipeline with only 44 parameters. In the noiseless scenario, the model exhibits favorable convergence. Furthermore, the quantum circuit proposed in this work, which terminates with Y-axis rotation gates, achieves better convergence than the counterpart terminating with X-axis rotation gates. Under the small-sample regime and across eight distinct noise intensity levels, the model demonstrates strong noise robustness, retaining over 99% of its noiseless convergence performance. Experiments on five-character quatrain generation reveal that the model preliminarily exhibits three core capabilities: associative and divergent thinking, content generation across imagery and human-aligned word ordering.

</details>


### [205] [External-field-modified entropic exchange in confined quantum systems: static versus time-dependent electric fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35178)
*Vinod Prasad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该综述比较了均匀静电场与单色含时激光场对空间受限原子信息含量（以位置与动量空间香农熵 Sr、Sp 及其和 ST 度量，ST 服从 BBM 界）的重塑作用，并以受限氢原子（CHA）及其紧致极限——球盒中的粒子（PISB）为参照体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究外场如何改变受限原子体系的信息含量，并厘清静电场与激光场两类外场响应的差异，需要选取可解析对比的参考体系。

Method: 以 CHA 与 PISB 为模型体系，考察无场时总熵随受限半径的变化；并在受限半径 6 a.u. 处，对最低十个 m = 0 能级绘制熵的斯塔克图（Stark maps），对比两种外场下的响应。

Result: 无场时 CHA 的总熵随受限半径非单调变化，而 PISB 严格尺度不变；基态极化率在 CHA 中由自由原子的 4.5 a.u. 降至 4.06 a.u.，在 PISB 中则达 46.8 a.u.，两体系的熵响应也相应不同。

Conclusion: 空间受限显著改变原子的熵结构与极化率，且紧致极限（PISB）与有限受限（CHA）对外场的响应存在本质差异，表明边界约束与场类型共同决定信息含量。

Abstract: We review how external electric fields reshape the information content of spatially confined atoms, and we contrast the response to a uniform static field with that to a monochromatic, time-dependent laser field. The central quantities are the Shannon entropies in position and momentum space, Sr and Sp, and their sum ST = Sr +Sp, which obeys the BBM bound. Using the confined hydrogen atom (CHA) and the particle in a spherical box (PISB), its tight confinement limit, as reference systems, we first show that the field-free total entropy of the CHA depends non-monotonically on the confining radius whereas that of the PISB is strictly scale invariant. We then present Stark maps of the entropies for the ten lowest m = 0 levels at the confining radius of 6 a.u. The ground-state polarizability is reduced from the free-atom value 4.5 a.u. to 4.06 a.u. in the CHA but is 46.8 a.u. in the PISB, and the entropic response of the two systems differs correspondingly.

</details>


### [206] [Quantum state revivals, time quasicrystals, and entanglement steering in a Fibonacci-driven central-spin model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35555)
*Anushka Agrawal,Adithya A. Vasista,Tanay Nag,Sayan Choudhury*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出在无无序的斐波那契驱动（Fibonacci-driven）中心-卫星自旋模型中，通过调节相互作用强度或横向驱动场实现完美的量子复苏（perfect revivals）与时间准晶（time quasicrystals, TQCs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于寻找超越Floquet范式（Floquet paradigm）的非平衡物相（non-equilibrium phases of matter），即在准周期驱动体系中探索新的非平衡相。

Method: 采用解析方法分析具有中心-卫星自旋间Ising相互作用的斐波那契驱动中心自旋模型，考察相互作用强度λ与横向驱动场θ取特殊值时纠缠与磁化的动力学行为，并检验振荡在微调极限之外的鲁棒性及Clifford点上的行为。

Result: 当λ调至2mπ时，频闪时刻（stroboscopic times）中心与卫星自旋间无纠缠产生，卫星自旋磁化呈现无限长寿命的准周期振荡；当θ=π时（对具有确定热浴磁化的初态），卫星自旋磁化在斐波那契时刻表现出周期-3复苏。微调极限附近存在长寿命的π脉冲近似的TQC区域；在特殊Clifford点上出现依赖于宇称的傅里叶响应及Clifford近似的TQC，并在偶数卫星自旋下于Clifford点外仍持续。

Conclusion: 在Clifford点上系统沿纠缠轨迹演化，动力学生成Bell-cat态与spin-cat态，其集体自旋磁化涨落随卫星自旋数二次增长，从而实现海森堡极限（Heisenberg-limited）计量。

Abstract: Motivated by the search for non-equilibrium phases of matter beyond the Floquet paradigm, we propose a route to realize perfect revivals and time quasicrystals (TQCs) in a Fibonacci-driven central-spin model with Ising interactions between the central and satellite spins in the absence of disorder. We analytically demonstrate that when the interaction strength, $λ$ is tuned to $2 m π$, no entanglement is generated between the central and satellite spins at stroboscopic times, independent of the strength of the transverse driving field and the drive sequence. This leads to indefinitely long-lived quasiperiodic oscillations in satellite spin magnetization. Furthermore, for initial states with a definite bath magnetization, no entanglement is generated when the transverse driving field, $θ$ is tuned to $π$ for any value of $ λ$, and the satellite spin magnetization oscillations exhibit period-3 revivals at Fibonacci times. Examining the robustness of these oscillations away from this fine-tuned limit, we identify a parameter regime where the quasiperiodic response does not decay over the timescales probed, giving rise to a long-lived $π$-pulse-proximate TQC regime. We further show that at special Clifford points, the system exhibits perfect quasiperiodic oscillations of the satellite spin magnetization, with a parity-dependent Fourier response. This response persists over the timescales probed, even away from the Clifford points for an even number of satellite spins, leading to the formation of a Clifford-proximate TQC. Finally, we demonstrate that at this Clifford points, the system is steered along an entangled trajectory, leading to the dynamical generation of Bell-cat and spin-cat states. These states exhibit magnetization of collective spin fluctuates quadratically with the number of satellite spins, thereby enabling Heisenberg-limited metrology.

</details>


### [207] [Parametrically Enhanced Ergotropy and Charging Power in a Three-Mode Bosonic Quantum Battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35734)
*Mustapha Tarboun,Mohamed Amazioug*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用压缩光（squeezed light）增强量子电池（quantum battery）性能的理论方案，在由驱动充电腔、耗散辅助腔与储能电池腔构成的三腔系统中，通过非互易性定向能量流与简并光参量放大器（degenerate optical parametric amplifier）注入双光子激发，实现储能、充电功率与遍历功（ergotropy）数个量级的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池被视为为其他量子器件供能的潜在"燃料"，因此提升其可提取功（ergotropy）与充电功率具有实际意义。本文旨在通过压缩光主动驱动来突破无辅助构型下的性能限制。

Method: 系统由相干驱动的充电腔、强耗散辅助腔（作为中介）与储能电池腔组成；利用直接耦合路径与损耗工程间接路径之间的破坏性量子干涉（destructive quantum interference）建立量子非互易性（quantum nonreciprocity）与定向能量流。充电腔内集成简并光参量放大器作为相位敏感的主动能源，注入双光子量子激发与反常关联；基于Lindblad主方程（Lindblad master equation）的严格开放量子系统框架求解完整期望值动力学与稳态。

Result: 主动参量驱动显著提高储能与峰值充电功率，同时保持电池中的非互易能量囚禁（nonreciprocal energy trapping），储能、充电功率与遍历功均实现数个量级（multi-order-of-magnitude）的提升。纯退相位（pure dephasing）对充电功率、遍历功及可提取功质量的影响亦被分析：双光子泵浦引入的光子数涨落虽略微降低分数遍历功比 ${\cal W}_{\rm B}/{\cal E}_{\rm B}$，但绝对可提取功仍远高于无辅助构型。

Conclusion: 压缩光辅助的主动参量驱动是增强量子电池储能、充电功率与遍历功的有效途径，且非互易能量囚禁在增益下得以维持。尽管存在分数遍历功比的轻微下降，绝对可提取功的提升表明该方案在性能上总体优于未辅助配置。

Abstract: Quantum batteries are quantum systems that potentially serve as fuel for other quantum devices by receiving and supplying energy. In this work, we propose a theoretical scheme to enhance ergotropy and charging power of quantum Battery in the system under consideration through squeezed light. Moreover, The system comprises a coherently driven charger cavity, a strongly dissipative auxiliary cavity acting as a mediator, and an energy--storage battery cavity. Quantum nonreciprocity and directional energy flow are established via destructive quantum interference between the direct coupling path and the loss--engineered indirect path. A degenerate optical parametric amplifier integrated into the charger cavity acts as an active, phase--sensitive energy source that injects two--photon quantum excitations and anomalous correlations. Using a strictly open--quantum--system framework derived directly from the Lindblad master equation, we obtain the full expectation--value dynamics and stationary states. We show that active parametric driving drastically increases the stored energy and peak charging power while maintaining nonreciprocal energy trapping in the battery. We achieve a multi-order-of-magnitude boost in the battery's stored energy, charging power, and ergotropy. We also analyze the effects of pure dephasing on the charging power, ergotropy, and extractable work quality.. Although two--photon pumping introduces photon--number fluctuations that slightly lower the fractional ergotropy ratio ${\cal W}_{\rm B}/{\cal E}_{\rm B}$, the absolute extractable work remains substantially elevated compared to unassisted configurations.

</details>


### [208] [Twist-controlled imaginary-time Mpemba effect in quantum spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35784)
*Zhi-Yao Ning,Zi-Xiang Li,Naoki Kawashima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子自旋系统中构造了可控的虚时姆潘巴效应（imaginary-time Mpemba effect, ITME），通过空间调制的局域旋转在不改变初始双分纠缠谱与关联强度的前提下，将谱权重的重新分布确定为异常弛豫的唯一驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子姆潘巴效应（Mpemba effect）的机制解释常混杂多种因素，缺乏能排除纠缠谱与关联强度变化的可控平台，因此需要一种能够干净分离单一驱动因素的方法来严格检验异常弛豫的来源。

Method: 采用空间调制的局域旋转（local rotations）构造可控虚时姆潘巴效应，并借助紧致玻色子描述（compact-boson description）分析模式选择性；进一步在相互作用 XXZ 链与梯子上做计算，并用十二量子比特的变分量子虚时演化（variational quantum imaginary-time evolution）进行模拟验证。

Result: 发现高次谐波扭转（higher-harmonic twists）专一地占据高能驻波，从结构上绕过慢衰减的红外模式（infrared modes）从而加速收敛；推导出反转的精确解析交叉时间（crossing times），并证明刻意重新引入慢模布居会系统性延迟 ITME；该机制在 XXZ 链与梯子以及十二量子比特模拟中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为虚时姆潘巴效应提供了严格可控且精确的出发点，确立了利用异常非平衡弛豫（anomalous non-equilibrium relaxation）服务于量子基态制备的基础范式。

Abstract: We construct a controllable imaginary-time Mpemba effect in quantum spin systems using spatially modulated local rotations without changing initial bipartite entanglement spectra and correlation magnitudes. This feature unambiguously isolates the redistribution of spectral weights as the sole driver of the anomalous relaxation. Using the compact-boson description, we reveal that these twists act as precise mode selectors: higher-harmonic twists exclusively populate high-energy standing waves, structurally bypassing slow-decaying infrared modes to accelerate convergence. We derive exact analytical crossing times for these reversals and demonstrate that intentionally reintroducing the slow-mode population systematically delays the ITME. This exact mechanism is validated beyond the fixed-point theory via calculations on interacting XXZ chains and ladders. Furthermore, a twelve-qubit simulation of variational quantum imaginary-time evolution reproduces the reversals and their controlled delay, providing a concrete route to realize this protocol in realistic quantum circuits. Ultimately, these results provide a strictly controllable and exact starting point for realizing and investigating the imaginary-time Mpemba effect, establishing a foundational paradigm to harness anomalous non-equilibrium relaxation in quantum ground-state preparations.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Superregular solitonic solutions of the defocusing nonlocal nonlinear Schrodinger equation and their novel perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33560)
*Duan-Yun Xia,Xiu-Bin Wang*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文在散焦非局域非线性薛定谔方程（defocusing nonlocal NLSE）框架下系统构造了超正则孤子解（superregular solitonic solutions），揭示其结构比局域情形更丰富，并以数值模拟验证解析解的精度与抗噪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
