Motivation: 已有研究表明局域散焦非线性薛定谔方程（local defocusing NLSE）是调制稳定的（modulationally stable），因此弱局域扰动下描述调制不稳定性（modulational instability）非线性演化的超正则孤子解不存在；本文旨在非局域框架下探讨该问题。

Method: 采用穿衣方法（dressing method）与茹科夫斯基变换（Joukowski transformation），系统推导散焦非局域 NLSE 的一般 N-孤子解（N-solitonic solution），并给出单孤子、双孤子及超正则双孤子解的显式表达式，进而分析其动力学。

Result: 所得解表现出比局域 NLSE 更为多样的形式，例如由两个相互作用波分量组成的单孤子解，以及包含四个波分量的双孤子解；数值模拟确认了解析解的精度与噪声鲁棒性（noise robustness）。

Conclusion: 研究推进了非局域系统中超正则孤子解的理论，并为非局域非线性介质（nonlocal nonlinear media）中的波传播提供了新的物理理解。

Abstract: Previous studies have indicated that the local defocusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation (NLSE) is modulationally stable. Therefore, superregular solitonic solutions characterizing the nonlinear evolution of modulational instability under weak localized perturbations do not exist. In this work, we study the nonlinear stage of the modulation instability of a condensate in the framework of the defocusing nonlocal NLSE. By using the dressing method and Joukowski transformation, we systematically derive the general $N$-solitonic solution of the defocusing nonlocal NLSE, and the explicit expressions for one-solitonic, two-solitonic and superregular two-solitonic solutions are provided. Then dynamics of these solutions is also examined. We find that these solutions show a much wider variety than those in the local NLSE, such as the one solitonic solution composed of two interacting wave components and the two-solitonic solution containing four wave components. Finally, the accuracy and noise robustness of the analytical solutions are confirmed by numerical simulations. Such results advance the theory of superregular solitonic solutions in nonlocal systems and offer new physical understanding of wave propagation in nonlocal nonlinear media.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Orbital Hall response of spin-1 fermions with a curved central band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33211)
*J. H. Warnes,Dayana Joy,Pankaj Bhalla,Rhonald Burgos Atencia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在三带低能模型中研究轨道霍尔效应（orbital Hall effect），该模型同时含线性与二次动量项；作者用量子动理学方法导出内禀轨道霍尔响应，并揭示其微观来源为能带间相干与轨道特征的耦合，指出微分轨道霍尔响应可作为探测低能结构的直接探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨道输运研究多集中于两带模型，缺乏对具有弯曲中心带的多带体系中轨道霍尔效应的理解；本文旨在构建超越两带框架的最小模型，厘清多带量子几何与横向轨道响应之间的联系。

Method: 采用三带低能哈密顿量（Hamiltonian），同时包含线性与二次动量依赖项，其中线性项解除色散带间的简并；在量子动理学（quantum kinetic）框架下推导内禀（intrinsic）轨道霍尔响应，并以外部电场诱导的带间相干（interband coherence）分析其微观起源。

Result: 轨道霍尔电导率（orbital Hall conductivity）源于本征态动量依赖的轨道特征与带间跃迁之间的相互作用；自旋-1（spin-1）结构使不同带间通道的贡献可被分别识别；微分轨道霍尔响应表现出分别对应线性带与二次带的标度奇异性（scaling singularity），且与紫外截断（ultraviolet cutoff）无关。

Conclusion: 研究建立了自旋-1费米子的多带量子几何（quantum geometry）与其横向轨道响应之间的联系，并为超越两带模型的轨道输运研究提供了简洁框架。

Abstract: We investigate the orbital Hall effect in a three-band system described by a low-energy Hamiltonian containing both linear and quadratic momentum-dependent contributions. The linear term lifts the degeneracy between the dispersive bands, providing a minimal setting to study orbital transport in a multiband system with a curved central band. Within a quantum kinetic approach, we derive the intrinsic orbital Hall response and analyze its microscopic origin in terms of interband coherence induced by an external electric field. We show that the orbital Hall conductivity arises from the interplay between the momentum-dependent orbital character of the eigenstates and transitions between different bands. The spin-1 structure provides a particularly transparent platform in which the contributions associated with different interband channels can be identified separately. We further show that the differential orbital Hall response provides a direct probe of the low-energy structure, exhibiting distinct scaling singularities associated with the linear and quadratic bands, respectively, while remaining independent of the ultraviolet cutoff. Our results establish a connection between the multiband quantum geometry of spin-1 fermions and their transverse orbital response, and provide a simple framework for investigating orbital transport beyond two-band models.

</details>


### [211] [Unified First-Principles Theory of Finite-Wave-Vector Photocurrents in Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33264)
*Bumseop Kim,Sayed Ali Akbar Ghorashi,Yicong Chen,Ritesh Agarwal,Andrew M. Rappe*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作从第一性原理（first-principles）出发，建立了二阶直流光电流（second-order dc photocurrent）的理论，说明空间结构光（spatially structured light）的相位梯度与强度梯度可分别通过两个四阶张量 ξ 与 ζ 驱动不同的直流响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间结构光除偏振与频率外还携带独立的相位与包络变化，但既有理论未厘清这些光学自由度如何驱动晶体中不同的直流响应；传统体光伏效应（bulk photovoltaic effect）描述亦未涵盖有限波矢贡献。

Method: 将二阶直流响应视为同一频率、不同波矢的两光学场分量的耦合，并对波矢做一阶展开，识别出耦合相位梯度的四阶张量 ξ（含平面波光子拖曳（photon-drag）极限）与耦合强度梯度的张量 ζ；结合 Wannier 插值（Wannier-interpolated）计算单层 GeS，并用多极分析（multipolar analysis）分解光学修正。

Result: 单层 GeS 存在对称性允许的有限波矢响应，且在常规体光伏效应消失的中心对称结构中依然存活；对均匀相位梯度的高斯光束，相位梯度项使光束中心两侧电流同向，强度梯度项则使其反向，表明可通过波前与包络选择不同光电流图样；多极分析将修正分解为电四极（electric-quadrupole）、轨道磁偶极（orbital magnetic-dipole）与能带色散贡献。

Conclusion: 光的空间结构可作为非线性光电流产生的独立控制变量，该理论为在实际材料中预测此类响应提供了第一性原理途径。

Abstract: Spatially structured light carries independent phase and envelope variations in addition to its polarization and frequency. We develop a first-principles theory of second-order dc photocurrents (especially shift and injection) that resolves how these optical degrees of freedom drive distinct dc responses in crystals. The second-order dc response couples two optical field components at the same frequency but with generally different wave vectors. Expanding the response to first order in these wave vectors identifies two rank-four material tensors: $ξ$, which couples to the optical phase gradient and contains the plane-wave photon-drag limit, and $ζ$, which couples to the intensity gradient and describes a spatially varying dc current. Wannier-interpolated calculations for monolayer GeS show symmetry-allowed finite-wave-vector responses that survive at a centrosymmetric structure where conventional bulk photovoltaic effect vanishes. For a Gaussian beam with a uniform phase gradient, the phase-gradient contribution drives current in the same direction on both sides of the beam center, whereas the intensity-gradient contribution drives currents in opposite directions, showing that distinct photocurrent patterns can be selected through the wavefront and envelope of light. A multipolar analysis further resolves the optical corrections into electric-quadrupole, orbital magnetic-dipole, and band-dispersion contributions. Together, these results show how the spatial structure of light can serve as an independent control variable for nonlinear photocurrent generation and provide a first-principles route to predict this response in real materials.

</details>


### [212] [Chern non-trivial two-dimensional square-net nodal line semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33303)
*Kendall Ramos,Salvador Barraza-Lopez,Elias Andrade*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文在双亚晶格二维 $p_xp_y$ 方格子紧束缚模型中引入对称性约束的动量依赖自旋轨道耦合（SOC），使节线打开能隙并产生占据带陈数（Chern number）$C=2$ 的陈绝缘相，同时证明在该拟设下不存在非平庸 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索方格子二维体系中通过自旋轨道耦合获得陈拓扑（Chern topology）的唯象途径，并厘清可及绝缘相所受到的对称性约束（symmetry constraints）。

Method: 构建含两个亚晶格（sublattice）的二维 $p_xp_y$ 方格子紧束缚模型，引入对称性 informed、动量依赖的自旋轨道耦合（SOC），并给出其有限程实空间跃迁表示；通过能带、纳米带（nanoribbon）边缘态及陈数与 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 不变量的计算进行分析。

Result: SOC 的两个分量共同打开节线（nodal line）能隙，其中一个分量破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry），形成占据带陈数 $C=2$ 的陈绝缘相；纳米带计算显示跨越能隙的边缘局域态，每边有两个净手性模（chiral modes）。此外，守恒自旋扇区幺正等价，因而具有相同陈数，排除了非平庸时间反演保护的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 相。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对称性约束的 SOC 可为方格子二维体系提供实现陈拓扑的唯象路径，并明确了该拟设（ansatz）下可及绝缘相的对称性限制。

Abstract: We investigate a two-dimensional $p_xp_y$ square-net tight-binding model with two sublattices and introduce a symmetry-informed, momentum-dependent spin-orbit coupling (SOC) with a finite-range real-space hopping representation. Its two components jointly gap the nodal line, while one breaks time-reversal symmetry, producing a Chern-insulating phase with an occupied band Chern number $C=2$. Nanoribbon calculations reveal edge-localized states traversing this gap, with two net chiral modes per edge. We further show that the conserved spin sectors are unitarily equivalent and therefore have equal Chern numbers, ruling out a nontrivial time-reversal-protected $\mathbb{Z}_2$ phase within this ansatz. Our results establish a phenomenological route to Chern topology in square-net two-dimensional systems and clarify the symmetry constraints governing the accessible insulating phases.

</details>


### [213] [Dephasing-powered open quantum-dot battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33408)
*Alok Nath Singh,Rafael Sánchez,Andrew N. Jordan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由局部退相干（local dephasing）自主充能的开放量子电池（open quantum battery），系统为三量子点链，可达到高能效（ergotropic efficiency）并支持两种能量提取方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于寻找无需外部驱动即可为开放量子电池充电的自主资源，并检验局部退相干能否作为有效充能机制。

Method: 构建一端连接热库、另一端强失谐形成高能存储元的三量子点链模型，利用局部退相干将系统驱动至高激发态的非平衡稳态，并分析储能性能、充电时间与稳定性，进而提出循环幺正协议与电子负载耦合两种能量提取方案。

Result: 在特定极限下能效趋近于 1，而可提取功（ergotropy）约为能量失谐量的三分之一；退相干源关闭后带电态仍长寿命。循环幺正协议（仅调制失谐量子点门）可回收几乎全部可提取功；耦合电子负载可提取超过初始可提取功的电化学功，从而利用部分被动能量（passive energy）。

Conclusion: 局部退相干可作为开放量子电池的自主充能资源，并在实验可及的介观平台（mesoscopic platforms）上提供简单的储能与能量提取路径。

Abstract: We propose a simple realization of an open quantum battery powered by local dephasing. The setup consists of a triple-quantum-dot chain coupled at one end to a thermal reservoir, with the dot at the opposite end strongly detuned to form a high-energy storage element. Local dephasing populates this highly excited state and autonomously drives the system into a nonequilibrium steady state with high ergotropic efficiency. We find a limit where the ergotropic efficiency approaches unity, while the ergotropy itself approaches one-third of the energy detuning. We characterize the battery through its energy-storage performance, charging time, and stability, showing that the charged state remains long-lived after the dephasing source is switched off. We then demonstrate two complementary protocols for extracting the stored energy. First, nearly all the available ergotropy can be recovered through a simple cyclic unitary protocol involving only modulation of the detuned-dot gate. Second, coupling the detuned dot to an electronic load allows the extraction of electrochemical work exceeding the initial ergotropy, thereby accessing part of the battery's passive energy. Our results establish local dephasing as an autonomous resource for charging open quantum batteries and demonstrate simple routes for extracting the resulting stored energy in experimentally accessible mesoscopic platforms.

</details>


### [214] [Zeptojoule Microwave Calorimetry with Nanosecond Recovery in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33414)
*Seunghan Lee,Woochan Jung,Jayeon Kim,Dongsung T. Park,Seong Jang,Seongeun Jeon,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jong-Chul Park,Philip Kim,Kin Chung Fong,Gil-Ho Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过纳秒脉冲直接标定石墨烯约瑟夫森结微波量热计（graphene Josephson-junction microwave calorimeter），并独立测定同一器件的热学参数，发现电子热容（electronic heat capacity）比理想估计大约12.4倍、电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）大约50倍，从微观层面解释了石墨烯量热计灵敏度与理想预期之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯量热计（graphene calorimeters）理论上兼具极高能量灵敏度与快速热恢复能力，但实验表现长期远低于理想估计，其灵敏度差距（sensitivity gap）的微观来源尚不明确。

Method: 在110 mK下采用纳秒脉冲对石墨烯约瑟夫森结微波量热计进行直接标定，结合时间分辨双脉冲测温（two-pulse thermometry）、脉冲宽度依赖量热（pulse-width-dependent calorimetry）与测辐射热计式测温（bolometric thermometry）独立测定同一器件的热学参数。

Result: 标定脉冲测量给出特征开关能量尺度（characteristic switching-energy scale）18.1 meV（2.90 zJ），本征弛豫时间（intrinsic relaxation time）19.5 ns；电子热容为理想估计的12.4倍，电子-声子耦合约为理想估计的50倍。基于这些实测参数的热学模型预测值为21.2 meV，定量再现了观测到的开关响应。

Conclusion: 研究确定了石墨烯量热计灵敏度差距的微观来源，并为超快纳米尺度热探测器（ultrafast nanoscale thermal detectors）建立了自洽的标定框架（self-consistent calibration framework）。

Abstract: Graphene calorimeters are predicted to combine extreme energy sensitivity with rapid thermal recovery, yet experiments remain far from ideal estimates. Here, we directly calibrate a graphene Josephson-junction microwave calorimeter using nanosecond pulses and independently determine the thermal parameters of the same operating device. At 110 mK, calibrated pulse measurements yield a characteristic switching-energy scale of 18.1 meV (2.90 zJ), while time-resolved two-pulse thermometry gives an intrinsic relaxation time of 19.5 ns. Pulse-width-dependent calorimetry and bolometric thermometry show that the electronic heat capacity is 12.4 times and the electron-phonon coupling is approximately 50 times larger than ideal estimates. A thermal model using these measured parameters predicts 21.2 meV, quantitatively reproducing the observed switching response. These results identify the microscopic origin of the sensitivity gap in graphene calorimeters and establish a self-consistent calibration framework for ultrafast nanoscale thermal detectors.

</details>


### [215] [Second-Harmonic Bulk Response of Fractional Quantum Hall Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33606)
*J. Luke Pimlott,Pablo Reiser,T. Hans Hansson,Habib Rostami*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出分数量子霍尔流体二阶谐波霍尔响应的体态（bulk）描述，基于倾斜大质量狄拉克模型构建复合玻色子陈-西蒙斯理论，揭示该响应在有限频率下保留特征填充因子依赖并呈现强各向异性，为分数量子霍尔流体提供无需非线性边缘模式的体态探测手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall）流体非线性响应的理解多依赖基于非线性边缘模式色散（nonlinear edge-mode dispersion）的边缘理论，亟需与之不同的体态（bulk）描述来刻画破缺反演与旋转对称性下的二阶谐波霍尔响应。

Method: 将倾斜大质量狄拉克模型（tilted massive Dirac model）投影至单一低能带，构建最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level）的复合玻色子陈-西蒙斯理论（composite-boson Chern–Simons theory），保留投影诱导的非线性电磁耦合。

Result: 非线性霍尔电阻率在直流极限下消失，而有限频率响应保留特征填充因子依赖 ρ^(2)_TLL ∝ ν^(-2)；低频下实部与虚部分别按 (ω/ω_c)^2 与 ω/ω_c 标度，且响应相对晶格倾斜轴（crystalline tilt axis）呈强各向异性；二阶电导率中的 Kohn 模式共振（Kohn-mode resonances）在非线性霍尔电阻率中相消。

Conclusion: 研究确立有限频率非线性输运（finite-frequency nonlinear transport）可作为分数量子霍尔流体的体态探针，且无需依赖非线性边缘模式色散。

Abstract: We develop a bulk description for the second-harmonic Hall response of fractional quantum Hall fluids with broken inversion and rotational symmetries, distinct from edge theories based on nonlinear edge-mode dispersion. By projecting a tilted massive Dirac model onto a single low-energy band, we construct a composite-boson Chern--Simons theory for the lowest Landau level that retains projection-induced nonlinear electromagnetic couplings. The nonlinear Hall resistivity vanishes in the dc limit, while its finite-frequency response retains the characteristic filling-factor dependence $ρ^{(2)}_{TLL}\proptoν^{-2}$, where $ν$ is the Landau-level filling factor. At low frequency, its real and imaginary parts scale as $(ω/ω_c)^2$ and $ω/ω_c$, respectively, where $ω_c$ is the cyclotron frequency, and the response is strongly anisotropic with respect to the crystalline tilt axis. Notably, the Kohn-mode resonances present in the second-order conductivity cancel in the nonlinear Hall resistivity. These results establish finite-frequency nonlinear transport as a bulk probe of fractional quantum Hall fluids without requiring nonlinear edge-mode dispersion.

</details>


### [216] [Perpendicular Anisotropy and g-Factor of Sputtered Ni Films on Si(111) Studied by Angle-Dependent Ferromagnetic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33637)
*Dushyant Singh,Tharundev V,Subha Maity,Subhavenkadesh SS,Vigneshwari SR,Krista R. Khiangte*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用AFM、掠入射X射线衍射、X射线反射、磁测量与角度依赖铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance）表征溅射Ni薄膜，并用以一阶、二阶项描述的单轴各向异性模型拟合共振场角依赖，获得g因子与有效垂直各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对不同厚度Ni薄膜的磁性参数（g因子、有效垂直各向异性、阻尼）开展系统表征，并检验仅用面内与垂直两个取向共振的常规分析是否可靠。

Method: 在室温下用直流磁控溅射（dc magnetron sputtering）在Si(111)上生长312 Å与1011 Å厚Ni膜，结合原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy）、掠入射X射线衍射、X射线反射、磁测量与角度依赖铁磁共振；以含一阶和二阶项的单轴各向异性模型拟合从膜面到膜法向的共振场角依赖。

Result: 两膜均为多晶fcc Ni，表面平滑、晶格间距呈块体(111)特征、饱和磁化强度约480 emu/cm³且面内磁化。拟合得g=2.160±0.005（薄膜）与2.101±0.014（厚膜），有效垂直各向异性分别为(6.0±0.4)×10⁵与(4.5±0.6)×10⁵ erg/cm³；仅用面内与垂直共振的常规分析给出的g因子偏低0.02–0.04；垂直取向线宽给出Gilbert阻尼上限α≤0.030与≤0.038。

Conclusion: 角度依赖铁磁共振结合多阶单轴各向异性模型可自洽提取Ni薄膜的g因子与有效垂直各向异性，且表明仅依赖面内与垂直两个取向的常规分析会系统性低估g因子；阻尼参数仅能给出上限。

Abstract: Ni films with thicknesses of 312 and 1011\,Å were grown on Si(111) by dc magnetron sputtering at room temperature and studied by atomic force microscopy, grazing-incidence X-ray diffraction, X-ray reflectivity, magnetometry and angle-dependent ferromagnetic resonance. Both films are polycrystalline fcc Ni with smooth surfaces, a bulk-like (111) lattice spacing and a saturation magnetization of ${\approx}480$\,emu\,cm$^{-3}$, and both are magnetized in plane. The angular dependence of the resonance field, from the film plane to the film normal, is described by a uniaxial anisotropy model with first- and second-order terms. From this model, $g = 2.160\pm0.005$ and $2.101\pm0.014$ and effective perpendicular anisotropies of $(6.0\pm0.4)\times10^{5}$ and $(4.5\pm0.6)\times10^{5}$\,erg\,cm$^{-3}$ were obtained for the thinner and thicker film, respectively. The conventional analysis of only the in-plane and perpendicular resonances gives $g$-factors that are lower by 0.02--0.04. The perpendicular linewidths give upper limits for the Gilbert damping of $α\le 0.030$ and $\le 0.038$.

</details>


### [217] [Emergent parafermions with tunable localization in a quantum circuit network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33815)
*D. B. Karki,Andrew K. Mitchell*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 三量子点三角形器件中，几何阻挫诱导出三量子点共享的Z3仲费米子，且可通过栅压将其可调地迁移至任意一对量子点，为可扩展的非阿贝尔激发网络奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合金属-半导体量子点电路模拟器已在单点或两点线性电路中实现Kondo任意子，需进一步探索多点多几何构型下阻挫Kondo屏蔽所驱动的临界态及其可调控性。

Method: 研究三角形三量子点器件，利用几何阻挫（geometric frustration）产生三量子点共享的涌现Z3仲费米子（parafermion），并通过施加栅压（gate voltages）调控其位置。

Result: 仲费米子可被可调地迁移至任意一对量子点，且在弱隧穿“Kondo极限”与强透射“准弹道（quasiballistic）”两种区域均得到支持；临界态在强耦合至外部引线及其他量子点条件下仍可存活。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了可扩展且可重构的网络架构基础，在此架构中非阿贝尔激发（non-Abelian excitations）可被实现与操控。

Abstract: \noindent Quantum circuit simulators with hybrid metal-semiconductor dots provide a versatile platform to realize Kondo anyons. These arise at critical points driven by frustrated Kondo screening of dot charge degrees of freedom and have been demonstrated experimentally in linear circuits of one or two dots. Here we study a triangular triple dot device, where geometric frustration gives rise to an emergent $\mathbb{Z}_3$ parafermion shared among the three dots. We show that the parafermion can be tunably relocated to any pair of dots by applying gate voltages, and is supported in both the weak-tunneling `Kondo limit' and the strong transmission `quasiballistic' regime. The critical states survive strong coupling to external leads and other dots, establishing the basis for a scalable and reconfigurable network architecture in which non-Abelian excitations can be realized and manipulated.

</details>


### [218] [Momentum-selective graphene spectroscopy of moiré excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33989)
*Katsunori Wakabayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作以石墨烯作为动量选择性近场探针，研究WSe₂/MoSe₂异质双层中局域莫尔激子的跃迁偏振纹理，并据此统一计算远场与石墨烯能量转移两类速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远场光致发光（photoluminescence）仅探测激子动量内容中光锥（light-cone）角落的部分，而向邻近石墨烯（graphene）的能量转移可积分至由间隔层厚度决定的更大面内动量范围，因此需要一种能同时刻画两种探测几何的跃迁偏振描述。

Method: 基于依赖配位（registry-dependent）的矢量偶极纹理（vector dipole texture），构建每个局域莫尔激子的跃迁偏振 P_n(q)，并将其同时用于光学与石墨烯两类"浴"；在C₃对称的领头谐波（leading-harmonic）模型中，用第一性原理确定纹理相位的符号。

Result: 两类浴对态给出不同排序：石墨烯速率比值与光学比值不同，其顺序随间隔层厚度反转，且z=10 nm处的距离指数（distance exponent）相差超过两个单位；这些比值与跃迁偶极前因子无关。手性p₊与d₋态在法向入射下为暗态，但向石墨烯的转移速率与亮基态相当，且依赖远场不可见的纹理相位符号。

Conclusion: 石墨烯邻近效应可作为一种动量选择性近场探测手段，并可作为莫尔激子的实用光谱学（spectroscopy）方法；器件中该预言需要大于对称性破缺混合的分裂以保护手性伙伴。

Abstract: Far-field photoluminescence probes only the light-cone corner of an exciton's momentum content, whereas energy transfer to nearby graphene integrates in-plane momenta up to the inverse spacer thickness $1/z$. For a WSe$_2$/MoSe$_2$ heterobilayer we build the transition polarization $\bm{P}_n(\qv)$ of each localized moiré exciton from the registry-dependent vector dipole texture and use each $\bm{P}_n$ for both baths. The two baths rank the states differently: the graphene-rate ratios differ from the optical ones, their ordering inverts with spacer thickness, and the distance exponents at $z=10$\,nm spread by more than two units. These ratios are independent of the transition-dipole prefactor. Within the $C_3$-symmetric leading-harmonic model the chiral $p_+$ and $d_-$ states are also dark at normal incidence yet transfer to graphene at rates comparable to the bright ground state. Their graphene rate depends on the sign of the texture phase, a quantity invisible in far-field emission, which we determine from first principles. In a device this prediction requires a splitting, larger than the symmetry-breaking mixing, that protects the chiral partners. Graphene proximity thus acts as a momentum-selective near-field probe of moiré excitons, and we propose its use as a practical spectroscopy.

</details>


### [219] [Andreev Reflection at a Graphene Normal-Superconductor Interface: A Lattice-Continuum Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34270)
*Dar Faheem Ul Haq,S. S. Z. Ashraf*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文比较了洁净石墨烯（graphene）正常-超导（normal-superconductor）界面 Andreev 反射（Andreev reflection）的二次连续模型（quadratic continuum model）与最近邻紧束缚模型（nearest-neighbour tight-binding model），发现二者在角度响应上存在显著差异，并将原因归于界面匹配所需短波长辅助模式（auxiliary modes）与体色散之外的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能谷展开（valley expansion）常被视为石墨烯超导界面问题的有效近似，但其有效范围缺乏系统检验；本文旨在厘清二次连续模型与晶格模型在 Andreev 反射预测上的差异来源。

Method: 对化学势与配对势（pairing potential）突变、跃迁积分（hopping）不变的界面，紧束缚模型给出四个独立边界条件（boundary conditions）；将其反射概率与独立 Kwant 计算结果对比，并考察三角翘曲（trigonal warping）对动量-群速度关系的影响。

Result: 在参数 $(μ_N,μ_S,Δ_0,E)/t_0=(0.5,1,0.1,0.05)$ 下，$ξ=+1$ 谷的 Andreev 反射概率峰值在晶格模型中为 $19.9^\circ$，而二次连续模型为 $3.4^\circ$；晶格结果与 Kwant 计算在数值精度内一致。固定比例降低所有能量尺度时，中心角响应与电导（conductance）逐渐趋近晶格结果。

Conclusion: 二次连续模型的边界问题需要谷展开有效范围之外的短波长辅助模式，故差异不能仅归因于体色散（bulk dispersion）。激发能相对化学势较小并不保证连续模型有效，还需考虑相关能量尺度、两区域的模式及界面匹配假设。

Abstract: We compare quadratic continuum and nearest-neighbour tight-binding models of Andreev reflection at a clean graphene normal-superconductor interface. For abrupt changes in chemical potential and pairing potential, with unchanged hopping, the lattice model yields four independent boundary conditions. Its reflection probabilities agree with independent Kwant calculations to numerical precision for the channels examined.
  We investigate trigonal warping and its effect on the relation between momentum and group velocity. For $(μ_N,μ_S,Δ_0,E)/t_0=(0.5,1,0.1,0.05)$, the Andreev-reflection probability for the $ξ=+1$ valley peaks at $19.9^\circ$ in the lattice model, versus $3.4^\circ$ in the quadratic continuum model. These parameters lie outside the expected validity regime of the low-energy approximation. As all energy scales are reduced at fixed ratios, the central angular response and conductance become more consistent with lattice results.
  The quadratic continuum boundary problem also requires short-wavelength auxiliary modes outside the validity range of the valley expansion. Thus, differences cannot be attributed to bulk dispersion alone. A small excitation energy relative to chemical potential does not ensure continuum validity; relevant energy scales, modes in both regions, and interface matching assumptions must also be considered.

</details>


### [220] [Geometric tailoring of domain wall traps and diodes in ferromagnetic nanostripes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34526)
*Dmytro Karakuts,Kostiantyn V. Yershov,Denis D. Sheka*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过横截面几何调制（cross-sectional geometric modulation）在纳米条带中实现畴壁陷阱（domain-wall trap）与畴壁二极管（domain-wall diode），并用双轴铁磁体模型（biaxial ferromagnet model）与集体变量方法（collective-variable approach）理论解释了钉扎与退钉扎行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何剪裁磁性响应（geometric tailoring of magnetic responses）是现代磁学的核心主题之一，本文旨在探究横截面几何变化如何影响场驱动畴壁（field-driven domain walls）的动力学行为。

Method: 采用横截面几何调制：以纳米条带收缩（nanostripe constriction）构成畴壁陷阱，以楔形扩张（wedge-shaped expansion）构成畴壁二极管；并结合基于双轴铁磁体模型与集体变量方法的理论计算，与微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）进行对比验证。

Result: 畴壁可被横截面调制有效钉扎，且退钉扎场（depinning field）强烈依赖横截面梯度；弱场下畴壁在钉扎位点附近发生局域振荡（localized oscillations），强场下钉扎失效并再现典型二极管特性；理论计算与微磁模拟高度吻合。

Conclusion: 研究界定了使上述预测效应最显著的现实样品参数区间（parameter regime），为几何调控畴壁器件设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Geometric tailoring of magnetic responses is a central theme in modern magnetism. By employing cross-sectional geometric modulation, we investigate how geometric variations influence the dynamics of field-driven domain walls. This approach enables the realization of a domain-wall trap, achieved through a nanostripe constriction, and a domain-wall diode, realized via a wedge-shaped expansion. We demonstrate that domain walls can be efficiently pinned by cross-sectional modulation, with the depinning field exhibiting strong dependence on the cross-sectional gradient. Under weak applied fields, the domain wall undergoes localized oscillations near the pinning site; under strong fields, this pinning breaks down, reproducing the typical diode characteristics. Theoretical calculations based on a biaxial ferromagnet model and a collective-variable approach are in excellent agreement with micromagnetic simulations. Finally, we delineate the parameter regime for realistic samples in which these predicted effects are most pronounced.

</details>


### [221] [Synthetic staggered fields switch Neel vectors probed by magnetic resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34624)
*Yifang Hao,Zhiyi Che,Jack Brierley,Aakanksha Sud,Satoshi Iihama,Rishi Siddani,Chenghao Yang,Charlie W. F. Freeman,Troy Dion,Daniel Prestwood,Zekun Xue,Tianyi Wei,Murat Cubukcu,Craig Barton,Shunsuke Fukami,Shigemi Mizukami,Hidekazu Kurebayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用磁共振方法实现了对奈尔矢量（Néel vector）状态的直接电学检测，并在合成反铁磁体（synthetic antiferromagnets）中通过“合成交错场（synthetic staggered fields）”实现180°翻转，观察到整流电压的π相位跳变与滞回行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体宏观磁化消失，导致其序参量的电学检测困难，而磁共振手段此前多用于亚太赫兹自旋泵浦与非线性磁子动力学，尚需推广到电接触的片上器件中。

Method: 采用电流驱动的有效场，对每个磁子层施加极化相反的交错场以翻转奈尔矢量；结合相位敏感检测方案测量预旋运动的π相位移动所产生的滞回整流电压，并通过角度依赖共振分析与含缺陷的微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）解释结果。

Result: 翻转引起预旋运动的时间π相位移动，产生滞回整流电压；角度依赖分析指向超越宏自旋（macrospin）形式的模型，微磁模拟显示电流激发后出现碎裂畴态；提取的电流密度为7.4×10^9 A/m²，为室温奈尔矢量翻转的最低报道值，且在实验与数值上均随面内单轴各向异性（in-plane uniaxial anisotropy）缩放。

Conclusion: 合成交错场与基于共振的方法学为电驱动反铁磁织构（antiferromagnetic textures）的纳米尺度表征提供研究平台，可指导高频片上自旋电子架构的发展。

Abstract: Electrical detection of antiferromagnetic order is a critical challenge for antiferromagnetic spintronics owing to their vanishing macroscopic magnetisation. While magnetic resonance has demonstrated sub-terahertz antiferromagnetic spin pumping and nonlinear magnon dynamics, its application to electrically contacted on-chip devices has room for further exploration. Here we show that magnetic resonance enables direct electrical detection of Néel vector states. A $180^\circ$ reversal in synthetic antiferromagnets is realised by current-driven effective fields with opposite polarisation acting on each magnetic sublayer, which we term synthetic staggered fields. The switching induces a $π$ phase shift of precessional motion in time, producing hysteretic rectified voltages in our phase-sensitive detection scheme. Angular-dependent resonance analysis offers further insight into the Néel vector directions, pointing to a model beyond the macrospin formalism. We address this by defect-incorporated micromagnetic simulations that reveal fragmented domain states after current excitation. An extracted current density of $7.4\times10^{9}$ A/m$^2$ is, to our knowledge, the lowest reported value for the room-temperature Néel vector switching and we show that it scales with the in-plane uniaxial anisotropy both experimentally and numerically. Synthetic staggered fields and the resonance-based methodology provide a research platform for nanoscale characterisation of electrically driven antiferromagnetic textures, guiding the development of high-frequency on-chip spintronic architectures.

</details>


### [222] [Helicity-Selective Electron-Phonon Coupling Between $PT$-Partner States in $2H$-bilayer MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34830)
*Fred Sun,Jia-An Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究宇称-时间（parity-time, PT）简并电子双重态与手性声子（chiral phonons）的耦合机制，指出PT虽抑制声子单态手性，但手性声子双重态可通过类赝自旋（pseudospin）机制与PT伴侣电子态螺旋耦合，并在PT与C₃对称性共同作用下导出选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转对称性（rotational symmetry, C_n）对手性声子的约束已被认识，但其他对称性尤其是宇称-时间对称性如何影响手性声子相互作用，此前仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 采用理论与计算方法，以2H双层二硫化钼（2H-bilayer MoS₂）为体系，分析PT简并电子双重态与手性声子之间的耦合；并通过PT与C₃对称性的相互作用推导选择定则（selection rules）。

Result: PT对称性会抑制声子单态的手性，但手性声子双重态仍能经由类似赝自旋的机制与PT伴侣电子态发生螺旋式相互作用；对2H双层MoS₂的计算结果支持上述机制。

Conclusion: 手性声子可用于在PT体系中分辨谷内电子态（intravalley electronic states），为对称性调控手性声子-电子耦合提供了新的可能。

Abstract: Rotational symmetry ($C_n$) constrains chiral phonons, but how other symmetries affect their interactions remains largely unexplored. We perform a theoretical and computational study of chiral phonon coupling to parity-time ($PT$)-degenerate electron doublets in $2H$-bilayer MoS$_2$. Although $PT$ suppresses phonon singlet chirality, chiral phonon doublets can helically interact with $PT$-partner electronic states via a mechanism similar to pseudospin. Selection rules are then analyzed with the interplay of $PT$ and $C_3$. Our computational results of $2H$-bilayer MoS$_2$ suggest chiral phonons may be used to resolve intravalley electronic states in $PT$ systems.

</details>


### [223] [Nanoscale mapping of native defects in Td-MoTe2 and their impact on graphene-based van der Waals heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34924)
*Wojciech Ryś,Iaroslav Lutsyk,Mindaugas Lukosius,Przemysław Przybysz,Paweł Dąbrowski,Maciej Rogala,Paweł Krukowski,Paweł J. Kowalczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用扫描隧道显微镜与谱学（STM/STS）研究Td-MoTe2及其与石墨烯范德华异质结中的本征Te空位缺陷，发现空位对低能电子结构的影响为谱学上的弱扰动，且石墨烯覆盖层可进一步屏蔽该影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征缺陷（native defects）可能改变二维材料及异质结的低能电子结构，但其在Td-MoTe2与石墨烯/Td-MoTe2异质结中的具体作用尚不明确，需要原子尺度的实验表征。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunnelling microscopy, STM）与扫描隧道谱（scanning tunnelling spectroscopy, STS）对Td-MoTe2及石墨烯/Td-MoTe2范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructures）的表面缺陷与电子结构进行表征。

Result: STM观测到高密度圆形空心表面缺陷，归因于Te空位（Te vacancies）；原始MoTe2的STS呈半金属性V形dI/dV谱并含微弱低能态。空位抑制费米能级以下隧穿电导但不引入新的尖锐缺陷共振，符合Mo-Te谱权重的重分布而非新电子态生成；异质结中石墨烯保持MoTe2的这些特征，未出现明显掺杂或电荷转移（charge transfer）。

Conclusion: 孤立的Te空位对石墨烯低能电子结构构成谱学上的弱扰动（weak perturbations），石墨烯覆盖层进一步屏蔽其影响，使石墨烯/Td-MoTe2异质结对本征界面缺陷具有内在耐受性（intrinsically tolerant）。

Abstract: We investigate native defects and their impact on the electronic structure of Td-MoTe2 and graphene/Td-MoTe2 van der Waals heterostructures using scanning tunnelling microscopy and spectroscopy. STM reveals a high density of circular, hollow surface defects that we attribute to Te vacancies. STS on pristine MoTe2 shows semimetallic, V-shaped dI/dV spectra with subtle low-energy states. The vacancies suppress the tunnelling conductance below the Fermi level without introducing new sharp defect-related resonances, consistent with a vacancy-induced redistribution of Mo-Te spectral weight rather than the appearance of new electronic states. In the heterostructure, graphene preserves these MoTe2 fingerprints with no noticeable graphene doping or charge transfer. Our results demonstrate that isolated native Te vacancies act as spectroscopically weak perturbations to the low-energy electronic structure of graphene. The graphene overlayer further screens their impact on the electronic structure, making graphene/Td-MoTe2 heterostructures intrinsically tolerant to such interface defects.

</details>


### [224] [Dynamic helimagnetism via charged spin current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34927)
*Masaru Onoda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文从s-d交换哈密顿量推导局域自旋与传导电子的有效运动方程，发现一维自旋链在纯自旋流下形成静态螺旋、在叠加少量电荷流后形成相干进动螺旋，其螺距、进动频率与倾角均可由电流幅度连续调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究同时携带电荷流与自旋流的传导电子如何驱动经典局域自旋，并探索无需本征Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用的材料中由电流诱导螺旋磁序（helical order）的可能性。

Method: 基于s-d交换哈密顿量（s-d exchange Hamiltonian）推导自旋的有效运动方程，应用于周期性排布的一维自旋链，结合解析求解与直接数值积分，并借助启发式构造的有效拉格朗日量（effective Lagrangian）确定螺旋波数。

Result: 得到两种电流驱动的螺旋相（helical phases）：纯自旋流稳定的静态螺旋，以及加入少量电荷流分量后出现的相干进动螺旋；对给定螺旋波数，进动角频率ω与向硬轴的倾角比Δ可解析给出，数值积分在形成相干螺旋时复现全部三个可观测量。

Conclusion: 螺旋手性（chirality）由电流产生的长程DM型耦合决定，而非本征DM相互作用，因此无体相或界面DM相互作用的材料亦可承载电流控制的螺旋序；螺旋螺距、进动频率与倾角比随电流幅度连续可调，可作为自旋流的探针。

Abstract: We present a theoretical and numerical study of classical, localized spins coupled to conduction electrons that carry both charge and spin currents, deriving the effective equations of motion from the s-d exchange Hamiltonian. Applied to a periodically arranged one-dimensional spin chain, these equations yield two current-driven helical phases: a static helix stabilized by a pure spin current alone, and a coherently precessing helix that appears once a small charge-current component is added. For a given helical wavenumber, the precession angular frequency $ω$ and the canting ratio $Δ$ toward the hard axis follow analytically from the equations of motion, while the wavenumber $q_{\rm helix}$ of the precessing helix is selected by a heuristically constructed effective Lagrangian. Direct numerical integration of the same equations of motion reproduces all three observables wherever it yields a coherent helix. The helix chirality is set by a long-range, current-generated Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM)-type coupling rather than an intrinsic DM interaction, so materials without bulk or interfacial DM interactions could also host current-controlled helical order. Because the helical pitch, precession frequency, and canting ratio are all continuously tunable by the current amplitudes, these helices could serve as probes of spin currents.

</details>


### [225] [High-Q, high-power spin Hall nano-oscillators with thick NiFe layers enabled by SiC substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35245)
*Maha Khademi,Akash Kumar,Martina Ahlberg,Artem Litvinenko,Xiaotian Zhao,Saroj P. Dash,Johan Åkerman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在SiC衬底上进行热学衬底工程（thermal substrate engineering），可在较厚NiFe层中实现稳定的自振荡，获得平均输出功率13.1 pW与品质因数Q=23,000的自旋霍尔纳米振荡器（SHNO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHNO在局域自旋激发、神经形态计算（neuromorphic computing）和基于振荡器的伊辛机（Ising machines）中具有应用潜力，但需提升输出功率与相干性；增厚铁磁层是一条有希望的途径，却会带来更高的驱动电流、焦耳热（Joule heating）加剧与器件性能退化的问题。

Method: 制备Al2O3、Si与SiC三种衬底上其他条件完全相同的W/NiFe纳米收缩（nanoconstriction）SHNO，并对比其自振荡行为与性能随NiFe层厚度的变化。

Result: SiC的高热导率（thermal conductivity）使厚度达10 nm的NiFe层仍可维持稳定自振荡，最佳性能出现在7 nm；最佳SiC器件平均输出功率为13.1 pW，平均品质因数为Q=23,000，为单个自由运行（free-running）SHNO中已报道的最高同时输出功率与相干性组合。

Conclusion: SiC与热学衬底工程可作为实现厚铁磁层自旋电子振荡器（spintronic oscillators）的路径，用于纳米尺度器件中的相干微波产生。

Abstract: Spin Hall nano-oscillators (SHNOs) are nanoscale microwave sources with potential applications in local spin excitation, neuromorphic computing, and oscillator-based Ising machines. Further improvements in output power and coherence are essential for these applications, and increasing the thickness of the ferromagnetic layer offers a promising route toward this goal. However, this comes at the cost of higher drive currents, which increase Joule heating and can degrade device performance. Here, we show that this bottleneck can be overcome by SiC substrates. By comparing otherwise identical W/NiFe nanoconstriction SHNOs on Al2O3, Si, and SiC, we find that the high thermal conductivity of SiC enables stable auto-oscillations in NiFe layers as thick as 10 nm, while the optimum performance occurs at 7 nm. The best SiC device reaches an average output power of 13.1 pW and a mean quality factor of Q = 23,000, representing the highest reported simultaneous output power and coherence for a single free-running SHNO. These results establish SiC and thermal substrate engineering as a route to thick-ferromagnet spintronic oscillators for coherent microwave generation in nanoscale devices.

</details>


### [226] [Higher-harmonic spin-wave excitation by a superconducting antenna with vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35268)
*Anton Pokusinskyi,Paweł Gruszecki,Julia Kharlan,Oleksandr Dobrovolskiy,Jarosław W. Kłos*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作结合含时金兹堡-朗道（time-dependent Ginzburg–Landau）建模与微波近场谱分析，证明在Shubnikov相中超导微带天线可由近简谐涡旋（vortex/fluxon）振荡产生高次谐波并激发自旋波（spin wave）高次谐波模式，其中磁性层保持线性响应而超导天线充当非线性场源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导与磁性相互作用可为混合系统中非线性激发提供调控途径，直流驱动的阿布里科索夫涡旋（Abrikosov vortices）与自旋波的相互作用已有认识，但受限的近简谐涡旋振荡能否产生高频自旋波模式尚属空白。

Method: 采用含时金兹堡-朗道建模，并对微波近场进行谱分析，研究Shubnikov相中超导微带天线驱动下涡旋振荡产生的谐波及其与自旋波模式的耦合。

Result: 近正弦的涡旋运动及伴随的超导屏蔽电流（screening currents）重分布产生高次谐波，并与自旋波模式耦合；该响应源于空间到时间的转导（spatial-to-temporal transduction），即把空间非均匀磁场分布转换为更高的时间谐波。磁通量子（flux quanta）因此成为主动非线性元件。

Conclusion: 与传统依赖非线性磁化动力学的谐波产生机制不同，该系统中磁性层为线性接收者、超导天线为非线性源，从而拓展了超导—磁振（magnonic）混合体系中可及的自旋波频率范围。

Abstract: The interaction between superconductivity and magnetism provides new routes for controlling nonlinear excitations in hybrid systems. While dc-driven Abrikosov vortices (fluxons) are known to interact with spin waves, the ability of confined, nearly harmonic vortex oscillations to generate higher-frequency spin-wave modes remains unexplored. Here, by combining time-dependent Ginzburg--Landau modeling with spectral analysis of the microwave near field, we demonstrate higher-harmonic spin-wave excitation driven by a superconducting microstrip antenna in the Shubnikov phase. Unlike conventional harmonic-generation mechanisms associated with nonlinear magnetization dynamics, the magnetic layer remains a linear receiver, whereas the superconducting antenna generates the nonlinear microwave field. We show that nearly sinusoidal vortex motion and the accompanying redistribution of superconducting screening currents produce higher harmonics that couple to spin-wave modes. This response arises from spatial-to-temporal transduction, whereby harmonic vortex motion and the associated redistribution of screening currents convert a spatially nonuniform magnetic-field profile into higher temporal harmonics. Magnetic flux quanta thus act as active nonlinear elements for frequency conversion, extending the accessible spin-wave frequency range in superconducting--magnonic hybrids.

</details>


### [227] [Wrinkling dynamics of thin crystalline sheets and its dependency on topological defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35786)
*Emma Radice,Ingo Nitschke,Marco Salvalaglio,Axel Voigt*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用表面相场晶体-赫尔弗里希（SPFCH）模型研究单轴压缩下薄晶体膜起皱动力学，追踪晶格缺陷与皱褶子（wrinklons）并考察面内与面外弛豫对起皱起始及粗化（coarsening）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄晶体片起皱涉及晶序、面外形变与拓扑缺陷（topological defects）的耦合，晶格缺陷与起皱图样缺陷的演化与相互作用如何影响起皱起始与粗化机制尚待厘清。

Method: 构建SPFCH模型，耦合晶序与面外形变，可同时追踪六角晶格中的位错（dislocations）、晶界（grain boundaries）以及起皱图样中的皱褶子（wrinklons），解析其演化与相互作用。

Result: 晶格缺陷不改变粗化标度（coarsening scaling），但定量影响起皱起始与新生皱褶波长，促使皱褶图样更不规则、皱褶子密度更高，并改变长时间尺度形貌演化。

Conclusion: 面内与面外弛豫机制的相互作用决定起皱行为；晶格缺陷主要作用于起皱起始、波长选择与长时形貌，而非粗化标度律。

Abstract: We investigate the wrinkling dynamics of thin crystalline sheets under uniaxial compression using a Surface Phase-Field-Crystal-Helfrich (SPFCH) model, which captures the coupling between crystalline order, out-of-plane deformations, and topological defects. In particular, the model allows us to track and characterize two distinct classes of defects, namely those forming in the underlying hexagonal crystal lattice, such as dislocations and grain boundaries, and defects in the wrinkling patterns, known as wrinklons. By resolving the evolution and interactions of these defects, we investigate how the interplay between in-plane and out-of-plane relaxation mechanisms influences wrinkling onset and the coarsening regime. Our results indicate that crystalline defects do not affect coarsening scaling, but they quantitatively affect the onset of wrinkling and the emerging wrinkling wavelength, promote more irregular wrinkle patterns with a higher density of wrinklons, and modify long-timescale morphological evolution.

</details>


### [228] [Information-theoretic entropy imaging of quantum matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35790)
*Chen Wu,Leonard W. Cao,Lingyuan Lyu,Liam Cohen,Jiahui Nie,Can Uzundal,Yujia Hu,Quanjin Wang,Ziying Yan,Sneh Pancholi,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Feng Wang,Andrea F. Young,Tarun Grover,Monica T. Allen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出“熵成像（entropy imaging）”：以无损压缩（lossless compression）量化量子材料空间复杂性，并以可计算信息密度（computable information density, CID）作为实验可及的观测量。应用于石墨烯毫开尔文微波阻抗显微图像时，CID 在量子霍尔转变附近出现显著极大值，并可由无序 Hofstadter 模型重现，从而无需无序系综平均即可定位量子临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间平均探针无法观测相变产生的复杂空间组织（spatial organization），而对此类组织的定量刻画手段仍然有限，因此需要一种能从图像中提取空间复杂性、可预测性与有序性的信息论方法。

Method: 构建基于无损压缩的信息论技术“熵成像”，将涌现的空间纹理转化为实验可及的可计算信息密度（CID），该量在大系统极限下逼近香农熵率（Shannon entropy rate）；并应用于石墨烯的毫开尔文微波阻抗显微（microwave impedance microscopy）成像，辅以无序 Hofstadter 模型及 MoSe2/WS2 莫尔异质双层（moiré heterobilayer）的对照分析。

Result: CID 在量子霍尔转变（quantum Hall transition）附近呈现显著极大值，无序 Hofstadter 模型可重现该行为并可由图像序列识别量子临界点（quantum critical point）而无需系综平均；该框架亦可追踪分数量子霍尔转变并分辨孤立分数液滴（fractional puddles）。相比之下，MoSe2/WS2 莫尔异质双层的平庸带边交叉（band-edge crossover）表现出性质不同的行为，表明该增强并非导电—绝缘交叉的普遍特征。此外，CID 所编码的几何信息不可约化为关联长度（correlation length），甚至可区分具有相同多重分形统计（multifractal statistics）的空间纹理。

Conclusion: 无损压缩可作为量化量子材料中涌现空间组织的通用框架，并具备跨成像模态与物理系统的可移植性。

Abstract: Phase transitions can generate intricate spatial organization invisible to spatially averaged probes, yet quantitative measures of this organization remain limited. Here we develop "entropy imaging," an information-theoretic technique that harnesses lossless compression to quantify spatial complexity, predictability, and order in quantum materials. Using this approach, emergent spatial textures can be imaged and converted into an experimentally-accessible observable: the computable information density (CID), a compression-based measure of information complexity that approaches the Shannon entropy rate in the large-system limit. Applied to millikelvin microwave impedance microscopy of graphene, the method reveals a pronounced maximum in the CID near quantum Hall transitions, a quantitative measure of the information-rich spatial organization of the critical landscape. A disordered Hofstadter model reproduces this behavior and shows that the CID can identify the quantum critical point from a sequence of images, without ensemble averaging over disorder realizations. The same framework tracks fractional quantum Hall transitions and resolves isolated fractional puddles that coexist with a disordered background. By contrast, the trivial band-edge crossover in a MoSe2/WS2 moiré heterobilayer exhibits qualitatively different behavior, suggesting that the pronounced CID enhancement observed at the quantum Hall critical point is not simply a generic feature of a conductive-insulating crossover. We further show that the geometric information encoded in CID is not reducible to a correlation length, and it can even distinguish spatial textures with identical multifractal statistics. Together, these results establish lossless compression as a framework for quantifying emergent spatial organization in quantum materials, with portability across imaging modalities and physical systems.

</details>


### [229] [Modeling the CNOT gate in Germanium double quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35799)
*Mauricio J. Rodriguez,Yann-Michel Niquet*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在真实Ge/GeSi空穴自旋量子比特器件中建模交换相互作用介导的CNOT门，基于紧凑的多体单态-三重态缀饰基（dressed basis）框架实现快速含时模拟，系统评估非绝热性、AC Stark位移（AC Stark shift）与电荷噪声等保真度损失来源，并量化高保真两比特操作对势垒控制、微波驱动与电荷噪声稳定性的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规组态相互作用（configuration-interaction）方法计算成本过高，难以进行CNOT门的含时模拟；同时Ge/GeSi空穴自旋量子比特中交换门保真度的主要限制因素尚需定量厘清。

Method: 构建基于多体单态（singlet）与三重态（triplet）紧凑缀饰基的高效计算框架，对真实器件中交换相互作用介导的CNOT门进行快速含时模拟，并分析理想无噪声情形下的主要非保真度来源及准静态电荷噪声（quasi-static charge noise）的影响。

Result: 隧道势垒（tunnel barrier）斜坡的非绝热性（nonadiabaticity）因交换相互作用的横向分量而增强，限制CNOT门速度；条件共振的AC Stark位移与互补跃迁的离共振驱动显著降低保真度；量子比特附近电荷俘获会严重破坏条件操作，而纳米尺度电荷运动引起的保真度损失敏感依赖偶极的位置与强度。

Conclusion: 研究量化了Ge/GeSi空穴自旋量子比特实现高保真两比特操作对势垒控制、微波驱动优化与电荷噪声稳定性的要求，为器件与脉冲设计提供依据。

Abstract: We model a controlled-NOT (CNOT) gate mediated by exchange interactions in a realistic Ge/GeSi hole spin-qubit device. We use for that purpose a computationally efficient framework based on a compact dressed basis of many-body singlet and triplet states, which enables fast time-dependent simulations that would be prohibitive within a conventional configuration-interaction approach. We discuss the dominant sources of infidelity in ideal (noise-free) devices. On the one hand, the nonadiabaticity of the tunnel barrier ramps, enhanced by the transverse component of the exchange interaction, imposes limits on the speed of the CNOT gate. On the other hand, the AC Stark shift of the conditional resonance and the off-resonant driving of the complementary transition can significantly degrade the gate fidelity and call for a careful optimization of the driving conditions. Finally, we investigate the impact of quasi-static charge noise on the fidelity of the CNOT gate. We find that the capture of charges near the qubits can strongly disrupt the conditional operation, while the nanometer-scale motion of charges results in a loss of fidelity that sensitively depends on the position and strength of the corresponding dipole. Our results quantify the requirements on barrier control, microwave driving, and charge-noise stability for high-fidelity two-qubit operations in Ge/GeSi hole-spin qubits.

</details>


<div id='math.RA'></div>

# math.RA [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Classification of Finite-Dimensional Simple Novikov Algebras and Their Irreducible Bimodules in Positive Characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34896)
*Dong Liu,Yufeng Pei,Limeng Xia*

Main category: math.RA

TL;DR: 该论文在特征 p>2 的代数闭域上给出有限维单诺维科夫代数（simple Novikov algebra）的完整分类，补全此前文献中遗漏的族，并给出显式标准形（normal form）、同构关系及非平凡不可约双模（irreducible bimodule）的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Osborn (1992) 与 Xu (1996) 的既有分类存在遗漏族，且缺少显式标准形与同构刻画，故需完整重做分类并扩展到模结构。

Method: 在特征 p>2 的代数闭域上，对有限维单诺维科夫代数进行结构性分析，确定显式标准形（normal forms）并明确给出其间的所有同构，进而对非平凡不可约双模进行分类。

Result: 获得有限维单诺维科夫代数的完整分类（含此前遗漏的族）、全部显式标准形与同构关系，以及这些代数上非平凡不可约双模的完整分类。

Conclusion: 此前分类不完整；本文给出更完整的单诺维科夫代数分类框架及其不可约表示分类，为后续研究提供基础。

Abstract: We give a complete classification of finite-dimensional simple Novikov algebras over an algebraically closed field of characteristic $p>2$, including families missing from earlier classifications in \cite{Osborn1992, Xu1996}. We determine explicit normal forms and all isomorphisms between them, and classify all nontrivial irreducible bimodules over these algebras.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Inverse Modeling for Laser Pulse Shape Design in Inertial Confinement Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33096)
*Ricardo Luna Gutierrez,Vineet Gundecha,Rahman Ejaz,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Riccardo Betti,Sahand Ghorbanpour,Aarne Lees,Soumyendu Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出ICF激光脉冲波形设计系统（LPDS），一种生成式逆向建模（generative inverse modeling）框架，可将期望结果与靶丸配置直接映射为优化后的激光脉冲波形（Laser Pulse Shape, LP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性约束聚变（Inertial Confinement Fusion, ICF）是实现无碳聚变能的关键路径，而激光脉冲波形设计需在严格物理约束下优化驱动内爆，传统方法依赖高成本仿真与人工迭代，效率低。

Method: 构建生成式逆向建模框架直接映射期望结果与靶丸配置至LP；设计多目标损失函数（multi-objective loss function）以满足基础物理约束与实验要求；并引入约束条件化（constraint-conditioning）、图像修复式生成（inpainting）与基于梯度的LP编辑机制，实现对脉冲特征的细粒度控制。

Result: 生成的LP能够满足基础物理约束与实验需求，并支持对特定脉冲特性的精细调控；同时研究了该框架向少量真实实验发次（real experimental shots）的迁移表现。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了ICF中LP设计的数据驱动逆向设计（data-driven inverse design）框架，有助于推动实用且可持续的聚变能发展。

Abstract: The achievement of practical fusion energy remains one of the most pressing unsolved scientific challenges, with immense implications for carbon-free power. A critical factor in the success of Inertial Confinement Fusion (ICF) is the design of a Laser Pulse Shape (LP) that can optimally drive implosions under stringent physical constraints. Traditional LP design relies on computationally expensive simulations and manual iterative refinement. We introduce the ICF Laser Pulse Shape Design System (LPDS), a generative inverse modeling framework that maps desired outcomes and target pellet configurations directly to optimized LPs. Crucially, we design a multi-objective loss function to ensure the generated LPs satisfy fundamental physical constraints and experimental requirements. Furthermore, we present constraint-conditioning, inpainting, and gradient-based LP editing mechanisms for fine-grained control over specific pulse characteristics during generation. Moreover, we study the transfer of our framework to a limited set of real experimental shots. Our method establishes a data-driven inverse design framework for LP design in ICF, contributing to the advancement of practical and sustainable fusion energy.

</details>


### [232] [MatOS: An Agent Operating System for Materials Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34203)
*Dingyang Lyu,Jing Gao,Zhonghao Chang,Jingjie Huang,Xiangran Cheng,Xin He,Yongda Huang,Zefeng Li,Jiahui Liu,Jian Wen,Feng Yu,Bonan Zhu,Yongheng Li,Hongwei Du,Baole Wei,Ziheng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MatOS 是一个面向材料研究的智能体操作系统（agent operating system），通过分层科学资产图、有状态自适应工作流与证据驱动的资产演化三项机制，将数据、知识、工具、技能、角色与工作流组织为可复用、可演进的资产，从而支撑长周期多阶段材料研究任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料研究依赖分散且持续增长的数据、知识与仿真工具，单项研究常需跨文献分析、计算与实验的多日工作流；现有大语言模型智能体（large language model agents）虽可检索数据、运行仿真代码并接入自主实验室，但在扩展新能力、维持长任务稳定推进以及从历史任务与用户反馈中学习方面仍存在困难。

Method: MatOS 由三个核心组件构成：（1）分层科学资产图（layered scientific asset graph），将数据、知识、工具、技能、角色与工作流组织为可复用资产；（2）有状态自适应工作流（stateful adaptive workflow），以显式执行图（execution graphs）运行任务并嵌入科学校验，支持中断续跑与过程追溯；（3）证据驱动的资产演化（evidence-driven asset evolution），依据执行记录与用户反馈修订上述资产。

Result: 在 ResearchClawBench 上，相同基座模型下较此前最优智能体提升 11.15%，并扩展了成本—分数帕累托前沿（Pareto frontier）；同一基座模型下优于 Codex CLI 与 Claude Code，在 MatTools 上超出 Codex CLI 2.13%，在 SciAgentGym 上超出 4.17%。八个研究案例中，MatOS 将逾 3000 万个候选结构筛选至 97 个固态电解质候选物，识别出 89 个计算电导率高于母体结构的卤化物变体，将 11 个预训练原子间势（interatomic potentials）的力误差与其局域原子环境相关联，并支撑两项新方法分别相对基线降低误差 3.1% 与 13.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将研究能力组织为可复用且可演进的资产，可使智能体胜任长周期、多阶段的材料研究；代码已开源于 https://github.com/DingyangLyu/MatOS。

Abstract: Materials research relies on scattered and growing data, knowledge, and simulation tools, and a single study often spans multi-day workflows across literature analysis, computation, and experiment. Large language model agents can now retrieve materials data, operate simulation codes, and close design-synthesis-characterization loops with autonomous laboratories. However, extending these agents with new capabilities, keeping long-running tasks on track, and letting them learn from past tasks and user feedback remain difficult. Here we present MatOS, an agent operating system for materials research with three core components: (1) a layered scientific asset graph that organizes data, knowledge, tools, skills, roles, and workflows as reusable assets; (2) a stateful adaptive workflow that runs tasks as explicit execution graphs with scientific checks, so that they can be resumed and traced; and (3) evidence-driven asset evolution, which revises these assets from execution records and user feedback. On ResearchClawBench, MatOS improves on the previously best-performing agent by 11.15% with the same base model and extends the cost-score Pareto frontier. With a common base model, it outperforms Codex CLI and Claude Code, exceeding Codex CLI by 2.13% on MatTools and 4.17% on SciAgentGym. In eight research cases, MatOS narrowed more than 30 million candidate structures to 97 solid-electrolyte candidates, identified 89 halide variants with higher computed conductivity than their parent structures, linked the force errors of 11 pretrained interatomic potentials to local atomic environments, and supported two new methods that reduce errors by 3.1% and 13.6% relative to their baselines. These results show that organizing research capabilities as reusable, evolving assets enables agents to carry out long, multistage materials studies. Code is available at https://github.com/DingyangLyu/MatOS.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [One ring to rule them all: syzygy maximization for Argyres-Douglas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33151)
*Monica Jinwoo Kang,Craig Lawrie,Jaewon Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一构造：以单个不变多项式势的 Jacobian 环（Jacobian ring）编码 Argyres--Douglas 理论 $\mathcal{D}_p(ADE)$ 的 Higgs 分支（Higgs branch）与 Macdonald 指标（Macdonald index）。该势由相应 Coxeter 轨道空间的扭曲周期多项式得到，并可自然给出 Schur 与 Hall--Littlewood 极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Argyres--Douglas 理论 $\mathcal{D}_p(ADE)$ 的 Higgs 分支、指标以及 OPE 解耦关系（OPE decoupling）此前缺乏统一的代数刻画，本文旨在用一个统一的仿射概形（affine scheme）构造加以整合。

Method: 通过 Coxeter 轨道空间（Coxeter orbit space）的多项式扭曲周期（twisted period）构造单个不变多项式势，并证明其“syzygy-maximizing”性质；以该势的 Jacobian 环及其约化临界轨迹（reduced critical locus）描述 Higgs 分支，并用幂零根基（nilradical）的幂定义内蕴滤过以计算指标。经典类型给出行列式闭式表达，例外型 $E_6,E_7,E_8$ 给出显式势。

Result: 所得构造再现了猜想的 OPE 解耦关系，Higgs 分支表现为约化临界轨迹；对经典类型得到行列式形式的闭式表达，并给出 $E_6$、$E_7$、$E_8$ 的显式势。由内蕴滤过计算出的候选 Macdonald 指标在所研究例子中具有正确的 Schur 与 Hall--Littlewood 极限。

Conclusion: 该 Jacobian 环框架可扩展刻画幂零 Higgs 化（nilpotently Higgsed）理论。作者进一步猜想这些 Jacobian 环同构于边界可容许能级（boundary admissible level）下简单仿射顶点算子代数（VOA）的 Zhu $C_2$-代数及其量子 Drinfeld--Sokolov 约化。

Abstract: We propose a uniform construction of affine schemes encoding the Higgs branches and Macdonald indices of the Argyres--Douglas theories $\mathcal{D}_p(ADE)$, with $\gcd(p,h^\vee)=1$. The construction captures conjectural OPE decoupling relations via the Jacobian ring of a single invariant polynomial potential, obtained from a polynomial twisted period of the corresponding Coxeter orbit space. We show that these potentials are ``syzygy-maximizing'' in the first jet schemes and recover the Higgs branches as their reduced critical loci. We obtain closed determinant expressions for the classical types and explicit potentials for $E_6$, $E_7$, and $E_8$. Powers of the nilradical define an intrinsic filtration on the resulting Jacobian rings, from which we compute candidate Macdonald indices with the correct Schur and Hall--Littlewood limits, in the examples studied. Restricting the potentials to Slodowy slices extends the prescription to nilpotently Higgsed theories. We further conjecture that these Jacobian rings are isomorphic to Zhu's $C_2$-algebras of simple affine VOAs at boundary admissible levels and their quantum Drinfeld--Sokolov reductions.

</details>


### [234] [Construction of holomorphic SCFTs with minimal index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33165)
*Du Pei*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用三元码（ternary codes）与转移矩阵技术，构造了大量 N=1 超对称、Witten 指数为 1 的全纯顶点算子代数（holomorphic VOAs），并在 AI 编码代理辅助下获得了中心荷 c=12k 时的最小指数构造，从而回应了 TMF 576-周期性与 Segal-Stolz-Teichner 猜想相关的存在性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 是否存在任意正中心荷（central charge）c 的 N=1 超对称且 Witten 指数为 1 的全纯 VOA 一直是未解问题；其中 c=288 情形与 TMF 谱（TMF spectrum）的 576-周期性及 Segal-Stolz-Teichner 猜想密切相关。

Method: 沿 Gaiotto 与 Johnson-Freyd 提出的三元码方法进行扩展；用更小单元构成的链（chains of smaller cells）构建三元码以突破指数时间壁垒，并用转移矩阵（transfer-matrix）技术计算指数；借助对称轨道流形构造（symmetric orbifold construction）将 k=12、24 的问题归约到 k=6，部分搜索由 AI 编码代理完成。

Result: 构造出数千个此类 N=1 全纯 VOA；并对每个正整数 k 构造出中心荷 c=12k、指数为 24/gcd(24,k)（猜测为最小值）的全纯 N=1 VOA；同时建立了若干关于三元码及其指数的结果。

Conclusion: 借助三元码与 Landau-Ginzburg 轨道模型（Landau-Ginzburg orbifold models）的观察，论证三元码 VOA 的非手征双重（non-chiral doubles）总可以超对称地 gapped；结果支持相关存在性猜想并凸显计算方法与 AI 辅助搜索的作用。

Abstract: It has been an open question whether there exist holomorphic VOAs with N=1 supersymmetry and Witten index 1 for any positive central charge c. The existence of such a theory with c=288 is closely related to the 576-periodicity of the TMF spectrum via the Segal-Stolz-Teichner conjecture. Here we report the construction of thousands of such theories by extending the approach initiated by Gaiotto and Johnson-Freyd via ternary codes. In addition, for every positive integer k, we also construct holomorphic N=1 VOAs of central charge c=12k with index 24/gcd(24,k) -- conjectured to be the minimum -- in a computer-aided search carried out by AI coding agents. We overcome the exponential-time barrier by using chains of smaller cells to build ternary codes, whose indices can then be evaluated via a transfer-matrix technique, and we use a symmetric orbifold construction to reduce the problem of k=12 and 24 to that of k=6. Along the way, we also establish several results on ternary codes and their indices. Using an observation relating codes and Landau-Ginzburg orbifold models, we argue that the non-chiral doubles of the ternary code VOAs can always be gapped supersymmetrically.

</details>


### [235] [The Free Scalar as a Thermal Envelope for Three-Dimensional CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33179)
*Christopher P. Herzog,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出一个关于三维幺正共形场论（unitary three-dimensional conformal field theory）的普适不等式 $c_S/C_T \leq 16\pi\zeta(3)$，其中 $c_S$ 为热熵密度（thermal entropy density）系数、$C_T$ 为应力张量两点系数，自由标量（free scalar）取等号；并将其嵌入 $S^1\times H^2$ 上双曲热容（hyperbolic heat capacity）的上界猜想，在自由、大-$N$ 相互作用、全息（holographic）及蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）数据中检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是寻找三维共形场论中热熵与应力张量系数之间的普适约束（universal bound），类似已知的 $a/c$、$c_S/C_T$ 类不等式，用以刻画强耦合与全息理论的允许范围。

Method: 以自由标量理论作为基准提出平坦空间（flat-space）猜想，并将其推广为 $S^1\times H^2$ 上经 $C_T$ 归一化的双曲热容上界；在共形温度附近，对高温侧光滑且模哈密顿量（modular Hamiltonian）无边界项的理论，由共形对撞机（conformal collider）正性导出热容界；再以自由理论、大-$N$ 相互作用理论与全息理论进行检验。

Result: 自由标量满足等号；所检验的自由、大-$N$ 相互作用与全息理论均服从猜想；三维 Ising 模型与 $O(N)$ 模型的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）数据亦满足平坦空间界。

Conclusion: 结果支持“自由标量最大化 $C_T$ 归一化热熵密度/双曲热容”的图景，为非微扰 CFT 提供了新的可检验普适约束及由共形对撞机正性给出的部分证明路径。

Abstract: We conjecture that every unitary three-dimensional conformal field theory obeys $c_S/C_T\leq16πζ(3)$, where $s=c_ST^2$ is the thermal entropy density and $C_T$ the stress-tensor two-point coefficient, with equality for free scalars. We embed this in a stronger conjecture: on $S^1\times H^2$, the $C_T$-normalized hyperbolic heat capacity is bounded above by that of the free scalar for all $T\geq(2πR)^{-1}$. Near the conformal temperature, for theories smooth from the high-temperature side and without boundary terms in the modular Hamiltonian, the heat-capacity bound follows from conformal-collider positivity. We test the conjectures in free, interacting large-$N$, and holographic theories. Monte Carlo data for the three-dimensional Ising and $O(N)$ models also obey the flat-space bound.

</details>


### [236] [Spin-Induced Coordinate Noncommutativity of the Covariant Centroid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33236)
*Daniel Guimaraes Tedesco,Luiz Paulo Colatto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文以复合实现（composite realization）的 Tulczyjew-Dixon 质心（centroid）为出发点，确定其坐标对易子代数 $\{X^\mu,X^\nu\}=-S^{\mu\nu}/p^2$ 的严格成立域，并给出"两算符与三算符"判据以界定固定壳（fixed-shell）双矢量的有效构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该非对易双矢量（bivector）来自粒子自身横向自旋张量而非外部背景，但既有工作对其代数能否严格使用、在何种域上使用仅作定性限制；本文旨在把"仅对保壳矩阵元成立"的定性说法替换为精确判据。

Method: 把该括号及其康普顿尺度量级（Compton-scale magnitude）作为分析起点；通过 Jacobi 恒等式（Jacobi identity）闭合性要求 $p^{-2}$ 保持算符值，确定精确性域为凸类时内部（convex timelike interior）$\Omega_\mu$；再检验将 $p^2$ 冻结为壳值的替换在两算符与多算符层面的差异。

Result: 冻结 $p^2$ 在壳内不改变任何两算符对易子，故投影位置字典（projected position dictionary）、Berry 抵消（Berry cancellation）与端点横向性恒等式（endpoint transversality identity）均不受影响；但从三处插入起失效，残差为截断 Jacobiator $2\varepsilon^{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}W_\sigma/(p^2)^2$。

Conclusion: 该两对三判据明确了哪些基于固定壳双矢量 $\Theta_m$ 的构造仍然有效，并逐层检验了正能投影（positive-energy projection）、波包条件 Moyal 纤维（Moyal fibers）、康普顿路由和乐（Compton routing holonomy）与投影非结合积（projected nonassociative products）。除 QED 外无新预言；几何分支中物理振幅退化为 QED 振幅，横向匹配系数（transverse matching coefficients）尚未计算。

Abstract: The Tulczyjew-Dixon centroid of a massive spinning particle, built as a composite of canonical Poincaré variables, carries the exact coordinate algebra $\{X^μ,X^ν\}=-S^{μν}/p^2$, whose noncommutativity bivector is the transverse spin tensor of the particle itself rather than an external background. That bracket and its Compton-scale magnitude are already known, and we recover them here as the starting point of the analysis, not as a result. What the composite realization settles is the domain on which the algebra may be used. Closure of the Jacobi identity requires $p^{-2}$ to remain operator valued, and the exactness domain is the convex timelike interior $Ω_μ$, on which states supported on a single mass shell are distributional. We show that freezing $p^2$ to its shell value inside the centroid leaves every two-operator commutator unchanged on shell, so that the projected position dictionary, the Berry cancellation and the endpoint transversality identity are untouched, while the same substitution fails from three insertions onward, with residue the truncated Jacobiator $2ε^{μνρσ}W_σ/(p^2)^2$. This two-versus-three criterion replaces the qualitative restriction to shell-preserving matrix elements and states which constructions built on a fixed-shell bivector $Θ_m$ remain valid. We then follow the algebra through positive-energy projection, packet-conditioned Moyal fibers, Compton routing holonomy and projected nonassociative products, recording for each layer whether it falls on the safe side of the criterion. No prediction beyond QED follows. In the geometric branch the physical amplitude reduces to the QED amplitude, and the transverse matching coefficients remain uncomputed.

</details>


### [237] [Berends-Giele currents for higher derivative gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33693)
*Pratik Chattopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文推导了高阶导数标量量子电动力学（HDQED）与高阶导数杨-米尔斯理论（HDYM）中的Berends-Giele（BG）流，并用微扰子（perturbiner）形式与费曼图方法相互验证，进一步证明在壳无质量胶子情形下HDYM的BG流与纯杨-米尔斯流相同，而Merlin模式出现时高阶导数结构才显现实质效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶导数理论（如HDQED与HDYM）在极点结构、Ward恒等式与多粒子振幅方面缺乏系统的BG流与微扰子分析，本文旨在把BG递推方法推广到此类理论，并为高阶导数引力与壳外色-运动学对偶（colour-kinematics duality）提供基础。

Method: 从高阶导数拉格朗日量出发计算传播子与顶点，并讨论光子与标量的极点结构（pole structures）；以费曼图方式计算HDQED的BG流并在两点与三点上证明Ward恒等式，再用微扰子形式（perturbiner formalism）重算并比对；随后用BG递推推导HDYM的BG流，借助多粒子流（multi-particle currents）计算含Merlin模式的树图振幅。

Result: HDQED中微扰子方法与费曼图方法的BG流结果一致；HDYM的BG流在全部外线均为在壳（on-shell）无质量胶子时等于纯杨-米尔斯流，并以BG递推一般性证明、在低点数显式验证；含Merlin模式时高阶导数结构才真正进入。所有含一个Merlin粒子的树图振幅为零，三、四个Merlin粒子振幅与既有结果吻合。

Conclusion: BG递推可有效处理高阶导数规范理论，且在无质量在壳胶子扇区中HDYM与纯杨-米尔斯不可区分，差异集中于Merlin模。作者展望该方法用于高阶导数引力以及壳外色-运动学对偶的进一步研究。

Abstract: We derive Berends-Giele (BG) currents for higher derivative scalar QED (HDQED) and Yang-Mills (HDYM). In HDQED, we first compute the propagators and the vertices from the higher derivative Lagrangian and discuss the pole structures of the photon and the scalar. We then compute the BG currents using the Feynman diagram setup and prove the Ward identities for these currents at two and three points. Next, we used the perturbiner formalism to re-compute these currents and show that they are consistent with the Feynman diagram approach. In a similar fashion, we derive the BG currents in higher derivative Yang-Mills and find that remarkably, these currents are equal to pure Yang-Mills currents when all external on-shell legs are massless gluons. We prove this in all generality using the BG recursion and explicitly show this at lower points. However, the genuine higher derivative structure enters when one or many on-shell states are the so called massive Merlin modes. Using the multi-particle currents, we reproduce the result that all tree level amplitudes with one Merlin particle vanishes and the three and four Merlin amplitudes match the previously computed result. The future prospects of BG recursion for higher derivative gravity and off-shell colour- kinematics duality is also discussed.

</details>


### [238] [Fixed Points of the Yang-Mills Gradient Flow in the Vortex Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33874)
*A. M. Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 SU(2) 规范理论中中心涡旋在 Yang–Mills 梯度流下的演化，证明流动精确约化为径向剖面的线性扩散，多重绕数涡旋不稳定，最终流向绕数为 n mod 2 的 Abelian 核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心涡旋的动力学行为（尤其其在梯度流下的稳定性与最终归宿）缺乏解析刻画，本文旨在严格分析 SU(2) 中心涡旋在 Yang–Mills 梯度流（gradient flow）下的演化与稳定性。

Method: 对轴对称涡旋，将梯度流精确约化为径向剖面的线性扩散方程；对非对角扰动（off-diagonal perturbations）用一对径向薛定谔算子（radial Schrödinger operators）描述，结合变分计算与自相似变量（self-similar variables）分析不稳定模，并辅以标度论证。

Result: 高斯核心保持形状、宽度按 √(R²+4t) 增长且通量守恒，细涡旋是唯一有界不动点但非吸引子；冻结时刻的 Abelian 核心对任意绕数 n≥1 存在快子模（tachyonic mode），自相似变量下增长率（growth exponents）为 μ_{n,m}=n−1+m。

Conclusion: 多重绕数涡旋不稳定，基态涡旋精确边缘（marginally）稳定并保持 Abelian；标度论证排除横向平面内的静态涡旋，不稳定性具有几何意义——缩短增长回路 holonomy 在 SU(2) 中的路径并使绕数降低二。流动将每个涡旋驱向绕数为 n mod 2 的 Abelian 核心，非阿贝尔结构仅为多重绕数核心解绕（unwind）的瞬态。

Abstract: We study the Yang-Mills gradient flow of center vortices in SU(2) gauge theory. For axially symmetric vortices the flow reduces exactly to a linear diffusion equation for the radial profile: a Gaussian core keeps its shape while its width grows as $\sqrt{R^2+4t}$, the flux is exactly conserved, and the thin vortex is the unique bounded fixed point, though not an attractor. Off-diagonal perturbations are governed by a pair of radial Schrödinger operators, and a variational computation shows that, frozen at any instant, the Abelian core has a tachyonic mode for every winding $n\geq 1$. Since the core spreads while the mode grows, we pass to self-similar variables, where the unstable modes are known in closed form, with growth exponents $μ_{n,m}=n-1+m$. Multiply wound vortices are therefore unstable, while the fundamental vortex is exactly marginal and stays Abelian. A scaling argument rules out any static vortex in the transverse plane, and the instability acquires a simple geometric meaning that shortens the path traced in SU(2) by the holonomy of a growing loop, lowering the winding by two. The flow thus drives every vortex toward the Abelian core of winding $n \bmod 2$, with non-Abelian structure appearing only as the transient through which multiply wound cores unwind.

</details>


### [239] [Exact Stress Tensor Flows in Causal Electrodynamics: Courant-Hilbert Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33887)
*Zahra Pezhman,Komeil Babaei Velni,H. Babaei-Aghbolagh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 对仅含单一有量纲耦合的理论，二次不变量只依赖一个辅助变量；消去后得到与耦合、电磁不变量及 CH 参数无关的精确自洽流动 $\partial_\lambda\mathcal L=X\mathcal F(Y/X)$，并在广义 Born-Infeld、$q$-形变族、无 $\tau$ 最大值理论及因果 Russo-Townsend 对数理论四个算例中给出闭式结果，其奇点对应临界场、能量条件与强场行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文关注仅依赖单一有量纲耦合（dimensionful coupling）的理论中能量-动量张量（energy-momentum tensor）所控制的流动方程；此类理论中二次不变量 X 与 Y 仅依赖单一辅助变量，因此有望将 $\partial_\lambda\mathcal L$ 化简为仅含 Y/X 的自洽流动（autonomous flow）。研究动机在于给出该流动方程的精确结构，并检验其在若干具体非线性电动力学类理论中的闭式形式与物理含义。

Method: 在映射可逆的每个分支上消去辅助变量，得到 $\partial_\lambda\mathcal L=X\,\mathcal F(Y/X)$ 的精确自洽流动；随后将 $\mathcal F$ 按 $\sqrt{Y/X}$ 的幂展开，判别哪些流动由 X、Y 的整数幂构成、哪些需要半整数幂；并对四个理论逐一求出闭式（closed form）解及其解析结构。

Result: 四个算例分别为：广义 Born-Infeld 理论（generalized Born-Infeld theory），其流动在应力张量中呈二次型；$q$-形变族（$q$-deformed family），其展开的奇次系数在 $q=1/2$ 时为零、其余情形非零；无 $\tau$ 最大值理论（no-$\tau$-maximum theory）；以及因果 Russo-Townsend 对数理论（causal Russo-Townsend logarithmic theory），其流动涉及 Lambert $W$ 函数的下实分支并重现已知微扰结果。

Conclusion: $\mathcal F$ 的奇点（singular points）与各理论的临界场（critical fields）、能量条件（energy conditions）及强场行为（strong-field behavior）相关联，表明该流动方程可作为刻画非线性理论解析结构与物理性质的统一工具。

Abstract: It has been shown that for theories whose Courant-Hilbert function depends on a single dimensionful coupling, the energy-momentum tensor is controlled by the two combinations $τ\dot{\ell}$ and $\ell-τ\dot{\ell}$, so that the quadratic invariants $X=T_{μν}T^{μν}$ and $Y=(T^μ_μ)^2$ depend on a single auxiliary variable. Eliminating this variable in favour of $Y/X$ yields the exact autonomous flow $\partial_λ\mathcal L=X\,\mathcal F(Y/X)$, with no explicit dependence on the coupling, the electromagnetic invariants or the CH parameter, on every branch where the map is invertible. The expansion of $\mathcal F$ in powers of $\sqrt{Y/X}$ shows which flows are built from integer powers of $X$ and $Y$ and which require half-integer powers. In this paper, we work out four examples in closed form, each with its analytic structure and physical interpretation: the generalized Born-Infeld theory, whose flow is quadratic in the stress tensor; the $q$-deformed family, in which the odd coefficients of the expansion vanish at $q=1/2$ and are nonzero otherwise; the no-$τ$-maximum theory; and the causal Russo-Townsend logarithmic theory, whose flow involves the lower real branch of the Lambert $W$ function and reproduces the known perturbative result. The singular points of $\mathcal{F}$ are related to the critical fields, energy conditions, and strong-field behavior of each theory.

</details>


### [240] [Holographic entropy inequalities & typical states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33905)
*Guglielmo Grimaldi,Matthew Headrick*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该摘要提出围绕 Ryu-Takayanagi 公式（Ryu-Takayanagi formula）所蕴含的纠缠熵线性不等式，追问其普适性：是否在 RT 适用范围之外、乃至部分非全息态（non-holographic states）中仍成立，并综述已知答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由 Ryu-Takayanagi 公式可推出，AdS 量子引力（AdS quantum gravity）中的静态半经典态（static semiclassical states）其纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）满足一组特殊线性不等式；作者希望检验这些性质的一般性，即它们是否只是特定近似区域的产物。

Method: 属于综述性工作：以提出并梳理问题的方式组织，围绕三个层次的推广——离开 RT 适用区域（静态经典爱因斯坦引力，classical Einstein gravity）、以及可能的非全息态——回顾已有研究结论。摘要未涉及具体技术推导。

Result: 摘要未给出具体结论，仅说明将给出这些问题的动机并综述目前已知的答案；因此不存在可报告的具体结果。

Conclusion: 该工作定位为对全息纠缠熵不等式普适性问题的综述与问题梳理，强调这些不等式能否超越 RT 适用条件、甚至适用于部分非全息态仍是待明确的议题。

Abstract: As a consequence of the Ryu-Takayanagi formula, static semiclassical states in AdS quantum gravity share a set of special properties expressible as linear inequalities on their entanglement entropies. How general are these properties? Do they still hold when we move away from the regime of validity of RT, i.e. static states in classical Einstein gravity? Do they perhaps hold even for some class of non-holographic states? We will motivate these questions and review what's known about the answers.

</details>


### [241] [Revisiting monodromy defects in $\text{O}(N)$ conformal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33972)
*Jiechao Feng,Nayan Myerson-Jain,G. Shankar,Taige Wang,Meng Cheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用重整化群（renormalization group）、大N展开（large N expansion）与经典蒙特卡罗模拟（classical Monte Carlo simulation），系统研究了O(N)共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）中的部分单值性缺陷（partial-monodromy defect），给出了缺陷相的分类与稳定缺陷CFT的刻画，并数值验证了理论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单值性缺陷（monodromy defect）是带整体对称性（global symmetry）的扩展量子系统中的基本对象，而O(N) CFT刻画O(N)自发对称破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）转变的普适类（universality class），因此需要建立对其缺陷相的系统理论。

Method: 研究对象为部分单值性缺陷（对应变换仅作用于O(N)基本矢量场的部分分量）。作者结合重整化群（renormalization group）与大N展开（large N expansion）发展缺陷相的解析理论，并在改进格点模型（improved lattice model）中对O(3) CFT的单值性缺陷进行大规模经典蒙特卡罗模拟（classical Monte Carlo simulation）。

Result: 在一般时空维度3≤d<4中识别出多个缺陷CFT，其由缺陷附近矢量场（vector field）的边界条件（boundary condition）刻画。在d≈3时，大N极限下稳定缺陷CFT始终存在，其特征为（twisted）untwisted场分别取"standard"与"alternate"边界条件，对应缺陷关联被抑制与增强。数值提取的缺陷标度维度（defect scaling dimension）与理论定性一致。

Conclusion: 理论上稳定缺陷CFT的存在与边界条件刻画，得到O(3) CFT大规模数值模拟的定性支持，表明解析场论方法与格点数值方法在单值性缺陷研究上互为印证。

Abstract: Monodromy defects are fundamental objects in extended quantum systems with global symmetries. We study such defects in $\text{O}(N)$ conformal field theories (CFT), which describe the universality class of $\text{O}(N)$ spontaneous symmetry breaking transitions. In particular, we consider partial-monodromy defects, whose corresponding symmetry transformations only act on a subset of the fundamental $\text{O}(N)$ vector field. We develop an analytical theory of the defect phases, using both renormalization group and large $N$ expansion. In general (spacetime) dimensions $3\leq d<4$, multiple defect CFTs are identified, which are described by appropriate boundary conditions for the vector field near the defect. Near $d=3$, we show that in the large $N$ limit, a stable defect CFT always exists, characterized by the so-called ("standard") "alternate" boundary conditions for the (twisted) untwisted fields, whose defect correlations are (suppressed) enhanced. We then perform large-scale classical Monte Carlo simulations of monodromy defects in the O(3) CFT in an improved lattice model and numerically extract the defect scaling dimensions. The numerical results are in qualitative agreement with the stable defect CFT identified in the field theory.

</details>


### [242] [Lifshitz Phase of Quantum Gravity: The Infrared Fixed Point of the (2+1)D Unimodular Hořava-Chern-Simons Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34024)
*Maxim Zelenko,Dam Thanh Son*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出一种具有 \(w_\infty\) 规范对称性的 (2+1) 维非相对论引力理论（unimodular Hořava-Chern-Simons gravity），并分析其在大的 Chern-Simons 能级下的重整化群（renormalization group）流与可能的相变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于构造并理解具有 \(w_\infty\) 规范对称性的非相对论引力理论，考察其紫外（UV）与红外（IR）极限的标度性质以及相结构。

Method: 理论包含三个无量纲耦合（dimensionless couplings），其中一个是 Chern-Simons 能级（Chern-Simons level）\(\kappa\)；方法上通过分析大 \(|\kappa|\) 极限下的重整化群流来刻画理论行为。

Result: 在大 \(|\kappa|\) 下，理论从渐近自由的紫外不动点（asymptotically free UV fixed point）流向具有各向异性标度不变性（anisotropic scale invariance）的红外不动点，其动力学临界指数（dynamical critical exponent）为 \(z=2-\tfrac32|\kappa|^{-1}\)。

Conclusion: 作者推测，当 \(\kappa\) 低于某一临界值时，该理论会从标度不变相（scale-invariant phase）转变为禁闭相（confining phase），即发生相变；此为猜想性结论，尚待进一步验证。

Abstract: We consider a nonrelativistic gravity theory in (2+1) dimensions with $w_\infty$ gauge symmetry that we call unimodular Hořava-Chern-Simons gravity. The theory has three dimensionless couplings, one of which is the Chern-Simons level $κ$. At large $|κ|$, the theory flows from an asymptotically free UV fixed point to an IR fixed point with anisotropic scale invariance characterized by a dynamical critical exponent $z=2-\tfrac32|κ|^{-1}$. We speculate that unimodular Hořava-Chern-Simons gravity undergoes a phase transition from this scale-invariant phase to a confining phase when $κ$ drops below a critical value.

</details>


### [243] [Perturbative Holonomy Potentials and Vacua in Chiral Supersymmetric Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34240)
*Arash Arabi Ardehali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文建立了对ℝ³×S¹上手征超对称规范理论半经典真空扇区中微扰D-项数据的全局控制，给出一个单圈精确的D-项系数闭式，从而将三维Intriligator–Seigberg真空分类提升到ℝ³×S¹上的紧致完整 holonomy 空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维与四维紧致化下超对称规范理论的半经典真空结构缺乏对微扰D-项（包括Fayet–Iliopoulos参数与Chern–Simons耦合）的全局、一致控制，尤其在质量阈值附近与非平凡 holonomy 处，导致真空判定可能依赖局部信息而失真。

Method: 构造一个闭式、单圈精确（one-loop-exact）的D-项系数，在远离质量阈值处给出一致受控的领头势，并在阈值附近确定局域FI与Chern–Simons耦合；据此把三维Intriligator–Seigberg分类扩展到ℝ³×S¹的完整 holonomy 空间，并以阿贝尔模型与SU(2) Maruyoshi–Song理论作示例验证。

Result: 在简单阿贝尔模型中发现异常丰富的真空扇区集合；并在平空间上重现了SU(2) Maruyoshi–Song理论经R-扭曲约化得到Gang–Yamazaki理论的结果（该结果此前在S³×S¹上首次发现）。示例表明真真空可位于非平凡 holonomy 处，即便Coulomb切片D-系数取非零阈值，或虽在平凡 holonomy 处D-系数为零。

Conclusion: 全局匹配对真空判定至关重要：仅凭局部或平凡 holonomy 处的D-项信息不足，必须对完整 holonomy 空间上的D-项系数作统一控制，才能正确识别ℝ³×S¹上的半经典真空扇区。

Abstract: We establish global control of perturbative D-term data governing semiclassical vacuum sectors in chiral supersymmetric gauge theories on $\mathbb{R}^3\times S^1$. A closed-form, one-loop-exact D-term coefficient, including 4d Fayet--Iliopoulos (FI) parameters, gives a uniformly controlled leading potential away from massless thresholds and fixes local FI and Chern--Simons couplings near them. This allows lifting the 3d Intriligator--Seiberg classification of semiclassical vacua to $\mathbb{R}^3\times S^1$ across the compact holonomy space. The framework is showcased by finding a remarkably rich set of vacuum sectors in a simple abelian model and reproducing on flat space the R-twisted reduction of an $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ Maruyoshi--Song theory to the Gang--Yamazaki theory, first found on $S^3\times S^1$. The examples demonstrate the stakes of global matching: true vacua may lie at nontrivial holonomy even with nonzero threshold values of the Coulomb-slice D-coefficient, and despite vanishing D-coefficient at trivial holonomy.

</details>


### [244] [All-plus Yang--Mills and gravity wavefunctions in de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34261)
*Song He,Jiajie Mei,Yuyu Mo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为四维德西特空间（de Sitter space）中树级全正（all-plus）波函数提出“缝合表示”（sewing representation）：杨–米尔斯理论（Yang–Mills theory）的任意多重度公式表现为非交叉多边形剖分求和，爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）则由标记缝合树（sewing trees）的交替求和加局部引力陈–西蒙斯项（gravitational Chern–Simons term）构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在为四维德西特空间中树级全正波函数寻找显式且具有组合结构的表达式，使总能量极点（total-energy pole）缺失的性质与部分能量留数（partial-energy residues）的结构得以直接显现。

Method: 杨–米尔斯部分从自对偶杨–米尔斯解（self-dual Yang–Mills solutions）出发推导缝合表示；引力部分提出由标记缝合树交替求和与局部引力陈–西蒙斯项组成的公式，该局部项来源于自对偶爱因斯坦作用量（self-dual Einstein action），并由边界联络（boundary connection）导出其生成公式。

Result: 杨–米尔斯任意多重度公式为对非交叉多边形剖分（noncrossing polygon dissections）的求和，每个单元携带完整低点波函数的能量差，总能量极点缺失显式可见，部分能量留数由低点因子决定；引力公式中的局部项提供了内部能量割（internal-energy cuts）无法确定的边界数据，并给出至七点（seven points）的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 缝合表示以组合剖分与缝合树的形式统一刻画了规范理论与引力中树级全正波函数的结构，揭示了极点结构与边界数据的组织方式。

Abstract: We present sewing representations for tree-level all-plus wavefunctions in four-dimensional de Sitter space. For Yang--Mills theory, the arbitrary-multiplicity formula is a sum over noncrossing polygon dissections, with each cell carrying energy differences of a full lower-point wavefunction. The absence of a generic total-energy pole is manifest, while partial-energy residues are determined by the lower-point factors. We derive this representation from self-dual Yang--Mills solutions. For Einstein gravity, we propose an arbitrary-multiplicity formula consisting of an alternating sum over labelled sewing trees plus a local gravitational Chern--Simons term. This local term arises from the self-dual Einstein action and supplies boundary data not fixed by internal-energy cuts. We derive a generating formula for it from the boundary connection and give explicit expressions through seven points.

</details>


### [245] [Exact lattice bosonization of the double-scaled $c=1$ matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34376)
*Adwait Gaikwad,Gautam Mandal,Ajay Mohan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将矩阵量子力学中纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）的精确玻色化（exact bosonization）推广至 c=1 双标度矩阵模型（double scaled matrix model），证明其由有限非均匀格点上的玻色子描述，并据此得到有限的计算结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已表明，矩阵量子力学的纠缠熵可用晶格间距约 1/N 的格点玻色子做精确玻色化，该理论与基于费米子密度 ρ(λ) 的集体变量理论（collective variable theory）不同且更接近 Wigner 分布；本文意在进一步深化这一联系。

Method: 针对 c=1 双标度矩阵模型，即使 N 趋于无穷，该理论仍由有限且非均匀格点上的玻色子描述，格点间距约为 g_s(x) l_s，其中 g_s(x) = g_s cosech²(x/l_s) 为位置相关的弦耦合（string coupling）；由此显式推导纠缠熵以及费米子密度的一点与两点关联函数。

Result: 所得结果显式有限，而集体变量理论则给出发散结果。结果表明，纠缠熵与 ⟨ρ(λ)⟩ 等局域可观测量的短距离标度为唯一尺度 g_s(x) l_s，而各亏格（genus）下的 S 矩阵（S-matrix）等渐近可观测量的短距离标度为 l_s。

Conclusion: 论文阐明了上述两种短距离尺度如何产生，并讨论了其可能的 D0 膜（D0 brane）物理解释。

Abstract: In a recent paper by two of us, it has been shown that entanglement entropy (EE) of matrix quantum mechanics (or, equivalently, of a non-relativistic fermion model) has a natural interpretation in terms of an exact bosonization of the fermion theory in terms of a lattice boson with lattice spacing $\sim 1/N$ (where $N$ is the rank of the matrix, equal to the number of fermions). The theory of this lattice boson is distinct from a latticized version of the "collective variable'' theory which is formulated in terms of the fermion density $ρ(λ)$ ("collective variable''); it is, in fact, more closely related to the Wigner distribution of the fermions.
