In this paper, we develop this connection further for the $c=1$ double scaled matrix model. Even though $N$ becomes infinite, the double scaled theory is described by a boson on a finite and non-uniform lattice spacing, of order $\sim g_s(x) l_s$ (where $g_s(x) \equiv g_s {\rm cosech}^2(x/l_s)$ is a position dependent string coupling). We explicitly derive the $c=1$ EE, as well as the one and two-point functions of fermion density from the lattice boson. The results are explicitly finite, unlike the collective variable theory which is shown to yield divergent answers. Our results suggest that while local observables such as the EE and $\langle ρ(λ) \rangle$, are characterized by a unique short distance scale $g_s(x) l_s$, the asymptotic observables such as the S-matrix at every genus are characterized by a short distance scale $l_s$. We show how this arises and discuss a possible interpretation in terms of D0 brane physics.

</details>


### [246] [Spiralling into Dark Energy: Axio-Dilaton Attractors in Light of Current Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34380)
*Mario Ramos-Hamud*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究轴子-伸轻子（axio-dilaton）系统的宇宙学：伸轻子沿指数势（带多项式前因子修正）滚动，并通过动能函数与无质量轴子耦合；晚期吸引子沿场空间非测地线（non-geodesic）轨迹演化，使陡峭势也能实现加速膨胀，且被观测数据轻微偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦紧致化（string compactification）中伸轻子类模量（dilaton-like moduli）总是与轴子（axion）成对出现，二者通过弯曲场空间度规相互作用；需考察这种耦合系统能否在陡峭势下仍产生可行宇宙学。

Method: 采用动力系统分析（dynamical systems analysis）求解固定点与晚期吸引子；结合 DESI DR2 BAO、分箱 Pantheon 超新星（binned Pantheon supernovae）与 Planck 2018 距离先验（distance priors）开展贝叶斯分析（Bayesian analysis）。

Result: 晚期吸引子对应态方程 ω_φ=(λ-2ξ)/(λ+2ξ)，当 ξ≫λ 时陡峭势也可加速膨胀，且不存在物理的 phantom 固定点；95% 置信度下得 λ≳7–9、log₁₀ξ≳2.5，两参数存在简并（degeneracy）。模型较 ΛCDM 略受偏好，不同多项式修正尚不可区分。

Conclusion: 轴子-伸轻子系统通过非测地线吸引子可调和陡峭势与加速膨胀，与当前观测相容并轻微优于 ΛCDM；未来数据或可区分多项式前因子的具体形式。

Abstract: In string compactifications, dilaton-like moduli always come paired with axions, and the two fields interact through a curved field-space metric. We study the cosmology of such an axio-dilaton system, in which the dilaton rolls down an exponential potential corrected by a polynomial prefactor and couples to a massless axion through the kinetic function $W=e^{-ξφ_1/M_p}$. A dynamical systems analysis shows that the late-time attractor follows a non-geodesic trajectory in field space with equation of state $ω_φ=(λ-2ξ)/(λ+2ξ)$, so accelerated expansion is possible for steep potentials provided $ξ\ggλ$. No physical fixed point is phantom. A Bayesian analysis with DESI DR2 BAO, binned Pantheon supernovae and Planck 2018 distance priors gives $λ\gtrsim7$--$9$ and $\log_{10}ξ\gtrsim2.5$ at 95% confidence, together with a degeneracy between the two parameters. The model is slightly favoured over $Λ$CDM by current data, while the different polynomial corrections cannot yet be distinguished. This contribution is based on arXiv:2609.22539.

</details>


### [247] [Nonlinear Higher-Spin Theory in $O(4,1)$ de Sitter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34475)
*O. A. Gelfond,M. A. Vasiliev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出四种与含费米子的 $O(4,1)$ de Sitter 几何相容的高自旋规范理论（higher-spin gauge theory）版本，其中两版呈现 $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ 李超代数（Lie superalgebra）结构，另两版在玻色子部分保持幺正性（unitarity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是构造在 de Sitter（$dS_4$）背景下包含费米子且与 $O(4,1)$ 几何相容的高自旋规范理论，并探讨其超代数结构与幺正性问题，这与近期 Lang 与 Neiman 关于幺正性（unitarity）的提议及 Vasiliev 早期的 de Sitter 超引力描述相关。

Method: 方法为构造性建模：提出四种兼容 $O(4,1)$ de Sitter 几何的高自旋规范理论版本，其中两版采用作者之一最初为 de Sitter 超引力（de Sitter supergravity）提出的 $\mathbb{Z}_2\times \mathbb{Z}_2$ 李超代数结构；另两版类比 Lang–Neiman 方案，并引入赝对合（pseudo involution）以处理费米子。

Result: 结果为：两版模型具有 $\mathbb{Z}_2\times \mathbb{Z}_2$ 李超代数结构；两版在玻色子部分（bosonic sectors）为幺正；在含费米子时存在赝对合，暗示可能与 $dS_4$ 时间反演（time reversal）或时空加倍（space-time doubling）相关的（反）幺正性（(anti)unitarity）。

Conclusion: 结论是所提出的四种高自旋规范理论版本在 de Sitter 背景下同时容纳费米子与特定超代数结构，并借助赝对合给出（反）幺正性的可能解释路径，但其完整幺正性仍取决于时间反演或时空加倍的解释方式。

Abstract: Four versions of the higher-spin gauge theory compatible with $O(4,1)$ de Sitter geometry in presence of fermions are proposed. Two of them exhibit the structure of $\mathbb{Z}_2\times \mathbb{Z}_2$ Lie superalgebra proposed originally by one of the authors for the description of de Sitter supergravity \cite{Vasiliev:1985vtv}. Two of the proposed models are unitary in the bosonic sectors in analogy with the recent proposal by Lang and Neiman \cite{Lang:2026inn} and admit a pseudo involution in presence of fermions suggesting possible (anti)unitarity associated either with the time reversal in $dS_4$ or with the space-time doubling.

</details>


### [248] [Two-node Dualities from Elementary Dualities in 3d and 4d](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34752)
*Yuanyuan Fang,Sungjoon Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三维 \(\mathcal{N}=2\) 的“双节点”Seiberg 类对偶，类比于四维 \(\mathcal{N}=1\) 的 Intriligator–Leigh–Strassler（ILS）对偶，并通过椭球配分函数与 s-禁闭（s-confinement）方法的匹配提供强证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将四维 \(\mathcal{N}=1\) ILS 对偶的结构推广到三维 \(\mathcal{N}=2\) 理论，并检验“对偶生对偶（dualities from dualities）”这一思想能否仅由少数基本对偶导出新的对偶关系。

Method: 方法上，利用 s-禁闭（s-confinement）方法比较大量情形下的三维 \(\mathcal{N}=2\) 椭球配分函数（ellipsoid partition function）；推导仅假设两个基本三维 \(\mathcal{N}=2\) 对偶，即 Aharony 对偶与 Benini–Benvenuti–Pasquetti 对偶。此外，对原始 ILS 对偶给出基于逐节点对偶（nodewise dualities）的推导：对各个规范节点依次施加 Seiberg 与 Kutasov–Schwimmer 对偶，中间步骤进行希格斯化（Higgsing），随后积分掉有质量场。

Result: 结果显示，众多情形下三维 \(\mathcal{N}=2\) 椭球配分函数与所提双节点对偶的预言相符，构成该对偶的强证据；同时表明新对偶可由两个基本对偶导出，并为 ILS 对偶提供了逐节点构造式推导。

Conclusion: 结论是：三维 \(\mathcal{N}=2\) 双节点 Seiberg 类对偶得到配分函数层面的支持，且“对偶生对偶”的推导路径可行，ILS 对偶亦可由逐节点对偶序列重新导出。

Abstract: A new 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ two-node Seiberg-like duality, analogous to the 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ Intriligator--Leigh--Strassler~(ILS) duality, is proposed. As strong evidence, we check the agreement between the 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ ellipsoid partition functions for numerous cases using s-confinement method. Surprisingly, our derivation assumes only two elementary 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ dualities: Aharony and Benini--Benvenuti--Pasquetti, which reinforces the idea dualities from dualities. We also revisit the original ILS duality and provide a derivation based on nodewise dualities. In particular, we apply a sequence of Seiberg and Kutasov--Schwimmer dualities to individual gauge nodes, with Higgsing at an intermediate step, and subsequently integrate out the massive fields.

</details>


### [249] [Reparametrisation invariance, anomalies and pseudoscalar states in massless quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34858)
*Joshua Cesca,Archil Kobakhidze,Elden Loomes*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在规范理论（gauge theories）中，量子化会导致矢量流与轴矢流的散度出现反常（anomaly）歧义，可区分为相关的（relevant）与不相关的（irrelevant）两类。作者提出，通过对费米子场作轴矢重参数化（axial reparametrisation），可将整体轴矢对称性提升为规范冗余（gauge redundancy），并要求其量子层次守恒以消除不相关反常，从而导出轴矢反常（axial anomaly）及在拓扑非平庸时空上由希格斯机制（Higgs mechanism）产生的涌现传播自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化在电动力学及更一般的规范理论中引入歧义：矢量流、轴矢流或两者都可能获得反常散度，且这些反常可分类为与红外性质相关的“relevant”反常与源于重整化方案（renormalisation scheme）选择自由的“irrelevant”反常。论文旨在厘清这两类反常的关系并寻找消除不相关反常的物理原则。

Method: 对费米子场施加轴矢重参数化（axial reparametrisation），把整体轴矢对称性提升为规范冗余（gauge redundancy），并要求该规范对称性在量子层次上被保持，以此约束并剔除不相关反常（irrelevant anomalies）。

Result: 要求量子层次保持该轴矢规范对称性可消除不相关反常，仅留下一个非传播的赝标量场（pseudoscalar field），其作为拉格朗日乘子（Lagrange multiplier）强制实现通常的轴矢反常（axial anomaly）。进一步论证，在拓扑非平庸时空（尤其第二上同调（second cohomology）非平庸者）中，该轴矢规范对称性可发生希格斯机制（Higgs mechanism），使赝标量场成为涌现的传播自由度。

Conclusion: 轴矢规范对称性可作为筛选原理消除不相关反常并自然给出轴矢反常；在拓扑非平庸时空上，该对称性可经由希格斯机制产生涌现的传播赝标量自由度。论文还简要指出与共形反常（conformal anomaly）相关的类似涌现态。

Abstract: In electrodynamics, and more generally in gauge theories, quantisation introduces an ambiguity whereby the vector current, the axial current, or both may acquire an anomalous divergence. These anomalies can be classified as relevant, conventionally associated with the infrared properties of the theory, and irrelevant, arising from the freedom in the choice of renormalisation scheme. We show that, under a axial reparametrisation of the fermion fields, the global axial symmetry can be promoted to a gauge redundancy. Requiring this gauge symmetry to be preserved at the quantum level eliminates the irrelevant anomalies, leaving a non-propagating pseudoscalar field that acts as a Lagrange multiplier enforcing the familiar axial anomaly. We further argue that, on topologically non-trivial spacetimes, in particular those with non-trivial second cohomology, this axial gauge symmetry can undergo a Higgs mechanism, rendering the pseudoscalar field an emergent propagating degree of freedom. Finally, we briefly point out an analogous emergent state associated with the conformal anomaly.

</details>


### [250] [Graph-Based Algorithms for Worldline EFT Integrands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35007)
*Gregor Kälin,Jungwon Lim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出以图为中心（graph-centered）的算法，将世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory, WEFT）圈图被积函数的拓扑结构与代数结构分离为独立的“图-分子”（graph-numerator）对，并在 Rust 库 FeynRIR 中实现，可用于高效计算 WEFT 作用量变分、世界线运动方程及（含辐射反作用）冲量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统被积函数表示将传播子、动量路由与张量代数混杂在一起，导致张量缩并计算昂贵、积分族与扇区冗余；需要一种能解耦拓扑与代数结构、并优化分部积分（integration-by-parts, IBP）约化的表示框架。

Method: 将图与分子分离：图编码传播子、世界线源/汇顶点、动量路由、i0 规定与固有时依赖，分子仅保留最小代数与张量内容；提出广义 Berends-Giele 构造以减少张量缩并；通过世界线传播子“序列化”（serialization）与“自拍”子图（selfie-subdiagram）部分分式恒等式实现化简与规范化；并推广积分族与扇区概念，使各扇区携带自身最小的独立标量积集合，构成超完备全局族。

Result: 获得最小规范拓扑基；该图-分子表示自然推广了积分族及其扇区，通过充分利用扇区对称性减少所需积分扇区总数，并支持对传播子标号进行独立的 IBP 约化优化。算法已在 Rust 库 FeynRIR 中实现，可高效计算 WEFT 作用量变分、世界线运动方程及（含辐射反作用）冲量（impulse）的被积函数。

Conclusion: 图-分子分离表示为 WEFT 被积函数的构造、化简与 IBP 约化提供了统一且高效的框架，并在 FeynRIR 中得以落地实现。

Abstract: We present novel graph-centered algorithms for the construction and representation of worldline effective field theory (WEFT) integrands that separate the topological and algebraic structure of loop integrands into independent graph-numerator pairs. In this representation, the graph encodes all propagators, worldline source and sink vertices, momentum routing, i0 prescriptions, and proper-time dependence, while the numerator carries only the minimal algebraic, tensorial content. To construct integrands at fixed post-Minkowskian order we propose a generalized Berends-Giele construction that minimizes the number of expensive tensor contraction operations. We also discuss simplification and canonicalization strategies through "serialization" of worldline propagators and the application of "selfie"-subdiagram partial-fraction identities, yielding a minimal canonical topology basis. We further show that the graph-numerator representation naturally generalizes the notion of an integral family and its sectors, relevant for integration-by-parts (IBP) reduction: propagators are not constrained to a single master labeling, and each sector carries its own minimal set of independent scalar products, completing into an overcomplete global family. This setup minimizes the total number of required integral sectors through an optimal use of sector symmetries and enables independent optimization of propagator labeling for IBP reductions. The algorithms are implemented in the Rust crate FeynRIR, which is currently able to efficiently compute integrands for the variation of the effective WEFT action, the equations of motion for the worldlines and the (radiation-reacted) impulse.

</details>


### [251] [Relating Dark Dimension Size with Dark Energy via 5D Quintessence Potential and Casimir Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35124)
*Rashmikanta Mantry,Kazuyuki Furuuchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）框架下研究暗维度（dark dimension, DD）方案，其中暗维度尺寸由5D精质势（quintessence potential）与卡西米尔能（Casimir energy）的竞争决定，并系统考察5D与6D模型能否同时给出正确的4D暗能量（dark energy, DE）尺度与标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗维度方案需要具体的EFT实现，而最简单的5D模型虽将暗维度尺寸与4D真空能（vacuum energy, VE）联系起来，但其得到的4D真空能无法在标准宇宙学框架下充当4D暗能量，因而需寻找可行的推广模型。

Method: 先分析仅含无质量场贡献的5D模型，指出4D牛顿引力常数（Newton's gravitational constant）对胀子（radion）的依赖是真空能与暗能量不匹配的表现之一；进而构造4D爱因斯坦-希尔伯特（Einstein-Hilbert, EH）作用主要由量子效应诱导、使该依赖成为次主导的5D模型；再研究内部总体积固定的6D模型，分别讨论第5、6维尺寸同量级与存在大层级两种情形。

Result: 量子效应诱导EH作用的5D模型可给出有吸引力的宇宙学模型，实现开篇所述的暗维度尺寸决定机制；6D模型中，同量级情形下暗维度尺寸的物理起源被留给紫外完备化（UV completion），而大层级情形下5D精质势能标远高于观测推断的4D暗能量能标，需在4D中减去常数项进行调节。

Conclusion: 6D大层级情形所需的4D常数项减除阻碍了获得dSDC（de Sitter swampland conjecture）所预期的暗能量标度关系；因此，同时满足4D牛顿常数与胀子无关、并给出符合dSDC预期的暗能量标度关系，仍是暗维度EFT实现中的挑战。

Abstract: We explore effective field theory (EFT) realizations of the dark dimension (DD) scenario in which the size of the DD is determined by the competition between a 5D quintessence potential and the Casimir energy. When only contributions from massless fields are considered, the Casimir energy does not have a typical energy scale, and the 4D dark energy (DE) scale is set by the height of the 5D quintessence potential. The simple 5D model we first study has an attractive feature that the size of the DD is related to the 4D vacuum energy (VE), as outlined above. However, it turns out that the 4D VE thus obtained cannot work as the 4D DE in the standard cosmological setting. One of the discrepancies between the VE and the DE manifests in the radion dependence of the effective 4D Newton's gravitational constant. This leads us to explore a 5D model in which the 4D Einstein-Hilbert (EH) action is dominantly induced by quantum effects, leaving the radion dependence of the 4D Newton's constant subdominant. This model leads to an attractive cosmological model, realizing the mechanism explained in the beginning. We also explore a 6D model with the total internal volume fixed, to make the 4D Newton's constant independent of the radion. We first study a scenario in which the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions are of the same order. In this scenario, the physical origin of the size of the DD is left to its UV completion. Next, we turn to a scenario in which there is a large hierarchy between the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions. In this scenario, the energy scale of the 5D quintessence potential turns out to be much higher than the energy scale of the 4D DE as inferred from the cosmological observations, so we need to subtract a constant term in 4D to tune the 4D DE. This prevents us from obtaining the scaling relation for the DE expected in the dSDC.

</details>


### [252] [Exact Stress Tensor Flows in Causal Electrodynamics: Auxiliary Field Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35135)
*Zahra Pezhman,H. Babaei-Aghbolagh,Komeil Babaei Velni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在辅助场（auxiliary-field）表述下研究因果自对偶非线性电动力学（causal self-dual nonlinear electrodynamics）的非微扰 $T\bar T$ 型流，导出了精确流方程并给出核函数（kernel）与因果性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类理论具有拉格朗日量对两个洛伦兹不变量（Lorentz invariants）线性的简化结构，动力学由单辅助变量势 $W(\varphi)$ 编码；作者希望在此表述下系统刻画其非微扰 $T\bar T$ 型形变（$T\bar T$-like deformation）与 ModMax 型边缘形变。

Method: 无关（应力张量）形变表现为势的标度变换，边缘 ModMax 型形变表现为势真空的平移；据此精确推导流方程，证明无关流呈自治形式 $\partial_\lambda\mathcal L=X F(r)$，其中核 $F$ 仅依赖应力张量不变量之比 $r=-T^\mu_\mu/\sqrt{4X-Y}$，并给出由核反演辅助势的显式重构、线性辅助与哈密顿表述。

Result: 因果性给出微分不等式 $D(r)>1/(1-r)$，全局单值核要求 $r$ 沿辅助分支单调；讨论了精确流的偶性、势的 $\varphi$-宇称与拉格朗日量在电磁不变量中解析性之间的关系（含 ModMax 参数效应）；哈密顿流由同一精确流以相反符号支配；Born 自对偶（Legendre self-duality）、两个特征锥（characteristic cones）与能量条件均可用核表达，$r=(\varepsilon-p_\perp)/(\varepsilon+p_\perp)$ 具有直接物理意义。

Conclusion: 对广义 Born-Infeld、对数、q-形变（q-deformed）及 no-$\tau$-maximum 电动力学给出了精确 $T\bar T$ 型流的实例，表明该辅助场框架可统一处理多类非线性电动力学的形变。

Abstract: Causal self-dual nonlinear electrodynamics admits a simple auxiliary-field description: the Lagrangian is linear in the two Lorentz invariants of the field strength, and the dynamics is encoded in a potential $W(\varphi)$ of a single auxiliary variable. We study non-perturbative $T\bar T$-like flows of these theories in this formalism. The irrelevant (stress-tensor) deformation acts on the potential by a rescaling, while the marginal ModMax-type deformation translates its vacuum. We derive the resulting flow equations exactly, show that the irrelevant exact $T\bar T$-like flow takes the autonomous form $\partial_λ\mathcal L=X\,F(r)$, where the closed form kernel $F$ depends only on the ratio $r=-T^μ_μ/\sqrt{4X-Y}$ of stress-tensor invariants. We also give an explicit reconstruction of the auxiliary potential from a given kernel. Causality imposes the differential inequality $D(r)>1/(1-r)$, while a globally single-valued kernel requires the ratio $r$ to be monotonic along the auxiliary branch. We examine the relation between the evenness of the exact $T\bar T$-like flow , the $\varphi$-parity of the potential, and the analyticity of the Lagrangian in the electromagnetic invariants, including the effect of a marginal ModMax parameter. We construct the linear auxiliary and Hamiltonian formulations and show that the Hamiltonian flow is governed by the same exact $T\bar T$-like flow with the opposite sign. We also express Born's Legendre self-duality, the two characteristic cones, and the energy conditions in terms of the kernel. In particular, $r=(\varepsilon-p_\perp)/(\varepsilon+p_\perp)$ has a direct physical interpretation. Exact $T\bar T$-like flow s are obtained for generalized Born-Infeld, logarithmic, q-deformed, and no-$τ$-maximum electrodynamics.

</details>


### [253] [The one-loop QCD scale anomaly on ADE orbifolds as an index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35166)
*Herbie Warner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文借助扭量空间（twistor space）上的等变指标定理（equivariant index theorem），推导了轨道流形（orbifold）$\R^4/\Gamma$ 上 $\SU(N)$ 杨–米尔斯（Yang–Mills）理论的一圈标度反常（one-loop scale anomaly），并指出该反常可分解为 QCD $\beta$ 函数与定位于轨道流形不动点的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解轨道流形上规范理论标度反常的结构，特别是奇点（固定点）处是否出现区别于平坦空间的局域反常贡献，并将其与光滑 ALE 解消的引力外尔反常（gravitational Weyl anomaly）相联系。

Method: 采用扭量空间上的等变指标定理作为主要工具，对 $\R^4/\Gamma$（$\Gamma\subset\SU(2)$ 为有限子群）上的 $\SU(N)$ 杨–米尔斯理论计算一圈标度反常，并分析其在体区与固定点处的分解。

Result: 所得反常分解为两部分：QCD $\beta$ 函数贡献，以及定位于轨道流形不动点的局域项；该局域项继承光滑 ALE 解消的引力外尔反常在奇异退化下的集中效应，并额外包含一个不受保护的（unprotected）热核系数（heat-kernel coefficient）。

Conclusion: 结果表明轨道流形上的标度反常具有"体项 + 固定点局域项"的结构，且奇点处贡献与 ALE 空间的引力外尔反常及一个额外热核系数相关，为理解奇点对面反常的影响提供了指标定理框架下的刻画。

Abstract: We derive the one-loop scale anomaly of $\SU(N)$ Yang--Mills on the orbifold $\R^4/Γ$, with $Γ\subset \SU(2)$ a finite subgroup, from an equivariant index theorem on twistor space. The anomaly separates into the QCD $β$-function and a contribution localized at the orbifold fixed point. This localized term inherits the gravitational Weyl anomaly of the smooth ALE resolution, which concentrates under the singular degeneration, along with an additional unprotected heat-kernel coefficient.

</details>


### [254] [On memory effects in odd spacetime dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35283)
*Ana-Maria Raclariu,Binsong Xiong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在所有奇数时空维度中建立了一种新的电磁记忆效应（electromagnetic memory effect），该效应出现在库仑阶（Coulombic order）、与温伯格软定理（Weinberg soft theorem）直接相关，并依赖于光线上积分与 r→∞ 极限的非对易性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有电磁记忆效应研究多集中在特定维度，缺乏对任意高维的统一刻画；本文旨在所有奇数维建立新的记忆效应，并厘清其与软定理及大 r 展开次序的关系。

Method: 通过在大且有限距离处的零平面（null plane）上求解麦克斯韦约束（Maxwell constraint），对所有高维给出统一推导；并对无质量与有质量源分别计算库仑阶电磁记忆效应。

Result: 在奇数维中，计算记忆效应的光线上积分（lightray integral）与大 r 展开不可交换；仅当先取光线上积分再取 r→∞ 极限时效应存在，相反次序下效应消失，与已有结果一致。同时给出该效应与领头软光子定理傅里叶变换的关系及有限时间测量方案。

Conclusion: 该效应是恒定动量带电粒子所产生电磁场的普遍性质，并讨论了向引力情形的推广。

Abstract: We establish the existence of a new electromagnetic memory effect in all odd spacetime dimensions. This effect is a universal property of electromagnetic fields sourced by charged particles of constant momenta, appears at the Coulombic order and is directly related to the Weinberg soft theorem. We provide a unified derivation of this effect in all higher dimensions by evaluating the Maxwell constraint on a null plane at large, finite distance. We observe that in odd spacetime dimensions, the lightray integral computing the memory effect does not commute with the large-$r$ expansion. Our results necessarily assume that the lightray integral is taken prior to the $r \rightarrow \infty$ limit. The effect vanishes in the opposite order of limits, in agreement with previous results. We compute the Coulombic order electromagnetic memory effect for both massless and massive sources in all higher dimensions, explain its relation to the Fourier transform of the leading soft photon theorem and present a protocol for its measurement in a finite time parametrically larger than the distance of a detector from the sources. We conclude with comments on the generalization to gravity.

</details>


### [255] [Theory and Cosmology of Biverse Volume-Normalized Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35384)
*Siqing Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该博士论文提出“双宇宙体积归一化引力（biverse volume-normalized gravity）”框架，通过在五维体空间边界上放置我们的四维宇宙并取体积比趋于零的极限，统一了Tseytlin的体积归一化作用量与无鬼（ghost-free）大质量引力，从而给出宇宙学常数问题（cosmological constant problem）的一种新解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学常数问题体现为粒子物理预言的真空能密度（vacuum energy density）与宇宙学数据推断值之间约60个数量级的差异；已有的体积归一化作用量与引力子质量势两种思路各自受困于量子圈发散、第二度规来源不明及量子强耦合（strong coupling）问题。

Method: 受Tseytlin思想与弦论中AdS/CFT对应（AdS/CFT correspondence）启发，将(3+1)维宇宙置于(4+1)维体空间（bulk space）的边界上，并以两宇宙时空体积比趋于零的极限抑制大引力量子圈；同时把无鬼大质量引力并入该框架。

Result: 在该构造下，原本来路不明的第二度规张量（second metric tensor）自然成为体空间度规在边界上的拉回（pullback），其强耦合问题亦获解决；框架与所有已检验的粒子宇宙学（particle cosmology）及宇宙演化模型一致。

Conclusion: 双宇宙体积归一化引力为经典与量子引力提供统一描述，并对宇宙张量-标量比（tensor-to-scalar ratio）作出非平凡预言，即其值严格为零。

Abstract: This dissertation expounds the biverse volume-normalized gravity, a novel theoretical framework of classical and quantum gravity that provides an unusual solution to the cosmological constant problem, the 60-degree-of-magnitude discrepancy between the value of the vacuum energy density in our universe predicted by particle physics and that implied by cosmological data. In the literature, there exist two interesting ideas: 1) to use the "volume-normalized" action functional, the usual action of the particle Standard Model and general relativity but divided by the spacetime volume, first proposed by Tseytlin; 2) to generate the vacuum energy density dynamically by assigning a ghost-free massive potential to the graviton. However, each has a problem: Tseytlin's attempt suffers from diverging quantum loops and the massive gravity theory has a second metric tensor with questionable origin and the quantum strong coupling problem. Inspired by these two ideas, as well as the AdS/CFT correspondence in string theory, the biverse volume-normalized gravity extends Tseytlin's idea by placing our (3+1)-dimensional universe on the boundary of a (4+1)-dimensional bulk space. This new adaptation controls the large quantum gravity loops in the limit of vanishing ratio of the spacetime volume of our universe to that of the other universe. The ghost-free massive gravity can then be seamlessly incorporated, not only that its otherwise nebulous second metric tensor appears naturally as the pullback of the bulk metric on the boundary, but its strong coupling problem also solved. The biverse volume-normalized gravity is also consistent with all tested models of particle cosmology and the evolution of the universe, and nontrivially predicts that the tensor-to-scalar ratio of our universe is exactly zero.

</details>


### [256] [The topological vertex is finitely generated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35719)
*Kunal Gupta,Pietro Longhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文（note）表明，拓扑顶点（topological vertex）可由三个圆盘（disks）与三个环带（annuli）的边界相互作用（boundary interactions）生成，且该结论可推广至精化拓扑顶点（refined topological vertex）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑顶点可计算 $\mathbb{C}^3$ 中带三个环面拉格朗日（toric Lagrangian）膜的开拓扑 A 模型（open topological A-model）全亏格振幅，并经胶合（gluing）给出任意环面 Calabi-Yau 三维流形（toric Calabi-Yau threefold）的开闭弦振幅；本文旨在为其寻找更基本的生成机制。

Method: 将拓扑顶点表示为三个圆盘与三个环带的边界相互作用的生成结果，并考察该构造在精化拓扑顶点情形下的推广。

Result: 证明了拓扑顶点由三个圆盘和三个环带的边界相互作用生成，且相应结论对精化拓扑顶点同样成立。

Conclusion: 拓扑顶点具有以圆盘与环带边界相互作用为基础的生成描述，该描述在精化（refined）情形下保持有效，为顶点构造提供了更基本的表述。

Abstract: The topological vertex computes all genus amplitudes for the open topological A-model in $\mathbb{C}^3$ with branes on three toric Lagrangians and, via gluing, both open and closed string amplitudes on any toric Calabi-Yau threefold. In this note we show that the topological vertex is generated by boundary interactions of three disks and three annuli. A generalization of this result is shown to hold for the refined topological vertex.

</details>


### [257] [Compositional Operational Rank, Smooth Realizability, and Dimensional Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35764)
*Nikolay D. Tulupov,Alexander M. Tishin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出“闭包忠实性（closure-faithfulness）”等充分条件，使组合式操作秩（compositional operational rank）依次被实现为光滑积分叶的维数与可观测的Fisher信息秩，并为高维理论的维度约化提供操作判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维理论需区分微观描述维度与物理观测者可及方向数，二者关联不仅依赖维度约化（dimensional reduction），还需在不同描述层级间一致地保持操作独立性（operational independence）。

Method: 建立操作秩先作为光滑积分叶（smooth integral leaf）维数、再作为Fisher信息秩被保持的充分条件：核心为闭包忠实性，配合实可表示性、常秩、对合性（involutivity）与观测者/尺度兼容性；测量层面另需主识别条件，即独立指定的观测者像局部重合于主积分分布，且容许协议满足局部分辨、有限标记混合闭包与局部常值结果支撑。

Result: 在上述条件下，操作秩可由有限复合Fisher信息协议实现，且结论对任意有限秩成立。r=4时给出高维理论维度约化的条件性操作判据：可行约化须保持相关操作依赖结构，而非仅产生四个有效坐标；量子硬件负对照（negative controls）显示，受限Fisher秩可在更广的预设测量协议下掩盖不同的更大秩。

Conclusion: 该框架约束维度约化的操作结果，而不对微观状态空间施加普适的四维界限；维度约化的有效性取决于操作依赖结构是否被忠实保持。

Abstract: Higher-dimensional theories distinguish the dimensionality of a microscopic description from the number of directions accessible to a physical observer. Relating these notions requires not only dimensional reduction, but also a consistent preservation of operational independence across different levels of description.
  We establish sufficient conditions under which a compositional operational rank is preserved first as the dimension of a smooth integral leaf and then as an observable Fisher-information rank. The key requirement is closure-faithfulness: the smooth realization must preserve the dependency relations of the operational closure, rather than merely its total rank. Together with real representability, constant rank, involutivity, and observer/scale compatibility, this yields a local integral leaf whose dimension equals the operational rank.
  A further primary-identification condition is required at the measurement level. If an independently specified observer image coincides locally with the primary integral distribution and the admissible protocols provide local resolution, finite labeled-mixture closure, and locally constant outcome support, then the same rank can be realized by a finite composite Fisher-information protocol.
  The construction holds for arbitrary finite rank. At r = 4 it provides a conditional operational criterion for dimensional reduction in higher-dimensional theories: a viable reduction must preserve the relevant operational dependency structure, not merely produce four effective coordinates. Quantum-hardware negative controls further show that the same restricted Fisher rank can conceal different larger ranks under a broader pre-specified measurement protocol. The framework therefore constrains the operational outcome of dimensional reduction without imposing a universal four-dimensional bound on microscopic state spaces.

</details>


### [258] [Quantum Field Theory on the Tangent Bundle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35778)
*Joachim Herrmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在切丛（tangent bundle）上构建量子场论框架，将引力与内部规范结构分置于仿射切纤维几何（affine tangent-fiber geometry）的不同扇区，并由此导出分数夸克电荷（fractional quark charge）与标准QCD结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于寻求将粒子物理（particle physics）的规范结构与引力（gravity）纳入统一的几何框架，利用非零零矢量稳定子（stabilizer of a nonzero null vector）的投影表示（projective representation）来解释垂直谱与内部对称性的起源。

Method: 方法基于切丛上的量子场论构造：平移扇区给出余标架（coframe）并联系远平行引力（teleparallel gravity）；以中心扩展欧氏群（centrally extended Euclidean group）E^c(2)构造约化垂直谱问题，结合弱SU(2)、局域化色SU(3)_c、三重旋转覆盖（threefold rotation cover）上的整体商（global quotient），并建立物质与规范场量子化、BRST对称性（BRST symmetry）及反常约束（anomaly constraints）。

Result: 在所述投影假设下，该构造重现了观测到的分数夸克电荷分配，色扇区具有标准局域QCD结构，包括单圈β函数（one-loop beta function）与渐近自由（asymptotic freedom）；另提出额外规范玻色子与暗物质态作为假想扇区。

Conclusion: 该框架在共同几何背景下关联了粒子物理与引力的部分选定结构，但进一步的动力学与唯象学（phenomenology）发展仍属开放问题。

Abstract: We develop a quantum-field-theoretical framework on the tangent bundle in which gravitational and internal gauge structures are associated with different sectors of an affine tangent-fiber geometry. The translational sector supplies the coframe and its relation to teleparallel gravity. A reduced vertical spectral problem is based on the centrally extended Euclidean group \(E^{c}(2)\), motivated by projective representations of the stabilizer of a nonzero null vector. Twofold multiplicities of equivalent vertical representations in selected left-chiral sectors admit weak \(SU(2)\) actions, while an assumed threefold quark multiplicity admits a locally gauged color \(SU(3)_c\) symmetry. A proposed global quotient correlates the color center with vertical rotations on a threefold rotation cover. Together with the selected chiral spectrum and anomaly conditions, this construction reproduces the observed fractional quark charge assignments. We formulate matter and gauge-field quantization, BRST symmetry, and anomaly constraints. Under the stated projection assumptions, the color sector has the standard local QCD structure, including its one-loop beta function and asymptotic freedom. Additional gauge bosons and dark-matter states are proposed as hypothetical sectors. We also formulate a structural action for possible couplings between teleparallel torsion and additional Lorentz-sector potentials. The framework relates selected structures of particle physics and gravity within a common geometric setting; their further dynamical and phenomenological development remains open.

</details>


### [259] [How to get an attractive model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35798)
*Joseph McGovern*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文给出一个四阶 Calabi-Yau 微分算子（fourth-order Calabi-Yau differential operator），指出其在此前算子列表中未被收录，并强调其具有清晰的几何解释，且证据表明其存在秩二吸引子（rank-two attractor），据此猜测它给出一个新的具有模性（modularity）性质的 Calabi-Yau 三维流形（Calabi-Yau threefold）例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 Calabi-Yau 微分算子的系统整理（tabulations）可能遗漏了该算子，而其几何意义明确、且疑似具备秩二吸引子结构，值得提请关注。

Method: 方法为构造/给出一个四阶 Calabi-Yau 微分算子，并对其几何解释与吸引子性质提供证据（摘要未详述具体技术手段）。

Result: 得到一个新算子，此前列表未收录；该算子具有清晰的几何解释，并有证据支持其拥有秩二吸引子。

Conclusion: 作者 conjecturally 提出，该算子对应一个新的 Calabi-Yau 三维流形，其模性性质类比于有理数域（\(\mathbb{Q}\)）上椭圆曲线（elliptic curve）的模性；此结论仍属猜想层面。

Abstract: We give a fourth-order Calabi-Yau differential operator, which seems to have been missed by earlier tabulations. We draw attention to this operator because of its clear geometric interpretation and evidence that it possesses a rank-two attractor, conjecturally giving a new example of a Calabi-Yau threefold with modularity properties analogous to those of an elliptic curve over $\mathbb{Q}$.

</details>

### [260] [Hitchin's $α$-invariant and positive sectional curvature on homotopy $9$-spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33474)
*Yang-Hui He,Ziran Liu,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明光滑同伦 9-球（homotopy 9-sphere）存在正截面曲率（positive sectional curvature）黎曼度量当且仅当其 Hitchin α-不变量（Hitchin α-invariant）为零，亦等价于其存在正数量曲率（positive scalar curvature）度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于建立同伦球上正截面曲率度量的存在性与曲率不变量/正数量曲率可解性之间的精确判据；摘要未展开具体背景动机。

Method: 方法为数学证明（proof），以 Hitchin α-不变量为零作为核心判据，并论证其与正数量曲率度量存在性的等价关系；摘要未给出具体技术细节。

Result: 主要结果为三维条件的等价刻画：同伦 9-球上正截面曲率度量的存在性 ⇔ Hitchin α-不变量消失 ⇔ 正数量曲率度量的存在性。

Conclusion: 在 9 维情形下，正截面曲率与正数量曲率对光滑同伦球的可解性由同一不变量完全决定，即 Hitchin α-不变量；据摘要，该结论为充要条件。

Abstract: We prove that a smooth homotopy $9$-sphere admits a Riemannian metric of positive sectional curvature if and only if its Hitchin $α$-invariant vanishes, or equivalently if and only if it admits a Riemannian metric of positive scalar curvature.

</details>


### [261] [Volume of area-bounded PGL3-Hitchin moduli space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33417)
*Qiongling Li,Beibei Liu,Weixu Su,Zhe Sun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究 $\mathrm{PGL}_3$-Hitchin 模空间中 Hilbert--Busemann 面积有界子集的 Goldman 辛体积（Goldman symplectic volume），并给出显式多项式上界及面积加权体积估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带边界数据 $\mathbf L$ 的 $S_{g,m}$ 上无标记凸 $\mathbb{RP}^2$ 结构所构成的 $\mathrm{PGL}_3$-Hitchin 模空间，需要刻画面积有界子集的体积增长行为，以补充该模空间的定量几何测度信息。

Method: 取 canonical Hilbert--Busemann 面积（Hilbert--Busemann area）不超过 $t$ 的子集，用 Goldman 辛形式（Goldman symplectic form）测量其体积，其中上界显式依赖于 $g$、$m$、$t$ 以及最大总根边界长度（largest total-root boundary length）$\lambda$（$m=0$ 时取 $\lambda=0$）。

Result: 该子集的 Goldman 辛体积不超过 $C_{g,m}(t+\lambda^2)^{8g-8+3m}$。

Conclusion: 由上述体积上界可直接推出 $e^{-s\cdot\mathrm{Area}}$ 加权体积（weighted volume）的显式上界。

Abstract: For the $\mathrm{PGL}_3$-Hitchin moduli space of unmarked convex $\mathbb{RP}^2$ structures on the surface $S_{g,m}$ with fixed boundary data $\mathbf L$ when $m>0$, we investigate the subset whose canonical Hilbert--Busemann area is bounded above by $t$. We show that the Goldman symplectic volume of this subset is at most $C_{g,m}(t+λ^2)^{8g-8+3m}$, where $λ$ is the largest total-root boundary length for $m>0$ and is set to $0$ for $m=0$. As a consequence, We obtain the explicit upper bound for the $e^{-s\cdot\mathrm{Area}}$-weighted volume.

</details>


### [262] [Price's law for scalar waves on 1+4 dimensional subextremal Myers-Perry spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33812)
*Pieter-Bart Peters*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明1+4维双自旋参数Myers–Perry时空在全次极端范围内逆度规哈密顿流具有法向双曲捕获性，并据此在实模态稳定性假设下给出标量波方程的Price律及显式领头阶渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Myers–Perry时空是1+3维Kerr时空在更高维、多自旋参数情形下的直接推广，其波动方程解的晚期衰减规律（Price律）尚待严格刻画，需要先建立相应的动力学捕获结构。

Method: 对度规逆$g_{\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b},\mathfrak{m}}^{-1}$对应的哈密顿流（Hamiltonian flow）证明在全次极端范围$(|\mathbf{a}|+|\mathbf{b}|)^2<2\mathfrak{m}$内具有法向双曲捕获（normally hyperbolic trapping）；再在实模态稳定性（real mode stability）假设下分析标量波方程$\Box_{g}\varphi=0$解的渐近行为。

Result: 得到带显式领头阶项的Price律：解满足$\varphi(t,x)=ct^{-3}+\mathcal{O}(t^{-4+})$，其中常数$c\in\mathbb{C}$可显式计算。

Conclusion: 在双自旋参数Myers–Perry时空中，法向双曲捕获为波动方程晚期衰减提供了统一机制，所得显式渐近式将Kerr时空中的Price律推广至1+4维双自旋情形。

Abstract: We consider Myers--Perry spacetimes $(M,g_{\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b},\mathfrak{m}})$ in $1+4$ dimensions with two spin parameters $(\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b})\in\mathbb{R}^2$, which are a direct generalization of the well known Kerr spacetime in $1+3$ dimensions. We prove that the Hamiltonian flow associated to $g_{\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b},\mathfrak{m}}^{-1}$ is normally hyperbolically trapped for the full subextremal range \[
  (|\mathbf{a}|+|\mathbf{b}|)^2<2\mathfrak{m}. \] As an application, we prove a Price law with explicit leading order terms for solutions $φ(t,x)$ of the scalar wave equation \[
  \Box_{g_{\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b},\mathfrak{m}}}φ=0 \] under the assumption of real mode stability. Our precise asymptotics imply that $φ(t,x)=ct^{-3}+\mathcal{O}(t^{-4+})$, where $c\in\mathbb{C}$ is an explicitly computable constant.

</details>


### [263] [Determinants of Dirichlet and Neumann Laplacians on Constant Curvature Surfaces with Geodesic Polygonal Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35383)
*Victor Kalvin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文将常曲率紧黎曼曲面上带测地多边形边界的Dirichlet与Neumann拉普拉斯算子的zeta正则化行列式，用其闭圆锥双重（closed conical doubles）上的拉普拉斯行列式表示。公式对亏格（genus）与边界分支数无限制，包含显式的角点因子与上同调行列式线上Reidemeister范数与L^2范数之商。作为应用，给出欧氏多边形Aurell-Salomonson公式的严格证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带边界曲面上拉普拉斯行列式（Laplacian determinant）缺乏显式表达，而闭曲面情形已有较多已知结果；动机在于借助圆锥双重（conical double）将边界问题化归为闭曲面情形，并推广至任意亏格与边界分支数。

Method: 结合相对BFK粘合公式（relative BFK gluing formula）与Lück、Vishik关于带流形上解析挠率（analytic torsion）与组合挠率（combinatorial torsion）的比较定理；公式中显式给出角点因子（corner angle factors）及上同调行列式线（determinant line of cohomology）上Reidemeister范数与L^2范数的商。

Result: 得到对亏格与边界分支数无限制的Dirichlet与Neumann zeta正则化行列式（zeta regularized determinants）表达式；严格证明了欧氏多边形的Aurell-Salomonson公式；并借助双重曲面的已知公式，给出球面与双曲三角形（spherical and hyperbolic triangles）及其他测地多边形的显式行列式。

Conclusion: 边界拉普拉斯行列式可由闭圆锥双重上的行列式配合角点因子与挠率范数比精确刻画，该方法统一处理了欧氏、球面与双曲情形的多边形区域。

Abstract: We express the zeta regularized determinants of the Dirichlet and Neumann Laplacians on compact Riemann surfaces with constant curvature metrics and geodesic polygonal boundary in terms of the Laplacian determinant on their closed conical doubles. The formulae apply without restrictions on the genus or number of boundary components. They involve explicit corner angle factors and a quotient of the Reidemeister and $L^2$ norms on the determinant line of cohomology. The proof combines a relative BFK gluing formula with the comparison theorem for analytic and combinatorial torsion on manifolds with boundary, due to Lück and Vishik. As an application, we give a rigorous proof of the Aurell-Salomonson formula for Euclidean polygons. We also obtain explicit determinants for spherical and hyperbolic triangles and certain other geodesic polygons from known formulae for their doubles.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [Sliding Networks: A Family of Residual Structures Preserving Feature Learning and Feature Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33840)
*Datong Zhou,Blake Bordelon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种联合缩放配方（joint scaling recipe），在固定比值 N♭/(N♯L)→α 的条件下同时保持局部最大特征学习（feature learning）与残差流中的特征多样性（feature diversity）；理论与残差-MLP 实验支持收敛到 SDE 极限，但语言模型实验显示在固定参数量下重新分配宽度与深度仍存在性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度缩放（depth-scaling）方法被迫在保留局部最大特征学习与保留由通道间随机差异产生的特征多样性之间二选一，缺乏同时兼顾二者的方案。

Method: 考察一种联合缩放配方：以残差宽度 N♭、隐藏宽度 N♯ 与深度 L 构成的极限比值 N♭/(N♯L)→α 为核心量，并引入相关初始化（Half Copy）以部分放松形状约束；辅以理论分析与残差-MLP 实验，并在语言模型上做对比实验。

Result: 理论与残差-MLP 实验支持在固定正 α 下收敛到具有局部最大特征学习的 SDE 极限，且该极限下隐藏计算可在宽度与深度之间重新分配；但语言模型实验表明，在固定参数量的重新分组下性能存在差异。

Conclusion: 结果区分了“逼近某一极限（ODE 或 SDE）”与“为获得更优性能而缩放”两件事：收敛到极限并不等同于性能提升。

Abstract: Deep learning models tend to perform best at large widths and depths. The growing interest in scaling laws, especially in the language modeling domain, has motivated procedures for scaling up network widths and depths in order to preserve stability and computational efficiency of training dynamics. Existing depth-scaling approaches force a choice between retaining locally maximal \emph{feature learning} and preserving a form of \emph{feature diversity} in the residual stream arising from random differences between channels. We examine a \textit{joint scaling recipe} that preserves both. With residual width $N_\flat$, hidden width $N_\sharp$, and depth $L$, the key quantity is, by default, the limiting ratio $N_\flat/(N_\sharp L)\toα$, while correlated initialization (Half Copy) can partially relax the resulting shape constraints. Theory and residual-MLP experiments support convergence at fixed positive $α$ to an SDE limit with locally maximal feature learning. Hidden computations can then be redistributed between width and depth while preserving this limit, like turning melody into harmony and back. Language-model experiments nevertheless show performance differences under regrouping at fixed parameter count. These results distinguish approximating a limit (ODE or SDE) from scaling for better performance.

</details>


### [265] [When is early stopping beneficial under label noise? A phase diagram for kernel gradient flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33846)
*Fabrizio Boncoraglio,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在含不可约标签噪声（irreducible label noise）的分类任务中，早停（early stopping）并非总是必要：当模型的高阶核成分相对线性成分足够弱时，插值（interpolation）端点可取得优于任何有限训练时刻的泛化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为早停用于抑制对标签噪声的拟合，因此当训练标签中存在正比例的固有不可预测样本时，最优测试误差应出现在插值之前。论文检验这一直觉是否成立，并探讨后期退化在何种条件下消失。

Method: 在 CIFAR10 上引入随机标签样本进行实验，比较固定特征模型、浅层模型与更大更深卷积网络的表现；并在可解析设定下，对合成噪声分类任务分析核梯度流（kernel gradient flow），推导高维学习曲线（learning curves）并给出相图（phase diagram）。

Result: 实验显示固定特征与浅层模型出现测试误差的后期恶化，而大型深层卷积网络即便已完全正确分类随机标签，也基本不再需要早停。理论分析得到由线性核成分与高阶成分平衡所控制的相图：一个区域内泛化在有限时刻最优、早停必要；另一区域内泛化持续改善直至插值，但端点在不同核间次优；在边界处插值端点取得所考虑核族与全部训练时刻中的最低泛化误差。

Conclusion: 即使存在大量不可约标签噪声，插值也可能优于早停。神经网络实验如何使学习到的表示（learned representations）获得类似的有利几何结构，仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Early stopping is commonly used to prevent fitting label noise from degrading generalization. Does this mean that, when a positive fraction of the training labels is intrinsically unpredictable, the best test error must occur before interpolation? Experiments on a CIFAR10 classification task with randomly labeled examples motivate the opposite possibility: although fixed-feature and shallow models exhibit late-time deterioration of the test error, the need for early stopping essentially disappears in larger, deeper convolutional networks even after the random labels have all been classified correctly. We determine when late-time deterioration disappears entirely in a solvable setting. For kernel gradient flow on a synthetic noisy classification task, we derive sharp high-dimensional learning curves and obtain a phase diagram controlled by the balance between the linear kernel component that learns the shared relation between inputs and labels and the higher-order components that enable fitting the label noise. In one regime, generalization is optimized at finite time and early stopping is necessary. In another, generalization improves until interpolation, but the endpoint is suboptimal across kernels. At the boundary, the interpolating endpoint attains the lowest generalization error across the considered kernel family and all training times. Thus, even with extensive irreducible label noise, interpolation can outperform early stopping. The neural-network experiments leave open how learned representations acquire an analogous favorable geometry.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [Chemical Substitution as a Route to Anomalous Surface Superconductivity in NiBi$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33463)
*Anupam Barik,Subhadip Chakrabarty,Achintya Low,Sudip Pal,Setti Thirupathaiah*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在Pb取代的NiBi₃（NiBi₂.₈Pb₀.₂）中观察到约8.1 K的表面关联超导态（surface-associated superconducting state），约为体相转变温度4.1 K的两倍，并表现出二维特征及远超常规上限的Hc3/Hc2比值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在体相超导材料中稳定二维超导性，可为研究不同维度超导凝聚体（superconducting condensates）之间的相互作用提供独特途径。

Method: 通过Pb取代NiBi₃制备NiBi₂.₈Pb₀.₂，利用电阻测量确定转变温度，并以迈斯纳响应（Meissner response）、比热异常（specific-heat anomaly）、临界场角度依赖、非线性电流—电压特性及BKT转变（Berezinskii–Kosterlitz–Thouless transition）特征进行表征。

Result: 样品在T_c,onset*≈8.1 K出现电阻起始；高温转变处无对应迈斯纳响应与比热异常，临界场角度依赖呈尖点状并符合二维Tinkham模型，且表面超导在体相上临界场之外仍持续存在，Hc3/Hc2最高约4.3，远超Saint-James–de Gennes值1.695。

Conclusion: Pb取代可在NiBi₃中稳定异常增强的低维超导态，为研究体相超导体边界处的超导性提供平台。

Abstract: Stabilizing two-dimensional superconductivity in a bulk superconducting material provides a unique route to exploring the interplay between superconducting condensates of different dimensionalities. Here, we present evidence for a distinct surface-associated superconducting state in Pb-substituted NiBi$_3$. NiBi$_{2.8}$Pb$_{0.2}$ exhibits a resistive onset at $T_{c,\mathrm{onset}}^{*}\approx8.1$~K, nearly twice the bulk superconducting transition temperature of approximately 4.1~K. The absence of a corresponding Meissner response or specific-heat anomaly at the higher-temperature transition supports its non-bulk origin. A cusp-like angular dependence of the critical field, well described by the two-dimensional Tinkham model, together with nonlinear current--voltage characteristics and signatures of a Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless transition, establishes the low-dimensional character of this superconducting state. Remarkably, the surface-associated superconductivity persists well beyond the bulk upper critical field, reaching a maximum ratio of $H_{c3}/H_{c2}\approx4.3$, far exceeding the conventional Saint-James--de Gennes value of 1.695. These results demonstrate that Pb substitution can stabilize an anomalously enhanced low-dimensional superconducting state in NiBi$_3$, providing a platform for investigating superconductivity at the boundary of a bulk superconductor.

</details>


### [267] [Chiral Superconductivity Adjacent to a Wigner Crystal in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33808)
*Ke Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究在菱面体四层石墨烯（rhombohedral tetralayer graphene）中探讨驱动电子结晶（Wigner crystal）的关联效应能否在邻近金属区诱导超导。自洽Hartree–Fock计算给出公度三角Wigner晶体态；结合parquet与Bethe–Salpeter方程构造交叉对称的粒子–空穴顶点后，发现近结晶区关联增强产生有效吸引，支持手性f波配对，且超导仅局域于晶体旁狭窄金属区，与实验观察到的"超导套（superconducting sleeve）"定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：促使电子结晶（electron crystallization）的关联是否也能在相邻金属相中产生超导（superconductivity）。研究以菱面体四层石墨烯（rhombohedral tetralayer graphene）为平台，检验排斥性库仑相互作用（repulsive Coulomb interactions）下这一可能性。

Method: 采用自洽Hartree–Fock（self-consistent Hartree–Fock）计算识别公度三角Wigner晶体态；为研究金属侧配对（pairing），利用耦合parquet方程与Bethe–Salpeter方程构造交叉对称的粒子–空穴顶点（crossing-symmetric particle–hole vertex），并纳入超越极化泡屏蔽（polarization-bubble screening）的交换顶点修正（exchange vertex corrections）。

Result: 在接近结晶的区域，增强的电子关联产生有效吸引，倾向于手性f波配对（chiral f-wave pairing）。超导被限制在与晶体相邻的狭窄金属区域，与菱面体石墨烯中绝缘区附近观察到的超导套（superconducting sleeve）定性一致。

Conclusion: 手性配对的出现及其在相图中的位置可归结为有利于结晶的电子关联。该结果将超导套的成因与Wigner晶体关联联系起来，为菱面体石墨烯中的配对机制提供统一解释。

Abstract: Can the correlations favoring electron crystallization also generate superconductivity in the neighboring metal? We investigate this possibility in rhombohedral tetralayer graphene with repulsive Coulomb interactions. Self-consistent Hartree--Fock calculations identify a commensurate triangular Wigner-crystal state. To investigate pairing on the metallic side, we construct a crossing-symmetric particle--hole vertex using coupled parquet and Bethe--Salpeter equations, incorporating exchange vertex corrections beyond polarization-bubble screening. Near crystallization, the enhanced electronic correlations generate an effective attraction favoring chiral $f$-wave pairing. Superconductivity is confined to a narrow metallic region adjacent to the crystal, qualitatively consistent with the superconducting sleeve observed near an insulating regime in rhombohedral graphene. These results connect the emergence of chiral pairing and its location in the phase diagram to the electronic correlations favoring crystallization.

</details>


### [268] [Scattering and Schmid-Bulgadaev Transition in Qubits Based on Josephson Junction Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33814)
*Sviatoslav D. Krasin,Alexey Radkevich,Emil Rizvanov,Andrew G. Semenov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文对约瑟夫森结链中等离子体模式在边界SQUID上的散射进行理论分析，重点处理非微扰超导区，指出需对特定图进行重求和以正确计入SQUID势的涨落重整化，并给出频移与品质因数的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于约瑟夫森结的量子比特与结链系统可在实验上动态探测Schmid-Bulgadaev量子相变（Schmid-Bulgadaev transition）两侧，近期实验已实现等离子体模式在边界SQUID上的散射测量，亟需相应的理论刻画，尤其是非微扰超导区（non-perturbative superconducting regime）的行为。

Method: 采用解析理论方法分析结链中等离子体模式（plasma modes）在边界SQUID上的散射；在非微扰超导区进行特定的图论重求和（diagrammatic resummation），以正确计入SQUID势的涨落重整化（fluctuation renormalization）。

Result: 获得模式频移（frequency shifts）与品质因数（quality factors）的解析表达式，二者共同构成对散射的完整描述；非微扰超导相表现为频移随SQUID磁通呈幂律依赖（power-law dependence），以及品质因数随模式频率呈非单调依赖。

Conclusion: 非微扰超导相的特征可通过频移的幂律标度与品质因数的非单调行为加以识别，相关预言可在现有低温实验（low-temperature experiments）中直接检验。

Abstract: A Josephson junction subject to an ohmic dissipative environment undergoes a zero-temperature quantum phase transition called Schmid-Bulgadaev transition form an insulating state to a superconducting state upon increasing the impedance of the environment above the resistance quantum. Superconducting qubits based on Josephson junction chains represent a system where it is possible to dynamically explore both sides of this transition by probing the scattering of individual plasma modes of the junction chain on a SQUID at the boundary which was achieved in recent experiments. Here, we provide a theoretical analysis of the scattering in the system paying special attention to the non-perturbative superconducting regime. We find that in this regime, a specific diagrammatic resummation must be performed in order to correctly account for fluctuation renormalization of the SQUID's potential. We find analytic expressions for the modes' frequency shifts and quality factors which together provide a full description of scattering. We argue that the non-perturbative nature of the superconducting phase manifests itself through a power-law dependence of the frequency shifts on the SQUID flux and through non-monotonous dependence of the quality factors on the mode frequency. Our predictions may be readily verified in modern low-temperature experiments.

</details>


### [269] [Field misalignment effect on the in-plane magnetoresistance in the high-temperature superconductor FeSe/SrTiO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34168)
*Jie Liu,Zihan Cui,Xiaorong Liu,Yanzhao Liu,Ziqiao Wang,Jian Wang,Yi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作系统测量了SrTiO3衬底上超薄FeSe薄膜的面内磁阻（in-plane magnetoresistance）角度依赖，发现重新安装同一样品改变磁场失配（field misalignment）会导致两种定性不同的角度依赖，并提出几何模型对该效应进行解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面内磁阻的方位角依赖是探测超导体旋转对称性破缺（rotational symmetry breaking）的有力手段，对理解超导配对对称性（pairing symmetry）与共存序至关重要；但在面内与面外临界场各向异性强的二维或层状超导体中，面内磁阻测量对磁场失配极为敏感，构成测量难点。

Method: 对生长于SrTiO3衬底上的超薄FeSe薄膜开展系统性电输运测量（electrical transport measurements），并通过重新安装同一FeSe薄膜来改变磁场失配，比较不同失配条件下的面内磁阻角度依赖；同时构建考虑磁场失配效应的几何模型（geometric model）对实验结果进行拟合描述。

Result: 在超导转变区域观察到两种定性不同的面内磁阻角度依赖，表明该测量对磁场失配极端敏感；所观察到的各向异性面内磁阻可由考虑失配效应的几何模型很好地重现。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个实用框架，用于区分磁场失配效应与真实的面内旋转对称性破缺信号，从而为二维或层状超导体中面内旋转对称性破缺的研究奠定测量基础。

Abstract: The azimuthal angular dependence of the in-plane magnetoresistance provides a powerful probe for detecting rotational symmetry breaking in superconductors, which has significant implications for understanding the superconducting pairing symmetry and the intertwined orders. However, in two-dimensional or layered superconductors with strong anisotropy between in-plane and out-of-plane critical fields, measuring the in-plane magnetoresistance is particularly challenging as it is extremely sensitive to the field misalignment. Here we perform systematic electrical transport measurements on ultrathin FeSe films grown on SrTiO3 substrates. By remounting the same FeSe film to change the field misalignment, two qualitatively different angular dependences of the in-plane magnetoresistance are observed in the superconducting transition region, indicating the extreme sensitivity of the measurement to field misalignment. The observed anisotropic in-plane magnetoresistance can be well captured by our geometric model considering the field misalignment effect. Our work provides a practical framework for disentangling the misalignment effect and investigating the in-plane rotational symmetry breaking in two-dimensional or layered superconductors.

</details>


### [270] [Tilt-Controlled Multi-Gap Superconductivity in Type-II Weyl Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34315)
*Mingyu Lee,Myungjun Kang,Sangmo Cheon*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文针对具有 \(D_4\) 对称性的时间反演对称倾斜外尔半金属模型，分类其对称允许的格点配对通道，指出倾斜角作为微观调控参数可重分配配对磁化率并控制多能隙超导的出现与层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二类外尔半金属（type-II Weyl semimetals）中强倾斜外尔锥（Weyl cones）产生电子与空穴口袋共存，构成多带超导（superconductivity）的天然环境；但倾斜如何控制超导能隙数目与相对大小尚不清楚。

Method: 对具有 \(D_4\) 对称性、时间反演对称的简单倾斜外尔半金属模型，分类对称允许的格点配对通道（on-site pairing channels），并分析吸引型格点相互作用下的领头不稳定性（leading instability）与能隙结构。

Result: 全对称 \(A_1\) 通道被确定为领头不稳定性，支持全开隙的双能隙态（two-gap state）；倾斜在两大费米面（Fermi-surface）扇区之间重分配分带配对磁化率（band-resolved pairing susceptibility），而非简单缩放总态密度。倾斜充分增强次要通道后，稳健的次要能隙出现，甚至可在较弱有效耦合下超过主能隙，而能隙间耦合使两能隙锁定于共同临界超导温度。

Conclusion: 外尔锥倾斜是控制多能隙超导性（multi-gap superconductivity）出现及其层级（hierarchy）的关键机制。

Abstract: In type-II Weyl semimetals, strongly tilted Weyl cones produce coexisting electron and hole pockets, a natural multi-band setting for superconductivity, yet how the tilt controls the number and relative size of the superconducting gaps remains unclear. Here we classify the symmetry-allowed on-site pairing channels of a simple time-reversal symmetric tilted Weyl-semimetal model with $D_4$ symmetry and identify the fully symmetric $A_1$ channel as the leading instability for attractive on-site interactions, supporting a fully gapped two-gap state. The tilt acts as a microscopic control parameter, redistributing the band-resolved pairing susceptibility between the two Fermi-surface sectors rather than simply rescaling the total density of states. Once the tilt sufficiently enhances the secondary channel, a robust secondary gap develops and can exceed the primary gap even at weaker effective coupling, while inter-gap coupling locks both gaps to a common critical superconducting temperature. The Weyl cone tilt thus controls the emergence and hierarchy of multi-gap superconductivity.

</details>


### [271] [Universal Scaling of the Little-Parks Oscillation Amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34318)
*Yuelin Shen,Zixuan Huang,Xiaoying Xu,Wei Qin,Yufan Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出一个纳入热涨落（thermal fluctuations）的普适理论框架，用于解释低维超导环（superconducting rings）中 Little-Parks 振荡振幅的温度依赖性，并在多种超导材料与器件几何上得到定量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Little-Parks 效应（Little-Parks effect）对建立通量量子化（fluxoid quantization）与电子配对证据至关重要，且已成为探测超导序的相敏感手段；但其振荡振幅的显著温度依赖无法由传统平均场 Ginzburg-Landau 描述解释，长期缺乏完整理论说明。

Method: 构建纳入热涨落（thermal fluctuations）的低维超导环普适理论框架，并据此分析多种超导体的 Little-Parks 测量数据，提炼振荡振幅的标度行为（scaling）。

Result: 所得标度关系在广泛材料范围与器件几何下定量再现了温度依赖的振荡振幅，解决了理论与实验之间的长期矛盾。

Conclusion: 热涨落是理解 Little-Parks 振荡振幅温度依赖的关键因素，该框架为相敏感超导探测提供统一的理论描述。

Abstract: The Little-Parks effect played a pivotal role in establishing fluxoid quantization in doubly connected superconductors and providing early evidence for electron pairing, central to BCS theory. More recently, it has emerged as an important phase-sensitive probe of superconducting order. Yet the amplitude of the Little-Parks oscillation has never been fully understood theoretically, as the conventional mean-field Ginzburg-Landau description does not account for its pronounced temperature dependence. Here, we develop a universal theoretical framework incorporating thermal fluctuations in reduced-dimensional superconducting rings. By analyzing Little-Parks measurements across multiple superconductors, we show that the resulting scaling quantitatively captures the temperature-dependent oscillation amplitudes over a broad range of materials and device geometries, resolving the long-standing discrepancy between theory and experiment.

</details>


### [272] [Phase fluctuations dominated superconductivity in a thick Granular Boron-Doped Diamond film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34319)
*Sulagna Dutta,Subhajit Chatterjee,Vivas Bagwe,Pritam Das,S. Anantha Bharathi,C. Nikhil,M. S. Ramachandra Rao,Pratap Raychaudhuri*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过穿透深度测量证实，1.2 μm厚的颗粒状硼掺杂金刚石薄膜是研究三维超导体中长波相位涨落的理想模型体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究三维超导体中超流刚度极低时长波相位涨落（long-wavelength phase fluctuations）所起的作用，并寻找合适的模型体系。

Method: 采用双线圈互感技术（two-coil mutual inductance technique）直接测量1.2 μm厚颗粒状硼掺杂金刚石薄膜的穿透深度（penetration depth），并结合输运性质分析与Ambegaokar-Halperin相位涨落理论进行描述。

Result: 薄膜在T_c ~ 3 K处呈现尖锐超导转变；超流密度（superfluid density, n_s）在T_c以下大温度范围内线性变化，超低温下转为T^2依赖；低温穿透深度λ(0) ~ 8.8 μm，超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）低至0.5-0.8 K。

Conclusion: 颗粒状硼掺杂金刚石薄膜被确立为研究三维超导体中长波相位涨落作用的优秀模型体系。

Abstract: We investigate the superconducting properties of a 1.2-μm-thick granular boron-doped diamond film, where tightly packed grains form a disordered network of Josephson junctions. The film exhibits a sharp superconducting transition at a critical temperature, T_c ~ 3 K. Its transport properties are well described by the Ambegaokar-Halperin theory of phase fluctuations. Direct measurements of the penetration depth using a two-coil mutual inductance technique reveal classic phase-fluctuation-dominated behavior: below T_c, the superfluid density (n_s) varies linearly over a wide temperature range - a hallmark of thermal phase fluctuations - before crossing over to a T^2 dependence at ultra-low temperatures. Both the large low-temperature penetration depth, λ(0) ~ 8.8 μm, and the slope of the linear ns variation in the thermal regime reveal a remarkably low superfluid stiffness in the range of 0.5-0.8 K. These results establish granular boron-doped diamond films as an excellent model system for investigating the role of long-wavelength phase fluctuations in three-dimensional superconductors.

</details>


### [273] [Nodeless d-wave pairing in monolayer FeSe revealed by intra-unit-cell Josephson interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34382)
*Runxin Li,Yuchen Huo,Jiong Mei,Ming-Qiang Ren,Miaomiao Wang,Quanxing Wei,Fengyu Yao,Maowen Zheng,Qing Qu,Cui Ding,Hongtao Yuan,Zhuoyu Chen,Wei-Qiang Chen,Kun Jiang,Qi-Kun Xue,Yu-Jie Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用超导Nb针尖在单层FeSe/SrTiO3上实现原子尺度约瑟夫森干涉测量，揭示单胞内相位结构，并指认其为无节点d波配对的指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约瑟夫森干涉测量依赖人工结几何与晶向来提取相位信息，无法在单个晶胞（unit cell）内部直接分辨配对态的相位结构，因而需要一种局域的相位敏感探针。

Method: 以超导Nb针尖构建原子尺度约瑟夫森干涉测量（Josephson interferometry），测量单层FeSe/SrTiO3的局域约瑟夫森响应空间分布，并结合微观计算进行对比。

Result: 尽管准粒子谱（quasiparticle spectrum）完全有能隙，局域约瑟夫森响应呈显著的单胞内（intra-unit-cell）图样：极大值位于Fe-Fe键中心，沿Fe-Se方向存在稳健的节点线（nodal lines）。

Conclusion: 节点线源于符号相反的配对分量之间约瑟夫森隧穿振幅的相消干涉（destructive interference），微观计算复现该空间图样并将其识别为无节点d波（nodeless d-wave）配对特征；该工作确立了单胞内约瑟夫森干涉测量作为非常规超导体配对对称性的局域相位敏感探针。

Abstract: The Josephson effect provides a direct phase-sensitive probe of superconducting order and has played a central role in identifying unconventional pairing symmetries1-6. Conventional Josephson interferometry, however, extracts phase information through engineered junction geometries and crystallographic orientations, rather than by resolving the internal phase structure of the pair state within a single unit cell. Here, we use a superconducting Nb tip to realize atomic-scale Josephson interferometry in monolayer FeSe/SrTiO3. Despite a fully gapped quasiparticle spectrum, the local Josephson response exhibits a striking intra-unit-cell pattern, with maxima centred on Fe-Fe bonds and robust nodal lines along the Fe-Se directions. We show that these nodal lines arise from destructive interference between Josephson tunnelling amplitudes associated with opposite-sign pairing components. Microscopic calculations reproduce the observed spatial pattern and identify it as a fingerprint of nodeless d-wave pairing. Our results establish intra-unit-cell Josephson interferometry as a local phase-sensitive probe of pairing symmetry in unconventional superconductors.

</details>


### [274] [Magnetic susceptibility and superfluid density of inhomogeneous superconducting films: the Tc-mosaic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34796)
*Ruslan Prozorov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文通过随机 Voronoi 拼接模型论证：在完全能隙（fully gapped）超导薄膜中，局域临界温度 $T_c$ 的非均匀分布（mosaicity）难以产生可被常规整体/部分样品测量观测到的 $T$-线性伦敦穿透深度 $\lambda(T)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体样品（whole-sample）与互感部分样品（mutual-inductance part-sample）测量通常假设超导均匀，以由磁化率 $\chi$ 提取伦敦穿透深度 $\lambda(T)$；但空间分辨探针显示许多薄膜在微米尺度上非均匀。作者因此检验局域 $T_c$ 分布是否会伪装成线节点（line-nodal）、$T$-线性的 $\lambda(T)$。

Method: 将薄膜建模为随机 Voronoi 拼接（random Voronoi mosaic），各畴的 $T_c$ 在 $T_{c,\min}$ 与 $T_{c,\max}$ 之间分布，畴内采用 s 波（并对比 d 波）伦敦响应，考察屏蔽（screening）起始温度与 $\lambda(T)$ 的函数形式。

Result: 可探测屏蔽仅在逾渗温度（percolation temperature）$T_p$ 以下出现，转变随 $T_c$ 展宽而变宽。s 波畴中，只要 $T_{c,\min}>0$，$\lambda(T)$ 保持激活型；仅当 $T_{c,\min}=0$ 才呈幂律 $\Delta\lambda\propto T^n$（$n\approx1.7$），但此时屏蔽起始降至 $T_p=T_{c,\max}/2$。向 $T_c\to0$ 加权可令 $n\approx1.2$，却使 $T_p$ 低于 $T_{c,\max}$ 一个数量级以上。d 波畴中线性能保持留，且在 $T_{c,\min}=0$ 时相对斜率增强近四倍。

Conclusion: 若整体或互感部分样品测量中屏蔽起始温度位于 $T_{c,\max}$ 的数倍因子以内，则所观测到的 $T$-线性 $\lambda(T)$ 不可能源自完全能隙超导体中的 $T_c$ 拼接非均匀性。

Abstract: Whole-sample and mutual-inductance part-sample measurements of the linear magnetic susceptibility, $χ$, of superconducting films are routinely used to obtain the London penetration depth, $λ(T)$, assuming uniform superconductivity. However, spatially resolved probes show that many films of current interest are inhomogeneous on micrometer scales. Here we ask whether a distribution of local transition temperatures in a fully gapped film can produce an apparent line-nodal, $T$-linear $λ(T)$. The film is modeled as a random Voronoi mosaic of domains with $T_c$ distributed between $T_{c,\min}$ and $T_{c,\max}$ with s-wave (also d-wave, for comparison) London response inside each domain. We show that detectable screening appears only below the percolation temperature, $T_p$, and the transition broadens with the spread of $T_c$. For s-wave domains, $λ(T)$ remains activated for any $T_{c,\min}>0$. Only for $T_{c,\min}=0$ does it become a power law, $Δλ\propto T^n$ with $n\approx1.7$, but then the onset of screening drops to $T_p=T_{c,\max}/2$. Weighting the $T_{c}$ distribution towards $T_c\to0$ (more low-$T_{c}$ domains) can bring $n$ closer to 1 ($n\approx1.2$), but simultaneously pushes the apparent transition, $T_p$, more than an order of magnitude below $T_{c,\max}$. For d-wave domains, the linear term survives, and its relative slope is enhanced almost fourfold for $T_{c,\min}=0$.
  Hence, a $T$-linear $λ(T)$ measured in a film with screening onset within a factor of a few of $T_{c,\max}$ by whole-sample or mutual-inductance part-sample measurements cannot originate from $T_c$ mosaicity in a fully gapped superconductor.

</details>


### [275] [Momentum-resolved thermodynamics and barrier-free order-parameter collapse in spin-orbit-coupled superconductors under a Zeeman field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35381)
*Xusheng Wang,Gaomin Tang,Lianyi He,Shuai-Hua Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文通过自由能的动量分解（momentum decomposition）研究具有普遍时间反演对称自旋轨道耦合（SOC）的自旋单态s波超导体，揭示SOC垂直分量提升临界场、平行分量趋向Pauli极限行为，并解释Rashba与弱Rashba Ising超导体中序参量在临界场的无势垒突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）可提升洁净超导体临界Zeeman场至Pauli极限以上，但其在临界场处序参量突变（collapse）的机制尚缺乏统一的动量层面解释。

Method: 构建零温自由能（zero-temperature free energy）的动量分解方法，处理具有一般时间反演对称SOC的自旋单态s波超导体，并应用于Rashba超导体及弱Rashba Ising超导体。

Result: 垂直于Zeeman场的SOC分量提升临界场并抑制一阶（first-order）特征；平行分量则倾向Pauli极限的热力学行为。竞争的角度贡献使自由能景观（free-energy landscape）近乎平坦。

Conclusion: 临界场处序参量突变源于近乎平坦的自由能景观，形成无势垒（barrier-free）的序参量突变，区别于传统Pauli极限下的一阶相变。

Abstract: Spin-orbit coupling (SOC) can enhance the critical Zeeman field of clean superconductors beyond the Pauli limit. We develop a momentum decomposition of the zero-temperature free energy to investigate spin-singlet $s$-wave superconductors with general time-reversal-symmetric SOC. The SOC component perpendicular to the Zeeman field enhances the critical field and suppresses first-order features, whereas the parallel component favors Pauli-limited thermodynamic behavior. Applying this framework to Rashba superconductors and Ising superconductors with weak Rashba SOC, we identify the origin of the abrupt collapse of the superconducting order parameter at the critical field. Competing angular contributions produce a nearly flat free-energy landscape, allowing a barrier-free order-parameter collapse distinct from the conventional Pauli-limited first-order transition.

</details>


### [276] [Local Broadband Gigahertz Spectroscopy via SQUID-on-Tip DC Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35737)
*Senne Vervoort,Lukas Nulens,Julia Baumgarten,Daan B. Boltje,Jessalyn DeVine,Alejandro V. Silhanek,Joris Van de Vondel,Kaveh Lahabi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作将微波光谱、纳米级磁传感与形貌成像集成于敲击模式SQUID-on-tip显微镜中，通过约瑟夫森非线性响应产生的Shapiro台阶实现仅用直流读出对GHz微波信号的定量测量，并在超导共面波导谐振器上验证了局域谱与输运测量的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导微波电路的行为受其局域电磁环境影响，但标准微波测量无法分辨决定器件行为的局域场与器件特征。因此需要一种能够在纳米尺度上同时探测微波响应、形貌与磁结构的测量手段。

Method: 在单一敲击模式SQUID-on-tip显微镜（tapping-mode SQUID-on-tip microscope）中集成微波光谱（microwave spectroscopy）、纳米级磁传感与形貌成像。利用非线性约瑟夫森响应（Josephson response）将局域GHz激发转换为Shapiro台阶（Shapiro steps），从而以纯直流读出定量测量微波频率信号及其激发强度；并结合电磁仿真分析探针耦合对空间分辨率的限制。

Result: 应用于超导共面波导谐振器（coplanar waveguide resonator）时，局域谱重现了微波透射测量中观察到的功率依赖共振与非线性分岔（nonlinear bifurcation）；空间分辨测量在同步记录形貌与静态磁特征（包括涡旋（vortices）与迈斯纳屏蔽电流（Meissner screening currents））的同时绘制了微波响应分布。电磁仿真揭示了超导探针耦合如何限制空间分辨率并指导局域探测器设计。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个将微波电路动力学与超导器件局域结构及磁环境相关联的测量平台（platform），为研究局域电磁环境对超导微波电路的影响提供了新途径。

Abstract: Superconducting microwave circuits are shaped by their local electromagnetic environment, yet standard microwave measurements do not resolve the local fields and device features that govern their behavior. Here, we combine microwave spectroscopy, nanoscale magnetic sensing, and topographic imaging in a single tapping-mode SQUID-on-tip microscope. The nonlinear Josephson response converts local GHz excitations into Shapiro steps, enabling quantitative measurements of microwave frequency signals and their excitation strength using only DC readout. Applied to a superconducting coplanar waveguide resonator, local spectroscopy reproduces the power-dependent resonance and nonlinear bifurcation observed in microwave transmission measurements. Spatially resolved measurements map the microwave response while simultaneously recording topography and static magnetic features, including vortices and Meissner screening currents. Electromagnetic simulations reveal how coupling through the superconducting probe leads limits spatial resolution and guides localized detector designs. Together, these capabilities establish a platform for correlating microwave circuit dynamics with the local structural and magnetic environment of superconducting devices.

</details>


### [277] [Growth Phase Map of α and \b{eta} Tantalum as a Function of Growth Temperature and Oxygen Partial Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35768)
*Marius O. Eji,Tyler J. Kirkpatrick,Ty J. Cawein,Joseph P. Corbett*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过直流磁控溅射（dc magnetron sputtering）在不同温度与氧分压条件下于c面蓝宝石（c-plane sapphire）上沉积100 nm钽薄膜，构建了温度—氧/钽撞击通量比的三元相—取向生长图谱（growth map），确立氧调控的相与取向选择性生长窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钽薄膜用于低损耗超导量子电路（superconducting quantum circuits），但在c面蓝宝石上选择α-Ta(110)、α-Ta(111)与亚稳β-Ta的生长条件尚不明确，需厘清温度与氧的作用规律。

Method: 在300–900 °C、氧分压从5.5×10⁻¹¹ Torr超高真空基线至2.23×10⁻⁴ Torr范围内沉积薄膜，采用经吸收校正的X射线衍射织构系数（texture coefficients）构建以温度和氧/钽撞击通量比（2Φ_O₂/Φ_Ta）为坐标的三元相—取向生长图谱。

Result: 无添加氧时各温度均以α-Ta(110)为主；氧分压仅2.5×10⁻⁹ Torr时，α-Ta(111)在300–700 °C占归一化织构权重的98–99%，900 °C降至65%；β-Ta仅在最高氧剂量下大量生成，300 °C占主导并于900 °C消失；表观β(001)/α(110)选择能（selection energy）为1.07±0.16 eV，与Ta中氧扩散激活能相当。

Conclusion: 氧输运（oxygen transport）可能影响相选择，且氧会引起晶粒尺寸与微应变（microstrain）的取向相关变化；研究确立了面向超导器件的钽薄膜相与取向选择性生长工艺窗口（processing windows）。

Abstract: Tantalum thin films are used in low-loss superconducting quantum circuits, yet the growth conditions governing selection of $α$-Ta(110), $α$-Ta(111), and metastable $β$-Ta on c-plane sapphire remain uncertain. We deposited 100 nm Ta films by dc magnetron sputtering on Al$_2$O$_3$(0001) at 300-900 $^\circ$C while varying the O$_2$ partial pressure from a $5.5\times10^{-11}$ Torr ultrahigh-vacuum baseline to $2.23\times10^{-4}$ Torr. Absorption-corrected X-ray diffraction texture coefficients were used to construct a ternary phase-orientation growth map in terms of temperature and the oxygen-to-Ta impingement flux ratio ($2Φ_{O_2}/Φ_{Ta}\leq4.8\times10^{-6}$ to 19). Without added oxygen, $α$-Ta(110) dominated at every temperature. At an O$_2$ pressure of only $2.5\times10^{-9}$ Torr ($2Φ_{O_2}/Φ_{Ta}=2.2\times10^{-4}$), $α$-Ta(111) accounted for 98-99% of the normalized texture weight from 300 to 700 $^\circ$C, decreasing to 65% at 900 $^\circ$C. Substantial $β$-Ta formed only at the highest oxygen dose, dominating at 300 $^\circ$C and disappearing at 900 $^\circ$C. The apparent $β$(001)/$α$(110) selection energy of $1.07\pm0.16$ eV is comparable to the activation energy for oxygen diffusion in Ta, consistent with oxygen transport influencing phase selection. Oxygen also produced orientation-dependent changes in crystallite size and microstrain. These findings establish oxygen-controlled processing windows for phase- and orientation-selective growth of Ta films for superconducting devices.

</details>


### [278] [The fate of $s_{\pm}$ superconductivity in the bilayer nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35814)
*Kaustubh Roy,Piers Coleman*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文通过引入平均场分馏（mean-field fractionalization）方法，系统刻画半满 t-J 模型（t-J model）的完整 SU(2) 粒子—空穴对称性，导出常规 U(1) 处理中缺失的库仑赝势约束（Coulomb pseudopotential constraint），并将其应用于 La₃Ni₂O₇ 的简化双层 t-J 模型，说明该约束会抑制 s± 配对、偏向 d 波配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在块体与薄膜 La₃Ni₂O₇ 中观测到高温超导（high-temperature superconductivity），促使理解层间耦合（interlayer coupling）如何决定配对对称性（pairing symmetry）。已有工作多提出 s± 相与铜氧化物式 d 波（d-wave）态竞争，但在强耦合理论中库仑排斥（Coulomb repulsion）与双占据约束（double occupancy constraint）常被忽略。

Method: 提出一种平均场分馏（mean-field fractionalization）处理，保留半满 t-J 模型（t-J model）的完整 SU(2) 粒子—空穴对称性（particle-hole symmetry），并系统处理该对称性以得到新增的库仑赝势约束（Coulomb pseudopotential constraint）。随后将该分馏方案应用于 La₃Ni₂O₇ 的简化双层 t-J 模型。

Result: 系统处理 SU(2) 对称性所导出的库仑赝势约束在常规 U(1) 处理中不存在；在简化双层 t-J 模型中，该赝势抑制 s± 对称性的存在，转而支持 d 波（d-wave）配对。

Conclusion: 考虑库仑排斥与双占据约束的 SU(2) 对称分馏框架可修正强耦合下对 La₃Ni₂O₇ 配对对称性的判断，说明层间耦合背景下 s± 与 d 波竞争的结果受库仑赝势约束显著调控。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent measurement of high-temperature superconductivity in the bilayer nickelate system La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ in the bulk as well as thin film samples, we seek to understand the role of the interlayer coupling in this system in dictating the pairing symmetry. Many proposals posit the existence of an $s_\pm$ superconducting phase in close competition with the mroe conventional cuprate-like $d$-wave state in both weak- and strong-coupling theories. However, at least in the latter, the effects of Coulomb repulsion and the double occupancy constraint have been largely neglected. In this manuscript, we propose a mean-field fractionalization treatment that captures the full $SU(2)$ particle-hole symmetry of the t-J model at half-filling, and show how a systematic treatment of this new symmetry leads to an added Coulomb pseudopotential constraint - one that is absent in the usual $U(1)$ treatment. We then apply this new fractionalization scheme to a simplified bilayer t-J model of La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$, showing how the Coulomb pseudopotential suppresses the existence of $s_\pm$ symmetry in favor of $d$-wave pairing.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [279] [Initial Data Gluing for Asymptotically Hyperbolic Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33372)
*Aren Martinian*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文给出渐近双曲时空（asymptotically hyperbolic spacetimes）中 Corvino-Schoen 型粘合（gluing up to linear obstructions）的一个新证明，适用于任意维度，并放宽了以往所需的“小性”（smallness）条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Corvino-Schoen 型粘合结果对初始数据（initial data）施加的小性要求较为严格（包括 Chrusciél 与 Delay 的工作），需要改进这些要求并给出新的证明途径。

Method: 提出一种新的粘合论证（新证明），在适当小性条件下处理任意维度的渐近双曲时空；摘要强调该技术通用性强，适合高度各向异性（highly anisotropic）的几何设定。

Result: 复现并改进（retrieve and improve）了此前已知的、用于实施此类粘合的小性要求，涵盖 Chrusciél 与 Delay 的结果。

Conclusion: 所给技术具有较强通用性，适用于高度各向异性几何背景下的粘合问题；具体技术细节未在摘要中披露。

Abstract: We give a new proof of Corvino-Schoen-type gluing, also known as gluing up to linear obstructions, for asymptotically hyperbolic spacetimes satisfying appropriate smallness conditions in arbitrary dimension. The results retrieve and improve on previous known smallness requirements on initial data to conduct such gluing, including the work of Chrusciél and Delay. Our technique is highly versatile and well-suited for gluing results in highly anisotropic geometric settings.

</details>


### [280] [Characteristic gluing to extremal Kerr horizons and the third law of black hole thermodynamics in vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35836)
*Stefan Czimek*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在爱因斯坦真空方程（Einstein vacuum equations）框架下证明了一个非微扰、非轴对称的特征粘合（characteristic gluing）定理：Kerr黑洞外部通信区域中任一Boyer-Lindquist球面可沿单一出射零超曲面粘合到足够大质量极端Kerr黑洞的地平球面，并据此构造出违反黑洞热力学第三定律（third law of black hole thermodynamics）的真空初值数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在解决Kehle与Unger提出的两个猜想：极端情形下的特征粘合猜想，以及真空中第三定律是否可被严格违反。此前缺乏非微扰、非轴对称且匹配完整球面数据（含横向曲率）的粘合结果，也难以构造含精确Schwarzschild视界与后续精确极端Kerr视界的初值。

Method: 方法上，先通过非轴对称引力辐射（gravitational radiation）的旋转剪切脉冲（shear pulses）将角动量提升至极端值，再沿长的非膨胀颈（non-expanding neck）对入射数据作指数衰减；在退化目标视界处以全角频率求解线性化问题，利用四阶角算子的Teukolsky-Starobinsky乘积分解（factorisation）实现求逆。

Result: 所得粘合在两端均为精确的，并匹配包括横向曲率在内的完整球面数据，从而证明Kehle-Unger猜想在极端情形下成立。作为应用，构造出单端渐近平坦真空初值数据，其极大发展（maximal development）包含精确的Schwarzschild表观视界及较晚超前时刻的精确极端Kerr事件视界。

Conclusion: 该结果为真空中黑洞热力学第三定律的首个严格反例，并证明了Kehle与Unger的第二个猜想。其意义在于将极端视界的非微扰粘合技术应用于第三定律的严格研究，拓展了特征粘合方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We prove a non-perturbative, non-axisymmetric characteristic gluing theorem for the Einstein vacuum equations: every Boyer-Lindquist sphere in the domain of outer communication of a Kerr black hole, in particular every symmetry sphere of a Schwarzschild exterior, can be glued along a single outgoing null hypersurface to a horizon sphere of an extremal Kerr black hole of sufficiently large mass. The gluing is exact at both ends and matches the full sphere data, including the transversal curvature. This proves a conjecture of Kehle and Unger in the extremal case. We first raise the angular momentum to the extremal value by rotating shear pulses of non-axisymmetric gravitational radiation, and then damp the incoming data exponentially along a long non-expanding neck. At the degenerate target horizon we invert the linearised problem at all angular frequencies, using a factorisation of a fourth-order angular operator into an angular Teukolsky-Starobinsky product.
  As an application, we construct one-ended asymptotically flat vacuum initial data whose maximal development contains an exactly Schwarzschild apparent horizon and, at a later advanced time, an exactly extremal Kerr event horizon. This is the first rigorous counterexample to the third law of black hole thermodynamics in vacuum, and proves a second conjecture of Kehle and Unger.

</details>


### [281] [Dynamic Interpolation and Higher-Order Turbulence Scales in 3D Navier-Stokes Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33911)
*Moses Patson Phiri,Zoran Grujić*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过空间解析性（spatial analyticity）与强流体活动区域几何稀疏性（geometric sparseness）的相互作用研究三维无外力纳维-斯托克斯方程（Navier-Stokes equations），构造时间加权桥不等式（time-weighted bridge inequalities）并结合一维稀疏集上的调和测度极大值原理（harmonic-measure maximum principle），排除了强湍流爆破情形，使强解可解析延拓至候选爆破时刻之后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对三维无外力纳维-斯托克斯方程强解的爆破问题，寻求利用解析性半径与湍流区域稀疏尺度之间的关系，排除一类强湍流爆破（strongly turbulent blow-up）情形。

Method: 将高阶导数比值解释为湍流尺度（turbulence scales），引入时间加权桥不等式刻画低阶与高阶导数范数的关系；时间权重增强导数比值的灵活性，并保证解析性半径（radius of analyticity）下界优于超水平集（super-level sets）稀疏尺度；再结合一维稀疏集上的调和测度极大值原理（harmonic-measure maximum principle）得到导数范数的压缩。

Result: 解析性半径下界对稀疏尺度的控制表明流动进入耗散区（dissipation regime）；由此得到的导数范数压缩性排除了强湍流爆破场景。

Conclusion: 在该框架下，强解（strong solution）可解析延拓至候选爆破时刻之外，从而排除了一类潜在的有限时间爆破。

Abstract: We investigate the three-dimensional unforced Navier-Stokes equations through the interaction between spatial analyticity and the geometric sparseness of regions of intense fluid activity (i.e., regions where the fluid's motion varies sharply). Interpreting higher-order derivative ratios as turbulence scales, we introduce time-weighted bridge inequalities that quantify the relationship between lower- and higher-order derivative norms. The time weights give the derivative ratios greater flexibility while ensuring that the resulting lower bound on the radius of analyticity dominates the sparseness scale of the super-level sets, indicating that the flow has entered the dissipation regime. Combining this scale comparison with a harmonic-measure maximum principle on one-dimensional sparse sets yields a contraction in the relevant derivative norm, thereby excluding a class of strongly turbulent blow-up scenarios. Consequently, the strong solution extends analytically beyond the candidate blow-up time.

</details>


### [282] [Hydrodynamics for hypersingular interactions by $Γ$-convergence of gradient flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34136)
*Colin McSwiggen,Sylvia Serfaty*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维 Riesz 排斥相互作用粒子系统的平均场极限，证明经验测度收敛到多孔介质方程（porous medium equation）的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 Oelschläger 在更严格假设下得到的收敛结果，并将结论推广到超奇异（hypersingular）情形及一类凸短程相互作用。

Method: 采用 Sandier 与第二作者提出的梯度流 Γ-收敛（Γ-convergence of gradient flows）方法。

Result: 证明经验测度收敛到多孔介质方程的解；该方法同样适用于幂次小于维数的情形以及一类凸短程相互作用。

Conclusion: 证明凸显了相互作用凸性（convexity）的关键作用：它保证沿流不产生多余能量，从而给出所需的斜率不等式（slope inequality）。

Abstract: We consider the dynamics of $N$ particles with Riesz repulsive interaction in one dimension, focusing in particular on the hypersingular case (i.e. inverse power with power larger than or equal to the dimension). We prove convergence of the empirical measure to the solution of a porous medium equation by the method of $Γ$-convergence of gradient flows introduced by Sandier and the second author. This improves on the prior convergence result by Oelschläger obtained under more stringent assumptions. The method also works for the case where the power is smaller than the dimension, as well as for a class of convex short-range interactions. The proof highlights the crucial role played by the convexity of the interaction, which allows one to show that no excess energy can arise along the flow, implying the needed "slope inequality".

</details>


### [283] [The Inhomogeneous Kinetic FPUT Equation near Rayleigh-Jeans Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34636)
*Angeliki Menegaki,Haoling Xiang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究FPUT-β问题对应的空间非均匀动力学波方程（kinetic wave equation），在非奇异瑞利-金斯平衡态（Rayleigh-Jeans equilibria）附近证明全局时间适定性与渐近稳定性，并通过谱分析结合能量估计得到线性半群的代数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对空间非均匀（spatially inhomogeneous）动力学波方程在FPUT-β模型框架下的非线性动力学缺乏全局适定性与稳定性刻画，尤其是碰撞算子（collision operator）无谱隙（spectral gap）时如何获得衰减的问题。

Method: 在空间与动量加权的L∞范数下处理小初值扰动，要求扰动在几乎每个空间点处与碰撞核（collision kernel）正交；分析小空间频率（spatial frequencies）下原点附近的谱结构，识别阶数为|ξ|^{8/5}（分数阶）与ξ^2（扩散阶）的两个单特征值，并结合考虑输运效应（transport effect）的能量估计（energy estimates）研究线性半群（linear semigroup）。

Result: 证明了非奇异瑞利-金斯平衡态附近的全局时间适定性（global-in-time well-posedness）与加权L∞意义下的渐近稳定性（asymptotic stability）；尽管碰撞算子无谱隙，线性半群仍具有代数衰减（algebraic decay）。

Conclusion: 谱分析（spectral analysis）与含输运效应的能量估计相结合，可在无谱隙情形下建立代数衰减，并由此推出非线性稳定性，为FPUT-β动力学波方程的非均匀扰动演化提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study the nonlinear dynamics of the spatially inhomogeneous kinetic wave equation associated with the FPUT-$β$ problem. We prove global-in-time well-posedness near nonsingular Rayleigh-Jeans equilibria and their asymptotic stability in particular in weighted $L^\infty$ norms in space and momentum, for small initial perturbations orthogonal to the collision kernel at almost every spatial point. We describe the spectrum near the origin at small spatial frequencies, identifying two simple eigenvalues with fractional and diffusive orders $|ξ|^{8/5}$ and $ξ^2$, respectively. Combining this spectral analysis with energy estimates taking into account the transport effect, we establish algebraic decay for the linear semigroup despite the absence of a spectral gap for the collision operator. We then use these estimates to obtain nonlinear stability.

</details>


### [284] [The role of density in phase transitions and new perspectives on theta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35437)
*Kaixin Deng,Senping Luo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究泛函 θ(α;z) − bθ(2α;z) 在所有 α>0 与 b∈(0,√2) 下的极小化问题，给出了完整相图，并据此修正并解决了 Bétermin 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Montgomery 定理表明单个 theta 函数在所有尺度下均由六角格（hexagonal lattice）极小化，而近期 Bétermin–Faulhuber–Steinerberger 关于平移高斯格和的结果亦显示六角格在 min-max 结构中占优；本文旨在考察双 theta 函数竞争项如何改变最优格型的序列结构。
