Method: 定义 Λ = (1/√Im z)(ℤ⊕zℤ) 上的 theta 函数 θ(α;z)，对泛函 θ(α;z) − bθ(2α;z) 进行极小化分析，并通过显式阈值 b₁ = 0.0767767081⋯ ∈ (0,1/10) 对其相变类型进行分类讨论。

Result: 得到完整相图：当 0<b<b₁ 时为 Type I 相变，呈现“六角–菱形–正方–矩形（hexagonal–rhombic–square–rectangular）”序列；当 b₁≤b<√2 时为 Type II 相变，呈现“六角–正方–矩形”序列而不经过菱形格（rhombic lattices）。菱形相仅在 b<b₁ 时存在，并在 b≥b₁ 时消失。

Conclusion: 密度参数 α 决定最优格型的序列，竞争参数 b 控制菱形相的出现。作为应用，论文严格证明对于 b=1/10 的泛函在任何密度下都无菱形格为全局极小元（global minimizer），系统实际发生 Type II 相变，从而以修正形式解决了 Bétermin 猜想（Bétermin's conjecture）。

Abstract: Let $z\in \mathbb{H}:=\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\Im(z)>0\}$ and $Λ:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Im(z)}}\big({\mathbb Z}\oplus z{\mathbb Z}\big)$, and set $
θ(α;z):=θ(α;Λ)=\sum_{(m,n)\in\mathbb{Z}^2}e^{-πα\frac{|m+nz|^2}{\Im(z)}}. $
We study the minimization of the functional $θ(α;z)-bθ(2α;z)$ and establish a complete phase diagram for all $α>0$ and $b\in(0,\sqrt{2})$. For $0<b<\sqrt{2}$, an explicit threshold $b_1=0.0767767081\cdots\in(0,1/10)$ separates two distinct types of phase transitions.
  Type I: $0<b<b_1$: it admits a type of ``hexagonal--rhombic--square--rectangular'' phase transition.
  Type II: $b_1\le b<\sqrt{2}$: it admits a type of ``hexagonal--square--rectangular'' phase transition, without passing through the rhombic lattices.
  These phase transitions contrast with Montgomery's theorem \cite{Mon1988}, where the hexagonal lattice minimizes the single theta function at all scales, and the recent Bétermin-Faulhuber-Steinerberger theorem \cite{BFS2026} for translated Gaussian lattice sums, where the hexagonal lattice prevails in a min-max structure. In our setting, the density parameter $α$ governs the sequence of optimal lattice shapes, while the competition parameter $b$ controls the emergence of the rhombic phase, which exists for $b<b_1$ and vanishes for $b\ge b_1$.
  As an application, we resolve Bétermin's conjecture \cite{Betermin2021AHP}. Bétermin predicted a Type I phase transition for the functional $θ(α;z)-(1/10)θ(2α;z)$ with $b=1/10$. We instead rigorously establish that no rhombic lattice is a global minimizer at any density and prove that the system undergoes a Type II phase transition, thereby settling the conjecture in corrected form.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [285] [Optimal Riesz mean inequalities for the Coulomb Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34181)
*Rupert L. Frank,Bernard Helffer,François Nicoleau*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究库仑哈密顿量（Coulomb Hamiltonian）特征值的 Riesz 均值（Riesz means）能否被相应的半经典相空间积分（semiclassical phase space integral）所控制，并在二维和三维情形下确定了该界成立的最优 Riesz 指数（optimal Riesz exponent）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是检验库仑体系特征值的 Riesz 均值与半经典相空间积分之间的不等式关系是否成立，即探讨半经典界（semiclassical bound）在此类体系中的有效性范围。

Method: 采用 Riesz 均值这一特征值求和工具，与对应的半经典相空间积分进行比较，并针对二维和三维的库仑哈密顿量确定使该界成立的最优 Riesz 指数阈值。

Result: 在二维和三维情形下均确定了使该半经典界成立的最优 Riesz 指数，即给出了界成立的精确指数门槛。

Conclusion: 库仑哈密顿量的 Riesz 均值可被半经典相空间积分控制，其成立与否取决于 Riesz 指数是否超过某一与维数相关的最优阈值。

Abstract: We are interested in whether the Riesz means of the eigenvalues of the Coulomb Hamiltonian can be bounded by the corresponding semiclassical phase space integral. In dimensions two and three we determine the optimal Riesz exponent above which this bound holds.

</details>


### [286] [Scattering for the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map and the Steklov spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34588)
*Jeffrey Galkowski,Marcello Malagutti,Ruoyu P. T. Wang*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文为渐近锥型无界区域上的 Dirichlet-to-Neumann（Dirichlet-to-Neumann, DtN）映射建立散射理论，并据此将曲线多边形上的 Steklov 型特征值问题归结为边界上 Dirac 算子的逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有散射理论通常要求边界与算子具有较强正则性，而带尖角或延伸至无穷的边界区域缺乏相应框架；同时 Steklov 型问题的特征值渐近尚缺可控的局部化描述工具。

Method: 在渐近锥型（asymptotically conic）、内部仅在紧集上 Lipschitz 的无界区域上研究 DtN 映射，证明极限吸收原理（limiting absorption principle）并构造散射矩阵（scattering matrix）。

Result: 证明了 DtN 映射散射矩阵的存在性；并证明曲线多边形上 Steklov 型特征值可由边界 Dirac 算子的特征值逼近，其角点处棱边间的透射系数（transmission coefficients）由角点对应的渐近锥型区域 DtN 散射矩阵给出。

Conclusion: 在仅使用边界局部信息的所有可能渐近中，该逼近误差在特征值幂次意义下（至多相差任意幂次损失）是最优的，表明散射矩阵刻画了角点局部几何对谱渐近的贡献。

Abstract: We develop scattering theory for Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN) maps on asymptotically conic, unbounded domains $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^d$, that are only Lipschitz inside a compact set and whose boundary extends to infinity. Specifically, we prove a limiting absorption principle and demonstrate existence of the scattering matrix.
  As an application of the theory, we show that the eigenvalues of Steklov-type problems on curvilinear polygons, $\mathcal{P}$, are well approximated by the eigenvalues of a Dirac operator on $\partial\mathcal{P}$ whose transmission coefficients between edges meeting at a corner are given by the scattering matrix for DtN maps on asymptotically conic domains associated to the corners. Up to an arbitrary loss in the power of the eigenvalue, the error in the approximation is optimal among all possible asymptotics that use only local information about $\partial\mathcal{P}$.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [The Ising lattice gas and the surface diffusion of CO on Cu(001) at finite coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33147)
*S. Miret-Artés*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文把格子气体（lattice gas）中的相干中间散射函数（intermediate scattering function, ISF）记忆方程应用于Kawasaki动力学下的Ising格子气体，给出闭合形式的静态结构因子与有效跳跃率，并揭示相关时间、退相位率（dephasing rate）与平衡态量之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标在于厘清ISF衰减的记忆效应（memory effect）、求和规则（sum rule）与可测量退相位率之间的联系，并检验能否仅通过静态（平衡）量读取动力学信息，同时据此解释CO/Cu(001)实验测得的退相位率。

Method: 将先前针对格子气体导出的相干ISF记忆方程用于Kawasaki动力学下的Ising格子气体；解析求出一维通道（one-dimensional channel）上精确的静态结构因子与有效跳跃率（effective hop rate），结合求和规则构造ISF的单指数下界，并与精确弛豫谱对比。

Result: 单指数下界仅在弱耦合（weak coupling）下重现相关时间；强吸引时团簇（cluster）缓慢溶解使相关时间延长至多九倍，而排斥层在其结构峰附近近乎单模弛豫。拟合得到的退相位率趋近最慢弛豫率而非初始速率；求和规则只通过归一化衰减曲线下面积（即相关时间）与数据相符。对CO/Cu(001)，复现两个晶向的退相位率需最近邻排斥至少35–50 meV。

Conclusion: 仅由平衡态量构建的单指数描述适用范围有限；退相位率与相关时间在强相互作用下由最慢模式主导。CO/Cu(001)数据与Ising格子气体一致，要求存在较强的最近邻排斥，且所测速率接近相关时间的倒数，并预测记忆效应随覆盖度增大。

Abstract: The memory equation of the coherent intermediate scattering function (ISF), derived previously for lattice gases, is applied to the Ising lattice gas under Kawasaki dynamics. The ISF follows the decay of a density wave of adparticles, a superposition of exponential relaxation modes: the decay starts at a rate fixed by a sum rule and is then slowed by a memory. The static structure factor and the effective hop rate are obtained in closed form, exactly along a one-dimensional channel; with the sum rule they give a single exponential, a lower bound on the ISF. Exact relaxation spectra show that this single exponential, built from equilibrium quantities alone, reproduces the correlation time only at weak coupling; only there can a measured dephasing rate be read through the statics. For strong attraction the slow dissolution of clusters lengthens the correlation time up to ninefold, whereas a repulsive layer relaxes nearly as a single mode at its structure peak. The dephasing rate, fitted over a time window where it is nearly constant, approaches the slowest relaxation rate, not the initial one; the sum rule reaches the data only through the area beneath the normalized decay, which defines the correlation time. For CO/Cu(001), the lattice gas reproduces the dephasing rates measured along two crystal directions and requires a nearest-neighbour repulsion of at least $35$--$50$~meV, whatever the jump rates; the measured rate is then close to the inverse correlation time, and the memory is predicted to grow with coverage.

</details>


### [288] [Optimal Search Strategies under Finite Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33316)
*Hugues Meyer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限资源预算下的目标搜索问题，为一维扩散搜索者配置外部力、部分反射标记与自趋化线索三类资源，推导出精确的最优资源分配协议，发现知情搜索需空间集中分配、盲目搜索需更均匀分配，且非马尔可夫环境记忆在知情搜索中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往资源常被建模为有代价但可无限获取，而实际中资源往往只能有限量生产。论文旨在突破资源无限供给的假设，研究有限资源预算需在整个搜索过程中分配的情形。

Method: 为一维扩散搜索者（one-dimensional diffusive searcher）配备三类资源：外部力（external force）、部分反射标记（partially reflecting flags）与自趋化线索（auto-chemotactic cues），针对可连续获取历史与当前位置信息的知情搜索以及盲目搜索（blind search），分别推导精确的最优资源分配协议（optimal resource-allocation protocols）。

Result: 知情搜索的最优协议始终要求资源在空间上集中分配，而盲目搜索的协议则须更均匀分布；产生环境记忆（environmental memory）的非马尔可夫线索（non-Markovian cues）在知情搜索中即使受有限资源约束也能显著提升性能，但对盲目搜索收益有限，尤其当线索不提供方向信息时。

Conclusion: 资源分配的集中与均匀程度取决于搜索者是否具备位置信息，非马尔可夫环境记忆的价值依赖于搜索者的信息获取能力，有限资源约束会重塑最优搜索策略。

Abstract: Improving target search is a challenge faced by many natural and artificial agents, often requiring the use of energetic or material resources. These inputs may be supplied by an external controller or generated by the searcher itself, for example, through the deposition of cues along its trajectory. Although such resources are commonly modeled as costly but available without an explicit limit, in practice, they can often only be produced in finite amounts. Here, we go beyond the assumption of an unlimited supply of resources by investigating searches in which a finite resource budget must be allocated throughout the search process. To investigate this problem, we equip a one-dimensional diffusive searcher with three types of resources: an external force, a supply of partially reflecting flags, and auto-chemotactic cues. We derive exact optimal resource-allocation protocols for informed search processes in which the agent has continuous access to its past and current positions, which we find to always require resource allocation to be spatially concentrated. We then determine the corresponding protocols for blind searchers and show that resource usage must be more evenly distributed. Non-Markovian cues that generate environmental memory are found to substantially improve search performance when the agent has access to positional information, even under finite-resource constraints. In contrast, their benefits are considerably more limited for blind search, particularly when the cues provide no directional information.

</details>


### [289] [Distributional statistics of nonequilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33383)
*Harrison Hartle,Aanjaneya Kumar,David H. Wolpert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出一个受约束随机概率分布的参考建模框架，将Crooks的超系综方法推广为单纯形上的Jaynes最大不确定性先验，并以平均能量与Shannon熵约束为核心研究对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有非平衡建模缺乏一个能与Jaynesian逻辑和贝叶斯推断兼容的、关于随机概率分布本身的通用参考框架，故需要构建约束下的最大不确定性先验体系。

Method: 在类别分布的单位单纯形上构造Jaynesian先验（超系综），对平均能量施加精确约束以定义等能超平面及其上的均匀密度超系综，并进一步引入Shannon熵约束；采用解析推导与精确采样算法计算均值分布、众数分布及期望自由能。

Result: 平均能量约束唯一确定Boltzmann-Gibbs平衡态，同时对应一个与到平衡态距离无关的均匀超系综；该框架可预测一般非平衡性质，Shannon熵约束提供可调的非平衡区间参数化通道，且在熵最大化时退化回Boltzmann-Gibbs统计。

Conclusion: 该框架遵循常规Jaynesian建模逻辑、兼容标准贝叶斯推断并与其他非平衡建模方法广泛兼容；文中通过超统计（superstatistics）比较、随机非平衡稳态生成及超系综先验下的贝叶斯推断三个场景进行示例，并指出诸多理论扩展与实证应用前景。

Abstract: We establish a reference modeling framework for random probability distributions subject to constraints. Generalizing the hyperensemble approach of Crooks, we formulate Jaynesian priors of maximal uncertainty over the unit simplex of categorical distributions. This framework is entirely general, but we primarily investigate models with constraints on physically relevant quantities, namely, average energy and Shannon entropy. A constraint on the exact value of average energy uniquely determines a Boltzmann-Gibbs equilibrium, but also defines a hyperplanar subset of the probability simplex consisting of all distributions with that value of average energy. The associated hyperensemble has a uniform probability density across all such distributions, regardless of distance to equilibrium. Such reference models make predictions of generic nonequilibrium properties such as the mean and modal microstate distributions and the expected free energy of a random distribution, which we examine analytically and via exact sampling algorithms. Moreover, introducing constraints on Shannon entropy permits parametric access to tunably nonequilibrium regimes. The approach follows conventional Jaynesian modeling logic and is compatible with standard Bayesian inference, and reduces to Boltzmann-Gibbs statistics when Shannon entropy is maximized. We examine the physical predictions of this scheme, and demonstrate its wide compatibility with other approaches to nonequilibrium modeling. We illustrate the framework in three scenarios: (i) comparison to superstatistics quasi-equilibrium modeling, (ii) generation of random nonequilibrium steady states, and (iii) Bayesian inference under a prior hyperensemble. We highlight numerous prospects for theoretical extensions and empirical application.

</details>


### [290] [Large spin large deviations for interacting particle systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33523)
*Cristian Giardinà,Tomohiro Sasamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出在格点上直接分析相互作用粒子系统样本路径大偏差的新框架，通过大自旋极限（large-spin limit, s→∞）计算路径空间速率函数，并将对称调和过程的哈密顿量等同于经典可积非紧致Ishimori自旋链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典宏观涨落理论（Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory, MFT）在空间连续极限下存在紫外奇异性与密度爆破等问题，因此需要一个天然正则化的格点类比框架。

Method: 通过适当变量标度使过程集中于由格点扩散方程支配的确定性轨迹；基于Feng–Kurtz理论，从标度后的微观生成元显式计算路径空间大偏差速率函数，并以格点上的经典自旋变量用哈密顿量表述；同时采用Poisson噪声Martin–Siggia–Rose（MSR）计算与相对熵（relative entropy）论证。

Result: 对称调和过程（symmetric harmonic process）的哈密顿量被识别为经典可积非紧致Ishimori自旋链；该理论与传统MFT完全一致，在空间连续极限下可光滑恢复标准MFT哈密顿量，并给出格点涨落定理及其对称性起源。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了MFT的自然正则化格点类比，规避空间紫外奇异性和连续密度爆破，并可推广到部分对称排他过程（partial symmetric exclusion process）、对称包含过程、布朗能量过程（Brownian energy process）及可积热传导模型，其大偏差哈密顿量对应经典紧致与非紧致自旋链。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for analyzing the sample-path large deviations of interacting particle systems directly on a lattice by considering a large-spin limit ($s \to \infty$). Through an appropriate scaling of variables, the process concentrates around its deterministic trajectory, governed by the lattice diffusion equation. We quantify the exponential cost of observing atypical trajectories by explicitly computing the path-space large deviation rate function from the scaled microscopic generator, based on a theory due to Feng and Kurtz. The rate function is described in terms of a Hamiltonian on a lattice in terms of classical spin variables. Notably, for our main model, the symmetric harmonic process, the corresponding Hamiltonian is identified with the classical integrable non-compact Ishimori spin chain.
  Furthermore, we show that our large spin large deviation theory is fully consistent with traditional Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (MFT), smoothly recovering the standard MFT Hamiltonian in the spatial continuous limit. We also perform a Poisson-noise Martin--Siggia--Rose (MSR) computation to derive the non-linear lattice Hamiltonian directly from microscopic dynamics, use a relative entropy argument to obtain the density-current action, and establish the lattice fluctuation theorem alongside its symmetry origin. Overall, our framework provides a naturally regularized, lattice analogue of MFT that circumvents spatial ultraviolet singularities and possible continuous density blow-ups.
  We also apply our new scheme to partial symmetric exclusion process, symmetric inclusion processes, Brownian energy process and integrable heat conduction model, showing how their large-deviation Hamiltonians relate to classical compact and non-compact spin chains.

</details>


### [291] [Jensen-Mercer two-sided bounds for entropy production rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34112)
*Xiao-Kan Guo,Zhiqiang Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在Jensen下界之外，借助Mercer反射不等式构造出熵产生率（entropy production rate）的互补上界，并将其与热力学不确定性关系（thermodynamic uncertainty relation）组合为仅依赖平均漂移与局域流极值的双侧约束（two-sided sandwich）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵产生界（bounds on entropy production）可将有限观测转化为对隐藏耗散（hidden dissipation）的约束，但既有Jensen界仅给出下界，缺乏对应的上界来界定耗散的上限。作者希望利用同一凸性结构补齐这一缺口。

Method: 针对过阻尼朗之万动力学（overdamped Langevin dynamics）的二次熵产生泛函，证明Mercer反射不等式（Mercer's reflection inequality）与尖锐的Bhatia-Davis方差界（Bhatia-Davis variance bound）等价，且等于该泛函在局域流取值范围内最小仿射优函数（affine majorant）的凸对偶。方法还推广至位置依赖扩散（position-dependent diffusion）、欠阻尼动力学（underdamped dynamics）与倾斜周期势（tilted periodic potential）。

Result: 两个界共同给出由平均漂移（mean drift）与流极值（extrema of the current）控制的双侧夹逼，并组织成逐级加严的层级最优界（hierarchy of optimal bounds）；热力学不确定性关系提供独立的涨落下限（fluctuation floor）。对倾斜周期势，弱势垒级数（weak-barrier series）获得闭式表达，强势垒（strong-barrier）情形得到渐近解，并在布朗马达（Brownian motor）上完成数值验证。

Conclusion: 研究表明Jensen界的凸性结构可对称地生成上界，从而在有限观测条件下同时约束熵产生率的上下限，并为多种动力学设定提供可计算的最优界框架。

Abstract: Bounds on entropy production convert limited observations into constraints on hidden dissipation. The Jensen bound expresses a lower bound on the total and subsystem entropy production rates of continuous stochastic systems in terms of mean coordinate rates alone. We show that the same convexity structure yields a complementary upper bound, inspired by Mercer's reflection inequality. For the quadratic entropy-production functional of overdamped Langevin dynamics, Mercer's reflection inequality is identical to the sharp Bhatia-Davis variance bound and coincides with the convex dual of the smallest affine majorant of the functional over the range of the local current. The two bounds yield a two-sided sandwich controlled solely by the mean drift and the extrema of the current, organized into a hierarchy of optimal bounds in which each further observation tightens the ceiling. The thermodynamic uncertainty relation supplies an independent fluctuation floor. We also treat position-dependent diffusion, underdamped dynamics and the tilted periodic potential, for which the weak-barrier series is obtained in closed form and the strong-barrier regime is solved asymptotically. We check the bounds numerically on the Brownian motor.

</details>


### [292] [Extremal and global-time statistics of sluggish random walks with stochastic resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34568)
*Theophilus Dwamena Frimpong*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究带泊松重置（Poissonian resetting）的一维迟缓随机游走（sluggish random walk，扩散系数随距原点距离幂律衰减）的极值统计与全局时间统计，通过变量变换映射为带符号的贝塞尔过程（signed Bessel process）并借助更新理论（renewal theory）得到精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画扩散系数随位置幂律衰减的迟缓随机游走在重置机制下的极值分布与全局时间行为，并将其与已知的布朗重置结果进行对照。

Method: 将过程通过变量变换映射为带符号贝塞尔过程，使更新理论适用；推导有限窗口内最大值分布的精确拉普拉斯变换（Laplace transform），并分析最大值时刻、末次通过时间（last-passage time）与占据时间等量。

Result: 长时间下变换过程的中心化最大值服从Gumbel律，物理空间中典型最大值随窗口长度对数（logarithm）的幂增长；远尾速率函数（rate function）与贝塞尔变量中的α无关，故布朗重置中已知的二阶动力学转变（dynamical transition）对任意α均成立；最大值时刻在窗口内部近似均匀分布，边界层使其均值高于中点且随窗口长度对数偏移；末次通过原点的时刻具有精确密度，其后经过时间收敛到Gamma分布；反射对称性使正半轴占据时间与末次通过时间不相关。结果可退化为布朗情形并与模拟一致。

Conclusion: 迟缓随机游走在重置下的极值统计可由贝塞尔过程与更新理论精确处理，其定性结构与布朗重置类似但典型最大值增长形式不同，且二阶动力学转变的普适性由速率函数的α无关性保证。

Abstract: We study the extremal and global-time statistics of a one-dimensional sluggish random walk, whose diffusivity decays as a power law of the distance from the origin, under Poissonian resetting to the origin. A change of variable maps the process onto a signed Bessel process, so that renewal theory applies. We obtain the exact Laplace transform of the distribution of the maximum over a finite window. At long times the centered maximum of the transformed process follows the Gumbel law, and in physical space the typical maximum grows as a power of the logarithm of the window length. The far-tail rate function does not depend on $α$ in the Bessel variable, so the second-order dynamical transition known for Brownian resetting, between maxima produced by a single anomalously long inter-reset interval and maxima produced by an interval occupying essentially the whole window, holds for every $α$. The time of the maximum is uniformly distributed in the bulk of the window, with boundary layers that shift its mean above the midpoint logarithmically in the window length. The last-passage time through the origin has an exact density, and the time elapsed since it converges to a Gamma law. Reflection symmetry makes the occupation time of the positive half-line and the last-passage time uncorrelated. The results reduce to the Brownian case and agree with simulations.

</details>


### [293] [Reference-state dependence and interface scaling of superfluid response in a two-dimensional crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34664)
*Néstor Sepúlveda*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究利用张量响应、独立制备的双晶与实测界面长度，定量分离二维软核Gross–Pitaevskii晶体中超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）的晶界贡献与周围晶体贡献，并建立可比的量化协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶界（grain boundaries）同时改变输运路径与周围晶体状态，使晶界对超流刚度的贡献难以与体相（bulk）变化分离，需建立区分二者的定量方法。

Method: 采用张量响应（tensor response）、独立制备的双晶（bicrystals）及实测界面长度，在稳态二维软核GP晶体中比较不同体相参考下的相对超出量，并进行多体相替换、配对制备与固定面积配对检验。

Result: 晶格间距1%变化使体相刚度改变约5–9%，与边界贡献相当；同一缺陷态下两种体相参考给出0.027%与4.15%的相对超出。匹配晶粒应变与化学势后，三种相互作用强度下的配对制备均呈正对角超出，且该超出对288次体相替换稳健并近似服从长度–面积关系。

Conclusion: 结果可将边界增强与周围晶体变化区分开，为缺陷晶体中超流输运提供定量比较协议；但尺寸与方向依赖使该关系仅适用于特定晶体条件，约化链接网络（reduced link networks）亦无法精确解析所有小幅超出。

Abstract: Grain boundaries modify both transport paths and the surrounding crystal, making their contribution to superfluid stiffness difficult to isolate. We quantify these contributions in stationary two-dimensional soft-core Gross--Pitaevskii (GP) crystals using tensor response, independently prepared bicrystals, and measured interface lengths. A one-percent change of lattice spacing alters the bulk stiffness by approximately 5--9\%, comparable to the boundary contribution. For the same defective state, two bulk references give relative excesses of 0.027\% and 4.15\%. Matching the comparator to grain strain and chemical potential yields positive diagonal excesses in paired preparations at three interaction strengths. Within a twelve-state family with comparable grain interiors, the excess is robust to 288 bulk substitutions and follows an approximate length-per-area relation. Independent fixed-area pairs yield a line-response coefficient 3.1\% higher than the three-size estimate. Size and directional dependence restrict this relation to the specified crystal conditions. Reduced link networks relate the directional response to the organization of boundary connections but do not accurately resolve every small bulk-subtracted excess. The results distinguish boundary enhancement from changes in the surrounding crystal and establish a quantitative comparison protocol for superfluid transport in defective crystals.

</details>


### [294] [Simplified Parisi Ansatz III: Constructive Cavity Method and Ultrametricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34710)
*Simone Franchini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文为 Sherrington–Kirkpatrick 模型中的构造性空腔方法（Constructive Cavity Method, CCM）提供物理解释，将空腔变量的分层分解与 Aizenman–Sims–Starr 的随机重叠结构对应起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 CCM 表述缺乏清晰的物理图像，且存在歧义；本文旨在阐明其物理机制并修正早期表述中的含糊之处。

Method: 以相对基态的稀薄缺陷气体（dilute gas of defects）描述低温激发，先处理有限数目缺陷，再推广至指数级态数；通过缺陷在后代间的共享与新分支上涨落的更新来解释 GREM 结构与重叠的超度量性（ultrametricity）。

Result: 缺陷共享与涨落更新机制自然导出 GREM 结构和重叠的超度量性；在固定核（kernel）下，新场的 Gaussian 协方差将完整空腔变量（含其共同分量）与决定 Gibbs 质量（Gibbs masses）的同一层级结构联系起来。

Conclusion: CCM 的分层分解获得基于缺陷激发的物理解释，并与 Aizenman–Sims–Starr 随机重叠结构及 GREM 层级一致；同时澄清了先前表述的歧义。

Abstract: We present a physical explaination of the Constructive Cavity Method (CCM) for the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model, which identifies the decomposition of the cavity variables into layers with the random overlap structure of Aizenman--Sims--Starr. We describe the low-temperature excitations through a dilute gas of defects relative to the ground state. The sharing of these defects among the descendants and the renewal of the fluctuations along new branches produce the GREM structure and the ultrametricity of the overlaps. This mechanism is developed first with a finite number of defects and then extended to exponential populations of states. At fixed kernel, the Gaussian covariances of the fresh fields connect the complete cavity variables, including their common components, to the same hierarchy that determines the Gibbs masses. We also correct some ambiguities of earlier formulations.

</details>


### [295] [Learning by Confusion Locates a Parametrization-Dependent Fisher Information Maximum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34769)
*George Issa,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文为 Learning by confusion（LBC）方法建立了精确的贝叶斯最优混淆峰值平衡条件，证明对单一扫描参数下的平衡系统，峰值紧随对应 Fisher 信息（Fisher information）的极大值，从而给出其有限尺寸标度行为，并揭示峰值位置依赖于训练数据的采样参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LBC 可在不预设序参量的情况下定位相变，其特征精度曲线的中央极大值被解读为相变点，但该极大值的位置及其有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）由何决定长期不明。

Method: 推导贝叶斯最优混淆峰值（Bayes-optimal confusion peak）的精确平衡条件，并将其与单一扫描参数下平衡系统的 Fisher 信息极大值建立对应关系，随后在 Ising、Blume-Capel、Potts 与 Baxter-Wu 模型上进行数值检验。

Result: 峰值紧随 Fisher 信息极大值，由此直接给出有限尺寸标度；且同一批组态若按 T、ln T 或 β=1/T 均匀网格采样，可产生不同的有限尺寸伪临界（pseudocritical）轨迹。数值测试覆盖连续、三临界（tricritical）与一阶相变，均与预测一致。

Conclusion: 研究识别出 LBC 实际测量的统计量，并表明有限尺寸下的机器学习相变估计不仅取决于底层物理，也取决于训练数据的参数化（parametrization）方式。

Abstract: Learning by confusion (LBC) is a machine-learning method for locating phase transitions without assuming a known order parameter. Its characteristic accuracy curve contains a central maximum that is interpreted as the transition point, but what determines the location and finite-size scaling of this maximum has remained unclear. We derive an exact balance condition for the Bayes-optimal confusion peak and show that, for equilibrium systems with a single swept parameter, the peak closely follows the maximum of the corresponding Fisher information. This immediately gives its finite-size scaling and reveals an unexpected dependence on how the data are sampled: the same configurations arranged on grids uniform in $T$, $\ln T$, or $β=1/T$ can yield different finite-size pseudocritical trajectories. Tests on the Ising, Blume-Capel, Potts, and Baxter-Wu models confirm the prediction across continuous, tricritical, and first-order transitions. Our results identify the statistical quantity measured by LBC and show that finite-size machine-learning estimates can depend on the parametrization of the training data as well as on the underlying physics.

</details>


### [296] [Interaction dependence of the critical correlation length amplitude ratio in anisotropic scalar lattice $φ^4$ theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35081)
*Leonie Müller,Stefan Wessel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟重新检验弱各向异性二维标量格点 $φ^4$ 理论中的临界关联，发现关联长度振幅比（correlation length amplitude ratio）并非与此前报道的那样与局域相互作用强度无关，而是显著依赖于耦合强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献报道关联长度振幅比与局域相互作用强度无关，该结论若成立将简化对格点各向异性效应的理论描述；本文旨在检验该独立性是否真实成立。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟，在弱各向异性二维标量格点 $φ^4$ 理论中考察关联长度振幅比随相互作用强度（耦合）的变化。

Result: 模拟结果显示振幅比对相互作用强度有显著依赖；随着耦合增强，该比值在连续极限（continuum limit）与 Ising 极限之间过渡（interpolate），体现格点效应与局域相互作用之间的非平凡相互作用。

Conclusion: 先前的“振幅比与局域相互作用强度无关”的结论不成立，需对该原型格点场论（lattice field theory）中有限耦合下的各向异性效应给出修正的理论描述。

Abstract: We investigate critical correlations in the weakly anisotropic two-dimensional scalar lattice $φ^4$ theory and demonstrate that a previously reported independence of the correlation length amplitude ratio from the local interaction strength does not hold. Monte Carlo simulations instead reveal a pronounced dependence of this ratio on the interaction strength. As the coupling is increased, the amplitude ratio interpolates between the continuum and Ising limits, highlighting a nontrivial interplay between lattice effects and local interactions. These findings call for a revised theoretical description of anisotropy effects at finite coupling in this prototypical lattice field theory.

</details>


### [297] [Anomalous Mobility Enhancement in Restricted Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35324)
*Yuval Zippin,Stanislav Burov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在淬火陷阱模型（quenched trap model）框架下，研究几何受限（geometrical confinement）对反常介质中驱动输运的影响，解析给出纵向平均位移的标度律 \(\langle x_\parallel(t)\rangle \propto F_\parallel^{\alpha}t^{\alpha}\Omega^{-(1-\alpha)}\)，表明越窄的通道反常迁移率（anomalous mobility）越强，并经数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注几何受限如何改变无序介质中受驱粒子的反常输运行为，具体探讨通道横截面尺寸对纵向漂移的调控作用。

Method: 采用淬火陷阱模型：粒子在格点上进行偏置随机游走（biased random walk），淬火无序引入幂律分布的俘获时间（trapping times）；考虑沿一个方向无限、其余 \(d-1\) 个方向横截面有限的通道，在玻璃相（glassy regime，\(\alpha=T/T_g<1\)）中于长时间与弱力极限下推导纵向平均位移的解析表达式，并结合数值模拟检验。

Result: 解析得到标度关系 \(\langle x_\parallel(t)\rangle \propto F_\parallel^{\alpha}t^{\alpha}\Omega^{-(1-\alpha)}\)，其中 \(F_\parallel\) 为有效纵向力，\(\Omega\) 为横向截面格点数，故横向截面越小反常迁移率越大；结果适用于具有周期性横向边界条件的 \(d\) 维一般 Bravais 格（Bravais lattices）。数值模拟在不同晶格几何下证实了解析预测，且在反射边界条件下亦呈定量一致。

Conclusion: 几何受限可显著增强玻璃相介质中的反常输运，通道宽度为调控驱动输运提供了一个可解析标度的参数；该标度律对晶格几何具有普适性，并延伸至反射边界情形。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of geometrical confinement on driven transport in anomalous media within the framework of the quenched trap model. The particle performs a biased random walk on a lattice, where quenched disorder introduces power-law distributed trapping times. We consider channels that are infinite along one direction and have a finite cross-section in the remaining $d-1$ directions. In the glassy regime, where $α=T/T_g<1$, we derive an analytical expression for the mean longitudinal displacement in the long-time and weak-force limits. We find the scaling $\langle x_\parallel(t)\rangle \propto F_\parallel^αt^αΩ^{-(1-α)}$, where $F_\parallel>0$ is the effective longitudinal force and $Ω$ is the number of sites in a transverse cross-section. Thus, the anomalous mobility is enhanced for narrower channels. Our analytical result applies to general Bravais lattices in $d$ dimensions with periodic transverse boundary conditions. Numerical simulations confirm the analytical predictions for different lattice geometries and show quantitative agreement even for channels with reflecting boundary conditions.

</details>


### [298] [Microscopic Derivation of Stochastic Thin-Film Equation and Extensions to Two-Layer and Evaporating Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35422)
*Toby Kay,Serafim Kalliadasis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出一个自下而上的粗粒化框架，直接从N粒子哈密顿动力学推导随机薄膜方程，从而将分子尺度热涨落与宏观薄膜流体动力学连接起来，并给出含蒸发等非守恒界面动力学的新型随机方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典薄膜流体动力学模型虽能描述宏观液层，却无法刻画决定纳米尺度界面动力学的分子级热涨落（thermal fluctuations）；既有随机化处理依赖连续涨落Navier-Stokes方程的长波近似（long-wave approximation），属自上而下范式，与微观粒子轨迹缺乏形式化联系，且难以容纳复杂的非平衡边界机制。

Method: 基于广义涨落动态密度泛函理论（generalized fluctuating dynamic density-functional theory），构建系统的自下而上粗粒化（bottom-up coarse-graining）框架，从精确的N粒子哈密顿动力学（N-particle Hamiltonian dynamics）直接推导随机薄膜方程（stochastic thin-film equation）。

Result: 该第一性原理表述具备高度灵活性，可无缝整合多层界面与非守恒动力学（如蒸发，evaporation）；对这类复杂构型，框架严格给出全新的随机薄膜方程，其中包含刻画经典自上而下模型所缺失微观涨落的新兴噪声项（emergent noise terms）。

Conclusion: 研究建立了微观粒子动力学与随机薄膜流体动力学之间的严格联系，为含多层界面及非平衡边界过程的纳米尺度界面动力学建模提供了第一性原理基础。

Abstract: While classical thin-film hydrodynamics models widely govern macroscopic fluid layers, they fail to capture the molecular-level thermal fluctuations that dictate nanoscale interfacial dynamics. Standard stochastic extensions rely on the long-wave approximation of the continuum fluctuating Navier-Stokes equations; however, this top-down paradigm lacks a formal connection to microscopic particle trajectories and cannot readily accommodate complex, non-equilibrium boundary mechanisms. We resolve this limitation by deriving the stochastic thin-film equation directly from the exact underlying N-particle Hamiltonian dynamics. This is achieved via a systematic, bottom-up coarse-graining framework based on a generalized fluctuating dynamic density-functional theory. Crucially, this first principles formulation offers unparalleled flexibility, enabling the seamless integration of multi-layered interfaces and non-conserved dynamics such as evaporation. For these complex configurations, our framework rigorously yields entirely novel stochastic thin-film equations featuring emergent noise terms that encapsulate microscopic fluctuations missing from classical top-down models.

</details>


### [299] [Information-thermodynamic principles unveil the response of complex networks to perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35667)
*Andrea Filippo Beretta,Manlio De Domenico*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出基于密度矩阵（density matrix）的信息热力学（information thermodynamics）框架，刻画复杂网络（complex networks）沿任意动力学轨迹的扰动响应，突破标准扩散（diffusion）无法提升响应熵（response entropy）的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解复杂网络如何响应扰动是揭示其功能组织（functional organization）的关键；但在最大熵扰动先验（maximum-entropy perturbation prior）下，标准扩散过程无法增加响应熵，限制了既有描述能力。

Method: 建立沿任意动力学轨迹的、基于密度矩阵的扰动响应信息热力学；该框架奠基于信息论的最小耗散原理（principle of least dissipation），并引入广义自由能（generalized free energy）以量化相对瞬时扩散参考基准的偏离。

Result: 通过非线性复杂网络（nonlinear complex networks）示例展示该形式化框架，揭示出标准扩散描述无法捕捉的响应多样性增强（enhanced response diversity）区间。

Conclusion: 密度矩阵信息热力学为复杂网络扰动响应分析提供了超越标准扩散描述的普适工具，可识别响应多样性增强的动力学机制。

Abstract: Understanding how complex networks respond to perturbations is key to uncovering their functional organization. We show that, for a maximum-entropy perturbation prior, standard diffusion cannot increase response entropy, a measure of the diversity of the system's response. To overcome this limitation, we develop a density-matrix-based information thermodynamics of perturbation responses along arbitrary dynamical trajectories. We show that this framework is grounded in an information-theoretic principle of least dissipation and introduces a generalized free energy that quantifies deviations from an instantaneous diffusive reference. We then illustrate the formalism using nonlinear complex networks, revealing regimes of enhanced response diversity that cannot be captured by standard diffusive descriptions.

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [300] [Maximization of nuclear fusion reactivity via optimal energy distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35507)
*Roberto Onofrio,Kamel Ourabah,Carlo Presilla*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该研究考察核聚变反应率（nuclear fusion reactivity）相对于能量概率分布选择的优化问题：反应率平均可用动能呈凹函数时单一麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布（Maxwell-Boltzmann, MB）最优，呈凸函数时双峰MB分布最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清能量概率分布形态如何影响核聚变反应率，并检验广义分布（superstatistics）能否超越单一或双峰MB分布的优化上限。

Method: 以反应率对平均可用动能的凹凸性（concavity/convexity）为判据进行分布优化，并将结果置于三类超统计（superstatistics）广义能量分布（连续MB分布叠加）框架下对比，最后用氘-氘（D-D）与氘-氚（D-T）截面经验参数化验证。

Result: 在所考察的所有超统计情形中，反应率增益均被所提出的最优分布所界定；在低平均能量下采用最优双峰MB分布时，D-D与D-T反应率提升约2倍，该区间对磁约束等离子体（magnetically confined plasmas）尤为重要。

Conclusion: 最优能量分布由反应率曲线的凹凸性决定，超统计分布的增益受其约束；对磁约束聚变相关的低能区，双峰MB分布可实现约2倍反应率增强。

Abstract: We investigate the optimization of nuclear fusion reactivity relative to the choice of energy probability distribution. A single Maxwell-Boltzmann (MB) distribution is found to be optimal in the region where reactivity is a concave function of the average available kinetic energy, specifically near the reactivity peak. Conversely, in regions where the reactivity curve is convex, optimization is achieved through a bimodal MB distribution. We then discuss our results in the context of three classes of generalized energy distributions (superstatistics), which are modeled as a continuum of MB distributions. As expected, in all cases examined, the reactivity gains provided by superstatistics are bounded by the proposed optimal distribution. Finally, we apply these findings to deuterium-deuterium and deuterium-tritium fusion processes using empirical parametrizations of their respective cross sections. For both reactions, employing optimal bimodal MB distributions at low average energies-of particular interest for magnetically confined plasmas-yields a reactivity enhancement of approximately a factor of 2.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [301] [Exothermic Dark Matter with a Dark Higgs: The LZ 248 keV Event and the Galactic Center Excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25423)
*Jinheung Kim,Jongkuk Kim,Pyungwon Ko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）报告的 248 keV 核反冲事件，可由一个暗 \(U(1)\) 模型中的放热暗物质（exothermic dark matter）解释；该模型的暗希格斯（dark Higgs）同时产生暗光子质量与质量劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释 LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）观测到的 248 keV 核反冲事件，并检验放热暗物质（exothermic dark matter）框架能否在与现有天文与宇宙学限制相容的前提下给出该信号。

Method: 构建暗 \(U(1)\) 模型，由暗希格斯（dark Higgs）生成暗光子质量与激发态—基态质量劈裂；计算激发态占比（excited-state fraction），并对动能混合（kinetic mixing）\(ε=10^{-4}\) 求解，得到暗光子质量的两个分支。

Result: 在 \(ε=10^{-4}\) 下得到暗光子质量约为 1 GeV 与 26 GeV 的两个分支；INTEGRAL/SPI 对激发态衰变的限制约束了 LZ 事件率，宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）限制在其似然最小值处排除较轻分支。

Conclusion: 当暗希格斯玻色子质量接近暗光子质量时，较重分支与南天区银心（Galactic Center）\(γ\) 射线超出相符，从而可同时解释 LZ 事件与该 \(γ\) 射线信号。

Abstract: LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) reported a $248~\mathrm{keV}$ nuclear-recoil event. Exothermic dark matter can explain it in a dark $U(1)$ model whose dark Higgs generates the dark photon mass and the mass splitting. A computed excited-state fraction gives two branches with dark photon masses near $1$ and $26~\mathrm{GeV}$ for kinetic mixing $ε=10^{-4}$. INTEGRAL/SPI limits on excited-state decays bound the LZ rate, and cosmic microwave background limits exclude the lighter branch at its likelihood minimum. For a dark Higgs boson near the dark photon mass, the heavier branch agrees with the southern-sky Galactic Center $γ$-ray excess.

</details>


### [302] [A Low Scale for Quark-Lepton Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33152)
*Lawrence J. Hall,Claudio Andrea Manzari,Clara Murgui*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过重整化群分析Yukawa耦合演化，发现三代费米子质量关系指向约10^6–10^8 GeV的低能标夸克-轻子统一（Pati-Salam），并给出可被μ→e转换实验检验的具体理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量的费米子质量（fermion masses）可能暗示夸克-轻子统一（quark-lepton unification）能标，本文旨在检验质量关系是否在同一能标出现并据此构建低能标统一理论。

Method: 研究标准模型Yukawa耦合的重整化群演化（renormalization-group evolution），检查质量关系出现的能标，并构造包含层级味结构（hierarchical flavor texture）与Clebsch结构的具体统一理论，同时考察味混合与有限阈值修正（threshold corrections）的影响。

Result: y_τ=y_b与y_μ=3y_s几乎在同一能标（约10^6 GeV）出现；第一代在约10^8 GeV达到Georgi-Jarlskog关系y_e≃y_d/3。若电子质量有独立起源，较重两代关系可在低至(5–7)×10^5 GeV实现；两个具体理论分别给出4.6^{+1.2}_{-0.9}×10^6 GeV与1.5^{+0.7}_{-0.5}×10^7 GeV（后者预测|V_cb|=0.042±0.002），并固定矢量轻夸克（vector-leptoquark）耦合。

Conclusion: 该图景自然指向低能标Pati-Salam对称性，且味混合与阈值修正不破坏之；μ→e核转换（μ→e conversion in nuclei）可作为统一的直接检验，未来及拟建实验可部分或完全覆盖理论所选区域。

Abstract: The measured fermion masses may be pointing to the scale of quark-lepton unification. Studying the renormalization-group evolution of the Standard Model Yukawa couplings, we find that $y_τ=y_b$ and $y_μ=3y_s$ occur at nearly the same scale, around $10^6$ GeV, while the first generation reaches the Georgi-Jarlskog relation $y_e\simeq y_d/3$ at order $10^8$ GeV and can arise from the same Clebsch structure through a simple hierarchical flavor texture. This pattern naturally suggests low-scale Pati-Salam symmetry. We show that flavor mixing and finite threshold corrections preserve this picture. If the electron mass has an independent origin, the two heavier-generation relations can be realized in theories with a unification scale as low as $(5$-$7)\times10^5$ GeV. We identify two simple theories that accommodate all three relations and in which the measured masses fix the flavor structure. The first unifies all three generations at $4.6^{+1.2}_{-0.9}\times10^6$ GeV; the second, with symmetric Yukawa matrices, at $1.5^{+0.7}_{-0.5}\times10^7$ GeV, and predicts $|V_{cb}|=0.042\pm0.002$. Their shared light-family texture fixes the relevant vector-leptoquark couplings, making $μ\to e$ conversion in nuclei a direct test of unification: upcoming searches will probe part of the region selected by the two theories, and proposed facilities could cover it completely.

</details>


### [303] [Muon Tridents at FASER and NA64$μ$: Motivation, Predictions, and Prospects for Precision Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33153)
*Reinaldo Francener,Victor P. Goncalves,Felix Kling,Gabriel Rabelo-Soares*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 LHC 前向区高能缪子束在 FASER 实验中的缪子三体散射（muon trident scattering），改进理论描述并给出 Run 3 的事件率与微分分布预测，指出该过程可在 TeV 能区首次被测量，截面精度可达百分级，并可扩展至 NA64μ 实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC 质子-质子（pp）碰撞前向区产生的高能缪子（muon）通量极为强烈，为在 TeV 能区研究稀有电磁相互作用（electromagnetic interactions）提供了独特机会；缪子三体散射此前尚未在该能区被观测。

Method: 在 FASER 实验中考察缪子-原子核相互作用（muon-nucleus interactions）中的缪子三体散射过程，回顾并改进其理论描述；结合真实探测器接收度（detector acceptance）与主要本底（background）贡献，计算总事件率与微分分布，并研究核形状因子（nuclear form factors）处理方式及库仑修正（Coulomb corrections）等高阶 QED 效应的影响。

Result: 预计 FASER 已记录超过 10^5 个此类事件；预测表明该过程可借助 FASER 谱仪（spectrometer）在 TeV 能区首次被测量，截面（cross-section）预期精度达百分级（percent level）。分析扩展至 NA64μ 实验后显示，两者联合可在广阔且未被探测的能区考察缪子三体截面的能量依赖性。

Conclusion: 缪子三体散射是 FASER 与 NA64μ 可触及的可行物理课题，能够首次在 TeV 能区实现测量并提供高精度截面结果；核形状因子与高阶 QED 效应是影响理论预测的重要不确定来源，需在数据分析中审慎处理。

Abstract: The intense flux of high-energy muons produced in the far-forward region of $pp$ collisions at the LHC provides a unique opportunity to study rare electromagnetic interactions at TeV energies. In this work, we investigate the muon trident scattering in muon-nucleus interactions at the FASER experiment, which is expected to have recorded more than $10^5$ such events. The theoretical description of the process is reviewed and improved. Predictions for the total event rates and differential distributions expected during Run~3 of the LHC are presented, taking into account realistic detector acceptance and the main background contributions. The dependence of the predictions on the treatment of the nuclear form factors and the impact of higher-order QED effects such as Coulomb corrections are investigated. Our results indicate that the muon trident process can be measured for the first time at TeV energies using FASER's spectrometer, with a projected precision on the cross-section at the percent level. We also extended our analysis to the NA64$μ$ experiment and showed that, together with FASER, these experiments can probe the energy dependence of the muon trident cross-section over a broad unexplored energy range.

</details>


### [304] [Probing Orbital Angular Momentum Distributions in Elastic Vector Meson Production in Longitudinally Polarized Electron--Proton Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33255)
*Yuri V. Kovchegov,Brandon Manley*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在小 $x$ 的极化电子-质子碰撞中，弹性矢量介子产生的纵向双自旋不对称（double-spin asymmetry, DSA）的 $\cos\phi_{k\Delta}$ 方位角谐波可作为探测小 $x$ 处轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum, OAM）分布的有力观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道角动量（OAM）分布在小 $x$ 区域的实验提取仍缺乏明确观测量，本文受既有先驱性提议启发，寻找可探测小 $x$ OAM 分布的新的物理量。

Method: 在极化小 $x$ 形式体系（polarized small-$x$ formalism）内，计算纵向极化电子-质子碰撞中弹性矢量介子产生的纵向双自旋不对称的分子，并分别处理横向与纵向极化的矢量介子。

Result: 计算表明，在小的横向动量转移下，DSA 的 $\cos\phi_{k\Delta}$ 方位角谐波（$\phi_{k\Delta}$ 为偶极/Trento 参考系中出射电子与介子横向动量之间的方位角）对 OAM 分布敏感，可为其提供有前景的探测途径。

Conclusion: 该结果可推广至多种矢量介子种类，从而开辟一类新的观测量，用于从实验数据中提取 OAM 分布。

Abstract: We consider elastic vector meson production in longitudinally polarized electron-proton collisions at small $x$. Inspired by previous pioneering proposals in the literature, we calculate the numerator of the longitudinal double-spin asymmetry (DSA) within the polarized small-$x$ formalism for both the transversely and longitudinally polarized vector mesons. Our calculation demonstrates that the $\cosφ_{kΔ}$ azimuthal harmonic of the DSA for the elastic vector meson production at small transverse momentum transfer (with $φ_{kΔ}$ the azimuthal angle between the transverse momenta of the outgoing electron and meson in the dipole/Trento frame) provides a promising probe of the orbital angular momentum (OAM) distributions at small $x$. Our results can be applied to several different vector meson species, thus opening up a new class of observables that can be used to extract OAM distributions from experimental data.

</details>


### [305] [Dissecting flavor tagging with graph neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33757)
*Ankita Budhraja,Sunny Kumar Singh,Balbeer Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将ParticleNet的夸克/胶子判别能力分解为“多重数（multiplicity）贡献”与“固定多重数下的残余结构贡献”，并发现网络输出可解释为两部分对数似然比（log-likelihood ratio）之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（graph neural networks）如ParticleNet被广泛用于夸克/胶子喷注鉴别（quark/gluon jet tagging），但其判别能力在多重数与喷注其余结构之间如何分配尚不清楚，尤其缺乏在不同横动量（transverse momentum）与多重数下的差分研究。

Method: 在Pythia生成的Z+jet过程中训练ParticleNet，并在80≤p_T^jet≤400 GeV宽范围内对两类样本作横动量与快度（rapidity）匹配；随后按横动量与多重数分bin分析输出，并通过固定多重数下重标度组分径向分布（radial spread）来探测网络对喷注宽度的响应。

Result: 网络在每个p_T^jet bin中都学到胶子喷注比例随多重数的变化，包括两类等概率时多重数位置的移动，说明其联合使用喷注能标与子结构（substructure）；固定多重数下胶子概率均值与观测胶子比例一致但分布展宽较大，据此可将输出分解为多重数对数似然比与残余对数似然比，并正确识别20-34%多重数倾向相反类别的喷注。

Conclusion: 网络将更宽的喷注判为更像胶子，且该响应在低与中等多重数处最强——正是仅凭多重数无法决定喷注类别的区域；这表明结构信息主要在多重数判别力不足时发挥作用。

Abstract: Graph neural networks such as ParticleNet are widely used for quark/gluon jet tagging. We study how the discriminating power of such a network is distributed between constituent multiplicity and the remaining structure of the jet, differentially in jet transverse momentum and multiplicity. To this end, we train ParticleNet on quark and gluon jets from Z+jet production in Pythia, with the two classes matched in transverse momentum and rapidity over the wide range $80\leq p_{T}^{\rm jet}\leq 400$ GeV. We find that the network learns how the fraction of gluon jets changes with the multiplicity in every $p_{T}^{\rm jet}$ bin, including the shift of the multiplicity at which quark and gluon jets are equally likely, which implies that it uses the jet scale (reconstructed from its constituents) jointly with its substructure. At fixed multiplicity, the gluon probabilities it assigns agree on average with the observed gluon fractions, but are widely spread about the mean. This allows us to interpret its output as the sum of the multiplicity log-likelihood ratio and a residual log-likelihood ratio at fixed multiplicity, which allows the network to correctly identify 20-34% of the jets whose multiplicity favors the opposite class. By rescaling the radial spread of the constituents at fixed multiplicity, we find that the network considers broader jets more gluon-like, and that this response is largest at low and intermediate multiplicity, where the multiplicity alone does not decide the class of a jet.

</details>


### [306] [Radiative Higgs decay to $ψ\left(4230\right)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34117)
*Ahmed Ali,Muhammad Jamil Aslam*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了希格斯玻色子辐射衰变（radiative Higgs decay）\(H \to \psi(4230)+\gamma\) 在标准模型（SM）内及超出标准模型（beyond the SM）情形下的衰变率，并给出相应的分支比（branching ratio）预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \(\psi(4230)\) 是奇特四夸克态（exotic tetraquark state）的候选者，研究其与希格斯的辐射耦合可为理解该态的内部结构与希格斯-粲夸克耦合（Higgs-Yukawa coupling of the charm quark）提供探针。

Method: 采用 \(\psi(4230)\) 的轻子耦合（leptonic coupling）构造所谓“直接振幅（direct amplitude）”的实用参数化，以估计希格斯衰变振幅所涉及的短距离重叠（short-distance overlap）。

Result: 得到标准模型下的分支比 \(\mathcal{B}_{\rm SM}(H\to\psi(4230)\gamma)=4.88^{+4.71}_{-4.52}\times 10^{-7}\)，不确定度主要来自 \(\psi(4230)\) 轻子衰变宽度的实验误差；当粲夸克希格斯-汤川耦合取 \(y_c=-3\) 时，分支比升至约 \(10^{-6}\)。

Conclusion: \(H\to\psi(4230)\gamma\) 的分支比在标准模型下为 \(10^{-7}\) 量级，且对 \(y_c\) 的取值敏感，可增强至约 \(10^{-6}\)，因此该衰变道可作为约束粲夸克汤川耦合的潜在通道。

Abstract: We calculate the decay rate for the radiative Higgs decay $H \to ψ(4230) + γ$ within and beyond the SM. The vector state $ψ(4230)$ is a candidate exotic tetraquark state and has the quantum numbers $I^G(J^{PC})=0^-(1^{--})$, having a mass $m_{ψ(4230)}=4221.7 \pm 2.5$ MeV and a decay width $Γ_{ψ(4230)}=51 \pm 7$ MeV. We propose a practical parametrization of the so-called {\it direct amplitude}, using the leptonic coupling of the $ψ(4230)$ to estimate the short-distance overlap relevant to the Higgs decay amplitude. We find ${\cal B}_{\rm SM}( H \to ψ(4230) γ)=4.88^{+4.71}_{-4.52}\times 10^{-7}$, where the uncertainties are dominated by the experimental uncertainty in the leptonic decay width of the $ψ(4230)$. The branching ratio increases to about $10^{-6}$ for $y_c=-3 $, where $y_c$ is the Higgs-Yukawa coupling of the charm quark.

</details>


### [307] [Anomalous Soft Photons: A Brief Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34216)
*Klaus Reygers*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是对软光子之谜（soft-photon puzzle）的实验视角简明综述，概述其理论基础、代表性测量与主要实验挑战，并指向更详细的综述文献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子反应（hadronic reactions）中的软光子（soft-photon）数据与基于Low软光子定理（Low's soft-photon theorem）的预言之间存在长期偏差，该偏差即“软光子之谜”，需要从实验角度加以梳理。

Method: 会议论文集形式，采用紧凑的实验视角，总结理论基线（theoretical baseline）、代表性测量结果以及主要实验难点。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值或测量结论，仅说明汇总了理论基线与代表性实验测量，并归纳了实验层面的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 软光子之谜仍未在实验上得到澄清；详细的实验历史与理论记述参见Phys. Rept., 1097:1, 2024的综述文章。

Abstract: These conference proceedings provide a compact experimental perspective on the soft-photon puzzle. The puzzle refers to the long-standing discrepancy between soft-photon data in hadronic reactions and predictions based on Low's soft-photon theorem. We summarize the theoretical baseline, representative measurements, and the main experimental challenges. For a detailed account of the experimental history and theory, we refer to the recent review article Phys. Rept., 1097:1, 2024.

</details>


### [308] ["Inevitable'' nonstrange tetraquark states and $ρρ$ interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34741)
*Yanyue Pan,Yuying Hao,Shirui Zhao,Hongxia Huang,Jialun Ping*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在夸克离域色屏蔽模型（quark delocalization color screening model）框架下系统研究两个ρ介子在IJ=00,02,11,20,22五个通道的相互作用，发现存在强吸引势可形成束缚态，其中IJ=02最有望成为"必然"存在的非奇异四夸克态（nonstrange tetraquark）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在系统考察双ρ介子（ρρ）体系在各量子数通道中的相互作用，并借鉴d*双重重子（dibaryon）预言，寻找可实验搜寻的非奇异四夸克态候选者。

Method: 采用夸克离域色屏蔽模型对IJ=00、02、11、20和22五个通道的双ρ介子相互作用进行系统计算，并结合束缚能、集团间距（intercluster distance）与衰变性质分析进行判断。

Result: 两ρ介子间存在强吸引势，易于形成束缚态；束缚能最大的是IJ=02和IJ=00，均超过一百MeV，而IJ=20束缚最弱，仅几MeV。集团间距结果支持分子态（molecular）解释，且ρρ与ΔΔ相互作用具有相似性。

Conclusion: 综合d*双重重子预言的启发与衰变性质分析，IJ=02的ρρ态是最有希望成为"必然"存在的非奇异四夸克态的候选者，值得开展实验搜寻。

Abstract: Investigation of the interaction between two $ρ$ mesons with $IJ=00, 02, 11, 20$ and $22$ is conducted systematically in the framework of the quark delocalization color screening model. The results show that there is a strong attractive potential between two $ρ$ mesons, which makes it easier for these two mesons to form bound states. The largest binding energies occur for $IJ=02$ and $IJ=00$ with values over a hundred MeV, while the $IJ=20$ state is the least bound and has a binding energy of only a few MeV. Moreover, the results of intercluster distances favor a molecular interpretation of these states. The interaction analysis reveals that the $ρρ$ and $ΔΔ$ interactions share certain similarities. Drawing inspiration from the $d^*$ dibaryon prediction and from decay-property analyses, we suggest that the $ρρ$ with $IJ=02$ may be the most promising candidate of the ``inevitable'' nonstrange tetraquark state, which deserves experimental search.

</details>


### [309] [The Pion Parton Distribution Puzzle in the High x Region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34798)
*Misak Sargsian*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于价夸克谱函数表示提出一种描述强子部分子结构的新理论方法，并将其应用于π介子价区PDF的计算，发现π介子中残余质量需求很小、$q\bar q$团簇高度虚，且xPDF的峰高与峰位决定了价PDF在$x\to1$的解析行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前该方法已用于核子PDF计算并揭示核子的π介子云结构；本研究旨在同一框架下计算π介子在价区的部分子分布函数（parton distribution function, PDF），以检验该方法的普适性并刻画π介子的软部分子结构。

Method: 将无质量价夸克置于残余强子的平均场中，用价$q\bar q$团簇嵌入π介子残余场来描述软部分子结构；采用唯象光锥波函数（Light-Front wave functions）计算π介子PDF，并通过拟合经验π介子xPDF在起始标度$Q_0$处的峰高与峰位来确定团簇虚度与残余系统质量等参数。

Result: x加权价PDF（xPDF）的峰位依赖于$q\bar q$团簇虚度与残余系统质量参数；拟合表明，与核子情形不同，π介子仅需很小的残余质量；且要求$q\bar q$团簇高度虚、相互作用夸克携带团簇几乎全部动量。该图像与Feynman机制主导一致，并能实际描述近期在$x\to1$极限观测到的$\sim(1-x)$行为。

Conclusion: 该框架可自洽描述π介子价区PDF；一个非平凡的观察是，xPDF的峰高与峰位定义了价PDF在$x\to1$极限的解析行为，且PDF中的硬成分所占空间很小。

Abstract: We developed a new theoretical approach in the description of partonic structure of hadrons based on spectral function representation of valence quarks. In this approach massless valence quarks are considered in the mean field of residual hadron. This approach initially was applied to the calculation of parton distribution function~(PDF) of nucleons that resulted in an observation of pion-cloud structure of nucleon. In this report we present our calculation of pion PDF in the valence region within the same approach. In this approach we describe the soft partonic structure of pion through the valence $q\bar q$ cluster, embedded in the residual field of the pion. The pion PDF is calculated using phenomenological Light-Front wave functions for the $q\bar q$ cluster and the residual field. Our result indicates that the peak position of the x weighted valence PDF~(xPDF) depends on parameters characterizing the virtuality of the $q\bar q$ cluster and the mass of the residual system. These parameters are evaluated by fitting the calculated PDF to the height and the peak position of empirical pion xPDF at starting $Q_0$. They indicate that unlike the nucleon case, very little residual mass is needed in the pion to describe existing pion PDFs in the valence region. They also require the $q\bar q$-cluster to be highly virtual with the interacting quark carrying almost all of the momentum of the $q\bar q$ cluster. This picture is consistent with the dominance of the Feynman mechanism leaving little room for the hard component in the PDF. The calculation practically describes the $\sim (1-x)$ behavior observed recently at $x\to 1$ limit. Our non-trivial observation is that the height and the peak position of xPDF define the analytic behavior of valence PDF at $x\to 1$ limit.

</details>


### [310] [Probing the Collins effect at photon-photon colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35285)
*Simone Anedda,Carlo Flore,Francesco Murgia,Cristian Pisano*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出在双光子过程（γγ→h₁h₂X）中，通过测量近背对背无极化强子围绕喷注推力轴（thrust-axis）的方位角分布来研究柯林斯效应（Collins effect），并给出未来环形对撞机（FCC）与LHC超周边碰撞（UPC）可及运动学构型的数值估计，预言不对称度可达约10–15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柯林斯效应（Collins effect）关联夸克横向极化与碎裂强子的方位角分布，是理解夸克碎裂机制（fragmentation mechanism）的重要探针；现有柯林斯函数（Collins functions）存在多种模型，需要新的、干净的观测量加以区分。

Method: 以γγ→h₁h₂X 过程为对象，初态准真实光子（quasi-real photons）可由两个散射轻子或两个带电重核在超周边碰撞（ultra-peripheral collisions）中提供；通过分析近背对背无极化强子（主要为带电π介子）相对喷注推力轴（thrust-axis）的方位角分布提取柯林斯不对称性，并对FCC及LHC超周边碰撞实验可及的运动学构型作数值估计。

Result: 数值估计表明相应的方位角不对称度可以相当显著，最高可达约10–15%，在原则上可由FCC和计划中的LHC超周边碰撞实验测量。

Conclusion: 该测量将有助于阐明夸克到强子的碎裂机制，并在原则上可区分现有不同的柯林斯函数模型。

Abstract: We propose to study the Collins effect in the production of almost back-to-back unpolarized hadrons (mainly charged pions) by looking at their azimuthal distributions around the jet thrust-axis in the process $γγ\to h_1h_2X$. The quasi-real photons in the initial state can be emitted either by two scattering leptons or two charged heavy nuclei in ultra-peripheral collisions. We provide numerical estimates for kinematic configurations in principle accessible at the Future Circular Collider and at the planned experiments on ultra-peripheral collisions at the LHC. We find that the corresponding asymmetries can be sizable, even up to around 10-15%. The proposed measurements would thus shed light on the fragmentation mechanism of quarks into hadrons and, in principle, would allow one to distinguish among different existing models for the Collins functions.

</details>


### [311] [The neutrino fog for inelastic dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35789)
*C. S. Amarasinghe*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文刻画了非弹性暗物质（inelastic dark matter）在氙靶上散射时的中微子雾（neutrino fog）行为：非弹性运动学改变反冲谱并产生定性不同的雾状图景，部分参数区域雾完全消失，另一些区域雾先消散又在更大曝光下重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子雾会限制暗物质直接探测（direct detection）的发现极限（discovery limit），而非弹性散射的运动学可能显著改变信号反冲谱，因此需要系统评估非弹性暗物质情形下雾的形态及其对探测前景的影响。

Method: 基于非弹性暗物质在氙（xenon）靶上的散射运动学，分析其对反冲谱（recoil spectrum）的重塑，并据此考察不同非弹性参数空间区域内中微子雾的分布与演化，同时讨论多个归一化不确定的本底源（background sources）对雾起始行为的影响。

Result: 在部分非弹性参数区域，未来任意规模的氙实验都不出现雾；在另一些区域，雾先被消除但会在更大曝光（exposure）下重新出现。多个归一化不确定的本底源可使雾的起始点随暗物质质量与质量劈裂（mass splitting）发生不连续跳变，而发现极限则平滑变化。

Conclusion: 非弹性散射使中微子雾图景与弹性情形定性不同，且雾的起始与发现极限对参数和本底不确定性的响应并不同步；此外，中微子速率不确定度的减小可能反而抬高由雾不透明度（fog opacity）导出的中微子下限（neutrino floors），这是一个反直觉的机制。

Abstract: We characterize the behavior of the neutrino fog for inelastic dark matter scattering on xenon. The inelastic scattering kinematics reshape the recoil spectrum of the signal and produce a qualitatively different neutrino fog landscape. In some regions of the inelastic parameter space the fog is completely absent for future xenon experiments at any scale, while in other regions the fog is dispelled only to reappear at larger exposures. We find that multiple background sources with uncertain normalizations can make the onset of the fog jump discontinuously with the dark matter mass and mass splitting, while the discovery limit varies smoothly. We also describe a mechanism by which reduced uncertainties on neutrino rates may have the unintuitive effect of raising neutrino floors derived from the fog opacity.

</details>


### [312] [Dark Matter phase space distribution under a fully relativistic treatment. Eddington inversion according to general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33150)
*Jaime Hoefken Zink,Pellegrino Piantadosi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将经典Eddington反演推广为完全相对论形式，从暗物质密度剖面重构相空间分布函数，并应用于银河系中心强引力场情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Eddington反演基于牛顿引力，在黑洞附近等强引力场（strong gravitational field）中可能失效，需建立相对论性的相空间重构方法以支撑暗物质现象学研究。

Method: 将暗物质视为静态球对称时空中的有效理想流体（effective perfect fluid），用Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程确定引力背景，导出以无穷远处守恒粒子能量表示的Abel型反演（Abel-type inversion），并给出消除有限边界导致非物理发散的正则化（regularization）程序。

Result: 所得表达式在弱场非相对论极限下退化为经典Eddington反演；对含广义Navarro-Frenk-White晕与Gondolo-Silk尖峰的银河系模型，相对论与牛顿速度分布在约10^{-5} pc处开始偏离，靠近黑洞时相对论峰值速度可达牛顿预言约两倍；在中子星暗物质捕获（dark-matter capture）中，人马座A*附近相对论捕获因子比Maxwell-Boltzmann预言约小一个量级，而牛顿Eddington结果可高估约两倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，强引力场中相对论相空间重构对暗物质现象学具有重要影响。

Abstract: We develop a fully relativistic extension of Eddington inversion to reconstruct the phase-space distribution function of dark matter from its density profile. Treating dark matter as an effective perfect fluid in a static, spherically symmetric spacetime, we use the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations to determine the gravitational background and derive an Abel-type inversion that yields the distribution function in terms of the conserved particle energy measured at infinity. We show that the resulting expression recovers the classical Eddington inversion in the weak-field, nonrelativistic limit. We also investigate the finite-boundary contribution, which can introduce an unphysical divergence, and present a regularization procedure that removes it. We apply our method to a Milky Way model consisting of a generalized Navarro-Frenk-White halo and a Gondolo-Silk dark-matter spike around Sagittarius A$^*$, neglecting baryonic contributions. The relativistic and Newtonian velocity distributions agree at radii far from the Galactic center but begin to differ at distances of order $10^{-5}\,\mathrm{pc}$. Closer to the black hole, the relativistic peak speed can reach approximately twice the Newtonian prediction. Finally, we derive a general expression for dark-matter capture by neutron stars that accommodates arbitrary ambient velocity distributions. Near Sagittarius A$^*$, the relativistic capture factor is nearly an order of magnitude smaller than the Maxwell-Boltzmann prediction, while the Newtonian Eddington result can overestimate it by a factor of approximately two. These results demonstrate the importance of relativistic phase-space reconstruction for dark-matter phenomenology in strong gravitational fields.

</details>


### [313] [Catastrophe Structure and Stability of Axion Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33549)
*Fedor V Kusmartsev,Joudy J F Beek,Jack C M Hughes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合非相对论薛定谔–泊松描述与突变论（catastrophe theory），建立轴子星（axion star）稳定性的统一图像：在单参数高斯变分能量泛函 E(R;N) 中，平衡态经由 Arnold A2、A3、A4 突变出现或消失，从而对轴子星各相给出紧凑分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子星是QCD轴子或类轴子粒子的自引力玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose–Einstein condensate），其稳定性决定其能否作为暗物质结构长期存在，以及会产生何种天体物理观测信号，因此需要统一的稳定性判据。

Method: 从具有周期轴子势的爱因斯坦–克莱因–戈尔登系统（Einstein–Klein–Gordon system）出发，推导有效运动方程与守恒量，构造单参数星型解族；再借高斯变分试探函数（Gaussian variational ansatz）将问题约化为解析能量泛函 E(R;N)，并用突变论分析随粒子数 N 与相互作用强度变化的平衡态分岔。

Result: 在排斥性六次（sextic）相互作用下，出现两支稳定分支（稀薄与致密轴子星）以及分隔二者的不稳定分支；同一突变判据可推广至完整余弦势与含QCD修正的势，折叠条件可由完整变分能量给出并数值求解。文中还区分了集体坐标试探函数内的稳定性与完整径向微扰稳定性（radial perturbative stability），并界定非相对论处理的适用区间。

Conclusion: 该框架提供轴子星相态的紧凑分类，并识别出与坍缩、类玻色新星（bosenova-like）发射及黑洞形成相关的、依赖模型的临界质量。

Abstract: Axion stars are self-gravitating Bose--Einstein condensates of QCD axions or axion-like particles. Their stability determines whether they can survive as dark-matter structures and what astrophysical signatures they may produce. Here we combine a non-relativistic Schrödinger--Poisson description with catastrophe theory to develop a unified picture of axion-star stability. Beginning with the Einstein--Klein--Gordon system for a periodic axion potential, we derive the effective equations of motion and conserved quantities and construct a one-parameter family of star-like solutions. A Gaussian variational ansatz reduces the problem to an analytic energy functional $E(R;N)$. Within this model, equilibria appear and disappear through Arnold $A_2$, $A_3$, and $A_4$ catastrophes as the particle number $N$ and interaction strengths vary. A repulsive sextic interaction produces two stable branches---dilute and dense axion stars---separated by an unstable branch. The same catastrophe criteria apply beyond the sextic truncation: for the full cosine and QCD-corrected potentials, the fold conditions follow from the complete variational energy and can be evaluated numerically. We also separate stability within the collective-coordinate ansatz from full radial perturbative stability and specify the range in which the non-relativistic treatment is reliable. This framework provides a compact classification of axion-star phases and identifies model-dependent critical masses associated with collapse, bosenova-like emission, and black-hole formation.

</details>


### [314] [Fermion Mass and Mixing Patterns from Fusion Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34720)
*Jacques Distler,Can Kilic,Sanjay Mathai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用不可逆对称性（noninvertible symmetries），特别是融合代数（fusion algebras），为费米子汤川耦合矩阵的纹理（texture）提供对称性解释，并通过蒙特卡洛扫描发现秩为8的融合代数 FR^{8,6}_8 对质量矩阵拟合最佳，但无法同时准确拟合质量与混合角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型（Standard Model）中费米子质量等级（hierarchy）长期缺乏解释，一种思路是借助对称性原理规定费米子汤川耦合（Yukawa couplings）矩阵的结构（纹理）。

Method: 引入不可逆对称性中的融合代数，用其结构为各场赋标签，从而决定复合算符（composite operator）在拉格朗日量中被压低的程度；在紫外完备（UV completion）中这对应算符等级，在红外观有效理论（IR effective theory）中成为汤川耦合。在不对具体紫外模型作假设的前提下，对未知威尔逊系数（Wilson coefficients）进行蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）数值扫描。

Result: 秩为8的融合代数 $\rm{FR}^{8,6}_8$ 对上夸克、下夸克和带电轻子汤川矩阵的纹理给出最佳拟合；但对 CKM 与 PMNS 混合矩阵（mixing matrices）的预测表明，所研究的融合代数均无法同时准确拟合质量与混合角。

Conclusion: 融合代数结构保证所得到的纹理在重整化群（RG running）下稳定，并可延续至低能标；但仅凭融合代数尚不足以为质量与混合角提供统一的自洽描述。

Abstract: The large hierarchy of fermion masses in the Standard Model has long posed a challenge to be explained. One approach is to try to invoke a symmetry principle to dictate the structure ("texture") of the matrices of fermion Yukawa couplings to the Higgs field. Here we invoke noninvertible symmetries, specifically, fusion algebras, to provide such textures. In particular, the structure of the fusion-algebra can be used to assign labels to each field, which determines how suppressed any given composite operator is as a term in the Lagrangian. In UV completions, this can be interpreted as specifying a hierarchy of operators that in the IR effective theory become the Yukawa couplings to the Higgs field. Remaining agnostic of a specific UV model, we perform a Monte Carlo numerical scan over unknown Wilson coefficients, and we identify the rank-8 fusion algebra $\rm{FR}^{8,6}_8$ as the one providing the best fit for the textures of the up-quark, down-quark and charged-lepton Yukawa matrices. We also study the predictions for the CKM and PMNS mixing matrices, with the conclusion that none of the fusion algebras we study provide an accurate fit for the masses and mixing angles simultaneously. The fusion algebra structure ensures that the textures that we find are stable under RG running and persist down to low energies.

</details>


### [315] [Explaining the universal Hagedorn temperature in string models of hadrons with fixed endpoints or baryon junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34882)
*Yuki Fujimoto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文推导了固定重端点弦与含重子结（baryon junction）弦的强子弦模型的质量公式与态密度渐近表达式，证明这些弦与普通开弦具有相同的Hagedorn温度，从而为近期唯象发现的Hagedorn温度普适性提供微观解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期唯象研究发现Hagedorn温度（Hagedorn temperature）具有普适性，但缺乏微观层面的理论解释；同时重-轻介子与轻介子的Regge斜率不同，使得固定端点弦是否保持相同Hagedorn温度成为需要澄清的问题。

Method: 针对具有一个固定重端点（fixed heavy endpoint）或重子结（baryon junction）的强子弦模型，推导质量公式以及态密度（density of states）的更精确渐近表达式，并比较其Hagedorn温度、Regge斜率（Regge slope）与态密度的渐近质量依赖关系。

Result: 固定一个端点的弦在相同张力与极化数（number of polarizations）下与普通开弦具有相同的Hagedorn温度，尽管重-轻介子与轻介子的Regge斜率不同；重子结图像与夸克-双夸克（quark-diquark）图像共享该Hagedorn温度，态密度的渐近质量依赖相同，仅整体归一化（normalization）不同。

Conclusion: 结果为Hagedorn温度普适性提供了微观解释；观测到的重子质量谱（baryon mass spectrum）对重子结与夸克-双夸克两种图像区分能力有限，但重子结模型与观测Regge斜率的偏差更大。

Abstract: We derive the mass formulas and more accurate asymptotic expressions for the densities of states for string models of hadrons with one fixed heavy endpoint or a baryon junction. We show that strings with one fixed endpoint have the same Hagedorn temperature as ordinary open strings at the same tension and number of polarizations, despite the different Regge slopes of heavy-light and light mesons. This provides a microscopic explanation for the universality of the Hagedorn temperature recently found phenomenologically. We also find that the baryon-junction and quark-diquark pictures share this Hagedorn temperature and yield the same asymptotic mass dependence of the density of states, differing only by an overall normalization. The observed baryon mass spectrum provides little discrimination between the two pictures, although the baryon-junction model has a larger discrepancy with the observed Regge slopes.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [316] [Methyl radicals dynamics on (100)--(2x1):H diamond surfaces based on refined variational transition state calculations of gas-surface reaction rate coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34584)
*Emerick Y. Guillaume,Luc Henrard,Ken Haenen,Danny E. P. Vanpoucke*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作结合密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）与变分过渡态理论（variational transition state theory, VTST），精确计算了氢终止(100)金刚石表面上甲基相关反应的速率系数，为金刚石化学气相沉积（chemical vapor deposition, CVD）生长的大尺度建模提供原子尺度参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石生长的理论建模需依据实验条件估计生长速率，这要求描述竞争性的吸附（adsorption）、脱附（desorption）与迁移（migration）机制所涉及的全部反应；而CVD生长中甲基（CH₃）是主要生长前驱体，其表面反应速率系数尚需精确刻画。

Method: 将DFT与VTST相结合，从而把分析扩展至无势垒反应（barrierless reaction）；同时对表面CH₃吸附物的准自由旋转（quasi-free rotation）进行严格处理，以克服振动谱有限差分计算中的数值误差，从而可靠评估体系配分函数（partition function）。

Result: 结果表明，消耗CH₃浓度最多的机制是其在表面自由基位点上的吸附；同时观察到CH₃沿(100)-(2x1):H表面二聚体（dimer）迁移具有各向异性（anisotropy）。

Conclusion: 所计算的甲基反应与迁移速率系数，结合氢反应及其他主要气-表面反应数据，弥合了金刚石生长的原子尺度描述与大尺度建模之间的差距。

Abstract: Theoretical modelling of diamond growth should ideally provide estimates of the growth rate depending on experimental conditions. To that end, a description of all possible reactions related to the competing adsorption, desorption and migration mechanisms is required. Here, we provide precise calculations of the reaction rate coefficient of methyl-related reactions on hydrogen-terminated (100) diamond surface, as $\mathrm{CH_3}$ is known to be the main growth precursor in chemical vapor deposition (CVD) growth of diamond. To achieve this goal, we successfully combined Density Functional Theory (DFT) and variational transition state theory (VTST), allowing to extend our analysis to barrierless reaction. Our rigorous treatment of the quasi-free rotation of $\mathrm{CH_3}$ adsorbates on the surface allows us to overcome numerical errors for the finite difference calculations of the vibrational spectra for evaluating the partition functions of the system. Our results suggest that the mechanism that deplete the $\mathrm{CH_3}$ concentration the most is the absorption on a surface radical site. We also evidence the anisotropy of $\mathrm{CH_3}$ migration along the dimer of the (100)-(2x1):H surface. In a more general perspective, the calculated reaction rates coefficient for methyl reactions and migration presented in this work, together with the data for hydrogen reaction and other main gas surface reactions, bridge the gap between the atomic-scale description and the large-scale modelling of diamond growth.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [317] [Cooperation of itinerant and localized physics in the magnetic phase diagram of 214 Ruddlesden-Popper iridates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33196)
*Francesco Cassol,Michele Casula,Benjamin Lenz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）框架下计算含自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的三角格子$t_{2g}$哈伯德模型（Hubbard model）相图，发现Sr$_2$IrO$_4$与Ba$_2$IrO$_4$处于巡游电子与局域电子协同稳定反铁磁序的中间区域，光导率的双峰结构可作为该协同区间的可靠判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $5d^5$ Ruddlesden-Popper铱氧化物的反铁磁绝缘态起源存有争议，究竟属Slater型还是Mott-Heisenberg型尚无定论，相关实验与理论报道相互矛盾，亟需统一的描述框架。

Method: 针对真实的三轨道$t_{2g}$哈伯德模型，在纳入自旋轨道耦合的条件下，采用动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）计算相互作用-温度相图（interaction-temperature phase diagram）。

Result: 两种材料均落在巡游电子与局域电子协同作用的中间区间，且Ba$_2$IrO$_4$的关联强度略高于Sr$_2$IrO$_4$。奈尔温度（Néel temperature）附近能隙的行为与谱函数中的自旋极化子（spin-polaron）特征均无法唯一区分协同区间，而仅在该区间出现的光导率（optical conductivity）双峰结构构成可靠指纹。

Conclusion: 将两种化合物置于统一相图中，可调和看似矛盾的实验与理论结果，并为214系铱氧化物及其他自旋轨道耦合关联材料的研究提供统一框架。

Abstract: The $5d^5$ Ruddlesden-Popper iridates Sr$_2$IrO$_4$ and Ba$_2$IrO$_4$ have attracted considerable attention as spin-orbit entangled analogues of the cuprates, yet the origin of their antiferromagnetic insulating state, of Slater or of Mott-Heisenberg type, remains debated. We find that both materials sit in an intermediate regime in which itinerant and localized electrons cooperate in stabilizing the antiferromagnetic order, with Ba$_2$IrO$_4$ slightly more correlated than Sr$_2$IrO$_4$. We obtain this by computing, within dynamical mean-field theory, the interaction-temperature phase diagram of a realistic three-orbital $t_{2g}$ Hubbard model with spin-orbit coupling. Our study shows that neither the behavior of the gap across the Néel temperature nor the spin-polaron features of the spectral function unambiguously identify the cooperative regime, whereas the two-peak structure of the optical conductivity is reproduced only there and therefore constitutes a reliable fingerprint. By placing both compounds in a common phase diagram, our results reconcile seemingly contradictory experimental and theoretical reports, and provide a unified framework for future studies of the 214 iridates and of other spin-orbit coupled correlated materials.

</details>


### [318] [Exact Magnetic Confinement of a Doped Hole by Kinetic Frustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33585)
*Nikolai Peshcherenko,Yixin Zhang,Cristian D. Batista,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在有限开边界锯齿形（sawtooth）U=∞ 哈伯德链（Hubbard chain）中单空穴掺杂时，磁化强度（magnetization）M 的基态由 M 个极化三重态二聚体包裹一段长度 ℓs=L−M 的连通单重态区域；磁场通过选择 M 直接调控该受限长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有磁约束（magnetic confinement）机制通常依赖无序（disorder）或交换作用产生的弦张力（string tension，类似禁闭势）；本文旨在证明存在一种纯粹由动能阻挫（kinetic frustration）驱动的精确约束机制。

Method: 采用精确可解的有限开边界锯齿形 U=∞ 哈伯德链模型（单空穴掺杂），通过分析空穴在单重态区域内的驻波（standing wave）解及其在三重态背景中的指数尾，并考察直接跃迁与顶点介导跃迁（apex-mediated hopping）之间的干涉条件。

Result: 直接跃迁与顶点介导跃迁的相消干涉（destructive interference）使三重态键成为反射壁；在 t₂/t₁=[2+4cos(π/(ℓs+2))]^(−1/2) 时反射完全，形成位置简并的紧凑局域态（compact localized state）。任意固定磁场 0<h<h_sat 下，ℓs 在 L→∞ 时保持有限并随磁场增强阶梯式收缩，而 h=0 时单重态区域覆盖整个系统。

Conclusion: 磁约束无需无序或弦张力即可由动能阻挫精确产生，且约束长度可由外磁场按 M 连续选择、呈现阶梯式收缩，为相互作用量子链中的场致局域化提供了一种可解析的机制。

Abstract: We establish an exact mechanism of magnetic confinement driven purely by kinetic frustration. In a finite open sawtooth $U=\infty$ Hubbard chain of $L$ unit cells doped with one hole, the ground state at magnetization $M$ contains $M$ polarized triplet dimers surrounding a connected singlet region of length $\ell_{\rm s}=L-M$. A magnetic field selects $M$ and hence the confinement length. The hole forms a standing wave in the singlet region with exponential tails in the triplet background. Confinement requires neither disorder nor an exchange-generated string tension: destructive interference between direct and apex-mediated hopping makes the triplet bonds reflecting. At $t_2/t_1=[2+4\cos(π/(\ell_{\rm s}+2))]^{-1/2}$, they become perfectly reflecting, producing a position-degenerate compact localized state. At every fixed field $0<h<h_{\rm sat}$, the singlet region remains finite as $L\to\infty$ and shrinks stepwise with increasing field, whereas at $h=0$ it spans the system.

</details>


### [319] [Tuning from intrinsic to disorder-dominated quantum states in a kagome antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34230)
*Y. Tanaka,R. Kumakura,T. Yamaguchi,M. Kato,K. Totsuka,B. Li,Y. Zhao,Y. Li,R. Grasset,O. Cavani,A. Alessi,V. Sharma,Y. Kohama,S. Suetsugu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过在自旋-1/2笼目海森堡反铁磁体（kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet）YCu3(OH)6.5Br2.5中引入电子辐照产生的可控点缺陷，研究发现约0.05%的稀薄无序即可强烈压制1/9与1/3磁化平台（magnetization plateau），并诱导缺陷相关磁矩与磁比热标度行为的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1/2笼目海森堡反铁磁体是研究量子自旋液体（quantum spin-liquid, QSL）与磁化平台态的重要平台，但结构无序对其量子态的影响尚不清楚。关键实验挑战在于通过可控无序连接本征区与无序主导区，并识别无序所伴随的涌现自由度。

Method: 采用电子辐照（electron irradiation）在YCOB中引入可控点缺陷，并通过高场磁转矩（magnetic torque）测量追踪低能磁态的演化；同时测量磁比热（magnetic specific heat）随无序度的变化。

Result: 约0.05%的辐照缺陷即显著压制1/9和1/3磁化平台，表明这些量子多体态对稀薄无序极为脆弱；磁转矩仅在低温低场下出现符号反转，指示关联的缺陷诱导磁矩而非孤立自由自旋（free spins）；原始样品中缺失的磁比热特征标度随无序增加而逐渐出现。

Conclusion: 稀薄无序可作为有效调控参数，驱动笼目反铁磁体从本征量子态转向无序主导区；同时对比性响应表明原始YCOB是获取本征量子态的极洁净笼目反铁磁体。

Abstract: The spin-1/2 kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet (AFM) provides a paradigmatic platform for realizing quantum spin-liquid (QSL) and magnetization-plateau states. Experimental progress, however, has been hindered by structural disorder, whose effects on these quantum states remain elusive. A key experimental challenge is therefore to connect intrinsic and disorder-dominated regimes through controlled disorder and identify emergent degrees of freedom associated with disorder. Here we introduce controlled point defects through electron irradiation into the spin-1/2 kagome AFM YCu3(OH)6.5Br2.5 (YCOB) and track the resulting evolution of its low-energy magnetic states. High-field magnetic torque measurements reveal that the 1/9 and 1/3 magnetization plateaus are strongly suppressed by only ~0.05% irradiation-induced defects, demonstrating that these delicate quantum many-body states are extraordinarily fragile against dilute disorder. Moreover, the irradiation induces a sign reversal of the magnetic torque only at low temperatures and low fields, indicating the emergence of correlated defect-induced moments rather than isolated free spins. Remarkably, the characteristic scaling of the magnetic specific heat, absent in pristine YCOB, progressively emerges with increasing disorder. These findings establish dilute disorder as an effective tuning parameter that drives the kagome AFM from intrinsic quantum states toward a disorder-dominated regime. The contrasting responses further identify pristine YCOB as an exceptionally clean kagome AFM for accessing intrinsic quantum states.

</details>


### [320] [Controllable charge-spin conversion in sliding multiferroic metal Fe3GaTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34295)
*Tongrui Li,Lidong Zhang,Weiyi Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作以第一性原理计算、对称性分析与Wannier基线性响应方法研究滑动铁电双层Fe3GaTe2的Edelstein与自旋霍尔响应，揭示铁电翻转与磁化取向可协同实现分量选择性的自旋积累与自旋流符号反转，表明其为可重构自旋电子学平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单一磁性材料内实现电荷—自旋转换（charge–spin conversion）对于紧凑型自旋电子器件（spintronic devices）具有重要意义，需要可控且可重构的自旋响应机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）、对称性分析（symmetry analysis）与基于Wannier函数的线性响应（Wannier-based linear response），计算双层Fe3GaTe2的Edelstein与自旋霍尔响应，并进行动量分辨分析与磁化旋转对称性分析。

Result: 两个具相反层间滑移矢量与面外偶极矩的简并基态间开关势垒约16.3 meV/每单胞；Edelstein响应强烈依赖能量，在1 V/Å参考场下m∥+y时自旋积累接近2 ħ；横向自旋霍尔电导率达~10^3 (ħ/e)(S/cm)，并随铁电态与磁化方向显著变化；磁化在xy与xz面内旋转显示对称性允许的高阶角谐波。

Conclusion: 滑动铁电双层Fe3GaTe2可作为可调平台，用于可重构的自旋积累（spin accumulation）与自旋流（spin-current）产生；其TR-even与TR-odd响应的符号由Mz𝒯（面内磁化）与Mz（面外磁化）分别控制。

Abstract: Controlling charge--spin conversion within a single magnetic material is important for compact spintronic devices. Using first-principles calculations, symmetry analysis, and Wannier-based linear response, we study the Edelstein and spin Hall responses of sliding-ferroelectric bilayer \ce{Fe3GaTe2}. Two degenerate ground states with opposite interlayer sliding vectors and out-of-plane dipole moments are separated by a calculated switching barrier of about $16.3$~meV per unit cell. Magnetization orientation determines the allowed current-induced spin components, while ferroelectric switching induces component-selective sign reversals governed by $M_z\mathcal T$ for in-plane magnetization and by $M_z$ for out-of-plane magnetization. The Edelstein response is strongly energy dependent, with spin accumulation approaching $2~\hbar$ for $\mathbf m\parallel+y$ under a reference field of $1$~V/Å. Momentum-resolved analysis links the TR-even and TR-odd Edelstein responses to $Γ$-centered constant-energy contours and localized interband features, respectively. Spin-conductivity contributions show the opposite momentum-space character, with broad sign-competing spin Berry curvature for TR-even and narrow Fermi-surface contours for TR-odd. The transverse spin Hall conductivity reaches $\sim10^{3}\,(\hbar/e)(\mathrm{S/cm})$ and depends strongly on ferroelectric state and magnetization direction. Magnetization rotations in the $xy$ and $xz$ planes reveal symmetry-allowed higher angular harmonics in both spin accumulation and spin-current responses. These results establish sliding-ferroelectric bilayer \ce{Fe3GaTe2} as a tunable platform for reconfigurable spin accumulation and spin-current generation.

</details>


### [321] [Fermionic superfluid transition and pairing scale separation in the kagome flat band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34342)
*Xiang Li,Yu Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用大尺度行列式量子蒙特卡罗（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）模拟研究了吸引型 kagome Hubbard 模型中的平带超流相变，确立了平带填充 ρ=5/3 的有限温度相图，发现超流转变温度与相互作用强度呈线性标度 T_c=0.0314|U|，并揭示出连接正常费米气体与平带超流的宽广预配对玻色流体区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带体系（flat-band systems）因动能淬灭（quenched kinetic energy）与增强的相互作用效应，被视为探索奇异关联相（correlated phases）的有前景平台，但其平带超流相变的具体机制与相图尚待厘清。

Method: 采用经快速傅里叶变换（fast Fourier transforms）与延迟更新（delay updates）加速的大尺度行列式量子蒙特卡罗模拟，系统计算转变温度、自旋磁化率（spin susceptibility）与密度压缩率（density compressibility）。

Result: 在平带填充 ρ=5/3 处建立有限温度相图，发现 T_c=0.0314|U| 的线性标度关系；局域配对形成与全局相位相干之间存在宽广温标分离，形成稳定的中间超可压缩玻色流体（supercompressible bosonic fluid）区域；且平带下的转变温度与密度压缩率相比 van Hove 填充显著增强。

Conclusion: 平带增强的配对密度涨落（pair-density fluctuations）在分离配对尺度与相位相干尺度中起关键作用，为冷原子与 kagome 材料平台中探测平带超流与超导提供了明确的实验判据。

Abstract: Flat-band systems provide a promising platform for exploring exotic correlated phases owing to quenched kinetic energy and enhanced interaction effects. In this work, we investigate the flat-band superfluid transition in the attractive kagome Hubbard model using large-scale determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations accelerated by fast Fourier transforms and delay updates.
