Through systematic calculations of the transition temperature, spin susceptibility, and density compressibility, we establish the finite-temperature phase diagram at the flat-band filling $ρ=5/3$ and find a clear linear scaling of the superfluid transition temperature with the interaction strength, $T_{\mathrm{c}}=0.0314|U|$. Our results reveal a wide separation of temperature scales between local pair formation and global phase coherence, which stabilizes a broad intermediate supercompressible bosonic fluid region of preformed pairs that bridges the normal Fermi gas and the flat-band superfluid. We further demonstrate that both the transition temperature and density compressibility are significantly enhanced in the flat band compared to van Hove fillings. Our findings highlight the essential role of flat-band-enhanced pair-density fluctuations in separating the pairing and phase-coherence scales, offering clear experimental signatures for probing flat-band superfluidity and superconductivity in cold-atom and kagome-material platforms.

</details>


### [322] [Theory of the $1/9$ Magnetization Plateau of the Spin-$1/2$ Kagome Heisenberg Antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34439)
*Pratyay Ghosh,Frédéric Mila*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用无限投影纠缠对态（infinite projected entangled-pair states, iPEPS）研究自旋-1/2 kagome海森堡反铁磁体的1/9磁化平台，发现一个27格点单胞的自旋密度波（spin-density-wave）态，其变分能量低于此前方案，并仅保留三重旋转对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清kagome反铁磁体1/9磁化平台的微观磁结构：更高磁化平台可由非重叠六边形上的局域磁振子（localized magnons）良好描述并保持六重旋转对称，而1/9平台的性质尚不明确。

Method: 采用无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）进行变分计算，并以互补的团簇平均场理论（cluster mean-field theory）进行交叉验证。

Result: 1/9平台对应27格点单胞的自旋密度波态，能量低于以往方法；该态仅保持三重旋转对称，由三类通过±2π/3旋转相互关联的9格点团簇拼接而成。团簇平均场理论正确保持高磁化平台的对称性，但在1/9平台同样出现自发对称破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking），并给出定性相似的九种局域磁化强度分布。

Conclusion: 1/9磁化平台与高磁化平台在对称性与磁结构上存在本质差异：前者发生自发对称破缺，形成三重对称的27格点自旋密度波序，两种独立方法结论一致。

Abstract: We investigate the $1/9$ magnetization plateau of the spin-$1/2$ kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet using infinite projected entangled-pair states and identify a spin-density-wave state with a $27$-site unit cell and a lower variational energy than previous approaches. In contrast to higher-magnetization plateaus, which are well described by localized magnons on nonoverlapping hexagons and preserve a sixfold rotational symmetry, the $1/9$ plateau only keeps a threefold rotational symmmetry and consists of a tiling by three types of $9$-site clusters related by $\pm2π/3$ rotations. These conclusions are supported by a complementary cluster mean-field theory which correctly preserves the symmetry of the higher plateaus but leads to the same spontaneous symmetry breaking for the $1/9$ plateau with a qualitatively similar pattern of nine different local magnetizations.

</details>


### [323] [Symmetry-resolved quasilocal entanglement as a key to Mott and pseudogap physics in plaquette CDMFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34470)
*Gabriele Bellomia,Carlos Mejuto-Zaera,Massimo Capone,Adriano Amaricci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出对宇称超选择双位纠缠（parity-superselected two-site entanglement）进行对称性分辨（symmetry resolution），以费米子负度（fermionic negativity）与纠缠相对熵（relative entropy of entanglement）为度量，并在Hubbard模型上揭示Mott物理与赝能隙物理的不同侧面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在强关联费米子体系（strongly interacting fermionic systems）中识别内禀量子资源（intrinsic quantum resources），需要能够区分不同对称性扇区贡献的纠缠度量工具，从而更细致地刻画强关联态中的量子关联结构。

Method: 引入宇称超选择双位纠缠的对称性分辨，度量采用费米子负度与纠缠相对熵；并讨论将该对称性分辨推广至纯态与混合态双位态（two-site states）的全纠缠。应用对象为Hubbard模型，先取二聚体（dimer），再采用团簇动力学平均场理论（cluster dynamical mean-field theory, CDMFT）的斑片（plaquette）团簇，该方法适于准确捕捉短程关联（short-range correlations）。

Result: 在零温下沿相互作用驱动与密度驱动的Mott转变（Mott transitions）追踪模型演化，结果显示双位纠缠的三个对称性分辨分量分别探测Mott物理与赝能隙（pseudogap）物理的不同关键方面。

Conclusion: 对称性分辨的双位纠缠构成一套可操作的诊断工具，可在强关联费米子体系中区分并刻画Mott转变与赝能隙区域的量子资源特征。

Abstract: In order to identify intrinsic quantum resources in strongly interacting fermionic systems, we introduce a symmetry resolution of the parity-superselected two-site entanglement, as measured by both the fermionic negativity and the relative entropy of entanglement. We discuss how the symmetry resolution can be extended also to the full entanglement in pure and mixed two-site states. We apply our recipe to the Hubbard model, first for a dimer, and then for a plaquette cluster dynamical mean-field theory, an established method tailored to accurately capture short-range correlations. Following the evolution of the model through the interaction- and density-driven Mott transitions at zero temperature, we highlight how the three symmetry-resolved components of the two-site entanglement probe different key aspects of Mott and pseudogap physics.

</details>


### [324] [Quasiparticle Interactions in the $S = 1/2$ Heisenberg Antiferromagnetic Chain CuPzN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34536)
*Leshan Zhao,Timothy Reeder,Matthew Stone,Daniel G. Mazzone,Jakob Lass,Christof Niedermayer,Daniel H. Reich,Anna Keselman,Oleg A. Starykh,Collin Broholm*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用非弹性中子散射研究准一维自旋1/2海森堡反铁磁体CuPzN中自旋子相互作用：零场下呈Heisenberg链的多自旋子连续谱（multi-spinon continuum），随磁场增加在q→0方向出现能隙2Δ，该能隙直接量化自旋子背散射（spinon backscattering）相互作用，并与矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state）数值计算及解析理论高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向同性量子自旋体系中准粒子相互作用（quasiparticle interactions）的实验量化长期缺乏直接范例，本文旨在以可积自旋1/2链为平台，通过自旋子背散射相互作用的测量填补这一空白。

Method: 采用非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）测量CuPzN的激发谱，在零场与施加磁场条件下对比均匀磁化进动模式（uniform magnetization precession mode）与偏移后的自旋子连续谱；并将实验与矩阵乘积态数值计算及近期解析理论进行对比。

Result: 零场谱呈现Heisenberg链的典型多自旋子连续谱；随磁场增大，在动量q→0处出现能量隙2Δ，其大小直接反映自旋子背散射相互作用强度，且实验与数值矩阵乘积态计算及解析理论吻合极佳。

Conclusion: 以可积自旋1/2链为例，研究表明各向同性量子自旋体系中的准粒子相互作用可通过实验量化，所测能隙2Δ为自旋子背散射相互作用提供了直接度量。

Abstract: We investigate spinon--spinon interactions in the quasi-one-dimensional $S=1/2$ Heisenberg antiferromagnet Cu(C$_4$H$_4$N$_2$)(NO$_3$)$_2$ (CuPzN) using inelastic neutron scattering. At zero field, the spectrum exhibits the canonical multi-spinon continuum of the Heisenberg chain. With increasing field we demonstrate the appearance of an energy gap $2Δ$ towards momentum $q\rightarrow 0$ between the uniform magnetization precession mode and the shifted spinon continuum. $2Δ$ is a direct measure of spinon backscattering interactions. Comparison with numerical matrix-product-state calculations and a recent analytical theory shows excellent agreement. By examining the integrable spin-1/2 chain, we provide an example of how quasiparticle interactions can be quantified experimentally in isotropic quantum spin systems.

</details>


### [325] [Spontaneous magnetic diode effect in Co$_{1/3}$TaS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34586)
*Yining Hu,Taikang Chen,Jan Priessnitz,Zili Feng,Takashi Kurumaji,Linda Ye,Libor Šmejkal,Chunyu Mark Guo,Philip J. W. Moll*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在三角晶格反铁磁体 Co$_{1/3}$TaS$_2$ 中，手性晶格与本征手性磁序共同产生一种由磁性（而非结构）决定正向的“磁性二极管”效应，其非互易电阻可通过翻转磁手性而反号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子在磁金属的自旋织构中运动时受到电荷与自旋双重耦合，但这类机制几乎总是互易的，电阻不依赖电流方向；常规电-磁手性各向异性（electrical magnetochiral anisotropy）又需依赖外磁场驱动，因此需要探索不依赖外场、由内禀磁序决定的自发非互易输运。

Method: 以三角晶格反铁磁体 Co$_{1/3}$TaS$_2$ 为体系，其手性晶格承载独立可翻转手性的手性磁序；通过输运测量观测非互易电阻，并结合对称性分析（symmetry analysis）与模型计算揭示其微观起源。

Result: 观察到显著的非互易电阻（nonreciprocal resistance），其来源是净磁化强度为零的内禀非共面自旋织构（noncoplanar spin texture）；对称性分析与模型计算表明，该效应源自非共面自旋序与自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的相互作用，产生手性且破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal-symmetry-breaking）的电子响应。

Conclusion: 翻转磁手性可使非互易电阻反号，证明二极管极性可由磁性调控，从而在手性晶格与手性磁序相互独立的反铁磁体中实现区别于常规电-磁手性各向异性的自发磁性二极管效应。

Abstract: When electrons move through non-trivial spin textures in magnetic metals, conduction electrons couple to magnetic order through both their charge and their spin, acquiring non-trivial equations of motion and a dynamic spin polarization providing the foundation of spintronics. These mechanisms are, however, almost invariably reciprocal and the resistance a current encounters does not depend on its flow direction. Here we show that a chiral crystal lattice and an independently reversible chiral magnetic order together produce a diode whose forward direction is set by the magnetism rather than by the structure. In the triangular-lattice antiferromagnet Co$_{1/3}$TaS$_2$, whose chiral lattice hosts a chiral magnetic order of its own, independent handedness, we observe a pronounced nonreciprocal resistance. Unlike conventional electrical magnetochiral anisotropy, which is driven by an external magnetic field, our spontaneous magnetic diode effect arises from an internal noncoplanar spin texture with vanishing net magnetization. Symmetry analysis and model calculations show that the effect originates from the interplay of noncoplanar spin order and spin-orbit coupling, which generates a chiral, time-reversal-symmetry-breaking electronic response. Crucially, reversing the magnetic handedness reverses the sign of the nonreciprocal resistance, demonstrating magnetic control of the diode polarity.

</details>


### [326] [Non-magnetic impurities as a quantum microscope of finite size effects in spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34596)
*Quentin Faure,Shintaro Takayoshi,Virginie Simonet,Béatrice Grenier,Stéphane Raymond,Eric Ressouche,Xavier Fabrèges,Pascal Lejay,Thierry Giamarchi,Sylvain Petit*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用化学掺杂将一维量子反铁磁链（quantum antiferromagnetic chain）切割成有限片段，结合非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）与数值计算，直接观测到受边界约束的自旋子（spinon）与短链中的无经典对应的集体反转团簇模（cluster mode）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects）在量子物质中普遍存在，但在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下通常不可见；随着少自旋体系实验可控性提升，有限尺寸物理成为探测普适物理与量子信息应用的可行途径，因此需要可直接观测的模型材料平台。

Method: 选取准一维伊辛型量子反铁磁体（quasi-one-dimensional Ising-like quantum antiferromagnet）Ba(Co$_{1-x}$Mg$_x$)$_2$V$_2$O$_8$（x = 2% 与 5%），利用非磁性 Mg$^{2+}$ 离子将 Co$^{2+}$ 自旋链分裂为有限链段，并将非弹性中子散射实验与数值计算结合进行分析。

Result: 识别出低能量子激发性质截然不同的两类区域：被限制在链边界处的自旋子（spinons），以及较短链中对应奈尔有序链段的集体反转、无经典对应物的团簇模（cluster mode）。

Conclusion: 化学替代（chemical substitution）可充当“量子显微镜（quantum microscope）”，为强关联自旋体系（strongly correlated spin systems）中边界塑造的激发提供直接观测手段，并揭示有限尺寸下涌现的新奇低能物理。

Abstract: Finite-size effects are ubiquitous in quantum matter, yet typically invisible in the thermodynamic limit; when system size is restricted, boundaries and confinement reveal modified spectra, correlations, and emergent phenomena. With growing access to well-controlled few-spin systems, these effects are now directly observable and offer a route to probing universal physics and enabling quantum information applications. Here, we study finite-size physics in the quasi-one-dimensional Ising-like quantum antiferromagnet Ba(Co$_{1-x}$Mg$_x$)$_2$V$_2$O$_8$ with $x = 2\%$ and $x = 5\%$, where non-magnetic Mg$^{2+}$ ions fragment the Co$^{2+}$ spin chains into finite segments. Combining inelastic neutron scattering and numerical calculations, we identify distinct regimes with fundamentally different low-energy quantum excitations: spinons confined to chain boundaries, and a cluster mode in shorter chains corresponding to a collective reversal of the Néel-ordered segment without classical analogue. Our results demonstrate how chemical substitution acts as a quantum microscope providing direct access to boundary-shaped excitations in strongly correlated spin systems.

</details>


### [327] [Electronic Responses in Solid Oxygen with Coexisting Structural and Magnetic Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34794)
*Naomichi Sato,Makoto Naka,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用多轨道Hubbard模型结合Peierls型电子-晶格耦合，研究固态氧中结构序与磁序的耦合，Hartree-Fock计算给出反铁磁序与三种键序共存的多相图，并揭示磁电、压磁等响应特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态氧（solid oxygen）中每个O₂分子具有S=1自旋，分子间范德华相互作用弱使结构高度灵活，且分子间跃迁积分小、具多轨道强关联特征，是研究结构自由度与磁自由度相互作用的独特平台；近期高压实验提示磁序与结构序可能共存，需理论模型加以描述。

Method: 采用带Peierls型电子-晶格耦合（Peierls-type electron-lattice coupling）的多轨道Hubbard模型（multiorbital Hubbard model）作为固态氧结构-磁耦合的最小描述，并在Hartree-Fock近似（Hartree-Fock approximation）下求解基态相图。

Result: 在位库仑相互作用（onsite Coulomb interaction）强时出现反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic order），弱时出现三种非磁性键序（bond-ordered）态；中等相互作用强度下反铁磁序与三种键序分别共存。三种共存相均破缺时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry），其中一种保持空间反演对称性，另两种破缺该对称性；后者呈现线性磁电响应（magnetoelectric response），前者呈现交变磁自旋劈裂（altermagnetic spin splitting）与压磁响应（piezomagnetic response）。

Conclusion: 这些磁电、交变磁与压磁响应可作为探测高压固态氧中磁序与结构序共存（如近期实验所提示）的实验特征信号。

Abstract: Solid oxygen provides a unique platform for studying the interplay between structural and magnetic degrees of freedom, as each O$_2$ molecule possesses an $S=1$ spin and weak intermolecular van der Waals interactions allow substantial structural flexibility. In addition, because of its small intermolecular hopping integrals, this system is known to be a strongly correlated electron system with a multiorbital nature. In this study, we examine electronic states and magnetic and dielectric responses using a multiorbital Hubbard model with Peierls-type electron-lattice coupling. This model provides a minimal description of the interplay between structural and magnetic degrees of freedom in solid oxygen. We apply the Hartree-Fock approximation to obtain the ground-state phase diagram. Antiferromagnetic order appears when the onsite Coulomb interaction is strong, whereas three nonmagnetic bond-ordered states emerge when it is weak. At intermediate interaction strengths, antiferromagnetic order coexists with each of the three bond orders. These three coexistence phases all break time-reversal symmetry; one preserves spatial inversion symmetry, whereas the other two also break it. The two inversion-breaking phases exhibit linear magnetoelectric responses, whereas the inversion-symmetric phase exhibits altermagnetic spin splitting and a piezomagnetic response. These responses may provide experimental signatures of coexisting magnetic and structural orders in high-pressure solid oxygen suggested by recent experiments.

</details>


### [328] [Orbital-selective correlations in kagome flat bands of Co$_3$Sn$_2$S$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35255)
*Rongxiang Zhu,Huaiqiang Wang,Haijun Zhang,Jianzhou Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作结合DFT+DMFT与最小Kagome模型，指出CoSnS近费米能级的平带特征源于轨道与自旋依赖的自能驱动、由$d_{x^2-y^2}$主导的几何Kagome平带向$\EF$移动，而非重费米子式的动力学带宽重整化。平带位置移动增强费米面附近的局域Berry曲率，产生强能量依赖的反常霍尔电导与巨反常能斯特效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何平带（geometrical flat band）是研究电子关联与量子几何效应的理想平台。CoSnS通常被视为中等关联的铁磁Weyl半金属，但近期谱学实验在费米能级（Fermi level）附近观测到常规密度泛函理论（DFT）计算未给出的平带特征，其物理起源缺乏解释。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论加动力学平均场理论（DFT + DMFT）方法，并结合一个最小Kagome晶格模型，对CoSnS中平带能量位置的关联效应机制进行理论分析，重点考察轨道与自旋依赖的自能（self-energy）贡献。

Result: 该平带特征并非源自类重费米子（heavy-fermion-like）的动力学能带重整化，而是由轨道与自旋依赖的自能将$d_{x^2-y^2}$主导的几何Kagome平带推向$\EF$所致。平带位置移动增强了$\EF$附近的局域Berry曲率（Berry curvature），导致反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity）呈现强烈的能量依赖，并产生巨大的反常能斯特效应（anomalous Nernst effect）。

Conclusion: 研究确立了将几何平带定位于$\EF$的轨道选择性关联（orbital-selective correlation）机制，并提出反常热电输运（anomalous thermoelectric transport）可作为探测平带诱导拓扑（flat band induced topology）的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Geometrical flat bands provide an ideal platform for studying electronic correlations and quantum-geometric effects. Although the ferromagnetic Weyl semimetal \CoSnS is generally considered a moderately correlated system, recent spectroscopy experiments have revealed a flat-band feature near the Fermi level ($\EF$) that is absent in conventional density functional theory (DFT) calculations. Using DFT plus dynamical mean-field theory and a minimal kagome model, we show that this feature arises not from heavy-fermion-like dynamical band renormalization, but from the shifting of a $d_{x^2-y^2}$ dominated geometrical kagome flat band toward $\EF$ driven by orbital- and spin-dependent self-energies. The relocated flat band enhances the local Berry curvature near $\EF$, resulting in a strongly energy-dependent anomalous Hall conductivity and a giant anomalous Nernst effect. Our findings establish an orbital-selective correlation mechanism for positioning geometrical flat bands at $\EF$, and propose anomalous thermoelectric transport as a sensitive probe of flat band induced topology.

</details>


### [329] [Anisotropic magneto-elastic coupling in the quasi-2D Ising magnet Li$_2$FeSiO$_4$ and thermodynamic signature of low-energy orbital excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35341)
*Waldemar Hergett,Kevin Ackermann,Sven Spachmann,Martin Jonak,Mahmoud Abdel-Hafiez,Hend Shahed,Michael Merz,Maurits W. Haverkort,Rüdiger Klingeler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合热膨胀、比热测量与数值研究，揭示准二维Ising型磁体Li₂FeSiO₄中自旋、晶格与轨道自由度的关联，并从实验数据中分离出轨道信号、量化能隙及其压力依赖，结果与DFT预测高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在阐明Li₂FeSiO₄中自旋（spin）、晶格（lattice）与轨道（orbital）自由度的相关性，并检验轨道能隙及磁-弹耦合（magneto-elastic coupling）能否由热力学实验定量刻画。

Method: 采用热膨胀（thermal expansion）与比热（specific heat）测量，辅以数值研究；通过热力学与Grüneisen分析分离轨道特征，并结合DFT计算进行对照。

Result: 提取出能隙Δ~300–350 K及∂lnΔ/∂p_c≈-4.6 %/GPa，与DFT预测吻合良好；长程磁序起始处的热膨胀异常表明显著的磁-弹耦合，T_N的各向异性单轴应变依赖以面内（c轴）响应(∂lnT_N)/∂p_c≈15 %/GPa为主，a轴（负）与b轴（正）依赖较小。

Conclusion: 短程磁序（short-range magnetic order）延伸至T_N以上较宽温区，但并非由与T_N相同的能量尺度驱动，证据包括非单调的面内畸变、Grüneisen参数变号及Grüneisen标度（Grüneisen scaling）失效。

Abstract: The relevance of spin, lattice and orbital degrees of freedom in the quasi-2D Ising-like magnet Li$_2$FeSiO$_4$ is demonstrated by thermal expansion and specific heat measurements supported by numerical studies. By means of thermodynamic and Grüneisen analysis, we separate the orbital signatures and quantify the energy gap and its pressure dependence from the experimental data as $Δ\sim 300 - 350$ K and $\partial \ln Δ/\partial p_{\rm c} \approx -4.6$ %/GPa). Both values excellently agree with the theoretical DFT predictions. The thermal expansion anomalies associated with the onset of long-range magnetic order imply significant magneto-elastic coupling and are used to determine the anisotropic uniaxial strain dependencies of $T_{\rm N}$. They are dominated by the in-plane (i.e., $c$ axis) response $(\partial \ln{ T_{\rm N}} )/\partial p_{\rm c} \simeq 15$ %/GPa while the (negative) $a$ axis and (positive) out-of-plane ($b$ axis) pressure dependencies are much smaller. Short-range magnetic order extends well above $T_{\rm N}$ but is not driven by the same energy scale as $T_{\rm N}$ as evidenced by the observation of non-monotonic in-plane distortion, sign-change of the Grüneisen parameter, and failure of Grüneisen scaling.

</details>


### [330] [Collinear and Coplanar Altermagnetism in a Penrose Quasicrystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35855)
*Bai-He Yao,Zhi-Yan Shao,Ye Cao,Fan Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文将此前基于群论的准晶（quasicrystal, QC）共线磁性分类推广至一般磁态，以Penrose晶格为例，发现属于D₅点群二维不可约表示（irreducible representation, IRRP）的磁态（含共线与共面）均对应交错磁性（altermagnetism, AM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作仅针对准晶中的共线磁性（collinear magnetism）建立群论分类，缺乏对一般磁态（如共面态）的覆盖，因此需要将分类框架推广至更普遍的磁性状态。

Method: 以Penrose晶格为例，采用群论分析磁态与D₅点群二维IRRP的对应关系；以单轨道Penrose-Hubbard模型为例开展线性响应理论（linear-response theory）计算，并结合Ginzburg-Landau理论分析两个基磁态的极化方向取向。

Result: 线性响应计算表明，参数空间中最大比例为这类二维IRRP磁态所占据；Ginzburg-Landau分析显示二维IRRP中两个基磁态的极化方向可平行或垂直，分别给出共线或共面磁态。其零磁性由不同IRRP基函数相互正交的群论正交性定理（group-theory-orthogonality theorem）保护。

Conclusion: 群论正交性补偿机制与PT对称性缺失共同导致AM态，可通过自旋分辨角分辨光电子谱（spin-dependent ARPES）中的自旋劈裂及自旋电导（spin conductance）加以验证；此类共线与共面AM亦可出现在其他准晶中。

Abstract: We generalize the previous group-theory based classification of collinear magnetism in the quasicrystal (QC) to general magnetic states. Taking the Penrose lattice as an example, we find that all magnetic states belonging to 2D irreducible representations (IRRPs) of the $D_5$ point group, including collinear states and coplanar ones, correspond to altermagnetism (AM). Unlike conventional collinear AM wherein zero magnetism is protected by, e.g. rotation or mirror reflection, which globally flip the spin in the magnetic pattern, the zero magnetism in these 2D-IRRP states is instead protected by a special group-theory-orthogonality theorem that basis functions belonging to different IRRPs are always mutually orthogonal. Taking the single-orbital Penrose-Hubbard as an example, our linear-response theory based calculations show that the largest portion of the parameter space is occupied by these 2D-IRRP states, and our further Ginzburg-Landau theory based analysis reveals that the polarization directions of the two basis magnetic states in the 2D IRRPs can be either parallel or perpendicular, leading to collinear or coplanar magnetic states. The special group-theory-orthogonality compensation mechanism and the absence of the PT symmetry finally leads to AM states, which are to be verified by the spin split in the spin-dependent angle-resolved-photoemission-spectrum and the spin conductance. These intriguing collinear and coplanar AM can also emerge in other QCs.

</details>


<div id='cs.CC'></div>

# cs.CC [[Back]](#toc)

### [331] [The Thermodynamic Complexity of Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33193)
*Anne Condon,David Doty,Niels Kornerup,David Soloveichik,David Wolpert*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 该论文以热函数（heat functions）构建电路的热力学模型，研究电路的信息熵产生（entropy production），并给出电路热力学复杂度的若干新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑不可逆（logically irreversible）计算步骤会产生超出兰道尔极限（Landauer limit）且不可回收的额外热量，现代计算机中该成本远超兰道尔成本；随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）称其为熵产生并给出下界，本文旨在将其引入电路层面以刻画电路的热力学复杂度。

Method: 采用基于热函数的热力学电路模型，分析求最优热函数（最小化熵产生）的计算复杂性，并对若干特定电路族（均匀分布输入）推导熵产生的紧界，包括 n 元 AND 树、串行链、Batcher 奇偶归并排序网络（Batcher odd-even mergesort network）及随机置换。

Result: 求门最优热函数是 PP-完全（PP-complete），其乘性近似为 NP-hard，但加性近似存在多项式时间随机算法；深度 O(log n) 的 n 元 AND 树需 Ω(n) 熵产生，串行链在理想模型中为 O(1)，体现能量-深度权衡；Batcher 网络熵产生为 Θ(n)，低于其 Θ(n log² n) 门数；随机置换下为 Θ(n log² n)，超过排序的兰道尔成本，但前置 Leighton–Plaxton 近似排序网络可降至 O(n log n)。

Conclusion: 该框架可对任意可交换输入分布（exchangeable input distribution）实现零电路熵产生的排序，并给出随机热力学中失配成本（mismatch cost）与模块化成本（modularity cost）已知结果的简化证明。

Abstract: When any physical system executes a logically irreversible (non-injective) step of a computation, the excess heat released over the Landauer limit is an energetic cost that cannot be recovered. In modern computers this cost vastly dwarfs the Landauer cost. Modern stochastic thermodynamics refers to this cost as the ``entropy production'' and has derived fundamental lower bounds on its value. Here we introduce a thermodynamic model of circuits using heat functions and investigate its entropy production, deriving new results on the thermodynamic complexity of circuits.
  We first show that computing the optimal heat functions for gates in a given circuit (minimizing the entropy production) is PP-complete and multiplicative approximation of those heat functions is NP-hard, but additive approximation admits a polynomial-time randomized algorithm. We give tight bounds for entropy production for certain specific families of circuits (on uniformly distributed inputs): an $n$-ary AND tree of depth $O(\log n)$ requires $Ω(n)$ entropy production, while a serial chain achieves $O(1)$ in an idealized model, showing an energy-depth tradeoff. The Batcher odd-even mergesort network on $n$ bits has $Θ(n)$ entropy production, asymptotically less than its $Θ(n \log^2 n)$ gate count. On random permutations of distinct integers its entropy production is $Θ(n \log^2 n)$, asymptotically exceeding the Landauer cost of sorting; but preceding it with Leighton and Plaxton's almost-sorting network reduces the total heat released to $O(n \log n)$. Finally, we show how to sort $n$ values with zero circuit entropy production over any exchangeable input distribution. Our framework also yields streamlined proofs of known results in stochastic thermodynamics on mismatch cost and modularity cost.

</details>


<div id='nucl-ex'></div>

# nucl-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [332] [Jet-like dihadron correlations in OO and Ne--Ne collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 5.36$ TeV with ALICE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35331)
*Neelkamal Mallick,Joonsuk Bae*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 该报告利用 ALICE 探测器在 $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.36$ TeV 的 OO 与 Ne--Ne 碰撞中测量双强子关联（dihadron correlations），以探究小系统中的介质效应；结果显示近端关联峰宽度随带电粒子多重数发生依赖多重数的改变，且 OO 碰撞中远离端关联产额相对 pp 碰撞受到压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重离子碰撞研究旨在探测夸克--胶子等离子体（quark--gluon plasma, QGP），而 LHC 上 pp 与 p--Pb 碰撞中类似流体动力学流（hydrodynamic flow）行为的观测提示最小系统中也可能形成 QGP 液滴。轻离子碰撞被用于填补小系统与大系统之间的多重数间隙，并检验流体动力学描述的适用性。

Method: 基于 ALICE 在 $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.36$ TeV 下采集的 OO 与 Ne--Ne 碰撞数据，测量双强子关联；将近端关联峰宽度作为中快度区带电粒子多重数的函数，在不同横动量（transverse momentum, $p_{\rm T}$）区间内相对于 pp 与 pO 碰撞进行比较，并首次在 OO 碰撞中测量每触发归一化的近端与远离端关联相对产额 $I_{\rm AA}$（Ne--Ne 仅测近端）。

Result: 在 OO 与 Ne--Ne 碰撞中观测到近端关联峰宽度随多重数呈现依赖多重数的改变。OO 碰撞中远离端关联产额相对 pp 碰撞出现压低，与喷注--介质相互作用（jet--medium interactions）定性一致，但幅度弱于中心 Pb--Pb 碰撞。

Conclusion: 观测到的远离端产额压低可定性归因于喷注--介质相互作用，但事件选择偏倚（event-selection biases）与冷核物质效应（cold-nuclear-matter effects）等其它因素在当前运动学区间对压低的贡献程度仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Heavy-ion collisions are primarily studied to investigate the properties of hot and dense primordial nuclear matter, known as the quark--gluon plasma (QGP). However, recent observations of hydrodynamic flow--like behavior in pp and p--Pb collisions at the LHC suggest the possible formation of QGP droplets even in the smallest collision systems. To bridge the multiplicity gap between small and large systems and to examine the applicability of hydrodynamic descriptions, light-ion collisions have been studied for the first time at the LHC. This contribution presents dihadron correlation measurements in OO and Ne--Ne collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 5.36$ TeV recorded with ALICE to explore possible medium effects. The width of the near-side correlation peak has been studied as a function of charged-particle multiplicity at midrapidity, showing a multiplicity-dependent modification of the widths in various transverse momentum ($p_{\rm T}$) intervals in OO and Ne--Ne collisions relative to pp and pO collisions. Further, the per-trigger normalized relative yields of near-side and away-side correlations, $I_{\rm AA}$, are measured in OO collisions for the first time, while in Ne--Ne collisions, the measurement is performed for the near side only. The observed suppression of the away-side associated yield in OO collisions relative to pp collisions is qualitatively consistent with jet--medium interactions, albeit with a weaker magnitude than in central Pb--Pb. However, the extent to which other effects, such as event-selection biases and cold-nuclear-matter effects, contribute to the observed suppression in the present kinematic regime remains an open question.

</details>

Sasaki*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于超级神冈（Super-Kamiokande）5003.0天数据的光谱拟合分析，给出了漫射超新星中微子背景（DSNB）中电子反中微子（$\bar\nu_e$）信号的迹象，最佳拟合通量为 $3.6^{+1.6}_{-1.5}\,\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}}$（$E_\nu>13.3$ MeV），相对零假设的显著性为 $2.6\sigma$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测漫射超新星中微子背景（diffuse supernova neutrino background, DSNB）可累积宇宙全部历史超新星的中微子释放信息；本文旨在提升对其中 $\bar\nu_e$ 成分的搜寻灵敏度。

Method: 对超级神冈 5003.0 天数据集开展光谱拟合分析（spectral fit），相较以往搜寻改进了大气中微子本底（atmospheric $\nu$ background）剔除、更新了系统不确定性（systematic uncertainty）评估，并细化了拟合方法。

Result: 在代表性 DSNB 模型下，$E_\nu>13.3$ MeV 时最佳拟合通量为 $3.6^{+1.6}_{-1.5}\,\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}}$，相对零假设（null hypothesis）在单侧 $99.5\%$ 置信水平下呈 $2.6\sigma$ 的偏离；不同谱形的信号模型给出一致量级的显著性。

Conclusion: 结果为 DSNB $\bar\nu_e$ 的存在提供了迹象性证据（indication），尚非确证性发现；其显著性对所假设的信号谱形不敏感。

Abstract: We report an indication of $\barν_e$ from the diffuse supernova neutrino background (DSNB) based on a spectral fit analysis of a 5003.0-day dataset in Super-Kamiokande. Key improvements over previous searches include enhanced rejection of atmospheric $ν$ backgrounds, updated evaluations of systematic uncertainties, and a refined spectral fitting method. Using a representative DSNB model, the best-fit flux was determined to be $3.6^{+1.6}_{-1.5}~\mathrm{cm^{-2}\,s^{-1}}$ for $E_ν>13.3$~MeV, corresponding to a $2.6σ$ tension with a null hypothesis ($99.5\%$ C.L., one-sided). Similar significances were obtained with signal models of varying spectral shapes.

</details>


### [334] [The design and flight of the Payload for Ultrahigh Energy Observations (PUEO)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35675)
*PUEO Collaboration,Q. Abarr,S. Agarwal,J. Alfaro,S. Ali,P. Allison,J. Alvarez-Muñiz,T. Anderson,H. Barnett,A. Basharina-Freshville,J. J. Beatty,L. Beaufore,D. Z. Besson,M. Betts,R. Bose,D. Braun,B. Chamanbahar,P. Chen,Y. Chen,J. M. Clem,T. Coakley,A. Connolly,K. Couberly,L. Cremonesi,A. Cummings,P. Dasgupta,C. Deaconu,J. Flaherty,C. Godden,P. W. Gorham,C. Hornhuber,J. Hoffman,K. Hughes,A. Hynous,A. Jung,A. M. Kofman,Y. Ku,D. Kullgren,C. -Y. Kuo,P. Linton,L. Lisalda,T. C. Liu,W. Luszczak,S. C. Mackey,Z. Martin,K. McBride,C. Miki,J. Nam,D. A. Newmark,R. J. Nichol,A. Novikov,A. Nozdrina,E. Oberla,H. Pumphrey,D. Radomski,B. F. Rauch,R. Scrandis,D. Seckel,M. F. H. Seikh,J. Shiao,G. Simburger,J. Tutt,A. G. Vieregg,S. -H. Wang,D. Washington,C. Welling,P. Windischhofer,S. A. Wissel,C. Xie,J. Yao,R. Young,E. Zas,A. Zeolla*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: PUEO（Payload for Ultrahigh Energy Observations）是一个气球搭载的射电天线阵列任务，通过探测南极冰中超高能（ultrahigh energy, UHE）中微子相互作用产生的阿斯卡里安辐射（Askaryan emission）来观测宇宙中微子，本文报告其设计考量与建造验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高能宇宙线（ultrahigh energy cosmic rays）在源区或宇宙学尺度传播过程中预期产生同量级能量的中微子（neutrinos），其通量、味（flavor）与能量测量可揭示宇宙线加速与传播机制；但预测通量极低，探测需要巨大探测体积。

Method: 采用气球搭载（balloon-borne）天线阵列方案，利用南极冰作为探测介质，探测超高能中微子相互作用产生的阿斯卡里安辐射（Askaryan emission）。

Result: 论文给出 PUEO 的设计参数及建成后的验证（as-built validations），涵盖从设计参数到实际建造实现的设计考量。

Conclusion: PUEO 为超高能中微子探测提供了一种基于气球平台的大体积射电探测途径，其设计实现与建造验证为后续观测奠定基础。

Abstract: Sources of cosmic rays at ultrahigh energies (UHE, $\gtrsim$ 1 EeV) are anticipated to give rise to neutrinos at similar energies by production via propagation interactions either within the source region or on cosmological scales. Measurements of the neutrino flux, flavor, and energy provide significant insight into those underlying processes governing UHE cosmic ray acceleration and propagation. Detecting neutrinos at these energies requires immense detector volumes due to their low predicted flux. We present the balloon-borne mission PUEO (Payload for Ultrahigh Energy Observations) which is an antenna array designed to detect the Askaryan emission from UHE neutrino interactions in the Antarctic ice. This paper reports on the design considerations and implementations for constructing PUEO, from the design parameters to the as-built validations.

</details>


### [335] [Magnetoelastic avalanches in magnetar crusts with plastic islands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33865)
*Arthur G. Suvorov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文通过数值代码求解壳层（crust）中含非弹性区域（塑性岛，plastic islands）的轴向（axial）线性化爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-欧拉方程（Einstein-Maxwell-Euler equations），研究了磁星（magnetar）壳层失效对扭转模式（torsional modes）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星高能爆发可能与壳层破裂或星震有关，磁弹性模式（magnetoelastic modes）被激发可解释X射线尾部观测到的准周期振荡（quasi-periodic oscillations），但既有扭转模式计算均假设壳层完整、未考虑失效本身，因此需要纳入失效动力学。

Method: 发展了一个数值代码，在含非弹性区域的腔体中求解轴向线性化爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-欧拉方程；随壳层元素位移追踪剪应力（shear stress）随时间的变化，使计算单元可在弹性与非弹性状态间动态切换。

Result: 壳层中塑性岛的存在促进相位混合（phase mixing）；当反射波相长混合且初始扰动足够强时，大范围区域会集体失效（雪崩，avalanche）。由于弹性支撑的逐步丧失，出现瞬态光谱特征（transient spectral features）。

Conclusion: 上述瞬态光谱特征可解释巨耀斑（giant flares）与磁星星暴（magnetar storms）之后观测到的振荡所呈现的多变（temperamental）性质。

Abstract: High-energy outbursts from magnetars may be associated with crustal failures or quakes. The magnetoelastic modes of the star can be excited to large amplitudes in such a framework, providing a natural explanation for the quasi-periodic oscillations observed in the X-ray tails of some events. However, previous calculations of torsional modes have been carried out assuming an intact crust without accounting for the failure itself. We develop a code in this paper that solves the axial sector of the linearised Einstein-Maxwell-Euler equations in a cavity that hosts inelastic regions or 'plastic islands'. As crustal elements are displaced, shear stresses are tracked as a function of time to allow cells to dynamically switch from elastic to inelastic states. The presence of islands in the crust promotes phase mixing and, when reflected waves mix constructively, large sections fail collectively ('avalanche') if the initial kick is strong enough. Transient spectral features are observed as a result of the progressive loss of elastic support, which may explain the temperamental nature of the oscillations observed following giant flares and magnetar storms.

</details>


### [336] [Torque lags from magnetic unlocking in neutron stars and gravitational-wave smearing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35117)
*Arthur G. Suvorov,Armen Sedrakian*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在典型扭曲环面（twisted-torus）磁流体平衡模型中，环向场（toroidal field）局限于极向场（poloidal field）磁力线闭合的区域，这些磁场"未锁定"区域可能比受电磁制动部分转得更快，从而产生频率高于极向成分的连续引力波（continuous gravitational waves）四极辐射；但耗散过程与有限观测窗口会使该四极成分的谱线在频带上展宽或移位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星表面施加的自转减慢力矩（spindown torques）未必能有效传递至磁未锁定区域，因此环向场区或磁空腔（magnetic voids）的转动可能快于被电磁制动的主体，作者据此考察由此产生的非共转四极（non-corotating quadrupole）引力波信号及其对搜寻策略的影响。

Method: 基于典型扭曲环面磁流体平衡模型的解析与简单模型分析：估计控制角动量输运的耗散微观物理（diffusive microphysics）所对应的时标及其对微观物理的敏感性，并对有限观测窗口内的谱重分布（spectral redistribution）进行定量刻画。

Result: 耗散过程可抑制长寿命的转速滞后（long-lived lags）；更重要的是，图案速度（pattern speed）与观测自转不同的四极成分会在有限频带上被移位或抹平（shifted or smeared），因此单模板（single-template）或窄带（narrowband）上限只约束落在搜索频率窗内的四极贡献，而不必然约束频率积分的总四极。

Conclusion: 连续波（continuous-wave）搜寻的现有上限解释需考虑谱重分布效应：窄带结果不能等同于对总四极强度的限制，作者据此讨论了该效应对连续引力波搜索策略的含义。

Abstract: In canonical twisted-torus models of hydromagnetic equilibrium in neutron stars, the toroidal field is confined to regions where the poloidal field lines are closed. As spindown torques applied at the stellar surface need not be communicated efficiently to such magnetically-unlocked regions, toroidal zones or magnetic voids may rotate faster than the electromagnetically braked part of the star. Continuous gravitational radiation from the associated non-corotating quadrupole may therefore be emitted at higher frequencies than poloidal ones to a degree that depends on the diffusive microphysics controlling angular momentum transport. Dissipative processes can suppress long-lived lags, and we estimate the relevant timescales and their sensitivity to microphysics. Nevertheless, we show that a quadrupole component whose pattern speed differs from the observed spin is shifted, or smeared, over a finite frequency band. Single-template or narrowband upper limits therefore constrain only the quadrupole contribution lying inside the searched frequency window, not necessarily the full frequency-integrated quadrupole. Using simple models, we quantify this spectral redistribution over finite observation windows and discuss its implications for continuous-wave searches.

</details>


### [337] [Tidal contributions to neutron star orbital precession around Sgr $A^*$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35776)
*H Lalrinfela,P. C. Lalremruati,Zodinmawia,N Malsawmtluangi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文研究恒星绕人马座A*（Sgr A*）轨道进动中的潮汐（tidal）贡献，表明其数值极小、远不及广义相对论（general relativity）进动，直接探测极为困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在量化中子星（neutron star）绕超大质量黑洞（supermassive black hole）Sgr A* 轨道进动中潮汐效应的量级，并与广义相对论轨道进动比较，以评估其可探测性。

Method: 采用 Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff 方程（Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations）描述恒星构型，考察潮汐进动对恒星致密度（compactness）、近心点距离（pericentre distance）及四极潮汐Love数（quadrupolar tidal Love number）k₂ 的依赖关系。

Result: 潮汐角偏移随近心点距离及致密度增大而显著减小，Love数越大潮汐贡献越强，反映其对 (R/r_p)^5 的强依赖；但预测潮汐偏移仅约 3.5×10⁻¹⁸ μas，潮汐与相对论进动之比约 10⁻³³–10⁻²⁸。

Conclusion: 在所采用模型假设下，相对论进动占绝对主导，通过 Sgr A* 轨道进动直接探测中子星潮汐贡献极为困难（exceptionally challenging）。

Abstract: We investigate tidal contributions to the orbital precession of neutron stars around the supermassive black hole Sgr A*, using stellar configurations described by the Tolman - Oppenheimer - Volkoff equations. We examine the dependence of tidal precession on stellar compactness, pericentre distance, and the quadrupolar tidal Love number $k_2$, and compare its magnitude with general relativistic orbital precession. The calculated tidal angular shift decreases strongly with increasing pericentre distance and, for the stellar configurations considered, with increasing compactness. Larger Love numbers enhance the tidal contribution. This behaviour reflects the strong dependence of tidal precession on the stellar-radius-to-pericentre-distance ratio, $(R/r_p)^5$. Despite the enhancement during closer encounters, the predicted tidal shifts remain extremely small, reaching approximately $3.5 \times 10^{-18},μas$ within the displayed parameter range. The ratio of tidal to general relativistic precession spans approximately $10^{-33}$ - $10^{-28}$, indicating that relativistic precession overwhelmingly dominates. Our results suggest that direct detection of the neutron-star tidal contribution through orbital precession around Sgr A* would be exceptionally challenging under the assumptions of the adopted model.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

### [338] [Virtual defect mediated quantum melting in supersolids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34299)
*Lazaros Tsaloukidis,Matheus Grossklags,Luca Salasnich,Alejandro Mendoza-Coto*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文指出，在浅外势晶格（shallow external lattice）稳定的软核准一维超固体（supersolid）中，零温熔化并非平均场（mean-field）所预言的能�量最小化机制，而是由虚拟孤子—反孤子对（virtual soliton–antisoliton pairs）增生驱动的 Kosterlitz–Thouless 转变，并给出第一性原理推导的重整化群（renormalization group）方程以确定临界性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对超固体熔化（supersolid melting）的理解主要基于平均场理论，认为转变源于结构能量最小化机制；作者希望考察该图像在低维、软核体系与浅晶格条件下是否仍然成立。

Method: 采用第一性原理推导对应的重整化群方程，并据此确定体系的临界性质；物理图像上以虚拟孤子—反孤子对的增生作为熔化机制。

Result: 零温下超固体可熔化为具有准长程序（quasi-long-range positional order）的超流相，转变属 Kosterlitz–Thouless 类型；临界点附近拓扑缺陷（topological defects）的增生对密度调制（density modulation）与超流行为（superfluidity）均产生强烈重整化。

Conclusion: 该转变本质上是超越平均场（beyond mean-field）的效应；在平均场层面，外势晶格仅在弱调制与强调制状态间给出平滑过渡（smooth crossover），无法描述所观察到的转变。

Abstract: The current understanding of supersolid melting is largely based on mean-field theories predicting that such transitions arise from a structural energy minimization mechanism. Here, we show that soft-core quasi-one-dimensional supersolids stabilized by a shallow external lattice can instead melt at zero temperature into a superfluid phase with quasi-long-range positional order through the proliferation of virtual soliton--antisoliton pairs, leading to a Kosterlitz--Thouless transition. We derive the corresponding Renormalization Group equations from first principles and use them to determine the critical properties of the system. Remarkably, the proliferation of topological defects near criticality strongly renormalizes both the density modulation and the superfluidity behavior. The transition described is intrinsically a beyond mean-field effect, since the external lattice produces only a smooth crossover between weakly and strongly modulated states at the mean-field level.

</details>
